Absolute Regression
===================

Chapter 751: Alright, You Come In Too
-------------------------------------
The Saber King awakened the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s savage nature.

It was a nature he had buried especially deep after meeting Geom Mugeuk.

He had awakened the violence of those days when he had driven Manak Sanin to suicide, that temperament from a time when he had no faith in human beings at all.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon was a man who possessed both that side of himself and the side that enjoyed calmly reading books.

“Sometimes even I get confused as to which is the real me. Well, I suppose it doesn’t matter.”

Whoooong!

As if loosening up, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon swung the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber.

The qi the two men were emitting had already begun the fight. If a street thug saw it, he would feel as though he were standing in an alley after a gang fight had broken out; if a true master saw it, he would feel as though he were standing in the middle of hell. Such was the contest of qi between the two.

For the Saber King, it was a first. This was the first time he had faced an enemy’s aura that meshed so well with his own cold and terrifying qi.

“Old Demon Venerable, you and I are of the same kind.”

The Saber King genuinely felt that way, but the Blood Heaven Saber Demon denied his words.

“The fact that being called the same kind displeases me more than being called old tells me, at the very least, what kind of person I wish to become.”

It was a statement made not to the Saber King, but to himself, and at the same time, to Geom Mugeuk and the One Flower Sword Venerable.

Geom Mugeuk did not let those words pass.

“Elder, you look the most splendid when you are reading.”

A faint smile formed at the corners of the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s mouth.

After living for long years as a Demon Venerable, and after reading so many books, he realized that he had never seriously considered what kind of person he was. Even after meeting Geom Mugeuk, he had tried to look into Geom Mugeuk rather than himself.

The fact that such thoughts arose during his fight with the Saber King was surely because he had seen his own past in that merciless, cold qi. His hidden nature, the one he wanted to dismiss as nothing more than the past.

Behind them, Taesu’s eyes were trembling as he listened to their entire conversation.

When the Saber King first called the Blood Heaven Saber Demon an old Demon Venerable, he thought he must have misheard. It must have been some epithet that began with “ma.”

But just now, he had heard it clearly.

‘Demon Venerable? Really, a Demon Venerable of the Demonic Cult?’

In this martial world, was there truly anyone who was not a demon practitioner yet could use the name Demon Venerable as an epithet?

Taesu’s heart pounded as if it might burst.

He immediately sent a sound transmission to Jihan.

—Did you know that that person is a Demon Venerable of the Demonic Cult?

Even as he sent the transmission, he secretly hoped for a reply like this.

Demon Venerable? What nonsense is that? Something that would tell him he had misheard again.

Isn’t it only natural to call him a horse venerable when this is a place that raises a thousand horses? He even hoped for an answer as absurd as such a delusion.

But the sound transmission that came back from Jihan was shocking.

—I knew that the two of them were Demon Venerables.

Truly, a gasp escaped him on its own.

A chill ran down Taesu’s spine.

‘Why did I never once think they might be demon practitioners?’

The answer came easily. It was because of Geom Mugeuk. How could one look at that man and associate him with a demon practitioner?

Only now did Taesu realize that he had been under an utterly ridiculous misunderstanding.

Of course. There was no way this was an escort agency, was there? There was no way those people were escorts or an escort chief, was there?

Judging from the aura and martial skill of the Sword King and the Saber King who had appeared as enemies, if such people were their enemies, just what was this escort agency supposed to be transporting?

Even if they were transporting the most precious thing in the world, such incredible enemies would not appear.

But even though the truth had already been revealed, he still could hardly believe it.

He had ridden in a carriage with Demon Venerables, and swum with Demon Venerables?

Demon Venerables had stood in line to eat five-spice pork?

Weren’t Demon Venerables the kind of people who killed everyone in line and every customer, then sat down amid the strewn corpses and said to the cook, “Bring out the five-spice pork”?

—Those two are the Eight Demon Venerables known as the Blood Heaven Saber Demon and the One Flower Sword Venerable.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s identity had been revealed. Jihan decided there was no particular need to hide it from Taesu.

Taesu could not grasp the fact that the Blood Heaven Saber Demon and the One Flower Sword Venerable had been with him all this time. They were people he had truly only heard about in rumors. And even those rumors had never once come from anyone who had actually seen them.

—Then who is that young man?

Geom Mugeuk joked comfortably with the two Demon Venerables and treated them without reserve. Just who was he?

Jihan told him even Geom Mugeuk’s identity.

—He is the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

—!

Taesu, startled out of his wits, shouted.

“Do not lie to me!”

He immediately covered his own mouth in alarm. He had been conversing by sound transmission, only to cry out without realizing it.

Everyone’s gaze focused on him.

Of course, among those gazes was Geom Mugeuk’s as well. With eyes that said he knew exactly why Taesu had shouted, Geom Mugeuk spoke.

“Now do you understand? Why I told you to meet my father before making your decision?”

Geom Mugeuk looked at the horrified Taesu, then turned his gaze to Dan Sojin and the Sado Alliance warrior standing beside him.

Geom Mugeuk had already noticed that Dan Sojin had known his identity.

Yet the man from the Sado Alliance with the sword wound was not surprised by this situation at all. It seemed he too had known the identities of himself and the two Demon Venerables.

In any case, one thing was certain. To the bastards behind this, the existence of these people was important. Especially that Dan Sojin.

Meanwhile, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon and the Saber King once again rushed toward each other. As the qi they emitted had grown even more intense, their movements had also changed.

The greater the master, the less emotion they put into their attacks.

But these two did not hide their emotions.

An exchange between masters who did not conceal what they felt.

Attacks imbued wholly with the emotion of I don’t like you! contained a killing intent that would not stop until the opponent was dead.

Kang! Kaaang! Kang-kang-kang-kang!

The three sabers that clashed seemed as though they might shatter at any moment.

Each time the twin sabers crossed and flew, it looked as though the moon set and the sun rose.

Each time the two men’s sabers were swung, powerful saber winds whirled and swept forth. If one did not raise internal energy to withstand them, they had the force to seize and fling a person away.

Beyond the battle, the Sword King looked at Geom Mugeuk.

The Sword King could tell. Geom Mugeuk was preparing to intervene if the Blood Heaven Saber Demon were to fall into danger.

That was why Geom Mugeuk did not look at the Sword King. He was completely absorbed in the fight. Only if he did not miss even a single breath would he be able to intervene.

‘Yes, this is the kind of person you are.’

A person who strives never to lose those he cherishes, a person who cherishes others even more than himself, and yet a person freer than anyone.

Kwaang!

The Heaven-Destroying Great Saber drove into the ground and blocked the incoming attack.

The Saber King followed up, flying in as he aimed for the Blood Heaven Saber Demon standing behind the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber. His body bent like a snake as he entered, swiftly sending out his sabers. It was truly a decisive strike.

‘!’

But behind the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon was not there. In that instant, a single thought flashed through the Saber King’s mind.

There is no way he would have let go of his saber.

The instant that thought turned into a sense of danger, the Saber King instinctively hurled himself away.

Whoaaaaang!

Kwaaaaang!

A palm force dropping from above swept through the spot where the Saber King had been. The floor shattered, the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber embedded in the ground tilted, and the Blood Heaven Saber Demon, who had been sitting on its hilt, stepped down to the floor and took up his saber.

The moment the Saber King’s attack came flying in, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon had leapt up onto the hilt of the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber embedded in the ground. It was a move made in anticipation that the Saber King would immediately raid the space behind the great saber.

“You’re quite something.”

The Saber King acknowledged that last move.

He had not expected the Blood Heaven Saber Demon to use such an unorthodox trick. It was a move that did not suit him at all, nor did it suit the battle up to that point. For that reason, this single move had been truly threatening. Was that why? This time, the word “old” was missing.

Swaeaeaaeng! Swaeaaeng! Swaeaaeng!

When they clashed again, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s way of fighting had changed. His use of the saber had become faster.

One would think that if the opponent wielded swift twin sabers, he would instead push back with power, yet the Blood Heaven Saber Demon increased the speed of that enormous great saber.

It was the first time Geom Mugeuk had ever seen the Blood Heaven Saber Demon move the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber so quickly and lightly. Perhaps in today’s battle, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon was feeling and learning many things.

Kakakakakang!

The Heaven-Destroying Great Saber that grazed past the tip of the Saber King’s nose swept past his shoulder again.

In the process, the Red Saber and Black Saber brushed past the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s neck and thigh.

Even amid the fierce battle, they were clearly maintaining the boundaries of the fight. One could tell simply by seeing that, despite this terrifying battle unfolding, not a single doll beside them had been knocked over.

But the area of that battle began to move.

It slowly shifted toward where Geom Mugeuk and the others stood. It was the Saber King’s intention. If they fought around them, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon would naturally have to pay more attention.

But that was the Saber King’s miscalculation.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon did not pay any attention at all. The Saber King did not know how great his trust in Geom Mugeuk and the One Flower Sword Venerable was.

And in the end, this choice provoked the Saber King’s temper.

It was because of Geom Mugeuk.

The Red Saber that had aimed for the Blood Heaven Saber Demon missed and grazed past right in front of Geom Mugeuk’s face.

Whoaaaaaaang!

Though the saber passed with tremendous force, Geom Mugeuk stood there without taking a single step.

At that moment, the Saber King saw it. He saw Geom Mugeuk looking at him and smiling without so much as blinking.

‘Insolent!’

But in a fight against the Blood Heaven Saber Demon, that brief surge of emotion brought about great danger.

Kaaang!

Puaak!

He barely blocked the incoming great saber with his twin sabers, but blood sprayed from the Saber King’s waist.

If it had gone just a little deeper, the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber would have cut him in half at the waist.

It was the first moment blood had been drawn since the two began fighting.

When he saw his own blood, killing intent burst from the Saber King’s eyes.

Wooong.

Red and black saber force began to coil around the Red Saber and Black Saber.

Then saber force also gathered around the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber. The pure white saber force looked as though snow had fallen upon it.

Watching, the One Flower Sword Venerable could tell that the color of the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s saber force had changed. Originally, it had not been such a pure white.

‘Your martial realm has certainly changed, brother.’

In the end, the Saber King was the first to unleash his unique martial art.

Sun-Moon Dark Blood Saber Art (日月暗血刀法)

First Art: Blood Dragon Sunders Heaven (血龍破天)

The Saber King stepped forward with his right foot, stomped down with a tremor step, and thrust out his saber.

As the ground split open with a crack, red astral force from the Saber King’s Red Saber whirled into the shape of a dragon and came flying in.

Kwaaaaaaaaaa!

The astral force that flew in with its jaws wide open was not an attack that could be blocked with ordinary saber force.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon also unleashed his unique martial art.

Heaven-Destroying Demon Saber Form, First Form: Heaven-Destroying Strike.

It was the Heaven-Destroying Demon Saber Form displayed from the hands of the Blood Heaven Saber Demon, now stronger than before he had left the cult.

Shiiiiiiiik!

A single swift and powerful strand of astral force poured from the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber.

Puaaaaak!

The head of the oncoming dragon was cut off, and the astral force that followed behind scattered in midair.

To break it so easily? The Saber King was neither surprised nor flustered. His opponent had already shown sufficient skill. If he failed to break it, that would more likely be the trap.

The Saber King immediately unleashed the second technique.

Sun-Moon Dark Blood Saber Art, Second Art: Extreme Sun Wheel Slash (日輪極斬).

This time, the Black Saber emitted a dazzling radiance.

Chwaaaaaang!

With a sharp sound, saber force poured down like sunlight. Dozens of strands of astral force rained toward the Blood Heaven Saber Demon.

What opposed Extreme Sun Wheel Slash was the Heaven-Destroying Demon Saber Form, Second Form: Heaven-Destroying Wave.

Kwaaaaaaaaaa!

The saber qi extending from the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber flowed like waves.

They were not the waves the Blood Heaven Saber Demon had shown before. These waves were deeper and grander.

Kwak kwak kwak kwak kwak kwak kwaang!

Dozens of strands of saber qi sank into the waves and were swept away. One could clearly see the saber qi failing to extend forward, instead being caught in the waves and driven backward.

But the aftermath of this collision shook the walls and the very axis of the earth.

At this rate, if they used even greater techniques, the cave might collapse.

But the Saber King immediately unleashed his next move. Even if the cave collapsed, he intended to kill the Blood Heaven Saber Demon before his eyes without fail.

The Saber King drew up even more extreme internal power.

Third Art: Blood Saber Chain (血刀連環)

Astral force forming a ring came flying in.

It was as if chains heated red-hot in a fire were flying in without any set path.

An attack that twisted this way and that, flying in at random—an attack whose course could change anywhere, at any moment, to strike who knew where.

The third form of the Heaven-Destroying Demonic Blade Art: Annihilating Blade Bloodstorm.

Several strands of blade qi extended from the Heaven-Destroying Great Blade, spinning as they flew.

The next instant.

The Blade King, who had been watching, opened his eyes wide. It was an attack far more powerful than the two before it.

Pak! Paaak! Paaak! Pak!

The incoming blade qi cut precisely through the linked rings of astral qi. The astral qi, its rings severed, lost its irregularity and flew straight ahead.

Kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwang!

The astral qi that shot backward was all dispelled by Geom Mugeuk and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable as they swung their swords. Had those attacks flown on as they were, they might have smashed through the rear wall and brought this place down.

“Old Demon Venerable, let’s see this to the end!”

The Blade King drew up even more of his internal energy. A brilliant radiance burst from the sun and moon engraved on his swords. It was just as the sun and moon rose at the same time.

The Sword King glided forward and blocked the Blade King’s path.

Though he could be attacked by both men, the Sword King had fearlessly stepped into this fight.

No matter how worked up he was, the Blade King could not unleash a technique at the Sword King, and the light shining from the Red Blade and Black Blade slowly faded.

“You dare interfere in my fight?”

The Blade King glared at the Sword King with eyes so fierce they seemed ready to tear him apart alive.

The Blade King was not the only one enraged.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon pointed his great blade at the Sword King. Demonic qi that had yet to tire still wavered around his body.

“Fine, you come in too!”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s air was as if he would take on the Sword King as well.

Of course, he had only said it for the sake of momentum; he did not truly mean it. He had already sensed that the Sword King’s martial arts were stronger than the Blade King’s.

That was when Geom Mugeuk stepped forward.

“Please hold back. That man who looks even older than you is yours anyway, Elder.”

After tossing a remark at the Blade King, who always made sure to call others old, Geom Mugeuk turned his gaze to the Sword King.

When asked why he had come, the Sword King had said he had come to protect the person who was trying to kill Geom Mugeuk.

“I don’t think the person Brother Ak needs to protect is that man. Who is it?”

At the very least, he thought that person could not be the Blade King.

Geom Mugeuk had seen correctly. The reason the Sword King had stepped in was not to protect the Blade King.

The Sword King’s gaze turned toward the rear.

Srrrk.

Then the door at the back of the space where they stood swung wide open on its own.

From within, a beautiful melody began to flow out, utterly at odds with the bloody battle that had been filled with thunderous roars.

Chapter 752: You Brought That Leather Pouch
-------------------------------------------
Truly, it was a beautiful melody.

But Geom Mugik knew. The sweeter this melody, the closer it drew to death. This was a sound drifting from hell.

When the short performance ended, a man walked out through the open door.

Tall and thin as a stick, his body was so emaciated one might say only skin and bone remained.

His cheekbones jutted sharply, his cheeks were deeply hollowed, and where his eyes should have been sat dead, white pupils. He was blind.

His gaunt frame looked ready to collapse at any moment, yet oddly, a strange cadence could be felt in his movements.

Was he listening to music only he could hear? His two legs stepping forward seemed to beat a steady drum, and the way he swung his long arms looked as though he were dancing to a rhythm. A blind man walked like this? It was a gait—no, a footwork—that made one think so instinctively.

Most eye-catching among all this was the pipa he carried.

The old, worn pipa was peculiar in that it had no strings.

Therefore, the beautiful melody from moments ago was naturally not played by him.

But Geom Mugik alone knew that this man had played it. Because he was someone Geom Mugik knew.

The Tenth King of the Twelve Kings, the Music King, Sinryu.

In the field of sound arts, he was known as a true genius, said to have no equal among martial artists throughout history.

That stringless pipa was his signature weapon, the Ghost Melody Stringless Pipa.

Yet among the Twelve Kings, he was not a particularly noticeable figure. No one knew whom he had killed or what deeds he had done.

Nevertheless, he held his position among the Twelve Kings until the end. With that frail body that seemed on the verge of collapse, with those eyes that could not see.

Before his death, he was said to have completed a terrifying sound art. Rumors circulated of a technique so powerful that no master could stand against it. But what kind of martial art it was never became known to the martial world.

The Music King, who had lived such a life, had revealed himself here today.

Geom Mugik could tell instinctively.

That the reason the Sword King had come here was to protect that Music King.

As if proving that guess correct, the Sword King was the first to move.

The Sword King, who seemed bound to no one, slowly stepped forward and stood beside the Music King.

The Blade King, whose fight had been interrupted, still wore an angry expression. He made no particular effort to hide his fury from the Music King.

Yet he could not insist on continuing the fight. Whether due to orders or to strength, there was an inexplicable tension radiating from the Music King at this moment.

The Blade King glanced coldly at the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, then strode over to stand beside the Music King.

It was the first time since Geom Mugik had begun fighting the forces behind them.

That three of the Twelve Kings had gathered in one place.

It meant that the forces behind them considered this matter extremely important.

Even standing between the Sword King and the Blade King, the Music King did not lose an ounce of presence. Had the Music King always been such a figure? Even Geom Mugik was inwardly surprised by the presence he displayed.

He was undoubtedly the protagonist of today's gathering.

For the Tenth King to take the lead when the First and Second Kings were present?

Geom Mugik's gaze turned toward the Sword King. Their eyes met in the air.

Just what are you plotting today? Why have you come here?

But the Sword King silently accepted Geom Mugik's gaze without sending even a single word via voice transmission. He acted as if he were not there.

Geom Mugik considered this a good sign instead. If the Sword King had started making excuses this way and that, it would truly be the worst situation.

In response, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the One Flower Sword Venerable flanked Geom Mugik in open challenge. Indeed, their very bearing declared, "Come then, let us have a three-on-three."

Especially the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Blade King glared at each other as though ready to kill. The heat of their earlier battle had not yet cooled. Although the Blade King had been slashed in the waist, their fight had been evenly matched.

The Music King looked directly at where Geom Mugik stood, as though he could see. In his hollow eyes, no emotion could be read, giving him an air that was at once mysterious and chilling.

"You must be tired from the long journey, Young Cult Leader."

Contrary to his appearance, his voice was a pleasant, beautiful timbre.

Though he spoke softly like a whisper, his words flew clearly and embedded themselves in one's ears. From his voice alone, one could tell his internal energy was profoundly deep.

"It was a pleasant journey rather than a tiring one."

Geom Mugik continued, looking back at the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the One Flower Sword Venerable.

"Because it was a journey alongside those I deeply respect and cherish."

The Music King immediately greeted the Blood Heaven Blade Demon. He was exceedingly polite, just as he had been to Geom Mugik.

"I have long heard of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon's fame. It is an honor to meet you."

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon looked at his gray pupils and asked.

"Can those eyes see?"

"These eyes cannot see."

"Then how did you know I was here?"

"I noticed because the one with the fiercest aura in this place stands there."

The Music King's gaze turned toward the One Flower Sword Venerable.

"You possess an elegant and noble aura. I am pleased to make your acquaintance, One Flower Sword Venerable."

Despite the compliment, the One Flower Sword Venerable was not pleased at all. On the contrary, she reacted even more coldly than the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

"You are mistaken. I have nothing but thoughts of killing you; how could that be elegant or noble?"

The Music King was not offended by her blunt words in the slightest.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon continued after her.

"Now, enough with the honeyed words. Tell us why you summoned us here."

The appearance of three such experts could only mean they had been waiting for them. One needed only look at the puppets that had earlier attacked Geom Mugik together. This place had clearly been prepared.

The Music King answered calmly.

"Of course, I too have a reason. Just as all of you do."

The Music King's dead pupils turned toward the One Flower Sword Venerable, posing a question.

"Why have you come here?"

It was a question that seemed to presume she had a reason for coming.

The Music King posed the same question to the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, to Dansojin, and to the martial artists of the Sado League.

No one answered, but looking at it, everyone here had a reason. Even Jihan, who had become a subordinate, had a reason for following them here, and Taesu likewise had his.

Finally, the Music King asked Geom Mugik the same thing.

"Why have you come here?"

Geom Mugik turned the reason back on the Music King.

"I was drawn here by a most beautiful melody. Even if I were to die, I could not turn away from such a beautiful piece."

"The Young Cult Leader is a lover of music."

"I am merely a man who knows how to kill people, so take this opportunity to teach me about music."

The Music King's gray pupils turned silently toward Geom Mugik, then he turned his body.

"Everyone, please step inside this room for a moment."

He walked back into the place he had come from.

The Sword King, the Blade King, the Dark Blood Heretic, and the Iron-Masked Man followed him inside.

Geom Mugik looked at the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the One Flower Sword Venerable. For now, the initiative lay with the other side; it was a situation in which they absolutely could not let their guard down.

The two demon venerables nodded. They knew what Geom Mugik's gaze meant. It meant to be careful, and then doubly careful.

Geom Mugik's group, Dansojin, and even the martial artists of the Sado League followed the Music King into the room.

In the massive space resembling a plaza, broken blades and weapons lay strewn about, and more than ten skeletons were scattered around.

Traces of a fierce battle.

Some skeletons had collapsed on the floor; others were tangled together, swords still embedded in each other's bodies; and one skeleton leaned alone against the wall.

In the center lay a coffin.

Passions flashed across Taesu's face.

'This is it!'

He now knew they had finally reached the Sword Tomb. To come here, he had risked his life diving into the scramble for the treasure map.

It was a place he had only been able to reach after meeting the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Cult and the demon venerables.

The others might not know, but he, a thief, had come to obtain the treasures here. He wanted to examine the swords those corpses held right this instant, and he wanted to run and open that coffin to see what was inside.

Of course, those people had probably already opened it. Even knowing it would likely be empty, he still wanted to open it.

The Music King spoke to Geom Mugik.

"This is the Sword Tomb written of on the treasure map."

"The atmosphere is different from what I expected. I had imagined a far more lavish and grandiose place."

"We have touched nothing in this room."

Whether that was a trustworthy statement remained to be seen.

"Aren't you curious what lies inside that coffin?"

"Even so, one cannot desecrate the dead."

The Music King answered as though he truly had not opened anything.

Just then, Geom Mugik saw the One Flower Sword Venerable staring intently at one of the skeletons. It was a skeleton kneeling with one hand propped on a sword, and the One Flower Sword Venerable's gaze upon it was unusual.

Geom Mugik went to her and asked.

"Do you know this person?"

The One Flower Sword Venerable slowly nodded.

"The sword in his hand is Silence. He was my maternal uncle. He used to visit and play with me when I was young. The last time I saw him before he disappeared, he spoke of the treasure map."

Now he understood. Why the One Flower Sword Venerable had wanted to come to the Sword Tomb. She had wondered if her uncle might have come here.

"I came half-hoping, and indeed, he was here."

Geom Mugik could see.

That the forces behind all this had used this Sword Tomb to draw him in.

If he had not left the cult with the One Flower Sword Venerable, they would have captured her when she came here alone and then contacted him. Either way, they had intended for him to end up in this place.

Then Dansojin sent a voice transmission to Geom Mugik.

—A venomous insect is hidden inside that corpse.

They had even arranged poison on a corpse that held special meaning for the One Flower Sword Venerable.

Having poisoned them with Purple Cloud True Qi Poison while they passed through the gate earlier, they had set a scheme to poison the One Flower Sword Venerable with this skeleton.

Perhaps the Blade King had intended to kill the Blood Heaven Blade Demon in their earlier fight. It had failed because they had not known the Blood Heaven Blade Demon was this strong.

Geom Mugik immediately sent a voice transmission to Jihan.

Jihan answered as instructed.

"Something smells from that corpse."

"What kind of smell?"

"It smells like bugs."

It was a way to use Jihan's sense of smell to reveal what Dansojin had discovered without exposing her.

Geom Mugik fumbled at his waist and produced a small container and chopsticks.

"May I be so bold as to inspect for a moment?"

Geom Mugik asked the One Flower Sword Venerable for permission. Sensing that something was afoot from the exchange and Geom Mugik's gaze upon her, she readily nodded.

Geom Mugik formally greeted the skeletonized corpse with courtesy.

"I am Geom Mugik, Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Cult. I hold the One Flower Sword Venerable, your blood kin, in higher esteem than anyone."

Geom Mugik bowed respectfully to the skeletonized corpse.

"For the Sword Venerable's safety, I wish to examine briefly."

Just as Geom Mugik was carefully examining the corpse.

Swish.

Something sprang toward Geom Mugik. It was so fast as to be invisible to the eye.

Snatch.

Of course, Geom Mugik, who had prepared in advance, snatched it in one swift motion.

What writhed between the chopsticks was a venomous insect with many legs like a centipede. Geom Mugik recognized it as a creature close to a spirit beast.

"It's a Corpse Blood Poison Centipede. Wherever did you catch such a precious venomous insect?"

The Corpse Blood Poison Centipede was a venomous insect that mainly parasitized corpses, possessing a lethal venom that could instantly kill even a considerable master.

A master like the One Flower Sword Venerable would avoid instant death. Instead, she would have to fight while forcing the poison into one part of her body, unable to exert her full strength.

If one were not invulnerable to all poisons, the Purple Cloud True Qi Poison would have taken effect unnoticed, and this Corpse Blood Poison Centipede would have looked like an accident; they would have seized control of the situation without drawing attention.

Geom Mugik placed the Corpse Blood Poison Centipede into the container he had taken from his waist and sealed the lid tightly.

"Our Poison King would be delighted if I brought this to him."

Geom Mugik had indeed prepared the chopsticks and container specifically to give the Poison King. If it was a venomous insect meant for the One Flower Sword Venerable, it would surely be a precious specimen. He had obtained an insect even more valuable than he had expected.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon sneered at the Music King.

"You said you didn't touch anything in this room?"

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable stared at the Sound King with cold eyes. Her anger was only natural. He had targeted her, and he had desecrated her maternal uncle’s corpse.

The Sound King’s expression stiffened slightly, and his gaze turned toward the iron-masked man.

The iron-masked man answered calmly.

“I merely did the work entrusted to me.”

At the very least, it was clear the Sound King had not ordered him to do it.

Geom Mugeuk’s expression hardened slightly.

This was even worse. In other words, it meant that the Sound King was confident he could win even without using poison.

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable walked over slowly, bowed, and then stared at the white bones for a moment. When she had left the cult, she had not thought much of it, but now that she had actually found the remains of her uncle from long ago, her heart ached faintly.

“Uncle, I will take you back with me now.”

Geom Mugeuk took a leather pouch for the remains out of his leather satchel and brought it over. It truly was a satchel that seemed to contain every odd thing imaginable.

“I will help.”

Geom Mugeuk began gathering the bones with her.

At the sight, the Blade King was about to step forward, as if to ask what they thought they were doing at such an important moment.

Just then, the Blade King felt someone’s gaze. The one looking at him was the Bloodsky Blade Demon.

The Bloodsky Blade Demon shook his head with an expression telling him not to interfere in this matter. His eyes held no emotion whatsoever, yet the Blade King felt as if his heart had frozen over. It was colder than any gaze he had received during their earlier battle.

The Sound King had found him because he possessed the hottest aura? He had misread the man terribly.

In that instant, the Blade King felt it.

‘This man’s darkness is even deeper than mine.’

The Sound King silently watched the two of them gather the corpse. Beside him, the Sword King silently looked at Geom Mugeuk.

Meanwhile, Geom Mugeuk finished collecting the remains and hung the leather pouch beside his satchel.

“Please bear with the discomfort for a little while.”

He had gathered the bones in advance because there was no knowing what might happen later. If a great battle broke out, the cave might collapse.

The Bloodsky Blade Demon, who had been watching, suddenly spoke.

“You did well to bring that leather satchel.”

Geom Mugeuk said with a smile,

“You finally acknowledge it!”

The Sound King, who had waited until the two of them finished gathering the corpse, asked Geom Mugeuk,

“Do you know where this place is?”

Geom Mugeuk had heard from Taesu a fact almost unknown to the world.

“Isn’t it the tomb of Ju Gasin, the greatest master of the Evil Faction three hundred years ago and the leader of the Sado Alliance?”

At that, the Sound King shook his head. A truly astonishing fact flowed from him.

“All the Sado Alliance leaders of past generations are buried in one place.”

Everyone listening was startled. Not only Geom Mugeuk, but even the Demon Venerables had never heard this before.

“No one in the martial world knows its location. Even the Sado Alliance Leader himself does not know where he will be buried. Only the family called the Baekchong Family, which has guarded and managed the tombs for generations, knows the precise location.”

The Sound King’s dead eyes turned toward Dan Sojin.

“Is that not so? Head of the Baekchong Family, Dan Sojin.”

Chapter 753: None of You Can Enter
----------------------------------
Dan Sojin, the head of the Baekchong Family.

At last, the moment had come when his identity was revealed. Truly, he was a man of a status no one had ever imagined.

There was a family that managed the tombs of the successive leaders of the Sado Alliance?

It was a fact even Geom Mugeuk had not known. This was something he would have to ask the Sado Alliance Leader, Baek Jagang, in person to know for certain.

However, judging by Dan Sojin’s expression now, the Sound King’s words seemed to be true.

After all, Dan Sojin, who had been so adept at playing his part among people with shameless ease, was not denying anything.

The Sound King looked at Dan Sojin and spoke. He was still courteous.

“So I finally have the honor of meeting you. From your bearing, I can feel a will and spirit like steel.”

Now that Dan Sojin no longer intended to hide his identity, his bearing had already changed. It was not an aura that stood out because his martial arts were strong. The vitality dwelling in his eyes clearly revealed his upright character.

Geom Mugeuk asked the Sound King.

“Why were you looking for him?”

The Sound King answered without hiding anything.

“To enter that tomb, of course.”

“What’s in there?”

“It is the tomb of the successive leaders of the Sado Alliance. Would it not contain all manner of divine weapons, rare armaments, and secret martial arts manuals?”

Geom Mugeuk quietly stared at the Sound King, then smiled as he spoke.

“For someone like you, who loves music, harboring such greed somehow doesn’t seem to suit you.”

The Sound King’s expression changed subtly. It was similar to the expression he had worn earlier when he was mistakenly accused of having arranged poison in the skeleton.

I am not that sort of person!

He was not someone who could completely hide his emotions.

Yes, people like you wouldn’t cause all this chaos for a reason like that. Divine weapons and secret manuals are all things that exist in this world. There must be something in that tomb that does not exist in the world.

At that very moment, words flashed through Geom Mugeuk’s mind.

—If the Sado Alliance Leader does not die, the murim will be destroyed.

What if the Sado Alliance Leader died?

The Baekchong Family would inter him in the tomb, and no matter how secretly they moved, they would not be able to avoid the surveillance of those determined to follow them.

If that Sword King tailed them, they would definitely have the location of the tomb exposed.

‘So that’s why they tried to kill the Sado Alliance Leader!’

He thought that their attempt to kill the Sado Alliance Leader using the World’s Greatest Beauty Contest had ultimately been for the sake of entering that tomb.

At the end of all those thoughts, one conclusion naturally emerged.

‘The last bead is hidden there!’

They were the ones who, in their earlier scheme, had tried to steal the energy within his body. Now, with that final bead, they intended to target his energy once again.

What became certain from this fact was that they could not simply take this energy away. They could only absorb it if they had another energy.

If so, this opportunity was their last chance!

Because they valued this energy so much, what Geom Mugeuk had to do was naturally decided.

Since they would surely try to obtain that energy, he had to obtain the energy of the final bead first.

Geom Mugeuk slowly walked over to Dan Sojin and stood in front of him.

“If your identity’s been exposed, you should’ve quickly hidden behind me. Why make me come to you?”

Dan Sojin’s answer came from behind him.

“I believed you would protect me even from afar.”

“Look at the three people over there. Which one of them looks easy enough that I could protect you from all the way over there? Even now, when I’m blocking your path like this, they’re not reacting at all, are they? That means they’re confident they can kill us either way. Especially that man over there, barefoot with a sword at his waist. See him? That bland-looking fellow—I’ve fought him once, and I know he’s no easy opponent.”

The Sword King still stood there with an impassive expression.

Let’s see how long you can keep refusing to react, Brother Ak.

Geom Mugeuk trusted the Sword King. To trust someone completely was always to accept the risk of betrayal.

But if it was the Sword King, he could gladly accept that risk. It did not matter even if the shoes at his waist were not the ones he had given him as a gift. It would be fine even if that old iron sword had been replaced by a dazzling treasured blade.

‘Brother Ak, I trust you.’

The Sword King merely received Geom Mugeuk’s gaze in silence.

Watching that sight, the Blade King sent a sound transmission to the Sound King.

—You must not trust Ak Gunhak.

He did not like it, as it felt like sowing discord, but it was something he absolutely had to mention to the Sound King.

—I know.

—Then that is enough.

The Blade King had requested that the Dark Palace Lord entrust this matter to him.

The Sword King had returned without killing the Young Cult Leader, so he could not be trusted, and the Sound King did not have the temperament to lead this affair.

However, the Dark Palace Lord had named the Sound King as the one in charge of this matter. There was surely some reason he did not know, but the Dark Palace Lord had not told him. He had only said to trust the Sound King.

‘I hope the Palace Lord’s judgment was correct.’

Geom Mugeuk turned back to Dan Sojin and suddenly asked.

“By the way, you are on my side, aren’t you?”

“What do you mean by that?”

“I’m asking whether I have any reason to help you.”

Dan Sojin struggled to hide his bewilderment.

—You say that after taking my antidote?

—Ah, right. There was the antidote.

—I have staked the fate of my family on you.

—When have we ever even met? Aren’t you placing too great a burden on someone you’re meeting for the first time?

—That… I admit.

Dan Sojin clearly looked as though he had many things he wanted to say to Geom Mugeuk, but there was no leisure for such a conversation now. All that remained was how Geom Mugeuk would answer this sudden trust.

Geom Mugeuk continued the conversation from before the sound transmission.

“Why are you silent? If I help you, what will you do for me?”

“Is there something you want?”

“Grant me one request.”

Dan Sojin’s eyes flashed sharply. He had anticipated what request Geom Mugeuk would make. There was only one thing the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult would ask of him.

‘Young Cult Leader, not even you can enter that place.’

That was why he attached a clear condition.

“If it is a request I can grant, I will grant it.”

He was a clever man, so one might have expected him to alter the condition Dan Sojin had set, but for some reason, Geom Mugeuk readily accepted.

“Good. You’ve promised.”

Geom Mugeuk turned back toward the Sound King and asked.

“Then why did you drag me into this?”

Of course, Geom Mugeuk knew it was to absorb the energy inside his body. He asked to probe how the Sound King would answer.

The Sound King gave an unexpected answer.

“Because we want you to persuade that man to tell us the location of the tomb.”

Geom Mugeuk felt it. Surprisingly, those words were sincere. They intended to use him to learn the tomb’s location. Then, after entering together, they would steal his energy there.

“Couldn’t you just take him away and torture him, or use soul-capturing arts?”

There was a reason they could not.

“They do not work on him.”

The gray gaze that had been looking at Geom Mugeuk turned toward Dan Sojin.

“Torture and soul-capturing arts do not work on the bloodline of the Baekchong Family. If they fall under soul-capturing arts, or if they feel pain beyond what they can endure, the restriction implanted in them activates, severing their meridians and killing them.”

That was how they had thoroughly guarded their secrets.

Only now could Geom Mugeuk clearly understand why both sides were trying to use him.

One was an opponent hard to escape from, and the other was an opponent hard to uncover.

At least one thing was certain.

Entering the tomb through Dan Sojin would be harder than dealing with those before him. Judging by that resolute gaze, he was not someone who would ever allow outsiders into the tomb.

“And you want me to persuade him?”

The Sound King nodded.

“Young Cult Leader, if it is you, you will find a way. That is why we called you.”

“You overestimate me too much.”

“From what you have shown until now, I believe you can do it. Look at the puppets outside. Look at whom you have been facing.”

Geom Mugeuk smiled pleasantly.

“How did you know I’m weak to praise?”

“Let us divide the treasures in the tomb in half.”

“You even know I’m weak to material greed! You really did investigate me thoroughly.”

Dan Sojin, standing behind him, stiffened despite himself. He had thrown his decisive move by trusting Geom Mugeuk, but what if his judgment had been wrong? What if the Young Cult Leader really did join hands with them? Geom Mugeuk was more terrifying than they were.

At that moment, Geom Mugeuk abruptly asked about an unexpected person.

“By the way, why did you leave that Sado Alliance martial artist alive?”

When the Sound King did not answer, Geom Mugeuk asked the iron-masked man.

“You left him alive, so you must know the reason, right?”

But the iron-masked man also gave no answer.

Geom Mugeuk saw through the impure intent they could not voice.

“Could it be that you need someone to testify that the one who did this was the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult?”

He asked as if joking, but a silence flowed as though he had struck the mark. From that momentary reaction alone, Geom Mugeuk could tell his guess was correct. If that was not the case, there was no reason to keep that man alive.

“After getting what you want, surely you’re not planning to kill us all, use some high-level evil art on that martial artist, and have him report that the one who plundered the tomb was the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, are you? Surely someone who plays such beautiful melodies would not have prepared such a vile method?”

When music was mentioned again, the Sound King’s expression hardened. His opponent knew exactly what sort of words offended him the most.

“What happens if I refuse your offer?”

For that reason, the answer to Geom Mugeuk’s question could not come out gently.

“All of you will die. Today, the Eight Demon Supremes will become the Six Demon Supremes.”

At this blatant threat, the reactions of the two Demon Supremes were opposite. The Blood Heaven Blade Demon glared at the Sound King with utmost coldness, while the Ilhwa Sword Sovereign instead wore a faint smile. Of course, the meaning contained in their reactions was the same.

“That won’t do. Only the people who like my father the most are gathered here. If they all die on the same day at the same hour, my father would be far too saddened.”

The Sound King misunderstood Geom Mugeuk’s words.

“Mentioning the Heavenly Demon will not change the outcome.”

It seemed he thought Geom Mugeuk was trying to resolve the situation with the Heavenly Demon’s prestige.

“Even if the Heavenly Demon were here, the outcome would not change.”

At that truly arrogant confidence, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Sovereign looked at each other. There was no need for them to be angry at those words. They were words that would anger Geom Mugeuk the most.

Geom Mugeuk was not excited at all.

“While taking on this task, you must have looked into me. So I thought you might know me even better than I know myself.”

Geom Mugeuk slowly walked over and stood in front of the Sado Alliance martial artist.

“What is your name?”

“Just call me Pung.”

“Did you come under the Alliance Leader’s direct orders?”

Pung hesitated slightly before answering.

“If you were someone worthy of receiving direct orders, I believe you likely know the relationship between the Sado Alliance Leader and me. You probably have a rough idea of my relationship with Young Alliance Leader Bi as well. So you may answer at least that much.”

“I left the Alliance under orders to investigate this treasure map incident.”

“From now on, Warrior Pung is definitely on my side.”

When Pung looked at him with a surprised expression.

“If you die here, wouldn’t I have no face to show the Alliance Leader and Sain? Let’s be on the same side for now.”

Geom Mugeuk turned back to the Sound King.

“This is the kind of person I am. Even this man, whom I met for the first time today, must not die here.”

Geom Mugeuk moved again and walked to stand before Jihan.

“He was my enemy during this journey and then became my subordinate. I still don’t know much about him. I took him as my subordinate simply because I had such a good feeling about him.”

A smile formed on Jihan’s lips. More than words like because he was capable or because his martial arts were outstanding, hearing that it was because he had a good feeling about him sounded far more pleasing.

Geom Mugeuk quietly stared at Jihan and spoke as if making a declaration.

“I intend to absolutely keep this man from dying as well.”

This time, Geom Mugeuk stood before Taesu.

“This man is the thief who had the treasure map. He is a man with a good father. He probably had no idea this journey would turn out like this, so he must be very shocked right now.”

Taesu’s eyes trembled as he looked at Geom Mugeuk. Now that he knew Geom Mugeuk’s identity, he no longer dared to make even a single joke.

“He has not yet formed a relationship with me, but I will bring this man out alive too. After all, he succeeded in stealing my heart.”

Taesu was deeply moved. To the Young Cult Leader of the Demon Cult, it was nothing at all, something he could have simply ignored and been done with, yet he was making it into something so meaningful.

Watching this, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Sovereign understood. It was because Geom Mugeuk was this sort of person that he had become so strong. Because he had fought battles where he survived even while dragging along hostages one after another, he had become far stronger.

On the other hand, the Sound King could not understand Geom Mugeuk at all.

“Why are you trying so hard to keep everyone alive?”

No matter how politely he asked, the tone of his words made it clear that something had been omitted.

Even such insignificant people.

“There are characters that cannot be hidden even by beautiful melodies, I suppose.”

When music was dragged into it again, the Sound King finally shouted as if exploding.

“Shut your mouth!”

Then Geom Mugeuk roared.

“You shut yours!”

Everyone’s gaze focused on Geom Mugeuk.

“You dare spout nonsense in front of me about being able to kill my father? You dare threaten to kill my Demon Supremes?”

Even though he had not fully revealed his aura in earnest, Geom Mugeuk’s force was tremendous.

“Yes, you truly are one who cannot see ahead. Seeing you say such things in front of me, you are a man who sees nothing at all.”

Geom Mugeuk’s cold gaze slowly looked over the Sound King, the Blade King, the Dark Blood Death Apostle, and the iron-masked martial artist.

“None of you will enter that tomb. Because you’ll all die here and now.”

Geom Mugeuk spread his wings. As if they had been waiting for it, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Sovereign simultaneously drew the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber and the Ilhwa Sword at his left and right.

The place where Geom Mugeuk’s gaze finally stopped was the Sword King.

“Brother Ak, step outside.”

Chapter 754: What I hate is that ponytail of yours!
---------------------------------------------------
Geom Mugeuk revealed his demonic energy.

Swaaaaaaaaaah.

An immense wave of demonic energy flew toward those standing ahead.

Unable to withstand it as they were, the Dark Blood Apostle wrapped his body in his wind-repelling cloak and retreated, while the iron-masked man also drew up his inner force to resist the demonic energy.

It was rough, powerful demonic energy. Geom Mugeuk usually showed an aura as smooth as a sea reflecting the sky, but now he was different.

It was demonic energy that chilled the spine and sent shudders through the body. The same thought surfaced in everyone’s mind.

So this is demonic energy.

Demonic energy so powerful that fear made it hard to move.

Geom Mugeuk intended to sweep away everything before him with the Great Annihilation Form of the Nine-Flame Demonic Art. In any case, this would only end when one side was dead.

Can you really stop my Nine-Flame Demonic Art, which has reached great mastery? You, too, sleep here in this tomb!

Then the next treasure map would wander through the murim wrapped in even more sensational rumors.

So hurry up and leave, Brother Ak.

Everyone’s gaze turned toward Ak Gunhak.

As though burdened by those stares, Ak Gunhak blinked, then slowly moved his feet. As if he truly meant to leave, he walked toward Geom Mugeuk.

The Saber King asked, half in disbelief.

“What are you doing right now?”

“He told me to leave, did he not?”

The Saber King’s expression hardened at once. The fact that he could not tell whether it was a joke or the truth annoyed him first of all.

“You were on the Young Cult Leader’s side from the start, weren’t you? This whole situation was arranged in advance.”

“Can’t you see the Young Cult Leader is truly angry? When someone like that gets angry, he’s genuinely frightening.”

The Sword King walked back toward Geom Mugeuk and his group.

He stopped in front of Geom Mugeuk.

“Why are you so angry?”

“I can’t tolerate insults to my parents, you see. And the two behind me are no different from my parents as well.”

At those words, the eyes of the two Demon Venerables listening deepened. They knew he had not said it merely to sound pleasing.

Just when it seemed he might leave as he was, the Sword King turned around again and walked toward the place where the Sound King stood.

“Calm yourself now, and enjoy the music for the moment.”

In truth, Geom Mugeuk had sensed it. That he had never intended to leave in the first place. If he were the sort to leave just because he was told to, he would not have come at all.

“Brother Ak, move aside. I’m truly angry.”

“I know.”

Saying he knew, the Sword King stood squarely in front of the Sound King, as if to make a point.

The Sound King’s eyes, as he stood behind him and looked at Geom Mugeuk, were colder than ever. He did not understand why Geom Mugeuk was angry. The one who was truly angry was himself.

Who was it who had mocked his music and looked down on him?

The Sound King raised the pipa as if to play it.

“I did not think the Young Cult Leader would obediently listen to me.”

Ting.

The moment the Sound King plucked the pipa!

A low, deep ripple crossed the air.

Kaang!

The Black Demon Sword thrust into empty space trembled.

It had been a light, playful pluck, yet its power was formidable. Moreover, it was not ordinary gangqi. Just as sound could not be seen, the incoming attack was invisible to the eye. It had to be blocked by instinct. With a single flick of his finger, one could tell this was sound art of the highest level.

“You said you would protect them all? Then let us see you protect them.”

The Sound King plucked the stringless pipa again. For an instant, it seemed the air itself burst.

Tuaang!

Boom!

This time, Geom Mugeuk was blocking the attack with his sword in front of Jihan. He had moved in an instant and stopped the attack flying toward him.

This attack was even more powerful than the last. But from his relaxed expression, one could tell that he still had not truly exerted his strength.

“For the force of someone plucking pipa strings, that’s far too strong. You’d be better off becoming a woodcutter.”

Taaaaang!

Bang!

This time, he targeted a martial artist of the Sado Alliance. The Sado Alliance martial artist was far stronger than Jihan or Taesu, but that did not mean he was skilled enough to block this attack from the Sound King.

Jiiiiing.

The Black Demon Sword vibrated in the air. The vibration left by the sound art flowed along the sword. His wrist tingled.

“As expected of the Young Cult Leader. Then can you block this as well?”

Since it seemed an extraordinary attack was about to come, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon tried to step forward.

But the Ilhwa Sword Venerable stopped him. It meant they should leave it to Geom Mugeuk unless he asked for help.

Tarararang!

This sound art flew toward three people at the same time.

Boom! Boom! Baaang!

Geom Mugeuk appeared in front of all three at once and blocked it. His movement was truly like a flash of lightning.

At that astonishing divine might, the Sound King was instead delighted.

“For our Young Cult Leader, I shall have to play a very exciting piece.”

Geom Mugeuk shouted at the Sword King.

“Brother Ak! At this rate, your younger brother will die! Move aside!”

He knew it was exaggeration, but even so, was he going to keep blocking the front in this situation where he was helplessly being attacked?

“Is he perhaps a hidden child of yours?”

At that moment, Geom Mugeuk saw it. The Sword King was forcing himself not to laugh. Yes, the Sword King was still the same Sword King as before. Such a sight told him more than a hundred words could.

In truth, if he resolved to kill, he could kill the Sound King behind him even with the Sword King blocking the way.

He only had to use the Extermination Form of the Nine-Flame Demonic Art on the Sound King. Could he truly block the attacks of the four evil spirits of east, west, south, and north, now that they had reached great mastery?

Even without the Extermination Form, there were other methods.

What if he drove the Soul-Severing Demonic Strike down into the crowns of all of their heads?

Even so, the only reason he did not kill him was because of the Sword King.

Because of his trust in him.

Was the Sword King truly a rigid man? Was the Sword King truly a frustrating man?

No. He was more clever and more composed than anyone. When someone like that acted this frustratingly, there had to be a reason for it.

He had not sent even a single word by sound transmission, but he was speaking through his actions.

The Sound King must not be killed.

The fact that he was not telling him the reason must also be due to circumstances of his own.

It was right then. The Ilhwa Sword Venerable, who had only been watching, walked forward.

“Young Cult Leader, please leave this to me.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at her. Now that she had stepped forward of her own will, he could not tell her no. It was not as if she had stepped forward without knowing the relationship between him and the Sword King.

Now this fight was theirs.

“Yes, I entrust it to you, Sword Venerable.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at the Sword King.

“Brother Ak, now you are in serious trouble. You should have moved when I told you to. Our Sword Venerable has stepped forward.”

Even though the Ilhwa Sword Venerable had come forward, the Sword King still stood there silently.

Geom Mugeuk coldly warned the Sound King behind the Sword King.

“If you interfere in this fight and try to attack the Sword Venerable, you really will die. Even Brother Ak won’t be able to stop that. I talk so much nonsense that you may be confused about what is sincere, but I do not joke when it comes to matters of life and death. The moment you twitch even one finger, you die.”

The Sound King looked at Geom Mugeuk with a strange gaze, then answered with a smile.

“I have no intention of interfering.”

The Sound King removed his hand from the pipa entirely and fastened it at his waist.

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable stood facing the Sword King. He had expected her to treat him politely out of consideration for his relationship with Geom Mugeuk. But she did not.

“Move.”

When they first met, they had at least exchanged a light greeting with clasped fists, but now the Ilhwa Sword Venerable was cold.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon smiled faintly. Yes, it was not as if he was the only one living while suppressing his temper.

The Sword King, who had been quietly gazing at her, responded in kind.

“No.”

As if she had expected that, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable nodded.

“This is what I dislike. The fact that you have formed such a bond with the Young Cult Leader, those bare feet of yours, that ponytail of yours!”

Because the Young Cult Leader must have been drawn in by that unique charm that was hard to find in anyone else.

The Sword King tapped the hair he had tied tightly back.

“I cannot give this up, though?”

Geom Mugeuk cut in and said a word.

“That means you can give up on me!”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable steadied her mind. She did not regard this fight as one against someone the Young Cult Leader knew. This fight was against an enemy trying to protect the force behind the scenes.

“The more I look at you, the more dangerous you are.”

Just seeing him block the way in a situation like this was enough to prove it. His relationship with the Young Cult Leader was their own matter.

Right now, she was looking at him as the Demon Venerable, Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon silently nodded. He thought the same. If he could, he wanted to step forward himself.

But just as he had crossed sabers with that expert wielding twin sabers, this fight belonged to her.

The problem was that this barefoot man felt stronger than the one who had pointed his saber earlier.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon looked at Geom Mugeuk. The one who knew the barefoot man best was the Young Cult Leader.

Is it all right to let those two fight like this?

But for whatever reason, Geom Mugeuk was not stopping them.

Fine. If you believe in it, then I should believe as well.

But since it concerned the Ilhwa Sword Venerable, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon could not help feeling uneasy.

A cold chill flowed between the Ilhwa Sword Venerable and the Sword King. A tension as taut as the one between the Blood Heaven Saber Demon and the Saber King earlier—no, perhaps even more taut—filled the air.

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable had already heard from Geom Mugeuk just how powerful the person before her was.

A life-and-death battle against such a master was an opportunity that might not come even once in a lifetime.

The two charged toward each other.

Kaaang!

The moment sword met sword, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable felt the boundless power contained in the opponent’s blade.

He had looked like someone as light as the wind.

‘His sword is heavy.’

His sword was heavy, as though Mount Tai itself were crashing down upon her. This was not a matter of how much inner force was loaded into it.

Seen up close, the opponent felt different again. It was an entirely different impression from the way he had traded jokes with Geom Mugeuk.

Shiiik.

Letting the Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s blade slide away, the Sword King counterattacked.

Shiiik!

The sword edge flashed past before her eyes in an instant. Had she dodged even a little later, both her eyes would have been blinded by that move. It was truly a move that snapped her mind awake.

Changchangchangchangchang!

The swords of the two grew fast in an instant. There was no feeling each other out. Their swords raced at top speed from the start, flashing sword light through the air.

Aside from Geom Mugeuk, the Demon Venerables, the Sound King, and the Saber King, no one else could follow that speed with their eyes.

Flashes burst in all directions, one after another. It was like watching fireworks.

The trajectories and afterimages of the swords piled up, forming a single painting.

It was like bamboo swaying in the wind, and plum blossoms were blooming in the depths of winter.

Those who properly appreciated the painting were Geom Mugeuk, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon, the Sound King, and the Saber King. Even while seeing the same painting, each felt a different impression according to their own skill and inner state.

At times the sword strokes were thin; at times they were thick, like ink swept on with a broad brush.

And the empty spaces.

In the empty spaces they created, there were chances for counterattacks and surprise assaults.

The empty spaces were opportunities and, at the same time, the greatest danger. If one used an empty space wrongly, it meant immediate defeat and death.

Each time a new sword stroke filled an empty space, the two faced a moment between life and death.

‘This person’s sword is real.’

The Ilhwa Sword, a treasured blade, was unable to break that old iron sword.

The moment she sincerely acknowledged her opponent, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable felt it. Something was awakening in her heart.

‘I will win.’

Their fight was graceful and yet fierce. Moves that led to death with a single mistake came and went, but they did not fear those moves.

There was not even an opportunity to display their secret arts. The moment either tried to unleash a great martial art, an arm or a neck would be cut off as it was.

Those watching held their breath.

The chance to witness a sword duel between masters who had reached such a realm might come once or twice in an entire lifetime. For that reason, everyone watched the fight without even blinking. If they blinked, the two would already be exchanging a different move.

The Sword King was surprised. He had not known the Ilhwa Sword Venerable could fight this well.

At first, when he saw Geom Mugeuk permit this fight, he had thought it meant to teach her a move.

‘So it was not permission only for her sake.’

It was Geom Mugeuk’s goodwill, telling him to learn and grow through this fight as well.

‘Honestly, you!’

Even knowing this would become such a terrifying fight, you push your precious Demon Venerable into it.

Just how far do you trust me?

For that reason, to the Sword King, this fight was also a fight with Geom Mugeuk. His opponent was pressing him with skill that could not be taken lightly, yet he had to take care.

The Sword King exercised concentration to its absolute limit. Since the day he first took up the sword, this was the first time he had fought with such all-out devotion.

Shwaaaaaak.

The domain of the fight gradually widened.

The two exchanged swords as they passed before Geom Mugeuk, then passed by the Blood Heaven Saber Demon.

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon glared fiercely at the Sword King, but the Sword King had no leisure to receive his gaze.

The remaining four people stood like dolls, eyes tightly shut. Sword light was flashing beside them; if they moved rashly, it would mean death.

Astonishingly, even while fighting such an incredible battle, they did not injure anyone around them.

But the same could not be said for elsewhere.

Chwaaaaaak!

Hundreds of sword lines had already appeared on the wall, and one side of the ceiling had collapsed.

And this was even though they had not yet truly used sword qi or sword aura. That was how fiercely the two fought.

Sword fought sword, momentum fought momentum, and the lives the two had lived fought each other.

Through this fight, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable instinctively felt that she was growing. It was different from when she had sparred with Geom Mugeuk.

This fight was real. She attacked with the intent to cut off the opponent’s arm, and she had the resolve to lose her own arm in return.

At some point, she fell into a state of selflessness.

Gradually, the sound of sword striking sword lessened, and the surroundings began to grow quiet.

Everything began to move slowly.

Pat!

A drop of blood rose into the air. It was impossible to know whose body it had flown from.

At that moment, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable experienced time flowing slowly.

A single drop of blood suspended in midair.

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable saw her own face reflected in that blood. Was it an illusion? Or could she truly see it? Or had she already been cut?

Puaaaak!

Her face reflected in the blood split in half.

The next moment, time flowed as it should, and sound returned.

Chwaaaaaak!

Shwaaaaaak.

A silence that arrived in an instant.

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s body was twisted to the side.

One of her shoulders was soaked with blood. If she had failed to avoid it, perhaps her body would have been severed like the version of herself inside that drop of blood.

At the same time.

Srrrk.

The Sword King’s forelocks were cut off and slid down.

A faint sword line had appeared on his forehead. Fortunately, it was only a cut on the skin.

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable and the Sword King looked at each other. They could not hide the emotion in their eyes. Would they ever again meet such a magnificent opponent in their lives?

It seemed like a situation where they would clasp fists politely and say that it had been a good fight. It seemed like someone would say it had been the finest battle.

“I simply cannot allow this.”

One might have thought the Ilhwa Sword Venerable would say she could no longer fight because of her injury. But that was not it. She was saying she could not allow it because she was angry. She was saying she could not allow it because she was dissatisfied.

“I absolutely must cut off that ponytail today.”

Chapter 755: That Won’t Happen in This Fight
--------------------------------------------
Fighting spirit surged from the One Flower Sword Sovereign’s body.

Just as she was about to step forward.

“Wait.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon walked over to her and held out Golden Wound Medicine.

“When you get old, wounds don’t heal so well.”

The blazing fighting spirit of the One Flower Sword Sovereign subsided.

“Why are you sapping my strength like this?”

“Loosen up a bit. You’re far too tense right now.”

Behind the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, Geom Mugeuk nodded as if he felt the same way.

“He’s an opponent I can’t possibly beat if I loosen up, though.”

At her high praise for the Sword King, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon said calmly,

“Loosen up anyway.”

How could she not know that he was worried for her?

The One Flower Sword Sovereign opened the Golden Wound Medicine, dabbed some on her finger, and applied it to her shoulder. Though she had merely slipped her hand inside her clothes to do so, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon turned his back for her.

Meanwhile, Geom Mugeuk teased the Sword King.

“What now? Brother Ak’s most charming feature is that tightly tied hair.”

The Sword King touched the back of his neck beneath his hair.

“I’ll be lucky if only my hair gets cut.”

It meant the fight was that difficult.

“So you do know how to exaggerate your pain.”

“And whose fault would that be?”

In moments like this, he was exactly like the Sword King of old.

Geom Mugeuk looked at the Sound King standing behind him.

The Sound King was relaxed. As if there were music only he could hear, he slowly swayed his upper body from side to side. His long arms swung this way and that.

Geom Mugeuk stared at him quietly, then abruptly said,

“She’s beautiful.”

“Who? That person?”

“No. The woman Brother Ak likes. I’m saying she’s incredibly beautiful.”

“Is she?”

Ak Gunhak pretended not to know who he meant, though he did.

“Yes.”

Geom Mugeuk said no more. The Sword King would know full well why he had brought her up now.

Brother Ak, I’m going to take you back to our cult. And I’m definitely going to see that awkward meeting between the two of you.

The One Flower Sword Sovereign stepped forward again, and Geom Mugeuk withdrew.

Geom Mugeuk said to the One Flower Sword Sovereign,

“Please cut off that horsetail of his.”

Then he won’t be able to prattle on about freedom anymore. I’ll even put those shoes on him.

“Understood. Leave it to me.”

The Blade King watched the scene with displeasure. The person he disliked most of all was Ak Gunhak.

From long ago, the Dark Palace Lord had thought highly of that Ak Gunhak. He had always entrusted the most important missions to him.

‘To a traitor like that.’

He had already found the Sword King displeasing all along, but seeing him exchange jokes with Geom Mugeuk right before his eyes truly stirred killing intent in him.

‘Once this is over, I’ll kill him for certain.’

Because he did not hide that killing intent, the Sword King turned to look at him.

“Don’t forget that I’m watching you.”

Until now, he had at least maintained basic courtesy, but now he cast aside that formality.

At the warning not to try anything foolish, the Sword King smiled and turned back again. At that sight, the Blade King’s expression hardened even more.

The One Flower Sword Sovereign and the Sword King faced each other once more.

The reason she had stepped forward was partly to follow the Young Cult Leader’s will and make him move aside, but in truth, it was also because she wanted to cross swords with the Sword King one more time.

She felt she would never again have the chance in her life to fight this man. Especially not while fighting with such sincerity.

The One Flower Sword Sovereign fully revealed her aura.

Her aura was a single sword.

A single sword aimed at the Sword King’s face. It was an aura that instilled a terrible fear, as if it might fly forth at any moment and pierce his face.

A sword made the opponent feel many emotions. Fear, elegance, terror, passion, loneliness……

Her aura would be felt differently depending on the person who received it.

The emotion the Sword King felt was passion.

Beyond the sword, he could see her. On her face, without a trace of makeup, was fervor for the sword. An intense yearning to become stronger.

The Sword King knew. He knew who had made such emotions dwell on the faces of these Demon Venerables.

He knew who had made them carry this absurd gift at their waists.

Sssssssss.

The Sword King also revealed his own aura. It was an aura he did not show unless he acknowledged his opponent.

In the next instant, everything around them glittered, and the One Flower Sword Sovereign’s eyes filled with surprise.

Hundreds, thousands of sword blades surrounded her. Sharp blades packed tightly around her, as if she would be cut if she moved even slightly.

The One Flower Sword Sovereign could feel it. If she moved rashly, she truly would be cut. The blades within this aura were sharper than real ones.

‘So the true sword grave was here.’

That was the feeling the Sword King’s aura gave her.

It was truly the first time she had encountered such a murderous aura.

One sword and thousands of swords.

The two auras clashed tautly against each other.

Wooooong.

The One Flower Sword Sovereign infused internal energy into the One Flower Sword. Then pure white sword aura flowed along her blade.

But the Sword King did not raise any sword aura.

‘He intends to block my sword aura with that iron sword?’

Had it been anyone else, she would have raged, “How dare you!” But the One Flower Sword Sovereign instead grew tense. Could that sword truly withstand even sword aura?

And the result was indeed astonishing.

Kaaaang!

When the One Flower Sword and the Sword King’s iron sword collided head-on, the iron sword did not break.

‘It really doesn’t break even against sword aura?’

But the shock lasted only a moment. Looking closely, she sensed something invisible on the Sword King’s blade as well. Another shock surged over her.

“Formless Sword Aura!”

The Sword King’s sword aura left no trace at all. The Sword King, who used formless sword qi, had sword aura that was also invisible to the eye.

If one were to attack without knowing the opponent could use Formless Sword Aura, their sword would be cut in an instant, and their body along with it.

Kakakakakang!

Formless astral energy and pure white astral energy collided.

Each time the astral energies clashed, tremendous vibrations spread outward. Though it contained sword aura, it was astonishing that that iron sword withstood both the internal and external impacts. And that was not because the sword was special.

The opponent felt like a cool autumn breeze, but when he fought, he was a bitter winter wind.

‘Truly, he is this strong?’

After all, the Sword King was the strongest of the twelve experts raised by an organization that had prepared for revenge for three hundred years.

The One Flower Sword Sovereign had a premonition that perhaps this battle might become the opportunity for the One Flower Sword Art to rise from the eleventh star to the twelfth. That was how much inspiration and enlightenment the fight with the Sword King was giving her.

The One Flower Sword Sovereign displayed her signature martial art.

Her signature martial art was the One Flower Sword Art.

No longer bound by forms, she had carved out her own realm, and of the eight forms that had once existed, only three now remained.

When even these forms all disappeared, she would reach the pinnacle of swordsmanship.

The first form she had newly named was Bloom!

When the tip of her sword trembled lightly, over a dozen strands of sword qi bloomed like flowers.

Shuk shuk shuk shuk shuk shuk shuk!

The sword qi flying toward the Sword King was not some ordinary, trifling sword qi. Each and every strand contained profound understanding of swordsmanship that had reached a high realm.

The Sword King felt that unless he used his signature martial art, he could never block it.

The Sword King’s signature martial art was the Formless Divine Sword.

First Sword, Formless Return to Void.

The surrounding air changed, and the Sword King’s sword also trembled.

Whirrrrrrrr!

In an instant, a whirlwind seemed to form at the tip of his sword.

Her incoming sword qi was swept up by the whirlwind and vanished.

The One Flower Sword Sovereign was neither surprised nor flustered. That single move of his must also be a form he had honed as much as she had honed hers.

Her second signature martial art was unleashed.

One Flower Sword Art, Second Form: Falling Flowers.

Like flower petals falling in the wind, the sword qi swept down diagonally. At the most difficult angle to block!

Swaaaaaaaak!

This time as well, the Sword King responded with the Formless Divine Sword.

Formless Divine Sword, Third Sword: Formless Reversal of Heaven.

A pitch-black space seemed to appear in front of the Sword King.

Whoosh.

It swallowed the incoming sword qi in an instant and disappeared.

That was not the end.

In the next moment, darkness burst out from a tiny point, spitting back the sword qi it had swallowed.

Shwik shwik shwik shwik shwik shwik shwik!

The swords that had flown out returned to her.

She had never imagined her own attack would come back at her.

Papapapapapak!

Chang chang chang chang chang chang!

Her One Flower Sword embroidered the air. Her sword struck away the flower petals raining down.

Papak!

Sword qi she failed to block grazed her side, and blood sprayed again. It was the first time she had ever had to block her own form.

This time, the Sword King used the next form first.

Formless Divine Sword, Second Sword: Formless Flash.

In a flash, a line of light was drawn straight between the Sword King and the One Flower Sword Sovereign. No, it was as if that line had existed there from the beginning.

At the same time, her final form was also unleashed.

One Flower Sword Art, Third Form: One Flower.

It was the final third form, to which she had given her own name.

In a flash, the two forms crossed.

There was no sound at all.

Srrrk.

As she twisted her body to dodge, her bangs were cut away.

Piiit.

A line of blood was drawn across her forehead.

Had it been intentional? The same wound she had earlier drawn on the Sword King’s forehead now appeared on hers.

Craaaack!

A long diagonal line appeared on the wall behind the Sword King.

The Sword King stood in front of it. If the wall behind him had been cut, it seemed his body should have been cut as well.

Jing.

His iron sword, thrust forward, was vibrating. He had blocked even the third form.

It was right then.

Pat.

The cord that tightly bound the Sword King’s hair was severed, and his hair came loose.

Instead of cutting his hair, the One Flower Sword Sovereign had cut the cord that tied it.

The two looked at each other. This time as well, they did not exchange a courteous bow. But their gazes already contained respect for one another.

At last, it’s over, the One Flower Sword Sovereign thought, relaxing her tension.
