That was when the Blade King moved.

‘I knew you’d spare her again!’

That was why he had been waiting only for a chance to ambush her. Since he knew Ak Gunhak would never kill her, he had been aiming for the chance to kill her himself. Now was the perfect opportunity to kill her.

Swaaaaaaek!

The instant the Red Blade was about to strike the One Flower Sword Sovereign’s back.

Boom!

What blocked the incoming Red Blade was the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s Heaven-Destroying Great Saber. The One Flower Sword Sovereign had relaxed, but the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had not. The moment the Blade King moved, he moved at the same time.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s fury erupted.

“You dog-like bastard, how dare you go after her!”

Enormous demonic energy and killing intent exploded from the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s body.

The Heaven-Destroying Great Saber, filled with astral energy, split the air.

Swaaaaaaaek!

The Blade King raised his twin blades to block it. Since they had clashed several times earlier, he knew the power of that attack.

Boom!

The moment blade and saber collided, the Blade King’s eyes widened. Even in their earlier battle, he had never seen a blow this powerful.

It was a strike imbued with the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s rage.

Boom! Booom! Boom!

The Heaven-Destroying Great Saber rained down on him.

Even under powerful attacks that felt as if they would break his wrists, the Blade King was rejoicing.

‘Yes, get excited! The more excited you become, the more chances will come to me!’

Boom! Booom! Boooom!

The Blade King’s form was gradually pushed back. The Blood Heaven Blade Demon surged in like a tidal wave.

Truly overcome by madness, he brought his saber down like a madman.

‘This old man’s strength!’

Boom! Booom! Boom!

Judging that the Blade King was at a disadvantage, the Dark Blood Four Blades hurled themselves forward at the same time. Just as the Blade King had aimed for the One Flower Sword Sovereign’s back, their target was the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, who was in the middle of fighting.

At that moment, Geom Mugeuk moved. In a flash, Geom Mugeuk’s form shot out like light and swept past them.

Shiiiiik! Slice!

He cut down four people, yet there was only one sound.

The sight of them being cut was like the same scene being endlessly performed in a place where mirrors faced each other. Four different men were being cut down simultaneously as if they were one person.

“I told you not to move a single finger, didn’t I?”

Geom Mugeuk’s warning was not directed at the Dark Blood Four Blades, who were already dead.

Geom Mugeuk aimed his sword at the iron-masked man and the Sound King and said,

“Don’t even dream of it!”

He had not stopped the Blade King earlier because the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had already thrown himself forward. But he absolutely would not allow any other intervention.

At that, the Sound King said mockingly,

“You prevent us from entering, but surely you, Young Cult Leader, are not going to interfere?”

“There will be no need for that in this fight.”

It was absolute faith in the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

Even during that exchange, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s attacks continued.

They were truly frenzied attacks, as if he could see nothing else.

Bang! Kwaang! Bang! Bang!

But even while driven onto the defensive, the Saber King laughed inwardly. If his opponent had been any other master, he would already have fallen to this momentum. But he was not so weak that he would be killed by an excited foe.

For a master to become this excited meant he had already chosen defeat.

Sure enough, the opportunity came. Because he had struck down in such a frenzy, his saber hit the wall behind and bounced away.

The Saber King did not miss that instant. The Red Saber aimed for the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s fingers, and as he evaded that attack, the strength with which he held the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber weakened.

Swaaah!

Tung!

Struck by the Black Saber, the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber flew from the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s hand into the air.

‘It’s over!’

In a fight between masters like them, losing one’s weapon meant the fight was over. There would be no chance to pick it up again.

Swish! Swaaah!

As the twin sabers’ attacks poured in after that, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon chose not to fling himself backward to evade, but instead dug in toward him and seized both his wrists.

At that string of blunders, the Saber King smiled. Grabbing his wrists with bare hands?

Wooooong.

Saber aura began to gather on the Red Saber and the Black Saber. If he so much as moved the blades imbued with that saber aura and cut, the old man’s wrists would be severed.

“Old Demon Venerable, did you like that woman?”

His mockery did not end with a sneer.

“I’ll kill you, then tear that woman apart alive.”

He had expected to see anguish on his opponent’s face.

But an inscrutable smile formed at the corners of the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s mouth.

“Heaven-Destroying Demonic Saber Art, Fifth Form: Annihilating Saber Direct Strike (滅刀直擊).”

“What?”

The instant the Saber King raised his head at the chilling sensation, his eyes flew wide open.

“!”

The Heaven-Destroying Great Saber, which he had thought he had sent flying far away, was hovering above his head. Pointed at him, cradling a blue saber aura.

His opponent had not been excited like a madman. He had not been an ignorant old fool. That fox-like old man had planned this, aiming for this single moment. The one who had truly grown excited at his feigned frenzy had been none other than himself.

Swaaaaaah!

He tried to dodge, but he could not. The Blood Heaven Saber Demon had drawn up his inner force to its utmost limit and was gripping both his hands tightly.

Boom!

The Heaven-Destroying Great Saber plunged down like light, tearing through his protective astral qi and burying itself in his shoulder.

Crunch! Squelch!

“Uaaaagh!”

A scream burst from the Saber King’s mouth on its own. As that enormous Heaven-Destroying Great Saber lodged in his shoulder, horrific pain surged through him.

Clang.

The Red Saber fell to the ground first. It was the first time he had ever dropped his saber in a fight.

At that moment, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon, who was gripping his wrists, pulled his arms to either side.

In an instant, the Saber King’s eyes stretched wide.

‘He’s trying to tear me to death.’

This was no longer the time to be saying refined things like “old Demon Venerable.”

“You crazy old bastard! Stop!”

He tried to raise saber aura on the Black Saber again and cut off the wrist.

Squelch!

But the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber lodged in his shoulder carved through flesh and bone, driving even deeper.

Unable to endure the pain, he dropped the Black Saber to the ground as well.

Even in the midst of this, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon began pulling his arms left and right with even greater force.

“You bastard! I told you to stop!”

The Saber King drew up his internal energy to the limit and tried to hold on, but the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber had already cut halfway through his body.

The Saber King saw it. He saw crimson demonic qi flowing from the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s eyes. The madness he had seemed to show earlier in his excitement had not been madness. This sight before him now was his true, hidden nature.

“……Please!”

Squelch.

The Heaven-Destroying Great Saber drove even deeper. There was no mercy in the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber.

“Uaaaaagh!”

From his shoulder downward, the Heaven-Destroying Great Saber split his body completely in half and embedded itself in the ground.

Splaaash!

Boom.

Covered in blood, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon looked down at the side with the head attached and spoke coldly.

“If you catch my eye even in hell, I’ll kill you again.”

Chapter 756: We Believe as You Do
---------------------------------
The stench of blood spread sharply, and a heavy silence settled over them.

None of those standing behind Geom Mugeuk could bring themselves to speak.

Taesu trembled all over. He had seen plenty of people die, but never so horribly as this.

‘So he was that terrifying.’

He had known that a Demon Venerable of the Demonic Cult was frightening, but when had he ever truly experienced that fear for himself? Only now could he feel that the man was not merely a master, but a Demon Venerable.

Jihan, too, realized just where he had entered. If he made a mistake, he would end up like that as well. The demonic qi the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had unleashed when killing his opponent just now had stirred an utterly overwhelming terror.

Pung of the Sado Alliance also felt fear, while Baekchong Family Head Dan Sojin merely watched the situation in silence.

‘Was my choice truly right?’

Because the people he had asked for help were precisely these people. Had he, in trying to avoid a wolf, brought in a tiger instead? Only now did that worry arise.

The iron-masked man took a step back with a sickened look in his eyes, and the Sword King silently watched the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, who stood with his back turned.

The one who broke the silence was the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“Mugeuk, give me some water.”

When Geom Mugeuk extended his hand, the leather pouch opened on its own, and a bamboo canteen came out from within. He had not let his guard down, lest the Sound King move the moment his attention shifted to the pouch.

Even in the face of the Blade King’s death, the Sound King showed no particular emotional disturbance and continued swaying gently. Little by little, he was falling into his own world, moving with the rhythm.

Geom Mugeuk approached the Blood Heaven Blade Demon. The Blood Heaven Blade Demon, still without turning around, merely reached his hand back. Realizing that he did not want to show him his face, Geom Mugeuk placed the bamboo canteen in his hand.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon had not asked for water because he meant to drink.

Splash.

Still turned away, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon poured the water over his face and washed away the blood.

Only then did the Blood Heaven Blade Demon turn around. He had returned to his usual appearance. He had become the Blood Heaven Blade Demon who used to sit by the window and read. The terrifying demonic qi from moments ago was gone.

As if the fight between him and the Blade King had never happened, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon conveyed to the Ilhwa Sword Venerable the impressions of her battle that he had not been able to share earlier.

“It was a splendid fight. Is your forehead all right?”

Geom Mugeuk understood. He had not wanted to look at him and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable with his face covered in blood.

How could the Ilhwa Sword Venerable not know that feeling?

“I’m fine. It’s only a light scratch.”

“Apply medicine properly in advance so it doesn’t leave a scar.”

“Yes, I will.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon glared at the Sword King. Geom Mugeuk spoke in his place, expressing his feelings.

“How could you leave a wound on such a precious face?”

The Sword King looked at Geom Mugeuk with an aggrieved expression. His eyes seemed to say that if he could, he wanted to lift his bangs and show his own forehead. Was this face not precious?

If it had just been the two of them, he surely would have done so. But because the Blood Heaven Blade Demon was there with them, he did not pointlessly provoke him.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon said politely to the Sword King.

“Step aside now.”

“I cannot do that.”

At the Sword King’s answer, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s eyes twitched. He disliked him enough already, and now the man was refusing to listen to him until the very end. Then again, if he would not even listen to the Young Cult Master, there was no way he would listen to him.

“Fine. You must have your own circumstances. Then you will understand ours as well, won’t you?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon walked forward. His momentum made it clear that he intended to kill the Sword King too.

The Sword King did not retreat. He was making his will clear: no one was to touch the Sound King today.

This fight, at least, had to be stopped. The earlier fight with the Ilhwa Sword Venerable had also been a life-and-death struggle, but even so, it had been a fight with a line drawn.

It was a line drawn by the Sword King, and the Sword King and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable had not crossed that line to the end.

But a fight with the Blood Heaven Blade Demon was different. Given the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s personality, if he truly charged in to kill him, it would become a fight where lives were genuinely at stake.

“Senior!”

“Do not stop me.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon answered without looking back.

“I won’t stop you.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon glanced back at Geom Mugeuk with an expression that seemed to ask, Really?

“If you’re going to fight anyway, make sure you win! Snap that light little sword of his in two, the one that looks like it would break if you tapped it.”

“You say things you don’t mean.”

How could the Blood Heaven Blade Demon not know Geom Mugeuk’s heart? He also knew better than anyone that the person most troubled right now was Geom Mugeuk.

If Geom Mugeuk had taken that horsetail-headed fellow’s side and tried to stop him from fighting, he might have simply fought in a burst of anger.

But since he was taking his side and telling him to make sure he won, the desire to do so actually disappeared. That cunning brat—how well he knew his heart.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon, who had been stepping forward, turned back again. The Ilhwa Sword Venerable nodded to him with an expression that said he had done well.

“I told you, apply medicine first.”

As the Blood Heaven Blade Demon returned like that, Geom Mugeuk looked at the Sound King.

The Sound King was still merely swaying, and had not once looked at the Blade King’s corpse. Even without looking at his eyeless sockets, one could tell he showed no sign of grief or worry.

A powerful man like the Blade King had died, and yet he was not worried? Just what was giving him that confidence? Did he trust that Sword King so much?

“Were you on bad terms with the dead man? Even so, isn’t the temperament of a musician far too barren?”

Geom Mugeuk provoked him again with music. He had realized that this was the most effective taunt. Since it was impossible to get anything out of the Sword King, he meant to provoke this Sound King and draw out whatever he could.

The Sound King, who had been swaying, turned his head toward Geom Mugeuk.

“Young Cult Master, you underestimate me far too much.”

“I’m not underestimating you. I’m afraid of you.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward the Sword King.

“When that man is trying so hard to protect you, how could I not be afraid? If you didn’t have something truly remarkable, would he protect you like that?”

The Sound King looked at the Sword King.

“He certainly is a reassuring person.”

His words and expression carried genuine trust, but Geom Mugeuk sensed it.

‘This man does not trust the Sword King.’

Yes, considering their relationship, of course he could not trust him.

But then why was he so relaxed?

If he was not trusting the Sword King, that meant he was trusting himself.

Even now, he was bobbing his head slightly, feeling the melody. Geom Mugeuk could sense from him a heat that made it seem as though he might leap out at any moment and shake his body wildly.

‘Something has changed.’

When the Blade King died, the Sound King actually became even more confident. As if he had moved on to the next stage and was enjoying it.

The Sound King said to Geom Mugeuk,

“The smell of blood is far too strong here. Let us move elsewhere.”

Without waiting for Geom Mugeuk’s answer, the Sound King began walking first. As he passed in front of him, Geom Mugeuk could have cut him down ten times over.

Geom Mugeuk looked at the Sword King.

You know I can do that, don’t you? That I’m letting him pass before me because of my trust in you.

How could the Sword King not know? But still, he showed no reaction.

“I misjudged you! I was fooled by those bare feet and that horsetail hair, and forgot for a moment that you were originally this kind of villain.”

Then the Sword King, up ahead, said,

“In the martial world, you’re probably the greater villain.”

They moved to the previous room where the dolls had been. The iron-masked man quietly followed behind.

Geom Mugeuk looked at the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

Will you continue to go along with that bastard’s wishes? The Blood Heaven Blade Demon could easily have exploded in anger.

The two stood silently at Geom Mugeuk’s left and right. In their eyes, he sensed a single emotion.

Just as you trust that man, we trust you.

Geom Mugeuk bowed his head politely to the two of them in gratitude, then set off again.

Geom Mugeuk’s group also followed them back to the previous room.

Once they changed rooms, the smell of blood was certainly weaker and bearable.

Then Taesu shouted,

“Look over there!”

The place he pointed to with his finger was the dolls that had been set up earlier.

What had fascinated him most in this room before had been those very dolls. That was why he had studied them closely, and when he returned to this room, the first thing he looked at again was the dolls.

But they had changed.

At that change, not only Geom Mugeuk but also the two Demon Venerables were startled.

There was one more doll among the Twelve Earthly Branch Kings that had been attacking Geom Mugeuk in concert.

Before they knew it, the dead Blade King’s doll had also been placed there.

Between the Fighting King and the Blood King, the Blade King was wildly swinging his blade.

Geom Mugeuk slowly walked toward the doll. The Blade King doll, like the dolls from earlier, had truly been crafted with astonishing precision. Its expression was so vivid that it brought to mind the scene of him fighting the Blood Heaven Blade Demon just moments ago.

“You expected this man to die.”

The Sound King did not deny those words.

“Did you make a doll of yourself too? Where would you like to stand?”

Then the Sound King’s voice came from behind him.

“Young Cult Master, there is no doll of me.”

Geom Mugeuk turned back to him and asked,

“Did you make these dolls?”

For some reason, he thought that might have been the case, but the Sound King asked back with an incredulous expression.

“You cannot be asking that sincerely after seeing my eyes, can you?”

“I asked because you are more sinister than you look, and I wondered if you might actually be seeing everything with those eyes.”

“Conversely, there are many people who open their eyes and yet fail to see properly.”

As if to say that Geom Mugeuk and the Demon Venerables were such people, the Sound King slowly looked around at them all. The confidence of the one with closed eyes overwhelmed those with open ones.

“Young Cult Master, it is still not too late. There is no need to sacrifice all these people here, is there?”

It meant he was confident he could kill them all.

Geom Mugeuk stared at him quietly, then suddenly asked,

“You had an encounter with some fortuitous opportunity recently, didn’t you?”

Otherwise, there was no way he would have come here with the First King and the Second King at his sides.

‘Could it be?’

In his life before the regression, the man had completed a terrifying sound art before his death. It was said to be so powerful that no master could contend against it.

‘He completed it!’

Just as the fates of all the people inside those dolls had changed, his fate must have changed as well.

Geom Mugeuk was convinced there was a high possibility of that.

“You completed your sound art, didn’t you?”

As expected, the Sound King did not deny it. Now it was clear where his confidence came from.

“Would you care to hear me play?”

“Sounds good. We’re the sort who dance when there is music. I’ll give you a refreshing sword dance to match it.”

When Geom Mugeuk stepped forward, the Sword King moved to block his path.

“Let’s dance together.”

How could that sword dance possibly mean a real dance? Knowing that Geom Mugeuk meant to kill the Sound King, the Sword King had stepped in.

At this point, he could have flared up, but Geom Mugeuk dealt with him gently.

“No, someone who was so afraid he’d be made to dance that he wouldn’t even come to the gathering—what dance are you talking about?”

At that moment, the sound of a pipa flowed from behind.

Trill.

“Breaking Sound!”

At the Sound King’s cry, Taesu and Jihan, who stood behind, along with the warrior of the Sado Alliance and Dan Sojin, covered their ears. A sound wave they could not withstand had come flying in.

“Ugh!”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable released their internal energy and blocked the surging sound wave.

Only then did the expressions of the four behind them ease from pain.

Geom Mugeuk charged forward and shouted to the two Demon Venerables,

“While I hold onto this frustrating horsetail head, cut off that man’s arms and his pipa.”

Yes, if the Sword King insisted on saving him, there was no need to aim for his life.

Geom Mugeuk rushed at the Sword King like a thunderbolt and swung his sword.

“This time, Brother Ak, let’s see you block it!”

Geom Mugeuk’s sword and the Sword King’s sword clashed in midair. Sword light flashed with dazzling brilliance. It was the pinnacle of swordsmanship created by those who had truly reached the final realm of the sword.

Clang clang clang clang clang clang clang!

Even as they exchanged blows at a speed invisible to the eye, Geom Mugeuk stared into the Sword King’s eyes.

‘Why are you going this far to stop me?’

He could tell by instinct. Even if his arm were truly cut off, the Sword King would try to stop him and him alone.

Sword and sword moved back and forth so quickly they could not be seen. To the four people behind them, it did not look all that different from the Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s fight earlier.

But the substance of this fight was completely different.

Having achieved great completion of the Nine Flames Demonic Art, Geom Mugeuk’s sword was now overwhelming the Sword King’s. If he wanted to win, he could.

‘You must be feeling it too, no? You must know you cannot stop me in the end, so why are you going this far?’

Meanwhile, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable and the Blood Heaven Blade Demon rushed at the Sound King, swinging sword and great blade.

Whoosh! Shwaaaak!

Bang! Boom!

The sword and great blade of the two stopped in midair. It was not because the Sword King had flown over to block them.

Something invisible before the Sound King was blocking them like a membrane. It was a membrane of unseen sound.

“It’s a Soundproof Formation!”

As the Blood Heaven Blade Demon shouted, it was indeed the Soundproof Formation said to be possible only upon reaching the highest realm of sound arts.

Within it, the Sound King’s eyes shone intensely as he looked out.

The Sound King began playing the pipa in earnest.

Trill.

“Blood Thunder Sound!”

At the same time, sound arts crashed down from all directions in succession like bolts of lightning.

Rumble rumble rumble rumble rumble rumble rumble!

Attacks as powerful as lightning swept not only toward the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable, but also toward the four people standing behind them.

“I’ll take care of the rear.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon hurled himself backward and swung his great blade.

Boom! Boom! Bang!

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable dodged the sound art flying toward her, then charged toward the Soundproof Formation. If both of them defended, they would be played by him; trusting the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, she carried out the attack herself.

Boom!

The Soundproof Formation was powerful and did not tear easily. She immediately unleashed the Ilhwa Sword Technique.

Ilhwa Sword Technique, First Form: Blooming!

Shuk shuk shuk shuk shuk shuk shuk!

Her technique began tearing through the Soundproof Formation.

Within the Soundproof Formation, the Sound King remained absorbed in his playing.

Shwaaaak.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon, who had been guarding the rear, also charged in an instant. His Myeolcheon Great Blade pierced through the damaged Soundproof Formation that Blooming had slightly torn open.

Kwaaang!

Riiiiip.

At the very moment the two Demon Venerables violently tore through the Soundproof Formation and entered the Sound King’s domain—

The Sound King’s performance reached its peak.

Whoosh.

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s sword flew toward his arm. Just as it was about to truly cut his arm off—

The performance that had reached its peak abruptly stopped.

Fwoosh!

With the sound of a gust of wind, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable vanished from that place.

Chapter 757: Light Enters Through the Cracks
--------------------------------------------
Geom Mugeuk, who had been exchanging swords with the Sword King, lowered his blade.

Shiiik.

The Sword King’s incoming sword stopped by a hair’s breadth in front of Geom Mugeuk’s neck.

If he had not trusted the Sword King, he would never have ceased defending himself so defenselessly.

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze was fixed on the Sound King. The Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable, who had been attacking him, had vanished in an instant.

Without looking at the Sword King, Geom Mugeuk said,

“Brother Ak, I’m getting angrier and angrier.”

A quiet answer came back.

“I know.”

“Then that’s enough.”

Geom Mugeuk slowly began walking toward the Sound King. The Sword King silently followed behind him. Though he walked as if they were on the same side, it was clear that he was following to protect the Sound King.

Geom Mugeuk no longer paid the Sword King any mind. He regarded him as part of the scenery. He was a boulder blocking his path, and also a fallen log. He was an unreasonable Green Forest bandit, and also a girl weeping with a face full of stories.

And to Geom Mugeuk, he was still a fine piece of scenery.

Geom Mugeuk walked up before the Sound King. The soundproofing formation that the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable had torn apart earlier had already been deployed anew.

Thump, thump.

Geom Mugeuk struck the soundproofing formation with his hand. He could feel an invisible barrier.

“Where are those two?”

Staring straight into Geom Mugeuk’s eyes, the Sound King answered,

“In hell.”

It was a remark that might have made him flare up, but Geom Mugeuk did not get angry.

“They’ve gone in and out of hell as if it were their own home, so they’ll be fine.”

“This hell will be different.”

The Sound King began playing again the melody that had sent the two elsewhere. When that piece had reached its climax, the two had disappeared.

“This piece is called the Song of Despair. It is a piece I’m very fond of.”

The Sound King’s fingers, which had been plucking invisible strings, paused for a moment.

“It will likely not be easy to escape.”

The sound art he had unleashed was no ordinary sound art. It was a technique that used sound arts to imprison them within an illusion—the Illusory Sound Art.

Since it was the sound art that had made the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable vanish, it would indeed not be an easy attack to break, just as he said.

“No matter what kind of sorcery it is, there is always a way to break it.”

At that, the Sound King smiled leisurely from within the soundproofing formation.

“Does my performance look like mere sorcery to you?”

“I know it isn’t.”

But just as he trusted his own sound arts, Geom Mugeuk trusted the two Demon Venerables.

“But the people trapped inside aren’t mere experts either, are they?”

This was what Geom Mugeuk was curious about.

“Were the two of them trapped in the same place?”

“They were.”

When the Sound King nodded, relief brushed across Geom Mugeuk’s face.

“Then they’ll come out safely.”

“Why are you so certain?”

Why, you ask? Because you don’t know what that old man will turn into when he falls into hell together with the Sword Venerable.

“Have you ever tried to protect someone at the risk of your own life?”

The Sound King could not answer right away.

“You haven’t, have you? That’s why your music is mediocre.”

In that instant, the Sound King’s expression stiffened. Truly, no matter how much he tried to keep his mind composed, the Young Cult Leader kept turning his insides upside down.

“If you do not want to see the Demon Venerables come out torn to shreds, find out the location of the tomb from Family Head Dan.”

Geom Mugeuk looked back at Dan Sojin. He was staring this way with a tense expression. If they threatened the lives of the Demon Venerables, Geom Mugeuk would surely obey.

“If you say it so openly, would that man reveal the location?”

“That depends on you, Young Cult Leader. If you want to save the Demon Venerables, make him do so.”

“You overestimate me far too much.”

When Geom Mugeuk looked at him again, Dan Sojin made his will clear.

“I understand the situation is urgent, but I cannot tell you.”

Geom Mugeuk looked back at the Sound King.

“That’s what he says.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s almost playful response, the Sound King’s expression hardened.

“You said they were like parents to you. As expected, Young Cult Leader, you are a heartless man.”

Geom Mugeuk stared at him.

“Fine, let’s say I’m heartless. Why are you being so unreasonable?”

“What do you mean?”

“You know it too, don’t you? In this situation, even if the Lord of the Evil Alliance came in person, he couldn’t make him reveal it. You know full well it’s impossible, so why are you being so stubborn?”

“If it is you, Young Cult Leader…”

Geom Mugeuk cut him off.

“Yes, because you kept flattering me like that, I was confused at first. I thought, These people truly overestimate me. Well, yes, I am exceptional enough to be overestimated. They must be in quite a hurry. Yes, of course they’d be in a hurry.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze sharpened.

“But that can’t be right. Showing such a flaw at such an important moment? Yes, you might do that. Someone who spent all day plucking a pipa—what sort of thorough plan would you make? But the person who sent you isn’t that sloppy, is he?”

Geom Mugeuk stared into his two dead eyes.

“What is that person plotting? What order did he give you?”

Strictly speaking, this too was a remark that looked down on the Sound King. Geom Mugeuk kept provoking him without end, and the Sound King was struggling to maintain his composure.

“Stop wasting time. Even at this very moment, your Demon Venerables are dying.”

It was just as those words ended.

Pwaaaak!

The air in front of the Sound King tore open, and something shot out.

It was a sword covered in blood. The Sound King nearly had his face pierced by it.

Riiiip.

The sword began cutting through the air. As though an unseen curtain were being torn apart and revealing itself, a fissure formed in the empty space.

But soon the sword disappeared again. Through that torn gap came a rush of scorching heat, the smell of blood, and the repeated sound of something bursting apart.

Puuuk!

The sword shot out from that place again.

Riiiip.

This time, the sword descended straight downward, tearing the space open even longer.

Gush.

At that moment, blood trickled from the corner of the Sound King’s mouth.

The Sound King quickly began playing the pipa again.

Then the space that had opened began closing once more. From beyond it, the howling shrieks grew even louder.

But then.

Pwaaaaaak!

This time, something large burst through it.

It was the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s Heaven-Destroying Great Blade.

Blegh!

The Sound King spat blood. Unable to keep playing, he retreated backward.

Riiiiip.

The formless curtain was completely torn apart, and through the gap, the scene inside became visible.

It was a land entirely red, truly as though it were hell. Corpses lay strewn about, and blood flowed like rivers.

“Graaaaaah!”

What charged there in a frenzy were beasts that looked like savage monsters.

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s sword energy was sweeping through the place.

Swaaaaaak.

Paaaaak!

The Heaven-Destroying Great Blade spewed out astral energy. A wave of astral energy surged forward, shredding the monsters pouring in from that place and driving them back.

Boom! Kwaaang! Boom! Boom!

With explosive roars, the corpses of the monsters were smashed apart and sent tumbling.

The screams and explosions died down.

Then came silence.

Riiiip.

The space tore even wider.

Soon, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable walked out through it.

She went back inside again.

“That’s enough. Let’s go.”

And dragged the Blood Heaven Blade Demon out by force.

“Wait! I’ll take care of those things!”

Covered in blood, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon was still swinging the Heaven-Destroying Great Blade until the very instant he emerged. Monsters were reduced to bloody pulp and sent rolling. Beyond them, terrified monsters could be seen fleeing.

Once the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had come out as well, that space completely disappeared.

Ssssss.

Geom Mugeuk teased the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“Did you fight them all by yourself?”

Unlike the Ilhwa Sword Venerable, whose martial attire was clean, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon was drenched in blood.

“Damn it! Where’s water? Something to wipe with?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon wiped the blood from his face with his sleeve and frowned.

“Why does someone with blood in his epithet hate blood so much?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon said to Geom Mugeuk,

“You told me to cut off that man’s arms, didn’t you? Arms won’t be enough. I’ll cut off all four of his limbs.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon looked ready to charge in again at once, but Geom Mugeuk stopped him.

“Wipe off the blood first. There’s something in the leather pouch.”

After making the Blood Heaven Blade Demon step back, Geom Mugeuk walked forward.

A new soundproofing formation blocked his path.

“Will you mock my music again?”

He could have said that it had not been hell but a walking path for the two of them, but Geom Mugeuk shook his head.

“I told you. I’m doing this because I’m afraid of you.”

Without a word, the Sound King wiped the blood running from the corner of his mouth, then began to play again.

“The power of music is magnificent.”

As a strand of melody flowed through the place,

Taesu suddenly burst into laughter like a madman.

“Uahahahaha!”

Soon after, Jihan, standing beside him, burst into laughter as well. Their expressions and gazes showed that they could not control their emotions, but they could not stop laughing.

Geom Mugeuk did not get angry at his prank. Instead, he played along.

“It’s been a while since I’ve seen these two laugh. Draw up more internal energy and play!”

But the Sound King did not do so. Whether he could make the two Demon Venerables laugh or not was unknown, but to make those two laugh, he would have to expend an enormous amount of internal energy.

“I could make them laugh until they die.”

“That would be better than dying while crying.”

The Sound King’s playing changed. Then Taesu and Jihan, who had been laughing, stopped laughing in an instant.

And then they suddenly burst into tears. They were not merely making crying sounds. They truly shed tears in torrents.

For the first time since becoming sensible, Jihan shed tears. He was astonished that so many tears had been pooled within his eyes.

The Sound King’s playing changed again.

This time, Taesu and Jihan, their faces full of anger, threw punches at each other. It was not some invisible force controlling them. This rage was certainly their own.

He controlled people’s emotions through his pipa performance. Geom Mugeuk had seen many masters of sound arts, but this was the first time he had seen someone control a person’s emotions like this.

“You do not know how fragile human emotions are, do you? All humans are broken somewhere. Young Cult Leader, you must be no exception.”

Geom Mugeuk answered calmly,

“That’s what makes it good, isn’t it? Light comes in through those broken cracks. And that light shines on the pitch-black holes inside the heart. Ah, so this is where my hole was. Ah, so there wasn’t just one hole. You come to realize that too.”

The Sword King, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable understood what Geom Mugeuk’s words meant. After all, they were people who had been able to look into those holes because they had met Geom Mugeuk.

But the Sound King did not know the exact meaning of those words. He still thought Geom Mugeuk was mocking him.

“Young Cult Leader, look at your own hole!”

He began playing in earnest. The sound was clearly different from his earlier performance.

Trarararang.

The melody trembled finely and embroidered the space, planting a single illusion in everyone’s hearts. As if it were truly a lie, the scene changed in an instant.

Taesu saw his father. It was the very moment he had uttered words that wounded him.

—Are those principles of yours for the sake of good deeds? Or are they to justify thievery?

His father turned away without a word. At the time, Taesu had only vaguely thought that he had said something he should not have, but now he saw a wound far too deep on his father’s face.

‘Father, I’m sorry.’

Jihan saw the figure of Cheon Daeung, the Hwalong Unit Leader of the Sawanmun.

—You traitorous bastard! A bastard who betrayed the organization to save his own skin!

Leaving aside how much he had tormented him and treated him carelessly, Jihan was not someone who betrayed others. Without realizing it, that had remained as a deep wound.

Pungdo, a martial artist of the Evil Alliance, and Dan Sojin, head of the Baekchong Family, also each saw their own illusions for an instant.

And the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable, too, stopped moving for a brief moment.

Geom Mugeuk saw it as well. It was someone he truly had not imagined would appear before him at this moment.

It was Hwa Mugi from that day.

He was looking down with contempt at Geom Mugeuk, who lay collapsed after being pierced through together with Ian.

—The woman died, and you lived.

Of course, in reality, no such moment had existed. It was a scene created by his illusion.

It should have been a moment when rage exploded, but instead, a smile formed. I truly came a long way to see you.

—Good to see you, Hwa Mugi.

The next moment, Geom Mugeuk’s eyes snapped open, and he escaped the illusion. To Geom Mugeuk, Hwa Mugi was no longer a wound. Nor was he an object of hatred or fear.

It wasn’t that he thought, I’ll definitely kill you. Hwa Mugi was simply a part of his life. He must not fall ill, must not get into an accident, and must not lose to Hwa Mugi.

Was that why? The illusion truly passed in an instant. Because he felt no particular emotion toward him.

When Geom Mugeuk opened his eyes, the Sound King was plucking his pipa and shouting.

“Killing Sound!”

Swaaaaaaak!

The powerful energy created by the sound waves flew toward them all.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon came to his senses next after Geom Mugeuk.

Kwaang! Bang!

He struck away, one after another, the sound arts flying toward him and the One Flower Sword Venerable.

The One Flower Sword Venerable opened her eyes a step late. Seeing her dark complexion, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon wondered inwardly.

‘What did you see?’

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon did not ask. Nor would he ask in the future. From one step away, both the difficult things and the good things would be seen more clearly. Not so far as to be cold—only exactly that distance that became respect.

Puh-puh-puh-pung!

The one who blocked the attacks aimed at the others behind them was Geom Mugeuk.

The shock on the Sound King’s face deepened all the more.

“To awaken from the Soul-Wounding Melody so quickly!”

He stared at Geom Mugeuk with an expression of disbelief.

Soon, his shock turned to excitement. He cast aside the courtesy he had shown Geom Mugeuk all this time.

“Young Cult Leader, you truly are extraordinary. I admit it. You are qualified to hear my music!”

Light seemed to gleam in his faint, lifeless eyes.

“This is a piece I made recently! It seems you will be the first to hear it.”

As his hand brushed over the pipa, a clear, profound sound flowed out.

As the Sound King’s aura deepened, so too did the notes of the pipa.

Following his melody, the sounds flowing from the pipa circled around him. Gold and silver shone brilliantly, as though a score were being drawn in the air around him. The sight was like looking at stars in the night sky.

Music rang out from every direction, as if dozens of people nearby were playing in concert. That melody made the hearts of those who heard it pound.

Geom Mugeuk sensed it instinctively. This time, he intended to decide the match. Geom Mugeuk drew up his inner force.

A cold sneer formed on the Sound King’s face.

“You said you would protect them all? Then try protecting them. What are you? Do you think you’re a god?”

The moment his words ended, light poured from the things floating in the air.

At that very instant!

Shiiiiiiiiik.

There was a single streak of light cutting across that brilliant radiance.

Light cleaving light.

Seogeok!

When the light vanished, everyone could see it.

The Sword King stood before them with his back turned, facing the Sound King.

From the sword he held stretched out to the side, a single drop of blood fell.

The next moment.

Thud, roll.

The Sound King’s head fell to the ground and rolled to the side.

Formless Divine Sword, Seventh Sword: Formless Life.

With that single sword strike, the light was cut, the soundproofing formation was cut, and his music and neck were cut.

Swaaaaaaak!

Blood spurted into the air like a fountain from the Sound King’s severed neck. A rain of blood poured down over the Sword King’s body like a crimson shower.

Chapter 758: Fate Is Short and Life Is Long
-------------------------------------------
Brother Ak, are you kidding me? If this was how it was going to be, you should have killed him from the start!

The reason Geom Mugeuk did not shout those words was because he sensed a deep story in the man’s back as he stood there drenched in the blood pouring down. Because he had vaguely foreseen that such a situation might unfold.

Why had he protected the Sound King?

Because of that, even though another of the Twelve Zodiac Kings had been killed, he felt no joy at all.

Soaked in blood, the Sword King turned toward Geom Mugeuk.

“Listen carefully to what I’m about to say, Mugeuk.”

There was urgency in the Sword King’s words and expression.

“He completed his final piece. And that piece is not the sonic art he was about to unleash earlier.”

Just then, the sound of a quiet performance began to come from somewhere. It seemed to come from the left, then from the right. Gradually, the sound grew louder.

The bright, exhilarating melody did not suit the Sound King’s death at all. It was a melody that gave the feeling that the fight was not yet over. No, that the fight was only beginning!

The Sword King wore an expression as though he had already expected this situation. Through his mouth, a truth they had not known was revealed.

“The Dark Palace Lord bestowed the Heavenly Will Divine Pill upon him. The Heavenly Will Divine Pill is no ordinary elixir. It is the finest spiritual medicine of the Heavenly Will Palace, the only one of its kind, imbued with the energy of heaven, earth, and all things. The palace lord used something he had treasured and treasured for this occasion.”

The Sword King’s gaze turned toward the fallen Sound King. For all that, his death had been far too hollow, and the blood that had poured out of him was staining his pipa red.

“After taking the Heavenly Will Divine Pill, he completed his final piece. Once that piece was completed, it was always being played around him. We simply couldn’t hear it.”

Geom Mugeuk could guess that the tune the Sound King had been riding along with the entire time was that final piece.

“It is the Soul-Exchange Melody.”

The astonishing secret of the Soul-Exchange Melody was revealed.

“If he is killed while the Soul-Exchange Melody is being played, his soul will be exchanged with the soul of the person who killed him.”

Only now did he understand. Why the Sword King had fought so desperately to stop him from killing the man.

“From the very beginning, he intended to die by your hand. That was his goal.”

So that was why he had tried to take Geom Mugeuk’s body.

“Then that means? It means he’ll take over Brother Ak’s body now, doesn’t it? You killed him because you had a countermeasure, right?”

The Sword King could not answer yes. Because there was no countermeasure.

“Why didn’t you tell me? If you’d told me even by voice transmission?”

“If I had?”

Geom Mugeuk could not answer.

“You would certainly have tried to kill him yourself.”

The only people here with the ability to kill him were Geom Mugeuk, the two Demon Venerables, and himself. In this situation, Geom Mugeuk would never sacrifice someone else.

“You would have killed him, thinking you could overcome it somehow. You probably would have cut him down without hesitation.”

Geom Mugeuk let out a sigh. He had seen right through him. Trusting himself, trusting the Nine Flames Demonic Art, he would have killed him.

“Everyone is overestimating me far too much. Why would I cut him down? Not when I knew full well the consequences were so terrifying.”

Because he said that, it was even clearer. Geom Mugeuk would have killed the Sound King. Not only the Sword King, but the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable knew it too. The Young Cult Leader would undoubtedly have cut him down.

“Even you cannot overcome the Soul-Exchange Melody.”

“Then what about Brother Ak?”

As though he had already thought it through, the Sword King’s expression was calm.

“Before that bastard takes over me, you kill me.”

“!”

The Sword King had saved Geom Mugeuk and intended to sacrifice himself.

Even so, he could not have killed him beforehand. If he had, the man might have approached Geom Mugeuk while pretending to be him, and there was no telling what he might have done.

Cutting him down in front of Geom Mugeuk and revealing the truth like this was the best option.

The Sword King’s gaze turned toward the Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

“It would have been better if I had cut him down before the two of you suffered such hardship, but I hesitated. I kept agonizing and searching for another way besides this one.”

Because his life was at stake. Truly, until the very end, he had not wanted to cut him down.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon, who had looked displeased with him, now had a softened expression.

“I understand.”

Not only the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, but the Ilhwa Sword Venerable was inwardly astonished as well. Being with Geom Mugeuk meant experiencing all sorts of things, but what had happened today was also completely unexpected.

For such a powerful expert to sacrifice himself for Geom Mugeuk.

They had assumed there must be a reason he was protecting the one who used sonic arts, but they had not known it would be this. With that heart, he had fought, and with that heart, he had endured the misunderstanding that he was acting frustratingly.

He could have pretended to know nothing and guided them, rather than Geom Mugeuk, into killing him. But he had not passed the task on to someone else. Even though his life was at stake.

Geom Mugeuk asked the Sword King,

“Why are you going this far?”

Yes, he had asked himself that question countless times.

Did he really need to go this far for Geom Mugeuk? Honestly, he had agonized over it. He did not know how much he had agonized.

His bond with Geom Mugeuk was short, and the rest of his life was endlessly long.

But he had chosen to save Geom Mugeuk. He simply wanted to. Because Geom Mugeuk was the first friend in his life, and his only friend.

The Sword King gave a second reason.

“Because if that man takes over your body, this murim will be annihilated.”

If they seized Geom Mugeuk’s identity and everything he had built until now, the result would be terrible.

“No, why are you worrying about the murim? Was Brother Ak that kind of person? You were the one whose goal was to leave the murim!”

The Sword King gave a lonely smile.

“You’re right. I should have left long ago.”

He had meant to leave. But he had learned that the Dark Palace Lord had gone so far as to use the Heavenly Will Divine Pill to deal with Geom Mugeuk, and in the end, he had given up on leaving.

Though he told Geom Mugeuk to kill him, the Sword King knew.

“I know you cannot kill me.”

The Sword King drew his sword. He meant to take his own life.

“Wait! Stop!”

Geom Mugeuk rushed in like a thunderbolt and grabbed his arm. Of all the Wind God Four Steps he had ever performed, this was the fastest.

“Imagine if I wield sonic arts as well. Even you won’t be able to handle me.”

The Sword King and the Sound King combining? He would truly become a formidable enemy. Moreover, while Geom Mugeuk hesitated, unable to bring himself to cut off the Sword King’s head, those with him might be sacrificed. Even so, Geom Mugeuk was certain.

“I can handle it.”

“No!”

Geom Mugeuk shouted fiercely.

“I said I can, you bastard!”

Geom Mugeuk stared at the Sword King with blazing eyes.

“I can handle it! So don’t rush!”

Soon, Geom Mugeuk’s voice quieted, and he became respectful again.

“If this choice is Brother Ak’s choice, then you must give me the chance to choose as well. If you take your own life in front of me, that will leave me with a lifelong regret.”

The Sword King’s eyes wavered.

“Even if that bastard takes over Brother Ak, I will return you to how you were no matter what. If Brother Ak’s soul falls into hell, I will go all the way to hell and bring it back.”

Yes. Because Geom Mugeuk was this sort of person, he had made this choice. He had no regret over his decision.

“Trust me. If I decide there truly is no way, then I’ll kill you with my own hands. When that time comes, die by my hand.”

The Sword King did not believe those words. That was why his gaze moved past Geom Mugeuk’s shoulder and toward the Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

If the time comes later when I must be killed, you must kill me.

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable could read the earnest plea contained in that gaze.

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable silently nodded.

Then Geom Mugeuk turned that grave and somber atmosphere into suspicion.

“But something is strange.”

Geom Mugeuk stared hard at the Sword King and abruptly asked,

“You’re not our Brother Ak, are you?”

What kind of joke was he trying to make in this situation? With that thought, the Sword King looked at Geom Mugeuk.

“You already took over our Brother Ak, didn’t you? This much time has passed, so there’s no way he’d still be the same, right?”

Now that he mentioned it, a fair amount of time had passed while the Sword King explained this and that. As everyone’s gaze focused on him, the Sword King was the one who became flustered instead.

“No. I am still myself.”

He had thought there would be a process where the man invaded as a soul and he struggled to stop that invasion. Would he suddenly transform into that man at any moment?

Would he change the instant this music, still audible, ended?

“Brother Ak, where did we first meet?”

The Sword King shook his head with an incredulous expression.

“Seeing as you didn’t answer, you really are Brother Ak. Everyone ignores me and pretends not to hear what I say.”

The Sword King looked again at the Sound King’s corpse. Had something truly gone wrong?

Then Geom Mugeuk said something unexpected.

“That man probably won’t enter Brother Ak’s body.”

“I hope that is the case, but my willpower is not enough to block the sonic art he completed with the Heavenly Will Divine Pill.”

“No, that isn’t what I mean.”

Geom Mugeuk said something no one had expected at all.

“I don’t think their scheme is this.”

“What?”

Not only the Sword King, but the others all listened closely to Geom Mugeuk’s words.

“I kept thinking about it the whole time too. Why is Brother Ak acting like this? The first thought that came to me was, perhaps if that man is killed, the person who killed him dies with him? Is some cursed song being played? That was what I guessed.”

But that was not it. Because their objective was not to kill him, but to steal the energy dwelling within his body.

“I also considered this current possibility. Could it be that souls are exchanged? But I quickly discarded that guess too.”

“Why?”

“Because of Brother Ak.”

The Sword King wore a puzzled expression.

“If that were the case, why would they send Brother Ak along? Their palace lord knows that last time, we parted without killing each other, doesn’t he?”

“I said I had no choice but not to kill you because of the retrieval of the secret chest.”

“Do you really think the palace lord believed that?”

There was a reason Geom Mugeuk was suspicious.

“They staked everything on this affair. If I were the Dark Palace Lord, I would never have chosen anything that held even the slightest possibility of becoming a problem.”

It was clear that Geom Mugeuk had realized something.

“Then what do you think?”

Swiish!

Instead of answering, Geom Mugeuk swung his sword.

“You tell me.”

The flashing sword energy was aimed at none other than the iron-masked man.

Kaaang!

Screeeech.

A single line was drawn from the forehead of the iron mask down to its chin.

Astonishingly, even under Geom Mugeuk’s sword energy, only a line had been carved into the surface; the iron mask had not been cut apart.

A voice came from beneath the iron mask.

“To think you only show interest in me now. I’m disappointed, Young Cult Leader.”

It was a voice with a different feeling from the powerful, bright voice that had led the heroes earlier. The heavy aura he gave off also did not match the way he had dodged here and there earlier as though trying not to die in the fight.

He slowly removed the iron mask.

Then a middle-aged man with an ordinary face appeared. A man with such an ordinary impression that it seemed any appearance would suit him.

Geom Mugeuk knew who he was. Astonishingly, another of the Twelve Zodiac Kings had been here.

The Eleventh of the Twelve Zodiac Kings, the Iron King, Gi Sahu.

He was a figure who fought using all manner of implements. He mainly handled things made of iron, mixing iron with other metals to create a metal as strong as ten-thousand-year cold iron.

He was a genius maker of mechanisms, and he killed people using all kinds of hidden weapons as well as bizarre devices never before seen in the murim. The people of the murim called him the Ruler of Iron.

With the final of the Twelve Zodiac Kings revealing himself, this was at last the moment when he had met all of the Twelve Zodiac Kings.

“Young Cult Leader, I thought you would realize it a little sooner.”

The Iron King was smiling. Even though his identity had been exposed, he was not the least bit tense or flustered.

He should have realized his existence earlier, but in truth, he had not known then.

He was a man exceptionally skilled at concealing his aura, and rather than a pure martial artist, he was closer to an artisan. Of course, a terrifying artisan who could easily kill experts.

Moreover, after that, his mind had been occupied with the Sword King and the Sound King, and he had never imagined that four of the Twelve Zodiac Kings would be gathered in one place.

But just a moment ago, a thought had suddenly occurred to him. If they had truly staked everything on this affair, there was no way they would have left the remaining one idle.

“It’s you, then. The one who will clean up this mess.”

The Iron King nodded as he listened to the flowing performance.

“Isn’t this piece quite pleasant to listen to?”

As he said that, his gaze turned toward the Sword King.

“The Palace Lord already knew you would betray us.”

Geom Mugeuk said to the Sword King.

“It’s because you go around without shoes. You look like someone ready to drift away at any moment—who would trust that?”

The Sword King’s feelings were complicated. He was glad to be alive, but some greater conspiracy was unfolding.

A new truth came to light through the Iron King.

“This piece is not the Reverse Soul Song, but the Returning Soul Song (歸魂曲).”

If it was the Returning Soul Song, did that mean it returned souls?

“It was a piece that had been completed, yet was not yet complete.”

Beginning with those strange, inscrutable words, an astonishing truth emerged from the Iron King’s mouth.

“The Returning Soul Song is only completed with the performer’s death. In other words, it can only be played through once. So listen well. This is a piece you will never hear again.”

The music flowing around them was lively yet dreamlike, brisk yet chilling.

“To call back souls, would it not require the sacrifice of the living? The performer’s sacrifice is needed, and the one who kills that performer must also be someone sacrificing himself for another. By offering up that desperate heart, the souls of the other world are summoned.”

“!”

It meant that the Sound King’s death and the Sword King’s sacrifice had come together to complete the piece.

“The higher the caliber of the one who sacrifices himself, the purer the heart of that sacrifice, the more souls are called forth.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at the Sword King with worried eyes. This was a man whose caliber was far too high.

“Who on earth were you trying to call back?”

Just then, the music that had been playing reached its peak, and for a moment, the performance stopped.

Clank.

At the sound from behind him, Geom Mugeuk slowly turned around. What he looked at was a puppet standing far in the distance. As the puppet moved, it made the sound of metal.

Swoosh.

The puppet that slowly turned its head to look their way was the Fighting King.

Its eyes seemed to flash once, then changed into eyes that looked truly alive. When he discovered Geom Mugeuk, he began to emit killing intent.

“Young Cult Leader!”

From that blazing gaze, Geom Mugeuk instinctively knew. The soul of the real Fighting King had entered that puppet.

“Brother Ak must have liked me far too much.”

The Fighting King was not the only one who had begun to move.

Drip, drip.

The blood floating in midair fell downward, and a red energy began to flow from the Blood King’s two eyes.

Rustle, rustle.

The vines flowing out of the Wood King’s body began to writhe, and ghostly sounds began to come from the door the Illusion King had opened in the air.

Bones cracked from the Fist King’s clenched fist, and the door of the carriage the Dark King rode in opened slightly, a shadowy gaze gleaming from within.

The Killing King, who had been floating in midair, lightly dropped to the ground. From the black blade and red blade of the Blade King, who had been set up last, the sun and moon began to shine once more.

As majestic, lively music rang out again, all the Twelve Zodiac King puppets there turned their heads toward Geom Mugeuk.

Chapter 759: Why Are Wings Unfolding There?
-------------------------------------------
Nine of the Twelve Zodiac Kings, and even Hwanyeo, the Illusion King’s twin.

Thus, ten of the Twelve Zodiac Kings had been revived. The puppets had been made so vividly that it truly seemed as though they had come back to life.

But their minds had not returned perfectly as well. The memories and instincts of the battles they had been fighting just before their deaths had returned first, leaving them bewildered by the present situation.

Then, when they discovered Geom Mugeuk standing before them, their hatred exploded. And at the same time, they felt a conflicting emotion.

Fear.

The final moments of their deaths came back to them, and terror surged in. Even if they had not been struck down by Geom Mugeuk directly, Geom Mugeuk had been present at every one of their deaths.

At that moment, when fear floated like a plague and began spreading from one to another, the music that had been resounding grew softer and settled low.

Boom! Boom! Boom-boom!

The sound of drums mingled with the performance. The music changed as though a living person were watching this scene. And this music had power.

Like the drums of a battlefield, it drove away the fear the Twelve Zodiac Kings had felt.

Then their innate auras began to flow out.

The red energy streaming in the Blood King’s eyes was exactly the same energy he had possessed when he was alive. The Dark King was the same. The black energy surrounding the carriage was truly eerie, and the Illusion King’s aura was bizarre.

And that aura was even more powerful than what they had possessed before.

Boom-boom-boom!

The lively music was bolstering their momentum.

The one who shouted through the drumbeats was the Fighting King.

“Geom Mugeuk!”

The reason the Fighting King showed a particularly stronger hostility was simple.

Because he had died twice.

Once, while fighting the Leader of the Evil Dao Alliance, Baek Jagang, he had escaped at the moment of crisis, but had nearly died from the Poison King’s poison.

He had treated the poison through several great arts and returned again with the Dark King.

And then he had died again to Geom Mugeuk and Geugak Soma.

“If you die this time, it’ll be your third!”

At Geom Mugeuk’s provocation, the Fighting King could not restrain himself and shot forward.

Swaaaak.

The Fighting King flew in and thrust out his fist. As if he had truly been revived, he displayed the martial arts of that time exactly as they had been. It was even a blow properly imbued with inner force.

But Geom Mugeuk had reached a level different from when he had fought him then. He had become faster and stronger.

Whoosh! Whooosh! Woong!

Geom Mugeuk dodged the Fighting King’s fists as they flew in swiftly, and his own fist struck the Fighting King in the chest.

Thud!

The Fighting King slid backward. He had been struck cleanly, but he was fine.

“Why is he so hard?”

He looked like a person on the outside, but his bones were made of metal. And it was no ordinary iron.

“So it’s a puppet you made.”

The Iron King frowned.

“A puppet? In your eyes, does that look like nothing more than a puppet?”

Yes, he had every right to be proud.

“It is my masterpiece.”

The Soul-Calling Song that summoned souls was extraordinary as well, but he had created a vessel that could contain those souls without any side effects or sense of incongruity whatsoever.

The Twelve Zodiac Kings all walked forward at once.

Their auras contained terrifying killing intent. And it was not one, but ten.

Swaaaaaaaaa!

Their momentum was so tremendous that Taesu stumbled backward and fell on his backside. Jihan tried to endure until the end, but he could not withstand it and retreated to the rear wall. Even though the two Demon Venerables had raised their inner power to block it for them, that was all he could manage.

Amid that horrific aura whipping through the air, Pung, the warrior of the Evil Dao Alliance, had this thought.

Later, if he survived and returned to the Evil Dao Alliance, would the Alliance Leader truly believe his report?

A report that dead masters had been revived.

Judging from how things were going, they seemed to be figures on the same level as the master who had wielded twin blades and died earlier. Ten such masters had been revived.

And they were not the only danger.

Clank, clank, clank, clank.

The walls and ceiling on all sides opened. Inside them were hundreds of holes. They were mechanisms that launched powerful hidden weapons. The holes were so densely packed that if they were fired all at once, there would be nowhere to dodge.

Since they were mechanisms made by the Iron King, their power would be beyond description.

The Iron King opened the front of his garment. A lump of metal of unknown purpose was attached to his heart.

“The moment my heart stops, every mechanism will activate. Then no one in this space will survive.”

He did not underestimate Geom Mugeuk’s abilities.

“Yes, perhaps you, Young Cult Leader, who has mastered the Nine Flames Demonic Art, might survive. But everyone else will die.”

Those words sounded like a warning not to use the Nine Flames Demonic Art recklessly.

Geom Mugeuk slowly walked over and looked into a hole in the wall. Without the slightest fear, he peered into the place from which something seemed ready to shoot out at any moment.

“It’s too dark to see anything.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s composure, the Iron King also refused to be outdone and put on an air of ease.

He, too, fearlessly approached Geom Mugeuk. It was as if he were saying, If you’re so confident, try killing me, proving that his words were no mere threat.

“There are things inside that a human’s protective astral qi cannot block. Shall we test it?”

Even though he had come close enough for his neck to be severed if Geom Mugeuk swung his sword, he showed no fear.

The Iron King looked at Jihan and Taesu and said,

“You said you’d protect them, didn’t you?”

“Of course I have to protect them.”

The Iron King laughed. He was certain that even Geom Mugeuk could never handle this situation while protecting them.

“To think you won’t abandon such weaklings. This is a hell of your own making.”

At the Iron King’s words, Geom Mugeuk answered calmly.

“The hell you imagine and the hell I imagine seem to be different.”

When Geom Mugeuk turned his head, his eyes met Jihan’s, who stood behind him.

“I’m sorry, because of us.”

Jihan was furious at his own weakness. At a time like this, even if he could not help Geom Mugeuk and fight alongside him, he at least should not have become a weakness. The thought that he was becoming a burden wounded his pride, and he felt sorry as well. They themselves were forming one axis of this hell.

Then Geom Mugeuk, leaning his back outright against those terrifying mechanisms, spoke unexpected words.

“If I look back and you’re not standing there, then this place will truly be hell.”

When Jihan looked at him as if asking what he meant, Geom Mugeuk gazed up at the tightly sealed ceiling.

There, too, countless holes from mechanical devices were packed densely.

“The sky outside right now must be wide open and clear.”

He looked up at the ceiling with a deep gaze, as if he were looking at the sky beyond it.

“I have to kill people I once killed before, again, in this underground chamber beneath a lake. If you weren’t here, this place would be my hell.”

Was the gently flowing melody refusing to allow an attack? Even though Geom Mugeuk had his back turned and was conversing with Jihan, the Twelve Zodiac Kings did not rush in. The music was clearly communicating with them.

Geom Mugeuk continued speaking to Jihan.

“Fighting only for yourself, and fighting alone at that. If you keep walking through days like that, one day you may end up becoming a killing sword. Devoured by loneliness.”

Those words reached the Sword King’s heart most deeply. For he had spent most of his life fighting alone.

“There’s no need to be moved because I’m protecting you. This is a choice for my own sake. Fighting for myself is too hard, but fighting for you is less hard. It’s the same when training. So there’s no need to be moved by this selfishness, and no need to feel sorry.”

Jihan had a feeling he would never be able to forget Geom Mugeuk’s appearance in this moment. The moment when, with his back against mechanisms that were said to be impossible to block with a human’s protective astral qi, and with those terrifying Twelve Zodiac Kings behind him, he had said such words to him.

“I still don’t understand why you’re so good to me.”

“Aren’t you also a sword that has fought alone for a long time?”

“!”

From the moment he had been entrusted to his master so that he could live well by using his talent for scent. Yes, his life had been one of living alone, and he had been a sword that fought only for himself.

What more needed to be said when the path he had to walk was being shown to him? Jihan bowed respectfully and said,

“You give off such a wonderful fragrance, Young Cult Leader.”

“Of course. It is only proper to say that a leader always gives off a pleasant fragrance.”

When Geom Mugeuk was about to turn around, there was someone else who felt sorry.

“I’m sorry.”

It was the Sword King. His eyes had sunk coldly. Geom Mugeuk could feel it. He was truly angry.

“This happened because of me.”

He had been used thoroughly.

The Dark Palace Lord had known exactly how he would act. At the thought that he had been played by him, the Sword King could not restrain his anger.

“You should be thanked instead.”

“For what?”

“Thanks to you, I confirmed just how much Brother Ak cares for me, didn’t I?”

Geom Mugeuk formally greeted him.

“Thank you for saving me.”

“I didn’t save you.”

“You already did.”

It was no different from having already saved him. The moment he killed the Sound King, he had offered up his own life.

“When I meet that woman who likes Brother Ak, I’ll be sure to tell her about today. That the man she likes is this magnificent.”

He knew those words were being said to comfort him, but even knowing that, they made him feel better.

“You’re busy, comforting your subordinate and comforting me.”

Turning toward the Twelve Zodiac Kings, Geom Mugeuk said,

“There is one more person to comfort—no, one more soul to comfort.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward the Wood King.

“It may have been only a moment ago for you, but it has been a very long time.”

In their previous battle, Geom Mugeuk had tried to resolve the matter without killing him somehow. He had dreamed of revenge because he had misunderstood that the Murim Alliance Leader had annihilated his family.

He, too, was a man with many sins, but at the very least, Geom Mugeuk wanted to tell him this.

“I confirmed that it was not the deed of the Murim Alliance Leader.”

In his previous fight with Hwa Yulcheong, he had discovered that those behind all this had been involved in the destruction of the Mokcheon family.

At those words, the Wood King’s eyes wavered. He recalled the moment just before his death when Geom Mugeuk had struggled to save him. He could feel that Geom Mugeuk had not been lying then, nor was he lying now.

“The one who plotted that matter will eventually die by my hand, so now release your long-held resentment and misunderstanding.”

The Wood King said nothing, but Geom Mugeuk felt that the look in his eyes had eased.

Srrrk.

The Wood King’s eyes closed.

By his own will, his soul tried to leave this place.

Right then.

The music resounding around them grew even louder. Like the wail of ghosts, the notes tore and rose high. It was trying to stop his soul from leaving.

“Stop.”

Geom Mugeuk spoke in a low voice, as if warning. When those words, carrying demonic energy, rang out, the music stopped for an instant, then instead grew even louder.

“I told you to stop!”

Geom Mugeuk’s demonic energy poured out toward the Twelve Zodiac Kings.

Whoooooong.

As if a violent gale were whipping through, the bodies of the Twelve Zodiac Kings fluttered fiercely in the demonic energy.

And when the surging demonic energy ceased, the Wood King’s soul was gone. It was as though it had ridden the wind and flown far away.

The Wood King, his eyes closed, no longer moved, and the expression he wore at the end held a smile.

“In your next life, may you live neither a life of killing nor a life of being killed.”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon, who had been listening to those words, could tell that Geom Mugeuk had spoken them sincerely.

Perhaps that was the life Geom Mugeuk himself wished for. He was a fellow who wanted to live laughing, chattering, and joking around. Yes, the person most uncomfortable in this place was likely the strongest among them.

Geom Mugeuk walked forward and looked at the Iron King. His gaze was cold and solemn.

“You people have plotted all manner of schemes and conspiracies until now. But calling back those who are already dead was the one thing you should never have done. If you couldn’t win no matter what you did in reality, you should have given up like martial artists. Lived running an ironworks. Lived as musicians.”

As if it were listening to Geom Mugeuk’s answer, the sound of the music turned grotesque.

At that moment, the Sword King walked out from behind him.

“They aren’t people who will understand no matter how much you talk. I know that well.”

Riiip.

He tore a long strip from the hem of his clothes. Then he tied up his loosened hair tightly again. And he took off the shoes hanging at his waist and put them on.

Then he stepped forward alone and looked at those lined up before him.

Until he met Geom Mugeuk, he himself had been one of them. He had even been one who occupied the very top among them. At least, the Dark Palace Lord had treated him that way.

“You’ve all had plenty of complaints about me, haven’t you?”

In particular, cold killing intent flickered in the Saber King’s eyes.

Then a cold voice came from behind.

“I told you that if we met again, I’d kill you again.”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon slowly walked forward and stood beside the Sword King. He had not stepped forward because of the Saber King.

At first, he had found the Sword King displeasing, but he genuinely felt grateful that the Sword King had willingly sacrificed himself for Geom Mugeuk. He could not let him fight alone.

“For someone who uses a scrap of a sword, you’ve got spirit.”

Ilhwa Sword Venerable also stepped forward. The reason she did so was out of respect for the Sword King as a martial artist who used the sword.

“Older Brother, if you keep doing that, one day you’ll make a mistake in front of the Cult Leader.”

“Don’t say such things, even as a joke.”

The Sword King looked at the two people standing on either side of him and bowed his head slightly. He had not expected these two to step forward for his sake.

The three of them drew their sword and sabers at the same time.

Then a disgruntled voice came from behind them.

“No, why are my wings unfolding over there?”

An expression of helpless disbelief crossed the three of their faces. Truly, to try to make people laugh even at a moment like this.

“Why are only the three of you acting solemn and cool? Why are only the three of you pretending to be close without me? I’m going to fight too. Brother Ak, move aside. That’s my place.”

Of course, no one looked back, and no one answered.

The Iron King, watching them, felt Geom Mugeuk’s composure as an insult.

“Young Cult Leader, your arrogance will drive everyone to their deaths.”

When Geom Mugeuk looked back at the Iron King, all playfulness had vanished from his face.

“We had a difference of opinion about hell, didn’t we? I think I’ve seen enough of your hell…”

With eyes so cold it was hard to believe that such jokes could have come from that face,

“Now behold my hell.”

Snap.

Chapter 760: Where I Am Is Hell
-------------------------------
An endless field stretched out before them.

The Iron King’s eyes flew wide open. He was truly shocked and bewildered. In a single instant, he and the Twelve Zodiac Kings were standing in a different space.

There had been no presence, no sensation of entering or being moved anywhere.

‘What is this?’

The scent of grass carried on the wind was real.

There had been no intelligence that the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult could use an illusion art like this.

They were not the only ones surprised.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon, Ilhwa Sword Venerable, and the Sword King had come there as well.

“I brought you three along because I thought it would be all right for you to know that I can open a space like this.”

Ilhwa Sword Venerable examined the ground. It was real grass and stone. The soil was real too.

‘It isn’t a simple illusion. It’s real.’

The astonishments the Young Cult Leader had shown on this journey were not over yet.

Then the Iron King asked with a puzzled look.

“Is this the hell you spoke of?”

Geom Mugeuk looked up at the clear blue sky. Earlier, when he had spoken to Jihan, he had wanted to see the sky.

“Yes. This is hell.”

“Isn’t it too beautiful to be called hell?”

“Then were you perhaps expecting a hell like this?”

Snap!

When Geom Mugeuk snapped his fingers, the place changed again.

The blue space turned red. It was a sight that could truly be called hell.

A river of blood flowed, and flames erupted here and there like volcanoes.

The sounds of souls in agony rang out together with the shrieks of evil spirits. The wind that blew in was filled with the suffering of burned souls, enough to make one feel suffocated just by hearing it.

Just then, a hellhound spewing fire from its mouth charged toward Geom Mugeuk.

The hellhound rushed in as if to pounce, then came before Geom Mugeuk and wagged its tail. When Geom Mugeuk stroked the hellhound’s head, it spat fire from its mouth.

Whoosh.

The Sword King and the two Demon Venerables looked down at the sight in astonishment. This hell, and even the dog guarding it, were far too vivid.

“This kind of hell is hell too.”

Snap.

The place where they stood changed back into the original field.

“And this place is hell as well.”

The location did not matter at all.

“Wherever I am is hell for you.”

Swaaaaaa.

Geom Mugeuk’s cold demonic energy, carrying unmistakable killing intent, swept over them once more.

The Iron King’s expression hardened. By being dragged here, he had lost the hostages—his most powerful move.

With a gentle face completely at odds with the demonic energy he had just released, Geom Mugeuk turned to the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“They say looking at a lot of green is good for the eyes.”

“Are you treating me like an old man again?”

“No, it’s because you read so many books.”

There were two reasons Geom Mugeuk had brought them here with the Space-Time Transposition Art.

The first was to neutralize the Iron King’s mechanisms. The Iron King could operate his mechanisms at will, but at the very least he had to be in the same space as them.

The device in his chest that would supposedly activate when he died would also have to be in the same space.

This was a space created from the same location, yet it was a completely different world.

Now his mechanisms had been utterly neutralized.

The second reason was that, in order to use the Nine Calamities Demonic Art freely, he had to come here. If he had used the Nine Calamities Demonic Art back there, the cave might have collapsed and buried them all.

Just then, music began to play. Just as the souls of the Twelve Zodiac Kings resided in those bodies, the soul of the Sound King resided in that unending melody.

Geom Mugeuk looked in the direction of the music and said,

“It seems you want to become an immortal musician.”

At that, the music responded by turning sharp and irritable.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon stepped forward.

“You brought us well. Now we can fight to our hearts’ content.”

Seeing his excitement, Geom Mugeuk wore an apologetic expression.

“No, you cannot.”

“What?”

“I will fight. Alone.”

“Then why did you bring us here?”

“So you can watch me fight. I need someone watching for it to be exciting. What fun is there in fighting alone?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon looked dumbfounded.

The one more disappointed than the two Demon Venerables was, without question, the Sword King.

“This fight is my fight!”

Geom Mugeuk said to him,

“This fight is my fight.”

This was a change brought about by his own regression. The fact that he had come to fight them here was entirely because of him.
