The Sword King revealed the reason he had not left the organization.

“The reason I did not leave the organization was not because of the Dark Palace Lord, but because of you.”

He had stayed to protect Geom Mugik upon learning of the plan to call back the souls of the dead. Of course, that plan was something the Dark Palace Lord had intentionally leaked to draw him in.

“There is no need to go so far. You do not need to prove your heart, Brother Ak. I already know it all. Your very existence is more than I deserve.”

Look who’s talking! How could the Sword King not worry, when Geom Mugik was this kind of person?

“The Dark Palace Lord is strong. No, because we do not know how strong she is, she is all the more fearsome.”

That even the Sword King could not fathom her depth showed what level of martial prowess she possessed.

“We do not even know how old she is.”

That was not all.

“Aside from those you have killed until now, there are many experts leading directly under the Dark Palace Lord.”

It was only natural to think so. The Dark Palace was an organization that had spent a full three hundred years preparing.

“Aren’t you worried?”

“You need not worry.”

“Why?”

Geom Mugik’s gaze turned to the sky. Words he had spoken to his father long ago, he now spoke to the Sword King.

“Because my demonic path exists for this very moment.”

The reason Geom Mugik had worked himself to the brink of death was for moments like this, to say such words with conviction.

“How fearsome must an opponent be if she makes even Brother Ak nervous? A monstrous opponent whose age we do not know, whose strength we do not know. That is why no one would dare to even think of killing such an enemy.”

Geom Mugik’s gaze, which had been turned to the heavens, now faced the Sword King again.

“The conviction that I must strike down an absolute evil—a vicious, venomous wickedness that humanity cannot endure—that is my demonic path.”

Watching the Sword King gaze at him wordlessly, Geom Mugik calmly added.

“If we cannot kill her, who will? A demon must be devoured by a greater demon.”

* * *

And the next morning.

The warriors of the Ghost Shadow Corps were the first to assemble at the departure point. They wore black martial uniforms and masks, and on the black masks the characters for “Ghost Shadow” were embroidered in white.

Having donned the Ghost Shadow Corps’ formal attire and masks, there was nothing to fear when they gathered together.

Their shoulders squared with unwarranted strength, and they felt like showing themselves off, as if hoping to catch a glimpse of admiration somewhere.

Just then, the Escort Agency Chief personally emerged at the site.

The Escort Agency warriors brought horses matching the number of Ghost Shadow Corps members and distributed them.

“Branches of the Escort Agency are concealed throughout the Central Plains. You may change your horses for fresh ones whenever they tire.”

“Thank you, Chief.”

Ian expressed her gratitude politely to the Escort Agency Chief. Having launched such a large-scale operation for the first time, she had only now learned that the Escort Agency would provide such support.

The Ghost Shadow Corps warriors were secretly pleased. Hadn’t the Escort Agency Chief come out personally because they were setting out? Of course, that happiness was short-lived, and it was a misconception.

Other Escort Agency warriors arrived pulling carriages.

They were massive transport carriages. Not just one, but more than ten. The Escort Agency warriors lined them up without the slightest error.

The Ghost Shadow Corps warriors were bewildered.

They were riding horses; why had such massive carriages been brought? Were they to carry luggage? Even so, why carriages that large, and so many of them?

Then, a group of warriors arrived.

Even from afar, one could tell who they were at a glance. Warriors of enormous stature, they were the Demon Army—the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult’s elite organization. They appeared at the site exuding a terrifying pressure that was apparent at a mere glance.

At the appearance of the Demon Army, the Ghost Shadow Corps warriors’ eyes widened. They realized what the carriages were for. They were carriages for the Demon Army.

Could this be an operation with the Demon Army?

SCREECH.

They tore away the camouflaging paper attached to the carriage walls, revealing the Demon Army’s symbol and name written inside.

Demon Army.

The Demon Army usually moved in secret, but this operation was official. Just imagining these massive carriages racing in a line was magnificent in itself.

Demon Army Commander Jangho walked out from among the Demon Army.

“Commander Ian.”

“Lord Jang!”

Ian and Jangho exchanged glad greetings.

“It is the first time, is it not? Going on an operation together with you, Commander.”

“It is an honor, Lord. I still lack experience, so I ask for your guidance.”

“I am well aware that the Ghost Shadow Corps has been excellently trained. We ask for your cooperation as well.”

Jangho answered humbly.

The Ghost Shadow Corps was greatly surprised by the fact that this was a joint operation with the Demon Army, but their surprise was only beginning.

Demon Lord Seophon had arrived at the site.

As she strode in with a proud expression, the air around her seemed to change. She was one who exuded a chilling presence without even revealing her aura.

Everyone cupped their fists respectfully to Demon Lord Seophon.

Since they had not known this operation would be with Demon Lord Seophon, the Ghost Shadow Corps warriors grew even more tense. Only now could they understand why the Escort Agency Chief had come out personally.

An Escort Agency warrior brought a carriage. It was a carriage reserved for a Demon Lord—Demon Lord Seophon’s carriage. Its walls were painted with dizzyingly complex swirling patterns that made one’s head spin just by looking at them. Yet one thing was clear from those patterns alone: touch it carelessly, and your life would get complicated.

Then another Demon Lord walked over, yawning.

“Is there really a need to set out this early in the morning?”

The one who appeared with a face still flushed with drink was Chwima.

When Chwima appeared, the Ghost Shadow Corps warriors were even more surprised. This was an operation where not one, but two Demon Lords were deploying.

The Demon Army wasn’t enough, and now two Demon Lords were going out? What kind of operation was this?

The Escort Agency warrior brought Chwima’s carriage. It was a carriage modified to be much longer than the others.

“Due to my hangover, I shall rest a bit.”

Upon opening the carriage and entering, there was a bed inside, and the walls were lined with bottles of alcohol. Everyone’s eyes widened, for they had never seen such a carriage before.

Once again, Mabul walked in, radiating a golden glow.

The moment they saw him, the Ghost Shadow Corps warriors gasped.

Demon Lord Mabul is going too?

Not two, but three Demon Lords?

While politely greeting the appearing Demon Lords, the Ghost Shadow Corps warriors exchanged flustered glances.

Three Demon Lords, whom they had barely glimpsed once while in the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, were now appearing.

The carriage brought for Mabul was plated in gold. It was a carriage that would summon every bandit in the world.

Then, the fourth Demon Lord appeared.

Geugak Soma and the Thousand Flower Pavilion Lord had arrived. At the sight of that pure white mask, the Ghost Shadow Corps warriors froze.

Even Geugak Soma is going!

It was truly chaos itself, beyond mere bewilderment and surprise.

An Escort Agency warrior brought Geugak Soma’s carriage. It was a carriage made of pure white walls with nothing drawn on them.

The Ghost Shadow Corps warriors were all at a loss for words. The Young Cult Leader, four Demon Lords, and the elite Demon Army were all going? A single thought flashed through their minds.

Has a war broken out or something?

The Thousand Flower Pavilion Lord was surprised in a different way from the Ghost Shadow Corps warriors. She had heard that Geom Mugik would set out in full force, but she had not known he intended to muster to this extent.

She had actually considered going alone to meet the Holy Maiden Palace Lord.

However, the matter Geom Mugik had judged required this many people to deploy.

Then someone’s voice was heard.

“The Young Cult Leader emerges!”

The owner of the voice was Jeokyeon, head of the guards.

Clack, clack, clack, clack, clack, clack, clack.

The Young Cult Leader’s guards, wearing formal uniforms and masks, walked out and cleared a path. The guards who had devoted themselves to training did not lose in momentum to any of the warriors present. They naturally inspired the thought: if they are the Young Cult Leader’s guards, they ought to be at that level.

Geom Mugeuk walked in among them.

“The protagonist usually appears last, after all.”

The window of Chwima’s carriage opened, and Chwima came into view.

“So cool! We—”

Before Chwima could make a drunken scene, Geom Mugeuk lightly waved his hand.

He pulled the carriage window shut through the air.

“Your hangover must be severe, so please sleep a little longer.”

Judging by the fact that he still hadn’t sobered up, it seemed he had come straight from martial arts training that morning.

After formally paying his respects to the Demon Lords, Geom Mugeuk looked around at the other subordinates present. Spotting Jangho, he especially raised his hand to express his delight. Seeing this, the morale of the Demon Generals soared even higher.

“There are so many of you that if I said even a single word to each, the sun would probably set, wouldn’t it?”

Under normal circumstances, that ought to elicit a response of “Then keep it short!” But Geom Mugeuk was different.

“Even if the sun sets, say everything you wish to say.”

Smiles spread across everyone’s lips. Of course, no one here was tactless enough to actually speak up in such a situation.

Ah, though there was one among the outsiders.

“Why am I being left out!”

Somehow having found out, the Pungcheon Cult Leader strode in.

“I’m going too.”

“You cannot.”

“Why not?”

“Because I, too, would be uncomfortable. I am not taking Master with me.”

This meant that the Soul-Devouring Demon Lord, his disciple, would be uncomfortable if they went together. That they could even hold such a conversation in front of the Soul-Devouring Demon Lord was only possible because it was between Geom Mugeuk and the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

“It’s because you don’t know, but my disciple and I are incredibly close. They’ll be fine even if I’m there.”

“Being close is what Master thinks. Those above are always comfortable. We are not!”

“But I’m not your master, am I?”

Rather than replying to that irrefutable point, Geom Mugeuk flattered the Pungcheon Cult Leader.

“It is because the Cult Leader safeguards the main cult that I can depart so comfortably. That I can even take the Demon Generals with me is all thanks to the Cult Leader.”

“Is that so?”

Since it was a public place with many eyes watching, the Pungcheon Cult Leader squared his shoulders proudly for no particular reason.

“Then what about me!”

When he turned around, Hwangcheon Pavilion Master Seo Daeryong was there as well.

The martial artists of the Ghost Shadow Corps were once again surprised by the sight. The very person who had driven even the leader of that strict organization to come and beg to be taken along was the Young Cult Leader.

“Are you truly afraid the sun will set if I come along? I will keep my lips sealed tight.”

He wanted to follow Geom Mugeuk out on an operation like this, even if it meant throwing away his position as Hwangcheon Pavilion Master.

“No, you’d be an eyesore even to a newborn, so you can’t come.”

“I will endure it.”

“Not you, me. If I see you, I will constantly be reminded of a baby. You’re too pretty, so no.”

After rejecting him so pleasantly, Geom Mugeuk turned his gaze to those who would accompany him.

“Now then, since we are determined to properly show off this time, I am counting on all of you.”

The escort martial artists brought forth the Young Cult Leader’s personal carriage.

On the sturdy walls of the blade-proof carriage, the symbol of the Heavenly Demon Soul was drawn with fearsome grandeur.

“Have a safe journey!”

Leaving behind Seo Daeryong’s disappointment, everyone boarded their carriages.

The carriage carrying the Demon Generals took the lead, followed by Geom Mugeuk’s carriage, around which the escort martial artists rode on horseback as guards.

Behind them came the four Demon Lords’ carriages, and finally the Ghost Shadow Corps followed on horseback.

The escort martial artists, who had been giving hand signals to the coachmen to pull the carriages out in orderly fashion, all bowed their heads in unison as Geom Mugeuk’s carriage passed. Before them, the carriages and horses raced out in a long line.

It was the Young Cult Leader’s first departure from the cult, conducted with all due ceremony.

Chapter 780: How Could I Resist a Golden Carriage?
--------------------------------------------------
“We will rest here today.”

Jeokyeon, having received Geom Mugeuk’s order, announced it to everyone.

At his shout, the large transport wagons racing at the front slowed and came to a stop.

Not only did Geom Mugeuk know the roads well, he had abundant experience camping in the field, and he knew exactly where such a large group should rest.

The wagons left the road and stopped in a wide field. There were too many people for them to enter a village.

The Demon Army’s large wagons formed up as if building a wall around the outside, and within them, Geom Mugeuk’s carriage and the Demon Venerables’ carriages were likewise arranged in a circle.

The Demon Army had the greatest numbers, but they were quiet and swift. They were men who had truly experienced every kind of battle in the field.

Without being told, Ian watched them take their positions and gave orders to her Ghost Shadow Squad subordinates.

“We’ll take position over here.”

Pretending not to watch, Jang Ho observed them. Since Ian had never moved and managed troops on this scale before, Jang Ho had expected her to flounder somewhat.

So she had intended to inform her through sound transmission, but unexpectedly, Ian handled the placement of her subordinates splendidly.

What was even more splendid was this.

“Team Leader Sa.”

She called for Jihan.

“Yes, Unit Leader.”

“To be honest, much of what I know comes from books rather than experience. Since Team Leader Sa served as leader of the Soul-Chasing Unit, I imagine you’re more practiced at this sort of thing than I am. Is this deployment correct?”

She did not pretend to know what she did not. She showed a willingness to learn, and she did not let such matters wound her pride.

“Yes, it is better than what I would have done. However, I think the spacing between teams could be widened a little. Because when a surprise attack comes from outside…”

Ian listened intently to his explanation.

Watching her, Jang Ho thought she was doing well.

‘You are doing well, Unit Leader.’

And so the Demon Army and the Ghost Shadow Squad took their places on the outside, while Geom Mugeuk and the Demon Venerables settled in the center.

Campfires sprang up here and there, and Geom Mugeuk and the Demon Venerables also sat around a campfire in the middle.

Behind Geom Mugeuk, the warriors of the guard unit took position. Applying what they had trained in actual practice, they guarded Geom Mugeuk without the slightest gap.

Geom Mugeuk called the head of the guards, Jeokyeon.

“Jeokyeon.”

“Yes, Young Cult Leader.”

“To be honest, you wish someone would attack, don’t you?”

Jeokyeon was flustered by the sudden question.

“No, sir.”

“You do, don’t you? You trained so hard for this day. Someone has to attack for you to show what you can do, right?”

“Of course, as long as we are here, they won’t be able to touch so much as a hair on the Young Cult Leader’s head.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at the Demon Army and Ghost Shadow Squad outside.

“An enemy who made it through those people? And you’re saying you’re stronger? I’ll have to tell the Demon Army to work harder.”

“That’s not what I…”

The one who saved the flustered Jeokyeon was the Drunken Demon. He took a bottle of liquor from his carriage and came over.

“Young Cult Leader, why are you tormenting an innocent man?”

The Drunken Demon did not speak to him as a sworn brother in front of everyone. He treated him respectfully as the Young Cult Leader.

“When you speak so politely, it makes it seem like I really am tormenting him.”

Looking at the other three Demon Venerables seated before the campfire, the Drunken Demon said,

“Let us each have just one cup before we sleep.”

After pouring liquor for Geom Mugeuk first, the Drunken Demon also poured for the Demon Buddha, the Extreme Evil Soma, and the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable. This was the first time the four of them had shared drinks together out in a field like this.

Ever since leaving the cult, the Drunken Demon had been drunk the entire time. Everyone could sense it. The Drunken Demon was in the midst of martial training. The fact that he continued training even while traveling meant that the training he was undergoing was that important.

“This liquor is delicious too, Drunken Demon.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s admiration, the Drunken Demon lifted the bottle and said proudly,

“It is battle wine I saved for an occasion like this.”

“This isn’t exactly a departure from the cult worthy of such a grand meaning.”

The Drunken Demon looked around. The gesture seemed to say, You brought out enough troops to wage a war, and yet you say it is not?

The Demon Buddha looked at the warriors of the Ghost Shadow Squad and said,

“When did you raise such an excellent organization?”

“It wasn’t me. Ian raised them.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward Ian. Standing with her subordinates, she looked quite the unit leader. Though she complained that it did not suit her, she was doing well.

“They are the ones who will lead our cult after we retire.”

At those words, the Demon Buddha looked utterly dumbfounded.

“You are already thinking of retirement before you have even begun?”

“I cannot live like my father, after all.”

“And if you cannot?”

The Demon Buddha pressed him with a sharp gaze, but Geom Mugeuk did not explain in detail.

“I intend to live while enjoying myself. Now, let us have another drink.”

This time, Geom Mugeuk filled everyone’s cup. As he poured for the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable last, the Demon Buddha, who had received his drink first, abruptly asked,

“Have you no desire to unite the murim?”

At the Demon Buddha’s question, everyone’s eyes turned to Geom Mugeuk.

When he had gone to see the Drunken Demon before, the Drunken Demon had asked the same question. Recently, with the power of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult overflowing, the unification of the murim kept being mentioned.

Seeing an opportunity, the Drunken Demon joined in and sounded out Geom Mugeuk.

“I am curious what a murim led by our Young Cult Leader would look like.”

After a brief pause, Geom Mugeuk said,

“What is there to look forward to in a murim built by killing my friends?”

Everyone knew well of his relationship with Bisa-in and Jin Hagun, so the Demon Buddha took up those words.

“Just as you succeeded in the struggle for succession with your elder brother, could you not achieve the unification of the murim without bloodshed?”

If the person listening to those words had not been Geom Mugeuk, they would have been dismissed as nonsense. But if it was Geom Mugeuk? Everyone waited for his answer.

“You overestimate me far too much. Even if I conceded a hundred times and assumed that impossibility came true, could that peace continue to be maintained?”

Just as the Dark Palace had spent three hundred years nurturing its strength in darkness to seize the murim, the righteous and unorthodox factions would do the same. No, they would be even more vicious.

“There would be countless attempts to reclaim the fallen righteous path, to recover the lost unorthodox path, and in the end, as much blood would be shed as if a war had broken out.”

Geom Mugeuk added calmly,

“This murim has been divided into demon, righteous, and unorthodox for far too long. Becoming one is nothing more than an ideal.”

The Demon Buddha gazed at Geom Mugeuk and said,

“Depending on whose ideal it is, it may become reality.”

Geom Mugeuk could tell the Demon Buddha had said it with his father in mind.

“The moment will come when I must persuade all of you on this issue.”

Geom Mugeuk raised his cup and said,

“When that time comes, I will be in your care.”

A brief silence flowed.

The first to raise his cup was the Extreme Evil Soma. This toast was his will to follow Geom Mugeuk, even if it meant defying his father’s order.

Until now, the Extreme Evil Soma had been the only act of defiance.

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable raised his cup.

Though last time he had been unable to defy Geom Woojin’s will and had crossed over to the other side, now he intended to follow the Young Cult Leader’s orders. The Young Cult Leader was someone worth that much.

The Demon Buddha did not raise his cup until the end, and the Drunken Demon drank alone without making a toast.

“One day, the day will come when I toast with the two of you as well!”

With an expression that said the situation to unfold in the future would be amusing just to imagine, the Demon Buddha emptied his cup.

“That would be your ideal.”

* * *

Mak Chung, the chief of Dahu Stockade, had dreamed the previous night.

It was a dream of a gigantic golden whale swimming toward him. It was so vivid that even after waking, he remembered the scene clearly.

And now he was looking at that golden whale. No, to be precise.

“It really is a golden carriage.”

Standing atop the gorge, Mak Chung stared in rapture at the golden carriage racing toward them from far away.

When he had first received the report from the subordinate sent out to scout, he had thought it was a carriage painted yellow. What madman would go around coating a carriage in gold?

But judging from the way it reflected the sunlight, it had to be a carriage truly plated with gold.

As the dream came to mind, Mak Chung felt that the once-in-a-lifetime chance for a windfall that came to every bandit of the Green Forest had finally found him.

“How rich must one be to ride in a carriage made of gold?”

This was the moment to risk his life. There was only one thing that bothered him.

“They’re not from the Demon Cult, are they?”

The subordinate who had led the scouts was his right-hand man, Gong Pei.

“Chief, never once have we heard of demonic practitioners riding in golden carriages.”

“What are the chances they’re from a famous martial clan?”

“Look at the wagons at the very front. Martial clans do not use large transport wagons like those. It must be a merchant group moving expensive goods, or a tycoon relocating.”

“How many guards?”

“Quite a lot.”

At those words, Mak Chung was instead relieved. A martial organization with genuine experts did not travel in such a swarm.

It had to be a tycoon or a merchant group hiring a great number of wandering warriors to show off their might.

“On top of that, their formation is a mess. Look there. There are no guarding warriors around the large wagons, and the riders are trailing behind. They’re frightened and have merely filled out their numbers.”

“Still, there should be one or two real experts.”

As Mak Chung hesitated, Gong Pei said with an expression telling him not to worry.

“If we ambush them in the gorge, they will have no choice either.”

His hesitation did not last long. They had lived their whole lives as bandits of the Green Forest.

“No matter how dangerous it is, how can we let a golden carriage pass?”

* * *

The wagons traveling in a line through the narrow gorge slowed and came to a stop.

Jang Ho got down from her wagon and walked toward Geom Mugeuk’s carriage behind them.

“It’s the Green Forest.”

Then came Geom Mugeuk’s cheer, who had said about a hundred times that riding separately from the Demon Venerables was far too boring.

“Finally!”

As if he had been waiting, Geom Mugeuk quickly hopped down from the carriage. Up ahead, bandits of the Green Forest could be seen blocking the road. And atop the gorge on both sides, dozens of bandits had arrows aimed at them.

The arrows were not the problem. They had prepared more than ten enormous boulders with levers, ready to roll them down at any moment. If those fell, the people could dodge, but the wagons would be the problem.

“I’ve finally met them! Even though I went in and out of the cult so many times, I never met these fellows once. I was so bored I kept hoping I’d meet them just once, just once, and yet I never did. But now that I’ve brought everyone out like this, they appear? Are these blind bandits?”

As Geom Mugeuk looked dumbfounded, Jang Ho smiled and said,

“They seem to have seen the large transport wagons and thought a merchant group was moving cargo.”

Moreover, because there were so many people following behind, they had likely looked down on them instead.

“This is all the Demon Buddha’s fault. With such a glittering golden carriage running along, how could the Green Forest endure it? Even I couldn’t.”

Geom Mugeuk was not one to miss such a fine opportunity to tease the Demon Buddha.

“Really, what is someone of the Buddhist path doing with a golden carriage?”

From inside the carriage came the Demon Buddha’s awkward cough.

“What will you do? Will you send all those people to Buddhahood? Take responsibility.”

Then a single phrase came from inside the Demon Buddha’s carriage.

“That is not something I cannot do.”

As if he truly intended to kill them all, the Demon Buddha opened the carriage door and tried to come out.

Geom Mugeuk closed the door with remote manipulation.

“Our Blood Buddha will wait for a moment. Commander Jang, if those fellows see you, they’ll run away without even breathing. Hurry and hide. Guards, you withdraw behind there too! Everyone, don’t stand out! Absolutely not!”

Jang Ho climbed onto a large wagon with a laugh, and the guards withdrew behind them with expressions that seemed to ask, Are we really hiding?

In the meantime, the Green Forest bandits approached. The man at the front was Mak Chung, with Gong Pei close beside him. Behind them followed the most skilled men of Dahu Stockade.

Mak Chung first examined what sort of people his opponents were, and how great their skills might be.

‘The drivers are nothing special.’

The warriors who came out of the convoy looked like coachmen to anyone. In truth, they were men who could freely drive these large wagons even into narrow places.

‘It really is a tycoon’s procession.’

Was it because he wanted that to be true? Or because of the dream of the golden whale? He came to that hasty conclusion.

After passing more than ten large wagons, they found Geom Mugeuk waiting for them.

Because of that, they could not reach Geom Mugeuk’s carriage and did not see the picture drawn on it.

“Pleased to meet you, everyone of the Green Forest! This is my first time seeing you!”

Such a bright greeting? Mak Chung had never seen such a naive young fool before.

“I am Mak Chung, chief of Dahu Stockade.”

“Oh! So you are Hero Mak!”

Why is he so happy? We’re the Green Forest, you fool!

Mak Chung lowered his voice with dignity.

“This gorge is so dangerous and rugged that it is easy to be attacked here. We will protect you until you pass through.”

“I know, I know. Then we give you a protection fee, right? Right? I know this well too.”

Ah, what a naive young fool! Mak Chung thought he might truly seize the greatest fortune of his life. No matter how he looked, the young man’s martial skill seemed pathetic.

“Our young hero knows very well.”

Ordinarily, bandits of the Green Forest did not rob people of their goods and kill them. If they did, they would be attacked by the merchant alliance or the escort agency alliance.

Thus, it was the custom of the jianghu to tacitly receive a toll and let each other pass on amicable terms.

Of course, today’s glittering procession stirred a desire in him to take everything.

Even now, Mak Chung’s gaze was fixed on the sparkling golden carriage behind them.

He was so absorbed in it that he passed right by the words “Demon Army” written on the side of the large wagons. Then again, even if he had seen them, he would never have dreamed that this Demon Army was that Demon Army.

“How much should we give you as a toll?”

“Usually, we take ten percent of the value of the goods.”

Of course, they did not normally take that much. He had simply thrown it out there first, even if he later had to lower the price.

“Oh, ten percent! It does feel a bit expensive, but since this is my first time paying, I should give it to you!”

If he had known this would happen, he should have asked for thirty percent.

As Mak Chung felt regret, Geom Mugeuk let out a sigh.

“But what shall we do? I did not bring that much money with me.”

At that, Mak Chung spoke as if being generous.

“Originally, we only accept money, but today I will make a special exception. Since you have more than ten wagons loaded with goods, would it not do to hand over one of them?”

“Ah, would that be all right?”

When Geom Mugeuk readily agreed to give one, Mak Chung inwardly cried out in delight. Then greed was added to it.

“Now that I think of it, I’ll have to choose two. Counting the wagons, there are far more than ten, are there not? And since I am making a special concession by accepting goods, young hero, you should make a concession as well.”

This time too, Geom Mugeuk readily permitted it.

“Very well. Choose two. There are large wagons up front, and there are several other carriages back here. Take your time looking around and choose just two.”

With a bright smile, as if something pleased him greatly, Geom Mugeuk said,

“But you must take the carriages you choose!”

Chapter 781: I’ll Give You a Chance to Change It!
-------------------------------------------------
“What carriage should we choose?”

Mak Chung and Gongbae held a strategy meeting. Gongbae knew that his leader’s heart had been stolen by the golden carriage.

“Surprisingly, there may not be anything special inside that golden carriage.”

“It’s a disguise?”

“Couldn’t they have made us focus on that carriage while hiding the precious treasures somewhere else?”

Now that he heard it, it sounded plausible, but he could not forget that vivid dream of the golden whale.

Geom Mugeuk came over beside Mak Chung and said,

“Now, take your time looking around, and choose the one that gives you that feeling.”

Why was this bastard so excited when his own belongings were being taken?

Mak Chung guessed the reason in his own way. Ah, he’s so terrified that he’s acting like this.

After all, when people were truly terrified, they tended to pretend to be this calm. Thinking that, he felt a little sorry for the fellow.

“The carriage, of course, and everything and everyone inside it will belong to our Hero Mak.”

Mak Chung walked off together with Geom Mugeuk.

The first carriage was Geom Mugeuk’s carriage.

Mak Chung glanced at Geom Mugeuk’s carriage without much thought, then was startled.

“Good heavens!”

At the unexpected image, Mak Chung let out a cry.

“Damn it! What is this!”

He was so shocked that a curse burst from his mouth.

The face of the Heavenly Demon Soul was painted on the carriage.

At the sight, drawn so vividly, Mak Chung was horrified.

“Isn’t it magnificent?”

Mak Chung asked cautiously, just in case.

“Do you even know what this painting is?”

“It’s the symbol of the Demon Cult, isn’t it? Since I go around with this painted on, wicked people don’t dare think of attacking.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, Mak Chung was dumbfounded. Is this man insane? Or is he just lacking in the head?

“I’m not the sort of person who says things like this.”

And he had even had such a good dream.

“If you go around carelessly painting something like this, you’ll be in serious trouble.”

At that, Geom Mugeuk instead spoke proudly.

“I heard the person who painted this was a truly famous artist. And the person who made this carriage was also an extremely renowned craftsman. So even judging the carriage itself, it is an incredible work of art. It’s just hard to find a buyer, but you probably couldn’t put a price on its value.”

Because Geom Mugeuk had gotten out earlier, the carriage door was open, and beside the carriage seat lay a large leather bag.

“If you choose this carriage, you will gain that leather bag as well. It’s a bag that has everything. It contains many truly precious things. If you choose this carriage, you’ll turn your life around!”

But Mak Chung’s steps were already heading toward the next carriage.

“You must be planning to have me impersonate the Demon Cult and get me killed. You’ve used your head quite a bit, but I’m not that much of a fool.”

“Ah, how unfortunate that you don’t understand my sincerity.”

Mak Chung flinched again at the next carriage.

It was a carriage painted entirely white. Even the curtains drawn over the windows were white.

So there was another madman here. To make a carriage this white.

“The owner of this carriage is someone who loves white very much.”

“Even if they love it, this is too much.”

“It is the carriage of someone with an unbelievably charming smiling gaze. You will not regret choosing it. For reference, almost no one has ever seen that person’s smiling eyes.”

Mak Chung asked with a curious expression.

“Why has no one seen it?”

Because everyone who saw it died.

“Well, as for that reason, open the carriage door and confirm it for yourself!”

Mak Chung stared at the snow-white carriage for a moment.

“Will smiling eyes put food on my table?”

Having thus shaken off the temptation of the god of death, Mak Chung moved on again. From inside the carriage, a faint sound of the Cheonhwa Pavilion Lord’s laughter leaked out.

At the next carriage, Mak Chung frowned.

There was another one. A madman. No, a mad carriage.

All kinds of patterns like graffiti. The dizzying, complicated swirls painted on the carriage made him feel lightheaded just by looking at them.

“What kind of lunatic drew pictures like these?”

The moment he said that, Mak Chung felt even dizzier. It truly seemed as though the whirlpools painted on the carriage were spinning and pulling him in.

“Even in this complicated painting, there is order, and life is contained within it. I used to utterly hate this painting too, but now I have grown fond of it.”

At those words, the whirlpool stopped, and a gentle energy flowed out from the carriage.

“If you ride in this carriage, our Hero Mak will be able to see things he could never see while alive. Somewhere like hell, for instance. When else would you get to look around such a place?”

Mak Chung looked at Geom Mugeuk. What nonsense had he been spouting since earlier? He must be trying to confuse me so I can’t choose the carriage with valuables in it. It won’t work, you bastard!

At last, it was the golden carriage’s turn.

After examining the carriage closely, Mak Chung shouted,

“This is it!”

It truly was a carriage plated in gold. It was not crudely plated, but properly gilded. Thinking that the carriage alone would be worth a considerable sum, Mak Chung’s face brightened on its own.

“I happen to like gold.”

“Ah, then you’ll get along well with the owner of this carriage. That person is absolutely mad about gold.”

Flutter.

At that moment, the curtain over the golden carriage’s window fluttered. A cold energy flowed out from within.

Swoooosh!

Press!

Geom Mugeuk secretly released his energy and pushed that energy back into the carriage.

“Then will you choose this carriage filled with desire and greed?”

Swoooooosh!

Preeeess!

“Still, let’s look at the last carriage.”

Mak Chung approached the final carriage. From the carriage, which was longer than the others, the scent of liquor wafted thickly.

“This is a liquor carriage!”

An impostor carriage, a white carriage, a graffiti carriage, a golden carriage, and even a liquor carriage! Truly, to think he would see such a variety of carriages all at once.

“That’s right! There is so much precious liquor that you won’t regret choosing it. It should be full of liquor you truly couldn’t obtain even with money.”

“No matter how expensive liquor is, it’s still just liquor.”

At that, a hand suddenly emerged through the curtains of the liquor carriage’s window.

In the hand was a wine cup. It meant for him to try a sip.

“Have a taste and decide.”

Of course, Mak Chung did not drink it.

“No need. How would I drink when I don’t know what might be in that liquor?”

“Our Hero Mak keeps making regrettable choices. This is a chance to drink a famed liquor of the world.”

Geom Mugeuk went over instead, accepted the wine cup, and drank it.

“Kyaa, the taste is incredible.”

Mak Chung, who liked liquor, felt a slight tug of temptation, but he was not the sort to fall for such a trick.

Just then, Mak Chung’s gaze turned to the warriors of the Ghost Shadow Unit standing a little distance away from the last carriage.

The assassin women of the Third Squad had deliberately fallen all the way to the back, so they were not visible to Mak Chung.

You must absolutely not stand out!

In order to follow Geom Mugeuk’s command, the warriors of the Ghost Shadow Unit and the escort warriors stood there with deliberately dispirited expressions, not revealing their presence. They lowered their heads on purpose, trying not to meet anyone’s eyes. They looked perfectly frightened.

Even so, as Mak Chung looked at them, he felt a strange sense of incongruity. What is it? What is this unpleasant feeling?

At that moment, Gongbae came over and said quietly to Mak Chung,

“The carriages you just saw are obviously carriages people ride in, no matter who looks at them. Would it not be better to choose two of those large carriages over there? If you choose one loaded with silk or treasure!”

But Mak Chung could not give up on the golden carriage.

When he hesitated, Gongbae said,

“You can choose two, so open that large carriage first. Then decide whether or not to choose this golden carriage.”

“That is a good idea.”

Mak Chung and Gongbae walked toward the large carriage.

“The most precious goods will be loaded in the carriage at the very back.”

“Why?”

“Would they not have placed the most precious goods closest to themselves?”

“A good idea!”

Following Gongbae’s advice, Mak Chung walked to the large carriage right in front of Geom Mugeuk’s carriage.

What could be inside? Please be a carriage full of gold and jewels, porcelain and paintings!

Mak Chung opened the carriage door.

Just as he looked inside the carriage with a face full of anticipation!

“!”

Inside the carriage, about twenty demon soldiers were seated in two rows on the left and right. Jang Ho, who had hurriedly climbed aboard, was also in that carriage.

They all turned their heads at once and looked at Mak Chung.

Thud!

The moment their eyes met, Mak Chung reflexively shut the door.

Even if a sword had truly flown toward his face, he would not have reacted this quickly.

Because he opened and shut it so quickly, Gongbae, who was standing behind him, did not see what was inside.

“What was in there?”

At Gongbae’s question, Mak Chung gulped. That was what he wanted to ask.

‘What were all those?’

There was no way a transport carriage like this would be packed with warriors, was there? And men that huge at that. He had never seen such large people before.

‘Ah! Were they perhaps statues of warriors?’

When Gongbae tried to open the door,

“Don’t open it!”

At Mak Chung’s shout, Gongbae flinched and stepped back.

Statues, his foot! It had only been a fleeting instant, but they had clearly looked at him. It had been truly terrifying. He felt he would never forget the eyes of those massive men for the rest of his life.

Even so, Mak Chung pretended he had not seen inside. If he said he had seen them, it felt as though that carriage door would open and they would come bursting out.

“I didn’t see inside, so we should say I haven’t chosen yet.”

Mak Chung began insisting to Geom Mugeuk.

“My mind changed at the last moment. If you are a true man, shouldn’t you acknowledge at least this much?”

Even though he was being so unreasonable, Geom Mugeuk was all smiles, as though he found it delightful.

“That’s right. Even I would have trouble deciding. Goodness, how could anyone choose this in one go? Now, choose again.”

“Thank you.”

Mak Chung did not dare think of choosing a large carriage again, and returned to the golden carriage. His legs trembled so badly he felt he might collapse.

‘Yes, let’s quickly choose one and leave.’

The reason he could not leave despite seeing the scene inside the large carriage was greed. Normally, he should have left at once, but he could not leave that golden carriage behind. Yes, let’s just think of them as statues. They were all statues!

‘The mistake was choosing something else over this in the first place.’

Mak Chung stood before the golden carriage and declared proudly,

“I will choose this!”

Then he carefully opened the door of the golden carriage.

Inside the carriage, Mabul was sitting with his eyes closed.

The moment he saw him, Mak Chung shouted,

“A golden Buddha statue!”

Mak Chung was truly overjoyed, as though he might fly. Today, he kept seeing illusions.

“See? Was my choice not correct?”

Gongbae looked behind Mak Chung with a startled face.

“Ch-Chief. The Buddha statue has opened its eyes!”

“What?”

Mak Chung turned around in shock, and saw Mabul looking at him with his eyes open.

As though a Blood Buddha had manifested, red energy flowed from his two eyes.

“Uwaaak!”

So startled that he stumbled backward, Mak Chung fell flat on his backside.

When Mabul flicked his hand, the carriage door closed. He sent Geom Mugeuk a look that seemed to say, Are you going to keep bothering me like this?

Geom Mugeuk opened the door again with the Void-Seizing technique.

“Now, you have been chosen!”

Mabul looked at Geom Mugeuk with an incredulous expression.

“Our Hero Mak should ride in the carriage.”

Feeling the faint golden killing intent flowing from Mabul’s body, Mak Chung waved his hands frantically.

“I will say I did not choose it.”

“That won’t do. Did we not promise that you would definitely take the carriage you chose? Since he is someone who likes gold, if you speak together, you will get along well.”

But Mak Chung’s survival instinct told him that if he got into a carriage with that man emitting golden light, something terrible would happen.

“If you keep forcing me, that rock will fall on your head.”

Geom Mugeuk returned Mak Chung’s threat in a low tone.

“Then I will catch it with one hand and hand it to our Hero Mak.”

In that instant, Mak Chung flinched. He imagined the scene. The man before him catching that great boulder with one hand and throwing it to him, saying, Please accept it. He imagined himself beneath that boulder, going splat and turning into a bloody pulp.

Geom Mugeuk smiled and came to his side.

“It was a joke, a joke. How could I catch such a huge boulder with one hand? With two hands, perhaps. Ah, you still have to choose one more carriage, don’t you?”

Geom Mugeuk grabbed Mak Chung and dragged him along.

“No, I’m fine. I don’t have to choose. Please, just let me go!”

“That won’t do. You said you would choose two, so you must choose.”

“I’ll pay the protection fee. Please let me go!”

But Geom Mugeuk dragged him along with a grip he could not resist.

“Hurry now.”

It was not the same guileless gaze from before. Now he knew for certain. Things had gone wrong, terribly wrong.

From the Drunken Demon’s carriage, a wine cup emerged once more between the curtains. It was a signal telling him to choose him. And that was exactly what made it so frightening to choose.

Mak Chung’s gaze turned toward the pure white carriage.

He had been about to choose it, but changed his mind at the last moment. He chose the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable’s carriage instead.

“I’ll choose that one.”

Its exterior was chaotic, but he hoped there might be something unexpectedly calm inside.

Please! And luckily, his premonition proved right.

Clack.

When the door opened, a woman with a strange air about her was seated inside the carriage.

The woman looked this way and smiled faintly. Mak Chung thought she was smiling at him, but of course, the one that smile was directed at was Geom Mugeuk, who stood beside him.

Geom Mugeuk said with a regretful expression,

“I’ll give you a chance to change!”

He’s letting me change? This is it! This carriage is my way to live!

“No.”

Mak Chung ran over and climbed into the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable’s carriage as he pleased.

“I’ve decided on this carriage! So don’t make me ride in that golden carriage!”

“No, that one is even scarier……”

At that moment, the door of the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable’s carriage closed.

Clack.

And immediately, the carriage door opened again. The Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable was still seated there, but Mak Chung had vanished without a trace.

“Chief!”

Gongbae shouted, but Mak Chung did not appear.

“Who the hell are you people? If you lay a hand on us, every Green Forest bandit in the Central Plains will rise up!”

Geom Mugeuk said with a smile,

“If they find out who we are, they’ll all sit back down.”

Gongbae looked up toward the top of the valley. If he gave the signal, the attack would begin. A rain of arrows would fall, and those boulders would all come rolling down at once.

But Gongbae did not dare give such an order. He felt that he must not.

Gongbae dropped flat to the ground where he stood.

“Please spare us!”

The Green Forest bandits who had been watching from far behind, gauging the mood, all prostrated themselves at once as well.

Clack.

The door of the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable’s carriage opened again.

Mak Chung, who had disappeared, was seated in his original place.

Sitting there with his soul drained from him, Mak Chung let out a scream and leapt out of the carriage.

“Aaaaaagh!”

Terrified, he rolled across the ground. Truly, as if he had seen hell itself and returned, Mak Chung was consumed by fear.

The Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable had not killed him. She had judged that the Young Cult Leader would not kill them. The Young Cult Leader was not the sort of person to toy with someone he intended to kill.

Now, the aura Geom Mugeuk gave off as he stood before the two men had changed.

“Hero Mak.”

“Yes! No, I’m no hero! Please spare me!”

Mak Chung, terrified, begged for his life.

Since he had finished playing with him, it seemed like he would strike him down with a palm and kill him, but Geom Mugeuk spoke in the same gentle tone as before.

“I’m busy, so let’s choose the carriage when I return. Be sure to wait here!”

Mak Chung instinctively understood. Those words meant that if he was still living as a Green Forest bandit when they returned, they would all die. The dream of the golden whale had been a dream meant to change his life. In a direction he had never imagined at all, but still.

“Now then, we set off again!”

The carriages and horses started moving once more.

The Ghost Shadow Squad warriors, who had been cowed earlier, revealed their own auras as they passed before the two men. Among those following on horseback, not a single one seemed easy to deal with.

And so the golden carriage gradually grew distant, like a golden whale swimming away.

Watching that sight, Gongbae voiced his belated resentment.

“That is why I told you we should choose the large carriage, didn’t I?”

Mak Chung let out a long sigh and answered,

“That’s because you didn’t see what was inside.”

Chapter 782: This Is Why You Shouldn’t Accept Gifts Carelessly
--------------------------------------------------------------
Samamyeong was commanding the situation from the Tongcheongak Command Room.

Whenever the Young Cult Lord departed from the cult, Tongcheongak would be placed on high alert.

But this time was even more special. Never before had he left leading four Demon Lords and even the Demon Army. Furthermore, it was the largest procession, with the Ghost Shadow Corps and guard warriors all deployed.

A military officer came and reported on the Young Cult Lord's movements.

"The Young Cult Lord has finally entered Hunan."

This time, the Young Cult Lord's destination was Dongting Lake, located in northern Hunan.

On the map of the Central Plains, Geommugeuk's flag was moved from Guizhou to Hunan.

Soma (Little Demon).

Beside the Young Cult Lord's flag representing Geommugeuk, the Demon Lord banners were placed together.

On the golden flag was written the character for Buddha (佛), and on the snow-white flag was written the character for Smile (笑). On the purple flag was the character for Soul (魂), and on the sky-blue flag was the character for Wine (酒).

The Demon Lord banners symbolizing the four Demon Lords were clustered around Geommugeuk's flag at the center, and beside them was placed the Demon Army Banner symbolizing the Demon Army. There was also the Ghost Shadow Corps flag, slightly smaller than the Demon Army Banner, inscribed with the character for Shadow (影).

Then another officer came and reported.

"The Martial Alliance has requested to be informed of the purpose of this departure from the cult."

The reason the Martial Alliance had reacted before the Four Paths Alliance was that Hubei, where the Martial Alliance was located, was not far from Hunan.

Originally, they should have been informed that the Heavenly Flower Tower Lord was going to meet the Holy Maiden Palace Lord.

"Do not respond."

"Yes."

Samamyeong recognized the importance of this matter.

It was natural that Geommugeuk might go out with four Demon Lords, as he was close to them. But taking even the Demon Army meant that a great battle could break out this time.

Since it was a decision made by none other than Geommugeuk, it had to be taken as a dangerous situation.

In such a situation, if even a single drop of information about this matter leaked to the Martial Alliance or the Four Paths Alliance, it could greatly hinder Geommugeuk's mission.

Their non-response would no doubt strain relations with the Martial Alliance, but it was an unavoidable choice.

Then another officer came and reported.

"Officer Gowol of Eunwol requests an audience."

"Bring him in."

A visit from Gowol at a time like this? He had a premonition that something was amiss.

Shortly after, Gowol entered, guided by a Tongcheongak officer.

Only an extreme minority within the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult could enter the Tongcheongak Command Room, but Samamyeong had specially permitted Gowol to come and go.

Naturally, when organizations of similar nature existed, they were bound to compete with one another.

However, Eunwol claimed the role of thoroughly supporting Tongcheongak. All merits gained through information were passed to Tongcheongak. It was as though Eunwol of the Young Cult Lord were supporting Tongcheongak of the Cult Lord.

Because of that, there had been no conflict whatsoever between the two organizations until now.

"I greet the Chief Strategist."

"Welcome, Officer Gowol."

The two exchanged pleasant greetings. It was a meeting between the Chief Strategist of the current Cult Lord and the person who would become the Chief Strategist of the next Cult Lord.

Gowol glanced around the interior for a moment and felt the busy fervor of the Tongcheongak officers.

"Come, let's go in and have a cup of tea."

Samamyeong led Gowol into his office. Samamyeong personally brewed tea and asked,

"How have you been lately? Busy?"

With the Young Cult Lord's departure from the cult, the mental burden must be indescribable.

"I decided not to worry about the Young Cult Lord. If I treated him like an ordinary person, I wouldn't be able to endure it."

Samamyeong smiled at Gowol's words. How could he not understand that sentiment? He himself had thought countless times how fortunate he was to serve Geomujin rather than Geommugeuk.

The two sat facing each other and drank tea. After savoring a sip, Gowol immediately got to the main point.

"You are a busy man, so I will speak directly."

Gowol's gaze turned to the map of the Central Plains visible through the office window. The countless flags planted upon it.

He had naturally expected Gowol's gaze to turn toward where Geommugeuk was.

But the place Gowol's gaze turned toward was the main stronghold of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

The largest and most splendid flag of the Cult Lord.

Ma (Demon).

Beside it, two Demon Lord banners.

On the green flag was written the character for Poison (毒), and on the black flag was written the character for Fist (拳) in white letters.

On the gray flag symbolizing the Wind Heaven Cult Lord was written the character for Wind (風), and beside it, one new flag.

Gunhak (Flock of Cranes).

"What do you think when you see those flags?"

Samamyeong could not understand what Gowol meant by asking this.

As if he had not asked expecting an answer, Gowol continued speaking.

"Looking at those flags, this is what I thought."

Unexpected words flowed from Gowol.

"The balance is well maintained."

Hearing those words, Samamyeong looked at the flags again and found them indeed reasonable.

In return for only two Demon Lords remaining within the cult, there were the Wind Heaven Cult Lord and Akgunhak. They were both individuals possessing strength at the Demon Lord level or above, and the two remaining Demon Lords were the strongest—the Fist Demon and the Poison King.

Gowol looked at Samamyeong and asked.

"Why would the Young Cult Lord have arranged this balance?"

"You mean the Young Cult Lord intended this?"

If so, there was one thing it meant.

Samamyeong started in surprise and asked with a face that said, 'It couldn't be.'

"Surely not? You think our headquarters could be attacked?"

Then an astonishing reply flowed from Gowol's mouth.

"I believe so."

Samamyeong's expression hardened.

"It is a conclusion drawn after seeing how the Young Cult Lord arranged the main stronghold. Originally, their plan might have been to draw out the Young Cult Lord and the Demon Lords, then strike the empty headquarters. I thought that the Young Cult Lord might have foreseen this and thus arranged this balance."

Samamyeong was silent for a moment.

He had certainly recognized that this matter was important.

But he had not considered even the slightest possibility that the headquarters of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult would be attacked.

Precisely because it seemed absurd, he thought that he might have let his guard down.

"I felt I had to convey this thought to the Chief Strategist."

Under normal circumstances, he would have ended it with a mere "Thank you for telling me."

But Samamyeong knew better than anyone how excellently Gowol had developed the organization called Eunwol. So he asked.

"What would you have done?"

Gowol replied calmly.

"If it were me, I would have lit the Demon Flame."

"!"

Samamyeong was startled when the Demon Flame was mentioned.

Lighting the Demon Flame symbolically meant issuing a total emergency order for the main stronghold.

Once the Demon Flame was lit, the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult would enter a state of war. All cult members would assume emergency readiness, and all mechanisms and formations would begin operating at maximum capacity.

The main stronghold of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult would truly transform into an impregnable fortress. It was an undertaking that required enormous manpower and vast wealth.

"Is there a reason you worry to this extent? It doesn't seem to be merely because of that balance."

Indeed it was. And the decisive reason lay with Geommugeuk.

"From the first time I met the Young Cult Lord, I saw him exercise caution and prepare to an excessive degree against great dangers approaching."

Samamyeong had felt the same. He had wondered many times: why would someone so outstanding strive so hard and seek to become stronger? Especially since he was not someone with desire for power or ostentation.

"I believe the Young Cult Lord senses dangers that we cannot feel."

"!"

"If I am to read my lord's heart and act as a military officer, I believe that time is now."

A brief silence flowed between the two. If Gowol had come this far to say this, it was surely advice that must not be taken lightly.

"To light the Demon Flame, we need the Cult Lord's permission. He will never permit it."

If all the Demon Lords were present, he might allow it.

But if most of the Demon Lords were absent and they asked to light the Demon Flame?

That prideful Cult Lord would never accept it.

Gowol had expected as much.

"Then you and I must persuade him, sir. Is that not our duty? I will help you."

For him, not a Tongcheongak officer, to step before the Cult Lord over such a matter meant staking his life. That was how seriously he took this matter.

Samamyeong shook his head.

"Save that courage to display before your own Cult Lord later."

Samamyeong rose from his seat.

"My Cult Lord is mine to persuade."

* * *

The Sword King was silently staring at the wall.

The writings of astonishing people inscribed on the wall.

It was the second floor of the Fengliu Tavern. When he said he was a friend of Geommugeuk, Jochunbae yielded him this floor. On the table, untouched liquor and side dishes were laid out.

Had he not known that this was Geommugeuk's regular tavern, he would have thought the writings were pranks or forgeries.

Then someone walked up. Surprisingly, it was Geomujin.

"I greet the Cult Lord."

The Sword King politely greeted Geomujin.

Geomujin lightly nodded in acknowledgment.

Having finished greeting, the Sword King put on the shoes he had been wearing at his waist.

"If it's more comfortable to keep them off, leave them off."

"It's fine."

The Sword King put on his shoes.

Geomujin slowly walked over and stood beside the Sword King. His gaze turned to the writings of the Martial Alliance Lord and the Four Paths Alliance Lord.

"You have truly accomplished an amazing thing."

"You know well it is not something I did?"

The Sword King smiled faintly and said again,

"You have a remarkable son."

The very fact that he was conversing with the Cult Lord like this was thanks to that remarkable son. That he had received a precious gift from the Cult Lord was also thanks to that son.

"The sword you bestowed upon me is truly excellent."

The Sword King now wore the White Flower Sword instead of an iron sword.

"I'm glad it suits you."

"May I ask why you bestowed such a precious sword upon me?"

Bestowing such a magnificent sword surely meant there was something desired in return.

When a conflict with his son arose, to ask him to persuade the son?

He thought that might be the case. After all, even this Cult Lord would not find it easy to face that remarkable son.

But the Sword King's expectation was completely off the mark.

"Stand by that child's side later."

"!"

The Sword King looked at Geomujin with a surprised expression.

"That child will surely have a hard time."

From those words, the Sword King understood.

That the Cult Lord sincerely cared for his son and was certain that he would one day come to stand against him.

'The Cult Lord truly intends to unify the martial arts world.'

The Sword King hesitated to answer. Whether he unified the martial world or not was none of his concern. The issue was Geommugeuk.

To answer that he would do so was to make a promise with the Cult Lord.

Would he truly remain by Geommugeuk's side until then? If he made the promise, he would have to stay.

Geomujin did not press him for an answer. Rather, he felt favorable toward his cautious demeanor.

How long had he agonized? The Sword King answered calmly.

"Yes, I shall."

It was a brief answer, but a look of satisfaction crossed Geomujin's face.

"Have a drink with me."

Clink.

The Sword King respectfully received the liquor and drank it down.

Geomujin likewise drank the liquor the Sword King poured for him. It was a drink of promise.

After sharing the drink, Geomujin wordlessly left that place.

Watching his retreating figure, the Sword King let out a light sigh and looked down at the sword and shoes at his waist.

"This is why one shouldn't accept gifts rashly."

Damn these father and son!

* * *

When Geomujin returned to his quarters, Samamyeong was waiting.

"What brings you here?"

Samamyeong said with a smile,

"I wished to enjoy a quiet conversation with the Cult Lord after a long time, so I came to visit."

"Very well, come in."

And so the two sat facing each other with a baduk board between them. It was the very board Geommugeuk had made and given as a gift.

Clack, clack.

A few remarks about how excellent the board and stones were were exchanged, and then only the sound of stones being placed could be heard. Samamyeong focused silently on the game, as if he had truly come just to play.

In the midst of their exchange, Samamyeong deeply invaded Geomujin's territory.

"That is an unusually bold move for you."

It was a move so deep an invasion that surviving would be difficult.

"Do you have the confidence to survive after entering so deep into my thick territory?"

"If this move has struck the Cult Lord's weak point, will not a way somehow present itself?"

Geomujin, quietly observing the board, said something unexpected.

"You are worried too."

"!"

Samamyeong raised his head and looked at Geomujin. The Cult Lord had guessed from that single move why he had come.

"I suppose I grow more worried with age."

Geomujin continued to look at the board.

"The lad came to see me before he left the cult. He said he was leaving with the Demon Lords, so I told him to take them all. But he made this and that excuse and left the Poison King and Fist Demon behind."

A smirk formed on Geomujin's lips. That smile fully revealed an emotion of 'How impudent of him to worry about his father?'

Geomujin picked up a baduk stone.

Samamyeong naturally expected him to respond to his move that had jumped into his territory.

"I will not respond to this move of yours and will pull back."

Though not said aloud, it was as if he meant he would not light the Demon Flame.

"Accepting this unreasonable move would wound your pride, would it not?"

He had naturally thought it was because of pride.

"It is not because of that."

Clack.

Geomujin added a move to the fight in the center.

"If I respond there and suffer a loss, would it not become disadvantageous for the fight on this side?"

"!"

Samamyeong understood the meaning contained in that single move.

If the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult's main stronghold became an impregnable fortress, the enemy would eventually concentrate all their strength on the Young Cult Lord.

Geomujin had revealed his intention to draw the enemies here for his son's sake. The Cult Lord was revealing his heart through the game of baduk.

Don't touch my son. Come to me.

Samamyeong, who had been silently gazing at Geomujin, looked down at the board again.

"Because I pulled back and made an overplay, the fight in the center has grown difficult."

Samamyeong felt his heart grow hot.

That's right. The Cult Lord is not one to avoid a fight.

What mattered was that the Cult Lord was not letting his guard down.

And he knew better than anyone just how frightening the Cult Lord was when he was not careless.

When Samamyeong added another move to save his previously placed stones, Geomujin ruthlessly cut between two stones and began the fight.

Clack!

"Our guests must be met by us."

Chapter 783: The Demonic Cult Is the Demonic Cult!
--------------------------------------------------
“Damn those orthodox sect bastards! They’re worse than garbage!”

The man’s eyes were filled with hatred and killing intent, while a bizarre energy emanated from his body. He looked as if he had practiced demonic martial arts and gone mad.

Standing before him was Jin Hagun. The martial artists of the Demon-Slaying Squad surrounded the area behind him.

Although he had formally become the Young Alliance Leader, he still led the Demon-Slaying Squad in carrying out chivalrous deeds. He had no intention of avoiding such dangerous work simply because he was the Young Alliance Leader. He planned to continue until the day before he took the position of Alliance Leader.

“Calm down. It’s not as if you have anywhere left to run.”

Jin Hagun tried to calm his opponent. The man before him had attacked an orthodox sect and carried out a massacre. Based on everything they had uncovered so far, he had no grudge against that sect whatsoever.

“You’ll all be destroyed soon.”

Then came an astonishing cry.

“The Divine Cult is Immortal! The Heavenly Demon is Invincible!”

At the same time, black energy welled up in both of the man’s eyes. Jin Hagun hurled himself backward and shouted,

“Everyone, get out of the way!”

Kuaaaang!

A powerful explosion swept through the surroundings. The man had used a self-destruction technique.

Rumble.

Amid the fragments and clouds of dust raining down in every direction, Jin Hagun gathered his subordinates.

“Is anyone hurt?”

“No!”

Fortunately, thanks to Jin Hagun’s swift warning, no one had been injured.

Over the past several days, massacres by demonic practitioners had occurred one after another throughout the Hubei region. They had attacked orthodox sects and carried out massacres, while also assaulting and killing ordinary merchants who knew nothing of martial arts.

When even women and children became victims, the orthodox practitioners’ fury toward the demonic practitioners reached its peak.

Jin Hagun naturally believed these incidents had been the work of the people behind the scenes.

‘They’re plotting something again.’

Jin Hagun was furious. He was enraged by those who killed people without hesitation for the sake of their own goals.

If men like the one just now committed slaughter to their heart’s content and then killed themselves, how could the wrongs of the dead ever be avenged?

Once he became Alliance Leader, he would devote his entire life to minimizing such unjust deaths. He would make those who committed evil deeds live in pain and regret for the rest of their lives before dying miserable deaths.

At that moment, a martial artist of the Demon-Slaying Squad in charge of dispatches came running over and reported,

“The Alliance Leader has ordered you to return immediately.”

Jin Hagun spoke to the martial artists of the Demon-Slaying Squad gathered around him.

“Let’s go back!”

He shot into the air, and the martial artists of the Demon-Slaying Squad all followed after him.

* * *

When Jin Hagun returned, the Martial Alliance was in a state of emergency.

The White Tiger Order.

The Martial Alliance had four levels of emergency alert.

Starting with the lowest, the Black Tortoise Order, followed by the Vermilion Bird Order, the White Tiger Order, and finally the Azure Dragon Order.

The White Tiger Order was the emergency alert immediately below the Azure Dragon Order, which was issued during wartime. In other words, it was an alert issued when war was imminent.

This was the first time such an order had been issued since Jin Hagun became Commander of the Demon-Slaying Squad.

Although several incidents had occurred in succession recently, he had not thought the situation warranted even the White Tiger Order.

‘Could another incident have occurred?’

Jin Hagun entered the Alliance Leader’s Hall with a tense heart.

Inside were Jin Paecheon, the Martial Alliance Leader, and Jegal Hyeon, the Chief Strategist.

“Commander of the Demon-Slaying Squad, returning upon receiving the order.”

Jin Hagun respectfully saluted Jin Paecheon, then exchanged greetings with Jegal Hyeon as well.

“What happened during your mission?”

At Jin Paecheon’s question, Jin Hagun answered with an apologetic expression.

“I failed to capture him alive. And just before he died by using a self-destruction technique, he claimed that he belonged to the Divine Cult. However, I do not believe this incident was ordered by the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.”

Jin Paecheon thought so as well. The Geom Ujin he knew was not someone who would commit such an act, at least not in this manner.

“That is not important. The problem is that the orthodox practitioners will think it was.”

Jin Hagun felt an endless sense of helplessness at times like this. Only a few people had stirred things up, yet public opinion among the orthodox sects was already wavering.

Jin Hagun cautiously asked,

“Then why did you issue the White Tiger Order?”

Jegal Hyeon answered in his stead.

“The Young Cult Leader has left the Divine Cult with four Demon Venerables and the Demon Army.”

Jin Hagun had been away dealing with the recent incidents and had not yet learned of this.

“After entering Hunan, the Young Cult Leader has continued advancing north.”

From the word “advancing,” Jin Hagun could infer how Jegal Hyeon viewed this situation.

Hunan was the territory of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, but if they continued north, they would enter Hubei, where the Martial Alliance’s main headquarters was located.

Moreover, there was a decisive reason they were taking this matter even more seriously.

“When we asked why they had left the Divine Cult, the Divine Cult gave us no response.”

Only then did Jin Hagun understand why the emergency had been declared and why the atmosphere in the Alliance Leader’s Hall was so heavy.

The Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult had left with four Demon Venerables and even the Demon Army, yet refused to reveal the reason?

It was more than enough to warrant the White Tiger Order.

This time, Jin Paecheon asked his grandson,

“Did you receive any separate message?”

“No, I did not.”

Jin Paecheon’s expression stiffened slightly. He had expected Geom Mugeuk to send his grandson a separate message.

Jin Hagun, on the other hand, did not mind at all that Geom Mugeuk had not contacted him separately.

“I think I understand now why the recent incidents occurred. They are trying to prevent us from helping the Young Cult Leader. If public opinion toward the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult turns sour, the Alliance will be unable to assist them.”

Jin Hagun was anxious. The fact that they were trying to sever the connection between him and Geom Mugeuk meant that something might have happened to Geom Mugeuk.

Of course, Jin Paecheon and Jegal Hyeon had also considered that possibility.

But they could not simply assume it to be true. Their opponent was the Demonic Cult, after all.

Had all the goodwill they had shown until now been for the sake of this one moment?

They always had to keep open the possibility that such a question might arise.

Especially in a situation where Jin Hagun showed such unwavering faith in Geom Mugeuk.

“If he did not send me a message, I believe that in itself is a message.”

“What do you mean?”

“If he did not trust me completely, he would have sent word. He would have explained every little detail. The fact that he did not contact me means he thought I would understand without being told. The Young Cult Leader truly trusts me as a friend.”

And he had now passed beyond the stage of simply believing without question.

“I will not be disappointed that the Young Cult Leader did not send a message. Rather, this time I will send him one first.”

“What will you say?”

Jin Hagun spoke with an expression utterly free of doubt.

“I understand your intentions well, my friend.”

At Jin Hagun’s response, Jin Paecheon said nothing. He had been worried from the beginning about his grandson trusting Geom Mugeuk too much. That concern continued even now.

“I know very well what you are worried about, Grandfather. You may worry about me, and you may even declare an emergency. However, do not limit the target of that emergency to the Young Cult Leader. You must also consider the opponent who forced him to leave in such haste with so many troops.”

Jin Paecheon was still worried, but at the same time, he felt envious.

In all the long years of his life, had he ever trusted someone to that extent?

“So? Do you intend to go help the Young Cult Leader?”

Jin Paecheon expected his grandson to ask for permission.

But Jin Hagun’s answer was unexpected.

“No. I will not go.”

“Why?”

Jin Hagun recalled what Geom Mugeuk had said on the day they met to celebrate his inauguration as Young Alliance Leader.

―If anything strange happens, contact me immediately. Understood? Never try to handle it alone!

That was what Geom Mugeuk had told him.

“If he needed my help, he would have sent word and asked me to help.”

If he had, Jin Hagun would have rushed to him. Even if he had to stake his position as Young Alliance Leader, even if he had to stake his life, he would have helped him.

If that had been all he said, Jin Paecheon would still have worried about his grandson.

However, Jin Hagun had another reason for not going to help Geom Mugeuk. It was something just as important in his life as helping Geom Mugeuk.

“And right now, I have something more important to deal with. The people behind this may carry out another massacre to drive a wedge between us and the Divine Cult.”

Innocent people might become victims of their schemes.

“Stopping that is more important. Even if that is exactly what they intend, saving people must come first.”

Jin Paecheon nodded in satisfaction.

“Raise the emergency level to the Azure Dragon Order. That way, we can prevent them from carrying out another massacre with greater certainty. And inform the Divine Cult that we issued the Azure Dragon Order for these reasons. There is no need for us to engage in a battle of nerves by refusing to contact them simply because they did not contact us.”

Jin Hagun added with a resolute gaze,

“We must act according to our principles. I believe that is the way of the orthodox sects you taught me, Grandfather.”

The worry disappeared from Jin Paecheon’s face. He had thought his grandson was so infatuated with Geom Mugeuk that he could no longer distinguish right from wrong.

But if his grandson felt this way, there was nothing to worry about.

Jin Paecheon gave Jegal Hyeon an order.

“Raise the emergency level to the Azure Dragon Order and inform the Divine Cult.”

“Yes, understood.”

Then Jin Paecheon spoke to his grandson.

“Go and do what you must.”

“Yes, Alliance Leader.”

Jin Hagun respectfully saluted before leaving the room.

His vigorous footsteps carried his faith in Geom Mugeuk.

‘I trust you, so I’ll take care of my own work, my friend.’

* * *

The mounting tension between the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult and the Martial Alliance reached the Unorthodox Alliance as well.

“Alliance Leader Jin has no choice but to agonize over this. He is not afraid of the Young Cult Leader. He must be afraid of the Demonic Cult Leader.”

The Unorthodox Alliance Leader, Baek Jagang, also asked Bisa-in for his opinion.

“What do you think?”

Bisa-in answered confidently.

“I naturally believe this is the work of the people behind the scenes.”

“What makes you so certain?”

Bisa-in brought up something that had happened in the past.

“Do you remember the day we received the report about the treasure map?”

At Bisa-in’s words, Unorthodox Alliance Leader Baek Jagang nodded and recalled that day.

His subordinate’s report had been truly difficult to believe.

―They even summoned the souls of the dead. I saw it with my own eyes.

Yet even as he heard that unbelievable report, he had not felt a chill creep over his ears. That was when he had realized just how formidable—and how insane—those people were.

“Our current enemies are people capable of doing such things. They are beings that ordinary people, bound by ordinary destinies, can never stop. That is why I think this.”

Something rather unexpected flowed from his lips.

“I believe the instinct of survival within the martial world gave birth to the Young Cult Leader.”

“You mean the Young Cult Leader is an existence the martial world created in order to survive its destruction?”

“Yes.”

Baek Jagang laughed with an incredulous expression. He laughed because he felt no chill creep over his ears. His disciple truly believed such a thing.

“That does not mean I deify him. If anything, I pity him.”

“You pity him?”

“He has done everything so well, as if he were perfect. So if he makes even the slightest mistake, everyone will be disappointed and point fingers at him.”

The reason he could speak so comfortably with his master was all thanks to Geom Mugeuk. It was because he had struggled and struggled to look his master straight in the eye and see him clearly.

Before, that had been so difficult and burdensome. But after finally managing it, he sometimes even felt that speaking with his master was the most comfortable thing in the world.

Baek Jagang asked his disciple with an interested expression,

“What will you do if the Young Cult Leader makes a mistake?”

A faint smile appeared on Bisa-in’s frightening face.

“I’ll point fingers at him from the very front. I have some teasing to repay.”

The chill in Baek Jagang’s ears told him that, no matter how serious a mistake Geom Mugeuk made, his disciple intended to protect him.

And he made no attempt to hide those feelings.

“I want to go and help the Young Cult Leader.”

Bisa-in wanted to rush to Geom Mugeuk’s side and help him. Setting aside every other meaning, he wanted to do so simply as a friend.

Baek Jagang explained why he could not grant that request.

“As you know, the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult of this era possesses the greatest power in its history. Geom Ujin will never let that power go to waste. If that power is diminished while they fight their enemies this time, then we should welcome it.”

Baek Jagang hoped for the Demonic Cult’s power to decline, and he did not hide those feelings from his disciple.

“If not, then we will have to face an enemy several times more frightening than the people behind the current conflict.”

Baek Jagang looked toward the entrance of the Alliance Leader’s Hall and pictured a certain scene.

“The Demonic Cult Leader I know will definitely open that door and try to enter this place. Will you be able to face that moment?”

Bisa-in could not answer.

Because it was something he could not bear.

“Even after the years pass, can you be certain that the Young Cult Leader you trust so much will not open that door and enter?”

Bisa-in remained silent.

He had thought Baek Jagang would tell him never to help Geom Mugeuk.

Instead, Baek Jagang readily gave his permission.

“Well, what happens later can be dealt with later. For now, you should go help your friend. Go and return safely.”

Bisa-in looked delighted at the unexpected permission.

But then—

“I will not go.”

He had changed his mind in the meantime. Baek Jagang quietly stared at his disciple and asked,

“Why?”

“Even without my help, that friend will survive.”

“Because he is the one born from the martial world’s instinct for survival?”

“No. Because he took his guards and the martial artists of the Ghost Shadow Squad with him this time.”

Bisa-in did not know everything about Geom Mugeuk, but he knew one thing for certain.

“If he had not been confident that he could protect them, he would not have brought them along. That is the kind of person he is.”

“Then why did you say earlier that you would go help him?”
