After hesitating briefly, Bisa-in answered honestly.

“It was less for him than for myself. I wanted to boast that I had come to save you in such a situation. I have received too much from him until now, and I wanted to repay even a little of it.”

Bisa-in’s gaze shifted toward the entrance of the Alliance Leader’s Hall, the same place Baek Jagang had been looking at earlier.

“After hearing what you said, I realized that this is not the time to make such a show of it. This is the time to reinforce that door.”

Fighting spirit poured from Bisa-in. He would save every moment to train harder and invest himself in the Unorthodox Alliance.

“I will never allow the Demonic Cult Leader to sit in your position, Alliance Leader.”

In response to Bisa-in’s determination, Baek Jagang conveyed words he had wanted to tell him for a long time.

“Do not forget, Sain. Just as we cannot hide the unorthodox blood flowing through our veins, in the end…”

A sharp glint emerged from Baek Jagang’s small eyes.

“The Demonic Cult is the Demonic Cult!”

Chapter 784: I Already Have Countless Stars
-------------------------------------------
Even at that late hour, Eunwol’s operations room was bustling.

Because an emergency had been declared, the strategists were alert to even the smallest reports that flew in.

They were carefully watching not only the movements of the Martial Alliance and the Sado Alliance, but also those of every sect in the Central Plains. In particular, they grasped every movement along Geom Mugeuk’s route.

And at last, the report they had been waiting for arrived.

“The Young Cult Leader has safely reached the appointed location.”

The flags of the Young Cult Leader and the Demon Venerables were placed at Dongting Lake.

Socheon.

Tongcheongak had written “Soma” on Geom Mugeuk’s flag, but in Eunwol, it was Socheon. It was a name Gowol had chosen. To him, Geom Mugeuk was no different from the heavens.

‘He has finally arrived.’

The Young Cult Leader had left the cult countless times before, but never had Gowol been as tense as he was this time. An endless unease, as if something great were about to happen, kept pricking at him. That was why he had gone to see Sama Myeong.

At that moment, an unexpected person came to visit.

“The Chief Strategist has arrived.”

“Show him in at once.”

A moment later, Sama Myeong entered.

“Welcome, Strategist.”

He truly had not expected him to come here.

“I meant to come sooner, but I was delayed.”

Sama Myeong’s hands were full of food for the strategists. It was truly unexpected, and it did not suit him at all. That was why Eunwol’s strategists were even more moved.

The Chief Strategist of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult had personally brought food for them.

“Thank you, Strategist.”

When Gowol thanked him on behalf of the strategists, Sama Myeong smiled.

“Show me around a bit.”

“There probably won’t be much worth seeing. This place was made by thoroughly modeling it after Tongcheongak. The Young Cult Leader moved the most perfect intelligence operations room in the murim here.”

At Gowol’s praise, Sama Myeong gave a pleased smile. It was not mere flattery. The interior really did resemble Tongcheongak, as if it had been moved here.

As he looked around inside, Sama Myeong’s gaze turned toward the flags on the map of the Central Plains. The flags that had reached Dongting Lake. In Tongcheongak, those flags would have been moved to the same location as well.

After showing him around, Gowol tried to lead Sama Myeong to his office.

“Please come in.”

“As I was coming, I found the night air outside quite pleasant. Let’s go out and talk.”

“Yes, of course.”

The two of them left the Eunwol building.

They walked through the inner grounds of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, feeling the cool night air. After walking for a long while, it was Sama Myeong who spoke first.

“The demon flame will not be lit.”

As if he had expected it, Gowol was not surprised.

“I thought it would not be an easy matter.”

“It is not because of the Cult Leader’s pride.”

Sama Myeong explained to him why Geom Ujin did not wish to light the demon flame. After hearing the whole story, Gowol asked,

“Did you come to tell me this?”

“I did.”

Gowol looked at Sama Myeong with puzzled eyes. It was something he could have sent someone to tell him, or even something he would have learned without being told.

Sama Myeong revealed the reason.

“Even if no one else knows, you, at least, must know exactly how the Cult Leader thinks of the Young Cult Leader. I believe this is the most important thing for a Chief Strategist. To know exactly what kind of person the leader you serve is. A clever mind? Cold judgment? Those are all secondary.”

Gowol bowed his head respectfully.

“Thank you truly for this precious teaching.”

And he was grateful, too, that Sama Myeong had called him Chief Strategist. It meant he acknowledged him as the next Chief Strategist. For Gowol personally, it was an invaluable moment.

“Don’t stay shut up in the operations room all the time. Come out for air like this, too. Come out and look up at the sky.”

Hearing him say to look at the sky, Gowol thought of Geom Mugeuk. Young Cult Leader, are you looking at that night sky as well?

It was then, as the two of them looked up at the stars in the night sky.

Among the clustered stars, there was one that shone especially bright.

Seeing that star, Sama Myeong was startled and let out a short cry.

“Ah!”

At his surprise, Gowol asked,

“What is it?”

Disbelief gradually deepened on Sama Myeong’s face. Wondering if he was hallucinating from exhaustion, he closed his eyes and opened them again.

But that starlight remained unchanged.

In a trembling voice, Sama Myeong conveyed a shocking fact.

“The Heavenly Slaughter Star has appeared.”

“!”

Gowol was startled as well.

The Heavenly Slaughter Star.

It was a great baleful star said to shine when a great slaughterer appeared in the murim. It was not merely ominous. It was said that when the Heavenly Slaughter Star appeared, the murim would become a sea of blood.

“That star is the Heavenly Slaughter Star?”

Gowol’s voice trembled.

Because even Sama Myeong could hardly believe what he was seeing, he could not answer right away.

But it was certain. He possessed profound knowledge of astronomy as well, and there was no way he would fail to recognize the Heavenly Slaughter Star, of all things.

“It is. It is definitely the Heavenly Slaughter Star.”

“This is my first time actually seeing the Heavenly Slaughter Star.”

Surprisingly, it was Sama Myeong’s first time seeing it in person as well.

“The same goes for me.”

It was a star he had only learned of through books and hearsay.

Sama Myeong suddenly recalled the conversation he had had with Gowol last time. Could the danger the Young Cult Leader had sensed possibly be this?

“Was this what the Young Cult Leader was worried about?”

Gowol felt as though the haze that had been clouding everything until now was lifting.

“I believe so.”

If that was the case? Just how had the Young Cult Leader known that the Heavenly Slaughter Star would appear? This was not simply a matter of being well versed in astronomy. The Young Cult Leader had read the workings of Heaven.

“I will inform the Cult Leader, so you must relay this to the Young Cult Leader immediately.”

“Yes, understood.”

Suddenly, Sama Myeong realized that Gowol had somehow regained his composure.

“Are you not worried?”

“Rather, I feel reassured.”

The reason was none other than Geom Mugeuk.

“It means the Young Cult Leader predicted it accurately, does it not?”

Only someone with absolute faith in Geom Mugeuk could say such a thing. Gowol continued,

“If it is the Young Cult Leader, I suddenly feel that he may even be able to stop the one born under the Heavenly Slaughter Star.”

Sama Myeong was a man who believed there was no such thing as an optimistic strategist. A strategist was someone who always had to think of the worst outcome first.

But at least in this moment, he wanted to believe in Gowol’s optimism.

Even so, that was merely hope.

What had protected the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult until now had been his own preparations, made by thinking of the worst first. Even if the demon flame would not be lit.

“We must empty Maga Village at once.”

* * *

“We’ve arrived at Dongting Lake.”

In the distance, a lake as vast as the sea stretched out. Because it was night, the lights of the boats floating on the lake were beautiful.

“She told me to find the boat with a white flag here.”

The place where the Cheonhwa Pavilion Mistress was to meet the Divine Maiden Palace Master was one of the boats floating on Dongting Lake.

Geom Mugeuk called the Cheonhwa Pavilion Mistress, the Demon Venerables, Ian and Jang Ho, and the Ghost Shadow Unit leaders to his side.

“Tonight’s meeting will fall into one of two cases. Either the real Divine Maiden Palace Master called the Pavilion Mistress because she had something to convey in secret, or she and the Dark Palace Master have prepared a trap together. If the Divine Maiden Palace Master independently asked for this meeting, then there is no problem. We can simply enjoy a boat ride on Dongting Lake and return.”

But the Cheonhwa Pavilion Mistress was worried about one thing.

“The Divine Maiden Palace Master told me to come alone. She said she would not meet me if anyone else came with me.”

At her concern, Geom Mugeuk smiled and said,

“To me, that sounds like she’s desperately asking you to bring everyone.”

There was a reason Geom Mugeuk thought so.

“Even if the Divine Maiden Palace Master acted on her own to tell you something, I believe the Dark Palace Master knows about it.”

He had confirmed in the underground tomb that the Dark Palace Master was someone who used people’s minds. He had used the Sword King’s heart to call forth the souls of the Twelve Zodiac Kings.

If he had joined hands with the Divine Maiden Palace Master from the beginning, then he must know what kind of person she was. There was no way he would miss her deviation.

“Through our many battles thus far, the Dark Palace Master knows me well. That means he also knows about my relationship with Lord Soma.”

How could the Dark Palace Master not know? Those fierce battles fought together with the Extreme Evil Soma. Perhaps the Demon Venerable he hated most was the Extreme Evil Soma.

“For that reason, both the Dark Palace Master and the Divine Maiden Palace Master must know that I would never send only the Cheonhwa Pavilion Mistress to this meeting alone. In other words, whether this is a trap or not, the other side will not even think that the Pavilion Mistress will come alone.”

Those listening nodded.

“Even if we concede a hundred times over and say the Divine Maiden Palace Master truly asked to meet independently, and the Dark Palace Master does not know of this, there is still no need for you to go alone. The one who wants something is the Divine Maiden Palace Master, who contacted you first. Even if you go with someone, she will not be able to refuse the meeting.”

As always, Geom Mugeuk saw through the other side, and his conclusion was clear.

The Cheonhwa Pavilion Mistress asked Geom Mugeuk,

“Then will only the Young Cult Leader and I go to that place? Or?”

“No. One of the Demon Venerables will go with us.”

They had naturally assumed he would take the Extreme Evil Soma.

“Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable, you will go with me.”

The person Geom Mugeuk asked to accompany him was the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable. It was not merely an emotional decision.

“The other side uses dark demonic arts. The people most resistant to those dark demonic arts are me and the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable.”

Of course, the Demon Buddha was also strong against dark demonic arts, but he had something to do outside.

The Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable was pleased. He was not simply being brought along; he was being entrusted with an important duty.

“Yes, I will go with you.”

Geom Mugeuk’s instructions continued.

“Commander Jang.”

“Yes, Young Cult Leader.”

“You and the Demon Army will wait near the lakeshore. Move under the assumption that the enemy already knows the Demon Army has arrived.”

Jang Ho’s eyes gleamed sharply.

“Yes, understood.”

They were more reliable than anyone, so Geom Mugeuk did not worry about Jang Ho and the Demon Army.

Next, Geom Mugeuk gave orders to Ian and the four unit leaders.

“The Ghost Shadow Unit and the guards will disguise yourselves as men and women in pairs, or groups of three to five, as people who have come out for a night stroll. You are to search for traces of the enemy’s ambush. If this place is a trap, there will certainly be traces of them somewhere. If you find anything, inform the Demon Army at once and move together.”

“Yes, understood.”

Then he assigned roles to the remaining three Demon Venerables as well.

“Demon Buddha, Drunken Demon, how about a boat ride after so long?”

It was a request for them to take a boat out and guard the area near the boat where today’s meeting would take place.

The Drunken Demon was excited at the thought of drinking while floating on a boat on the lake.

“I knew this would happen, so I brought wine perfect for a boat ride.”

Lastly, Geom Mugeuk entrusted a role to the Extreme Evil Soma.

“Lord Soma, please command this operation.”

It was the role of operational commander, overseeing the entire situation from outside and responding to variables—the most important role.

“Understood.”

The Extreme Evil Soma understood this order to mean that Geom Mugeuk would protect the Cheonhwa Pavilion Mistress, so he should take good care of the Ghost Shadow Unit and the Demon Army.

Geom Mugeuk spoke especially to the Ghost Shadow Unit and the guards.

“Listen well. In my demonic path, there is no individual sacrifice for the whole. Prioritize your own safety above all else. It does not matter if you let the enemy escape, and it is fine if you show cowardice. What I cannot forgive is you getting hurt or dying. And above all, do not worry about me.”

It was Geom Mugeuk’s honest feeling for his subordinates.

At that, the Drunken Demon slipped in a comment.

“Can we do that too?”

Looking at the Demon Venerables, Geom Mugeuk said,

“Of course not. Our Demon Venerables, please prioritize the mission over your own safety. You must never let the enemy escape. I will tease you about it for ages. And above all, please worry about me! The Young Cult Leader comes before your safety!”

Only the two eyes within the Extreme Evil Soma’s eye sockets laughed brightly, while the other three Demon Venerables turned their heads as if they had not heard.

Looking around at everyone, Geom Mugeuk said with vigor,

“Now then, let’s finish this matter safely and enjoy a boat ride!”

Just as they were about to begin the operation in earnest.

The Cheonhwa Pavilion Mistress, who had been looking up at the sky and praying that today’s operation would end safely, cried out in surprise.

“That star!”

So shocked was she that her eyes widened. Because it was the first time they had seen her so startled, everyone looked up at the night sky in tension.

“The Heavenly Slaughter Star has appeared!”

At the words Heavenly Slaughter Star, everyone was startled. They all knew what it meant for the Heavenly Slaughter Star to appear. It meant the arrival of a great slaughterer who would dye the gangho in blood.

The problem was that the person signified by the Heavenly Slaughter Star was not the Dark Palace Master. He had already been living through this era all along.

The emergence of a new villain.

The Demon Venerables exchanged grave looks, and the Ghost Shadow Unit leaders also traded glances with Ian. Thanks to Geom Mugeuk, the atmosphere had been exceedingly relaxed even just before the operation, but in an instant it turned cold.

Everyone’s gaze naturally turned toward Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk silently looked at the Heavenly Slaughter Star. Only Geom Mugeuk knew whom that Heavenly Slaughter Star signified.

So you’ve finally appeared.

He had hoped that he would appear as late as possible.

Because he had not yet achieved Great Completion of the twelve stars of the Nine-Fire Demonic Art, nor had he learned the final ninth technique.

But Geom Mugeuk was not afraid.

Because he had run with all his might until this moment, without regret. This was why it was good to live with every ounce of one’s strength. Even with such an overwhelming enemy before him, he did not tremble.

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze, which had been directed at the Heavenly Slaughter Star, turned toward those looking at him.

“Do not worry. Stars are not found only in the sky. We have stars on this side as well. There is a smiling star, a drunken star, a shining star, and a strange star. There is a beautiful star, and a reliable star. That star is alone, but we are many.”

At those words, the Drunken Demon smiled with drunken eyes and said,

“And among them is one that shines the most brilliantly.”

Just then, the Extreme Evil Soma, who had been listening, suddenly spoke.

“The Young Cult Leader is not a star.”

Everyone looked at him with expressions asking what he meant.

“He is the very sky itself, which allows those stars to shine.”

To the Extreme Evil Soma, if not for Geom Mugeuk, they could not have become stars.

Even the Heavenly Slaughter Star could not stop the drunken Drunken Demon’s mischief.

“Not the sky, but darkness, surely. Darkness. After all, it is darkness that makes the stars shine. That black darkness that always arrives late.”

Whether sky or darkness, no one denied the words that Geom Mugeuk had made them all shine. They had all changed greatly because of Geom Mugeuk, and these days, they were shining like stars.

Geom Mugeuk did not miss this chance to show off.

“As expected! Only our Lord Soma recognizes my true worth. Now, in that spirit, I, as the sky, will say a word to that Heavenly Slaughter Star.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward the Heavenly Slaughter Star again.

“I already have countless stars…”

Geom Mugeuk’s clear, deep eyes shone more intensely than the Heavenly Slaughter Star.

“So you may lose your light in my world.”

Chapter 785: For My Son
-----------------------
Seo Daeryong led all the inspectors and enforcers of the Yellow Springs Pavilion into Magachon.

When more than a hundred of them entered Magachon, everyone in the marketplace stared at them in astonishment.

They had naturally assumed it was a procession setting out for somewhere.

The enforcers entered each house and shouted loudly.

“Now, everyone must evacuate your homes. Take only what you need and come out!”

The enforcers went from house to house throughout Magachon, making everyone come outside.

They began evacuating not only the shops in the marketplace, but also the residences outside it. Magachon was larger than it appeared, and quite a number of people lived there.

The Yellow Springs Pavilion martial artists, who had prepared an evacuation plan in advance, scattered into their assigned districts and brought the people out.

Seo Daeryong personally took charge of the Windflow Tavern.

Jo Chunbae, as well as everyone dining there, was astonished.

“You will have to stay in lodgings prepared by the Main Sect for the time being.”

“Has something happened?”

Seo Daeryong calmly answered Jo Chunbae’s anxious question.

“I’m sorry, but I cannot tell you the details. Please hurry and gather your belongings.”

The guests each ran to their own homes. The people of Magachon were at least less alarmed because the ones carrying out the evacuation were martial artists from the Yellow Springs Pavilion.

Ever since Geom Mugeuk established a branch of the Yellow Springs Pavilion in Magachon, its people had received tremendous help from them. Thanks to the Yellow Springs Pavilion, martial artists who ignored the residents or caused trouble had disappeared completely.

Even amid his surprise and confusion, Jo Chunbae made sure to ask about one person. He feared that something had happened to the Demonic Cult.

“The Young Cult Master is safe, isn’t he?”

Seo Daeryong answered with a smile.

“Of course he is safe.”

Despite his answer, Seo Daeryong was inwardly worried about Geom Mugeuk.

The order had come down in great haste, and he had not been told the exact circumstances. But they were likely evacuating the residents of Magachon because the Main Altar might come under attack.

If the Main Altar was in danger, then the outside would be no safer.

‘Young Cult Master! Please be careful.’

At first, Seo Daeryong had regretted being unable to follow Geom Mugeuk.

But now he considered it fortunate that he had not gone. There were people he absolutely had to protect here: his son and wife, and his sisters-in-law. Even if he had to give up his life, he would protect his family.

Jo Chunbae came out after gathering the few things he needed. Before leaving the entrance, he turned back.

He gazed anxiously at the wall on this floor. He prayed that when he returned, that wall would still be standing safely.

When Jo Chunbae came outside, those who had emerged earlier were murmuring among themselves. It was the first time they had ever been evacuated, so everyone was tense and frightened. They whispered that a war must have broken out.

There was one thing Jo Chunbae considered fortunate. His wife, Yang-in, happened to be staying at the home of one of their married children, far away.

To put it bluntly, he had lived long enough and had no regrets about dying at any time. But the people who always weighed on his heart were his wife, who had suffered all her life, and his children.

“Those who came from outside will have to come with us as well. And if any of Magachon’s residents have gone elsewhere, tell the martial artists of this pavilion where they went. We will send word separately and make sure they do not return for the time being.”

Though they were moving nearly a thousand people, the process proceeded smoothly.

No one argued with or made trouble for the enforcers. Anyone unfamiliar with the situation might naturally assume it was because they feared the Demonic Cult members, but that was not the reason.

Without the Yellow Springs Pavilion martial artists who had protected them all this time, or their deep bond with the Young Cult Master, they might have believed the Demonic Cult had brought them away to harm them.

But now, they trusted them. They knew this was being done to protect them.

“Now, everyone, let us go to the Main Altar.”

The Yellow Springs Pavilion inspectors went from house to house, checking whether anyone remained behind.

Then they all followed the enforcers into the Heavenly Demon Divine Church.

The residents of Magachon were so astonished by the grandeur of the Heavenly Demon Divine Church that their mouths fell open. Most of them had never entered this far inside before.

After walking for quite some time behind the martial artists, they found several hundred lodgings made from temporary tents.

“Bear with the uncomfortable sleeping arrangements.”

Inside the lodgings, water and food sufficient to sustain them for the time being had been provided, along with the necessities of daily life.

“If you need anything, go to the blue tent over there and tell them. They will take care of it.”

Two people were watching from afar as the residents entered the Church. They were Geom U-jin and Sama Myeong.

After parting with Goh Wol, Sama Myeong had gone straight to the Cult Master and reported that the Heaven-Slaying Star had appeared.

Geom U-jin’s reaction was exactly what Sama Myeong had expected.

―Is that so?

Sama Myeong knew that the Cult Master was not a man who believed in astrology or heavenly omens. He believed only in human will and the power of martial arts.

Yes, if such a great baleful star had appeared, the Cult Master would rather welcome it.

―For the Heaven-Slaying Star to appear at a time like this. What a curious coincidence.

At those words, Geom U-jin silently nodded. He had only heard the day before that the Demon Fire should be lit, and now the Heaven-Slaying Star had appeared.

―That is why I would like to evacuate Magachon. If that person comes to the Main Altar, he will certainly pass through Magachon. If he truly bears the Heaven-Slaying Star, he might massacre them all.

Sama Myeong had been cautious. The Cult Master might have asked why they were making such a commotion over something so trivial. It might even make him appear frightened.

Besides, the appearance of the Heaven-Slaying Star in the heavens did not mean that the great baleful star would appear immediately. No one knew whether it would come tomorrow, in a month, or next year.

Even so, Geom U-jin readily gave his permission.

―Very well.

That easy approval had already been unexpected, but the Cult Master went a step further.

―Bring them all into the Church and protect them.

When Sama Myeong stared at him in surprise, the Cult Master said:

―If we make the people of Magachon leave for somewhere else, won’t word spread that we are making preparations? Handle it quietly.

Sama Myeong understood. This, too, was meant to ensure that the person coming here would not possibly go to the Young Cult Master.

―Yes. I will see to it.

It was through this process that they had been able to enter the Church grounds.

Then the sound of people murmuring reached them.

“Whoa!”

“Look over there!”

A group of Demonic Cult members was approaching them from the direction they were watching.

They were Demonic Cult members who revealed a far more intense aura than the enforcers or the martial artists they had seen while entering the Church.

When they saw the man at the head of the group, the nearby enforcers bowed respectfully. Seeing this, the residents of Magachon lowered their heads as well.

A man terrifying even to look at stood at the forefront.

He was none other than Fist Demon, a man with enormous fists and muscles that looked ready to burst at any moment. Even without his frightening face, Fist Demon overwhelmed everyone there through his mere presence.

Behind Fist Demon came the Iron Fists of the Eastern Fist Sect. They were the Black Fists, the strongest among the Iron Fists. The person commanding the Black Fists was none other than Cheon Sohee, the next Fist Demon.

Even a single Black Fist was a master whom ordinary martial artists could not hope to contend with. With all of them appearing at once, their oppressive presence was enough to make it difficult to breathe.

Their gazes and bearing were as sharp as if they were heading into battle.

The Black Fists formed ranks on the training ground, while Fist Demon continued walking until he stood before Geom U-jin.

“I greet the Cult Master.”

“You have come.”

Geom U-jin congratulated Fist Demon on entering a new realm.

“Congratulations.”

Fist Demon respectfully clasped his hands before his chest with his massive fists and lowered his head.

“Thank you.”

That was all. There was no need to add anything else. Fist Demon knew that Geom U-jin’s congratulations were sincere. The two were men whose hearts understood one another without words.

The residents of Magachon watched in silence. Only now did they realize that the person standing in the distance was the Cult Master.

As Sama Myeong watched Geom U-jin and Fist Demon standing side by side, he recalled what the two had looked like in their youth. Their younger forms seemed to overlap with the figures before him now.

Then another group of Demonic Cult members appeared.

This time, the residents of Magachon were overwhelmed by a wholly different feeling.

When they had seen Fist Demon and the Iron Fists, their mouths had fallen open in admiration. But this time, they clamped their mouths shut in fear and merely swallowed.

Once again, everyone’s eyes focused on the person at the front.

He was a beautiful youth with an utterly boyish face, but he was none other than Poison King, the most powerful deterrent to war in the martial world.

The twelve poison pouches hanging from his waist were not decorated with the cute animals of the Chinese zodiac that he usually wore. If those cute designs had inspired fear precisely because of the contrast, these drawings were simply frightening.

Each of the twelve pouches bore a picture of a terrifying zodiac animal. The red-eyed rat was grotesque even to look at, while the tiger seemed ready to leap from its pouch and tear a person to pieces.

These poison pouches were what Poison King wore when he went to war. Thus, those who knew what they signified could tell from the pouches alone what sort of mood Poison King was in as he set out.

Behind Poison King came Sangseon, wearing nine poison pouches, followed by the Poison Fangs of the Heavenly Poison Grove.

Each of them wore a different number of poison pouches around their waists, and only Poison Fangs wearing at least five had come out.

The Poison Fangs formed ranks just as the Iron Fists had, and Poison King walked toward Geom U-jin. When facing the Cult Master, Poison King did not wear his usual distracted expression.

“I greet the Cult Master.”

“Welcome.”

Because of his youthful appearance, standing beside Geom U-jin and Fist Demon made him look like their son or nephew. But his presence was no less formidable than Fist Demon’s.

The tension already seemed ready to explode with just those two present, when yet another person appeared.

The person who emerged with an overwhelming aura was none other than the Windcheon Cult Master.

This was not the Windcheon Cult Master who usually spouted frivolous remarks. At this moment, he was the Windcheon Cult Master, the absolute ruler of the lands beyond the frontier.

After walking before Geom U-jin and offering a respectful greeting, he asked:

“I would like to repay you for all the meals I have eaten from you. Will you give me the opportunity?”

At the Windcheon Cult Master’s words, Geom U-jin nodded and answered:

“I would be grateful if you did.”

Sama Myeong found it unexpected. Given the Geom U-jin he knew, he would have politely declined, saying that this was the Church’s affair and that he should rest comfortably.

Yet, for whatever reason, he readily accepted the man’s offer to help.

Then another person appeared.

A man who yielded to none of the three masters who had appeared before him in either aura or presence.

The man who emerged with his hair tied tightly back and a single sword at his waist was none other than Sword King.

He walked out without a word, wearing shoes.

Whenever he met the Cult Master, he had removed his shoes. But this time, he had come wearing them, as though his footwear itself expressed his resolve to fight alongside the Cult Master.

When Sword King respectfully clasped his hands and paid his respects, Geom U-jin silently nodded once.

Having promised the Cult Master that he would help when Geom Mugeuk struggled in the future, he would have to remain in the Heavenly Demon Divine Church with Geom Mugeuk until then. From now on, anything that happened within the Heavenly Demon Divine Church was his own affair.

With the four masters gathered, Geom U-jin gave Sama Myeong an order.

“Light the Demon Fire.”

“!”

Sama Myeong was startled. He understood summoning the two Venerable Masters and accepting the help of the Windcheon Cult Master and Sword King.

But light the Demon Fire? The Cult Master had said he would not light it because if the Main Altar became impregnable, those people would go to the Young Cult Master.

At that moment, Sama Myeong understood.

‘Ah! The Cult Master intends to lead these four masters out of the Main Altar and stop the enemy at Magachon!’

He had intended to do this from the moment he gave permission to evacuate Magachon.

It did not matter even if the enemy learned of this. No, this was something they wanted the enemy to know.

The Heavenly Demon had left the Main Altar and was in Magachon with only a small number of people.

He was telling the enemy to come there.

Sama Myeong believed that Geom U-jin’s judgment was not wrong.

If the enemy was powerful enough to invade the Main Altar, and if he truly possessed the Heaven-Slaying Star, sending his subordinates to fight would only result in needless sacrifices. The enemy would use the battle to kill his subordinates.

If Geom U-jin had been the sort of man who sacrificed all his subordinates to wear down the enemy’s strength, it would have been better to stop them within the Church. But the Cult Master was not such a man.

The stronger the enemy, the more readily he placed himself at the front. That was the kind of man the Cult Master was.

Because he knew the Cult Master better than anyone, Sama Myeong calmly followed his will.

“Yes. Leave everything behind to me.”

The gazes of Geom U-jin and Sama Myeong met in the air.

‘Please be careful, Cult Master.’

Geom U-jin gave him a single nod before moving on.

Geom U-jin walked ahead, with Fist Demon and Poison King following behind him on either side. The Windcheon Cult Master and Sword King followed after them.

As they walked away, the Demonic Cult members and the residents of Magachon all bowed their heads in unison.

Sama Myeong gave an order to the military adviser attending him, who was waiting a short distance away.

“Light the Demon Fire.”

The military adviser ran off somewhere, and not long afterward—

Boom! Boom! Boom!

Heavy drumbeats began to resound throughout the Heavenly Demon Divine Church. Flames rose from the braziers held high by statues of evil spirits.

At the same time, formations and mechanisms prepared throughout the Church began to activate.

Formations would also be deployed around the tents where the residents of Magachon were staying to protect them.

Boom! Boom-boom! Boom!

Thus, five people walked out of the Heavenly Demon Divine Church as it began transforming into an impregnable fortress.

Each of them possessed enough power to throw the Central Plains into an uproar by leaving alone.

Under ordinary circumstances, Geom U-jin would have gone out alone to face the enemy. Or perhaps, remembering the old days, he might have gone out with Fist Demon alone.

Yet he had brought everyone with him because of Geom Mugeuk.

“My son has been afraid of someone all this time.”

Geom U-jin had also used this opportunity to confirm it. The appearance of the Heaven-Slaying Star had shown him why his son had worked so hard and why he had struggled so desperately to become stronger.

It seemed his son had known that this being would appear.

The four men standing behind him had also formed exceptionally deep bonds with Geom Mugeuk. That was why they understood better than anyone what Geom U-jin meant.

“If it is someone he feared that much, we must not take him lightly.”

It was consideration for his son, as well as faith in him.

If this was the enemy you feared so greatly, then I will face him with caution as well.

Geom U-jin looked back at the four men behind him. They were men who would never fall behind anyone when it came to battle. Men who would bristle if told they were not second, but third, in this gathering.

“This battle is neither for the sake of the Church nor for the sake of the martial world.”

Geom U-jin calmly added in an even voice:

“I intend to erase that man for the sake of my son.”

Chapter 786: No Matter How Busy, Spare a Word
---------------------------------------------
Boom! Boom! Boooom!

Heavy drumbeats rang out through the Cheonma Divine Cult, and demonfire rose.

Seo Daeryong came and informed the people of Maga Village.

“From now on, a formation will be deployed outside the tents.”

At the warriors’ cries, all the residents of Maga Village grew tense. It was the first time in their lives they had ever seen a formation activate before their very eyes.

Sssssss.

As everyone stared in a daze, mist began to rise around the tents.

Whooooosh!

Pillars made of mist shot up here and there, and on the ground, mist flowed in paths like a spiderweb.

Something rippled within the mist, almost as if it were alive. A sound rang out—whether voices or the wind, no one could tell—and light burst forth from place to place.

“Ahhh!”

It was a mysterious and profound spectacle, one that naturally drew exclamations of wonder. This was also Seo Daeryong’s first time seeing a formation unfold.

Sssssssss.

Once the formation had been fully deployed, only an ordinary, faint mist drifted through the surroundings.

“The Illusory Mist Maze Formation has been deployed. Even if there are intruders outside, they won’t be able to reach this place.”

At Seo Daeryong’s words, the people of Maga Village felt two emotions at once. They were moved that the cult would go so far as to deploy a formation to protect them, and they were frightened by the fact that the situation required a formation in the first place.

The Illusory Mist Maze Formation was not the only one deployed.

Formations of different natures were unfurled throughout the Cheonma Divine Cult. If the Illusory Mist Maze Formation was optimized for protecting those within it, the other formations were made purely for slaughter.

And that was not all.

Rumble, rumble.

Clank, clank.

The ground opened, and new devices began rising up all across the main headquarters of the Cheonma Divine Cult.

Some were stone lanterns, some were stone chairs made for passersby to sit on, some were railings, and others were animal-shaped statues.

They looked utterly ordinary, but each and every one of them was a mechanism possessing lethal killing power.

A place from which all of this could be watched.

On the upraised palm of a demon statue stood a massive brazier, and demonfire was rising from it.

Beside it stood one man.

The person gazing with a stiff expression at Geom Ujin and the four experts heading out into the distance was none other than Geom Muyang.

He trusted his father more than anyone else in the world, but for some reason his heart was uneasy. It did not feel real that a situation serious enough to light the demonfire had unfolded.

And he felt powerless for being unable to go out with his father. At times like this, he should have been even more steadfast in doing what he had to do, but it did not go as his heart wished.

At a time like this, that little brother of his should have been here.

If it were him, he would have made some foolish remark about who was worrying about whom. Or perhaps he would have struck right at the heart of the matter like this.

Hyung, are you depressed right now because you’re worried about Father? Or are you upset that he didn’t ask you to go with him? Or are you angry because you don’t have the skill to go with him?

That fellow would undoubtedly have seen straight through his true feelings and said something like that. It was the kind of thing that would make him bristle, yet he wanted to hear those words from his younger brother. Even if it was like that, he wanted to shake off these pathetic thoughts he should not be having.

Just then, someone’s voice came from behind him.

“So this is where you were.”

When he turned around, someone completely unexpected was standing there. The person who had somehow stepped onto the demon statue’s palm without a sound was Hwi.

Since he had never imagined Hwi would come looking for him, Geom Muyang was inwardly surprised.

“Did Father send you?”

Had he told him to bring him along? He felt a flicker of hope, but—

“No.”

“Then why?”

Hwi was not someone who usually came looking for him. More than anything, Hwi had left his father’s side.

To the puzzled Geom Muyang, Hwi said something unexpected.

“I thought the person whose feelings were most similar to mine right now would be you, Eldest Young Master.”

Before leaving the cult, Geom Ujin had given Hwi a separate order.

—You stay in the cult. It’s been a while, so I think I’ll fight while reminiscing about the old days.

By “the old days,” he meant the time when he had wandered the Central Plains with the Fist Demon. In other words, it meant that Hwi and the other guards were not to follow.

—Cult Leader!

—Hwi!

At that single word calling his name, Hwi could say nothing more. Within that one word lay Geom Ujin’s powerful will.

This battle was one in which the Fist Demon and the Poison King, the Cult Leader of the Wind Heaven Cult, and Ak Gunhak were going together.

If even Hwi, his guard, had to step forward? Then it would already be a lost battle.

Because he knew that, Geom Ujin intended to leave him behind. With the feeling that if, by some chance, something happened to him, Hwi should look after Geom Muyang, who remained in the cult.

After pondering for a moment, Hwi answered respectfully.

—I will be waiting in the cult.

Had it been any other situation, he would have been stubborn. Then again, if it had been any other battle, the Cult Leader would not have acted this way either.

Hwi said to Geom Muyang,

“The Cult Leader left me behind and departed.”

That itself was not surprising, but it was surprising that Hwi had obediently followed that order. He was someone who would never accept such an order, even if his father told him to stay.

“Is this not the first time you’ve been separated from Father?”

“Yes, it is the first time.”

At Hwi’s calmer-than-expected demeanor, Geom Muyang asked,

“Are you not worried?”

“I am worried.”

How could he not be?

“To the point that I could say I have never been more worried in my entire life.”

“Then why did you not follow him? If it were you, Warrior Hwi…”

He was someone who would have disobeyed the order and followed secretly if necessary.

“…I am not sure myself.”

If he had to put it into words, it was probably because of this.

“I felt that, before the battle had even begun, nothing should happen that went against the Cult Leader’s will.”

At Hwi’s honest answer, Geom Muyang nodded. He understood what Hwi must have felt. That was how heavily this battle weighed on him. Geom Muyang himself felt the same way now.

“To be honest, at this moment I even resent my younger brother. If I had been the heir instead of him, I would have learned the Guhwa Demonic Art, and I might have been able to go out with Father.”

Hwi firmly cut off that regret.

“That would not have been possible.”

“What?”

“Even if you had learned the Guhwa Demonic Art, the Cult Leader would have left you behind.”

“If I had learned it half-heartedly, perhaps. But if I had achieved great completion in the Guhwa Demonic Art, he would have taken me with him.”

But Hwi shook his head.

“Even if you had achieved not merely great completion, but the great completion of the twelfth stage, he would not have taken you.”

Geom Muyang looked at Hwi with an expression of incomprehension. If that were true, then he should not have taken the other four either.

With calm eyes, Hwi revealed why he thought so.

“There is no parent in this world who takes their child along when going to fight a powerful enemy whose outcome cannot be guaranteed.”

“!”

Their gazes entwined in midair.

“I have never raised a child myself, but I likely understand the Cult Leader’s heart better than anyone. The reason he left you behind has nothing to do with your martial prowess, so please do not feel disappointed. Even if the Young Cult Leader who left the cult were here, it would not have been any different.”

These words from Hwi were truly surprising. It was surprising enough that he had spoken to him about a private matter, but Geom Muyang had truly never expected him to offer such comfort.

Had he always been this kind of person? Probably not. His younger brother had changed even this lonely man, who had spent his whole life in concealment. Originally, he would have been a man who said only that much and stopped.

“Until the Cult Leader returns, I and the guards of the Cheonma Hall will attend you, Eldest Young Master.”

The Cult Leader had not given an order to protect his son, but there are things that can be understood without being spoken.

“Warrior Hwi, thank you.”

He was not thanking him because he had said he would protect him from now on. It was because the comfort he had offered just now had become a great source of strength.

“Not at all.”

The moment Hwi bowed respectfully, he vanished from sight. His presence could not be felt anywhere nearby. He was certainly somewhere around them, but…

A short while after Hwi disappeared, someone spoke from far below the demon statue.

“My neck hurts. When are you coming down?”

The person looking up from below was Gwak Yeong of the Iron Workshop.

“I can’t climb up there.”

Geom Muyang lightly leaped down and landed gently before her.

“I came because I thought you might be feeling troubled, but someone came to look after you first.”

When she had become able to forge swords at the Iron Workshop, they had shared a celebratory drink together.

Drunk and excited, she had shouted at the top of her lungs that they should be friends from then on, but thinking back on it now, it had been a drunken outburst that risked her life. Fortunately, Geom Muyang had let it pass without incident.

“Are you not busy right now?”

When demonfire rose, the Iron Workshop also went into emergency mode. It was the place that supplied not only weapons for every demon of the Cheonma Divine Cult, but also the weapons used in the mechanisms.

“I’m actually more free than usual.”

Since they still could not trust someone who had come from the Murim Alliance, in an emergency like this, they instead assigned her nothing at all.

“And no matter how busy I am, I should live with enough leeway to say at least one word to the person I’m grateful to for changing my life. In that sense, may I say one presumptuous thing?”

“Go ahead.”

Gwak Yeong looked into Geom Muyang’s eyes and said,

“The Cult Leader will be safe, so please don’t worry. I thought you, Eldest Young Master, would be worrying the most. Would the enemy even dare to come? They probably turned around on the way and went somewhere else.”

Geom Muyang also quietly looked at her, then suddenly asked,

“Make sure you keep your promise.”

“What promise? Ah! I’ll definitely buy drinks on payday.”

“That goes without saying.”

“What? Did I make another promise?”

Gwak Yeong’s eyes went round.

“Did you not say you would personally make me a treasured sword as magnificent as the Heavenly Demon Sword?”

“I did?”

Geom Muyang nodded. It seemed she had said all sorts of things while drunk.

“I’ll keep it. I will!”

Geom Muyang gave her a faint smile, then began striding away.

Once he had grown distant, Gwak Yeong said softly,

“Stay strong, my friend. I’ll stay strong too, and someday I’ll truly keep that promise.”

* * *

Countless boats floated on Dongting Lake.

The small skiff carrying Geom Mugeuk, the Mistress of Cheonhwa Pavilion, and the Soul-Subduing Demon Venerable was also slowly moving forward, cutting through the moonlight reflected on the water.

From somewhere came the sound of a tungso flute, and women’s laughter could be heard as well. Everyone was absorbed in enjoying a late-night boating excursion.

The one seated in the middle, rowing, was Geom Mugeuk. The two women sat on either side of him.

The three of them were scanning the surroundings to see if there was any boat with a white flag hanging from it.

Geom Mugeuk looked off in the opposite direction.

There was the boat carrying the Drunken Demon and the Demon Buddha. Though it was far away in the darkness, Geom Mugeuk could see it clearly.

The Demon Buddha had weakened the light coming from his body as much as possible. On top of that, several lanterns had been hung around him to conceal the fact that his body was glowing.

The Drunken Demon was drinking, and the Demon Buddha was tossing tiny lotuses into the lake.

They were Blood Lotuses used by the Demon Buddha.

The Blood Lotuses connected to the Demon Buddha drifted throughout Dongting Lake, detecting any internal energy or unusual aura beneath the water. Not only could they detect hidden watermen, but if anything unknown was concealed there, the Blood Lotuses would sense it.

Meanwhile, the Drunken Demon continued drinking. Watching from afar, Geom Mugeuk could tell. The Drunken Demon was gathering liquor energy in preparation for battle. The Drunken Demon when he was not drinking was frightening, but the Drunken Demon when dead drunk was truly terrifying.

This time, Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward the lakeshore. Because they had come far out, the people there looked as small as dots. Moreover, it was dark, so no matter how great an expert one was, their forms could not be made out—but Geom Mugeuk saw them.

Ian was walking together with Jihan, the Fourth Squad Leader, pretending to be lovers as they checked the surroundings for anything suspicious.

On the opposite side, Chai Ran was walking with several members of the Third Squad. Men nearby gathered around at the sight of the beautiful women. They chose instead to openly draw attention.

In the meantime, the other squad members were thoroughly searching the area around the lake. If they found even the slightest suspicious thing, they would immediately report it to the demon troops hiding in the forest behind them.

In that way, everyone was fulfilling their role from their respective positions.

It was then that the Mistress of Cheonhwa Pavilion cried out.

“Look over there.”

When Geom Mugeuk turned his head, a large ship appeared and began approaching them.

It was a massive vessel that could carry hundreds of people, and Geom Mugeuk recognized at a glance what kind of ship it was.

“It’s the Wealth and Honor Ship.”

It was a ship that both the Mistress of Cheonhwa Pavilion and the Soul-Subduing Demon Venerable had heard of.

“They say that if you board that ship, you can disembark as a rich person. These days, though, it has become the Wealth and Honor Ship because only the wealthy can board it. It is a gambling ship operated by the Water Dragon Gang.”

The Water Dragon Gang was a faction well known even in Hunan, and it ruled the area near Dongting Lake.

The Water Dragon Gang was involved in the interests around Dongting Lake in various ways, and among them, the most profitable was none other than the Wealth and Honor Ship.

They invited wealthy people from all over the Central Plains onto the Wealth and Honor Ship, drowning them in every kind of pleasure and gambling to make money.

It was then that the Soul-Subduing Demon Venerable discovered something.

“Look up there!”

Amazingly, a white flag was hanging from the very top of the Wealth and Honor Ship’s mast.

Geom Mugeuk was surprised, as were the two women.

“They told us to meet on the Wealth and Honor Ship?”

He had expected a white flag to be hanging from some gloomy boat that looked as if a ghost might appear from it.

It was truly a meeting place that overturned all expectations.

Compared to the Mistress of Cheonhwa Pavilion, who grew tense at the thought that they might finally meet the Palace Master of the Divine Maiden Palace, Geom Mugeuk was relaxed.

“Isn’t the reason I came properly dressed this time so I could swagger?”

Geom Mugeuk said as he rowed powerfully toward the Wealth and Honor Ship.

“There’s no better place to swagger than a gambling den.”

Chapter 787: As If I'd Come Alone
---------------------------------
As the small boat carrying Geom Mugeuk approached the Ship of Wealth and Honor, a martial artist appeared on the deck.

“Do you have an invitation?”

Geom Mugeuk took something from his robe.

“Here, the invitation.”

Whoosh.

The paper Geom Mugeuk flicked flew toward the martial artist standing on the ship. When the man caught it and checked, it was a draft worth one hundred nyang.

By the time he finished confirming the draft, Geom Mugeuk and the two women were already standing before him. Just from the lightness skill with which they had flown up soundlessly in an instant, he could tell what level their abilities were.

“Guide us to the shipmaster.”

With such skill, they could have easily subdued him and threatened his life to make him guide them, yet they were giving him money and asking for guidance?

“I must inform the shipmaster in advance.”

“That is fine.”

“Please follow me.”

The three entered the ship under the martial artist’s guidance.

Seophon Majon and the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion, who followed behind, found it unexpected. They had not thought Geom Mugeuk would try to meet the person in charge of operating this ship as soon as he boarded.

When they opened the first door and entered, a wave of heat washed over them.

The large hall-like space had been set up as a gambling den, and over a hundred people there were absorbed in gambling.

It was not something to look down on just because it was a gambling den built on a ship. On the contrary, its facilities were far better than most gambling dens on land.

Women carried trays of wine cups back and forth, and martial artists stood here and there, watching to see if anyone caused trouble.

Sighs of regret, shouts of joy, curses of anger came from every direction.

Even without seeing them directly, one could tell what situation each person was in and what they were feeling.

Geom Mugeuk’s party walked across the place, but no one paid them any mind. Their gazes were fixed solely on their own tiles, their opponent’s expression, and the rolling dice.

Though no one had asked, the martial artist displayed the power of one hundred nyang.

“There are seven more gambling dens like this on this ship. This is the lowest-grade one, and those with five hundred nyang may enter.”

Five hundred nyang was easy to say, but for ordinary people, it was an unimaginably large sum.

After walking down a narrow corridor, the martial artist knocked on an iron door. When he knocked according to the set signal, the thick iron door opened.

That place, too, was a gambling den.

The martial artist’s explanation continued. If the gambling den they had just passed through was a place one could enter with five hundred nyang, then this one required two thousand nyang to enter.

“If you win money here and make five thousand nyang, you can go up to the next room. Or you can come with five thousand nyang from the start.”

It meant one could continue ascending in that way. Naturally, the higher the grade of the gambling den, the larger the stakes became. It was a structure that exploited the desires of gamblers who wished to make great sums in great games.

I’ve gone that far!

Thus, it was making full use of the human desire to go all the way to the end.

The Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion once again felt puzzled. This was none other than the Divine Maiden.

‘Why did she insist on meeting in a place like this?’

No matter how much she thought about it, she could not understand.

She glanced at Geom Mugeuk. She believed that Geom Mugeuk surely had something in mind, and that made her feel somewhat at ease.

They passed through several rooms like that. The room they were passing through now required thirty thousand nyang for admission.

One might think that if there were many wealthy people, the atmosphere would be relaxed, but it was not at all. It was not as rowdy as the first room, but as the sums rose, the heat grew even more intense.

It was filled with a tension so thick it was suffocating. Regardless of the amount, gamblers were still gamblers.

In that way, the three entered the shipmaster’s office under the martial artist’s guidance.

The shipmaster responsible for the Ship of Wealth and Honor was Yang Dong.

A man with a sturdy build, he was one of the top three experts in the Water Dragon Gang and received the gang leader’s trust and favor all to himself.

Yang Dong examined Geom Mugeuk, the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion, and Seophon Majon. At a single glance, he could tell they were not ordinary.

“Where have you come from?”

“What importance does origin have when coming to a gambling den? What matters is how much money one has.”

“To us, origin matters more. There are many who try to recover the money they lost through other means. If you do not reveal a definite identity, you will have to leave the ship.”

As if he had come intending to reveal his identity already, Geom Mugeuk answered without hesitation.

“We came from the Divine Cult.”

In that instant, Yang Dong was startled out of his wits. From that unfeigned reaction, one thing was certain. This man had nothing to do with the Dark Palace.

“How am I to believe that?”

Flash!

Before he knew it, Geom Mugeuk’s sword was aimed at Yang Dong’s throat. A faint demonic energy flowed from the tip of the blade.

Yang Dong had not even seen the opponent draw his sword. And he was supposed to be one of the top three experts in the Water Dragon Gang!

‘An unparalleled master!’

He was an expert beyond comparison with himself.

Click.

Since he had not seen the sword being drawn, he could not see it being withdrawn either.

Yang Dong cupped his fist respectfully and greeted him.

“What brings such honored guests to this place?”

“We did not come to ruin your business, so do not be nervous. Rather, you could say we came to catch those who are trying to ruin your business.”

Yang Dong was not only skilled in martial arts; he was quick-witted as well. That was why he had been entrusted with the most profitable operation, the Ship of Wealth and Honor. At the very least, he clearly realized that the expert from the Demon Cult before him had not come to harm him.

“Just say the word.”

“Guide us to the VIP room.”

For an instant, Yang Dong flinched.

“You wonder how I knew there was a VIP room? Have you already forgotten who we are?”

A faint smile formed at the corners of Seophon Majon’s mouth. Before boarding the Ship of Wealth and Honor, when Geom Mugeuk said he would show off, she had naturally thought he meant he would display his skill among the gamblers.

But that was not it. The opponent Geom Mugeuk intended to show off to was the owner of this ship. Geom Mugeuk’s actions continued to exceed her expectations.

If it had been her, she surely would have infiltrated quietly, started gambling in the lowest room, and observed the atmosphere from there.

Perhaps she would still be in the first room, finding out from another gambler that there was a second room.

But Geom Mugeuk was already trying to go to the room where only the wealthiest of the wealthy gathered.

Thinking about it, the Young Cult Leader’s choice was right. If they had asked to meet in a place like this, their intention was surely not to meet in some deserted corner. If that were the case, they would have met on some quiet boat.

If it was not a quiet meeting, then they had to go to the most central location. Even if the person was not there, it made sense to search downward from that point.

In this way, Seophon Majon was learning how Geom Mugeuk handled matters.

“Guide us there.”

“Why do you wish to go there?”

Even if he had to give up his own life, he could not give up the great magnates in that place.

That was why Yang Dong did not back down easily.

“Even if it is you, I cannot take you there…”

Geom Mugeuk cut him off and revealed his identity.

“I am the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.”

Yang Dong’s eyes widened. A demonic practitioner would never dare impersonate the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

He had known the man was a frightening opponent, but to think he was the Young Cult Leader of the Demon Cult!

“I hate gambling. I hate people who gamble, and I hate those who set up gambling dens like this even more. A murderer at least kills only one person, but you people drive that person’s family and acquaintances into misery as well.”

This was Geom Mugeuk’s usual view on gambling.

“So stop making me angry. I will quietly handle my business and leave.”

If the opponent was the Young Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, there was no room for choice.

“Please handle it quietly.”

If you cause trouble, we will not stand by either.

He could not bring himself to add those words. Because even so, he would have to endure it.

Yang Dong personally led Geom Mugeuk and the two women to the VIP room.

After winding through the complicated cabins and passing through even a secret door, the VIP room appeared.

“Now then, new guests have arrived.”

At that, the gazes of those inside turned toward Geom Mugeuk’s party.

There was a single large round table there, and seven people were seated around it.

There were old men, middle-aged men, and young men. All of them wore expensive clothes, and anyone could tell they were wealthy.

Among them was one woman.

The instant she saw the woman, the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion knew. The other party was the Divine Maiden Palace Master. Though she had left the palace at a young age and was seeing the Divine Maiden Palace Master for the first time, she recognized her at a glance.

The Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion quietly informed Geom Mugeuk and Seophon Majon.

“That is her.”

Indeed, she was Gaye, the Divine Maiden Palace Master.

The very woman who had read the stars before the Dark Palace Master was sitting in the midst of this gambling table.

Gaye looked at the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion and said,

“You came? You arrived faster than I expected.”

Her tone was unexpectedly calm and bright.

“Have you ever tried this? It’s more fun than you’d think. Come over and play a round.”

The Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion did not know how to take her proposal. She had never imagined she would be sitting at a gambling table.

Instead of the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion, Geom Mugeuk strode forward and drew his sword.

“Anyone unrelated to this woman is to leave at once. If you remain, I will consider you on her side.”

Cold demonic energy burst from the sword.

“Even if you’re mad with gambling, you can grasp the mood this much, can’t you?”

The demonic energy Geom Mugeuk released was not something ordinary people could withstand.

Those seated at the round table rose, trembling, and Yang Dong gathered them up and took them outside.

Yang Dong thought this was better, if anything. If these precious people stayed there and were swept into a fight and killed, and if rumors spread because of it, the Ship of Wealth and Honor would be finished that very day.

‘I just need to keep them alive.’

The VIPs who came outside with Yang Dong finally began to vent their anger.

“Is this how you manage your guests?”

“I apologize. There are circumstances, so please understand just for today.”

“There’s another room, isn’t there?”

Even in this atmosphere, rather than thinking of going home, they wanted to continue gambling.

Yang Dong smiled and said,

“Of course. Now, this way.”

Yang Dong took them and left. More important than what was happening inside that room right now was keeping these wealthy men maddened by gambling.

Now the only person left in the room was Gaye. As though she truly had come alone, no one else remained.

Rather, Geom Mugeuk thought that was not good. Being alone meant she trusted in something unseen.

The Divine Maiden Palace Master’s gaze turned toward Geom Mugeuk.

“You are more handsome than I expected.”

By contrast, Geom Mugeuk’s appraisal was harsh.

“You are more shameless than I expected.”

“Shameless?”

“I heard you told our Pavilion Master to come alone.”

“I did.”

“Think of all the things you people have done until now. And yet you told her to come alone? If we invited you to come to our cult alone, would you really have come alone?”

Gaye did not refute him. Instead, she smiled.

The Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion walked over slowly and sat facing the Divine Maiden Palace Master. The two women gazed at each other. They were both divine maidens who received prophecies, yet they had lived utterly different lives.

At last, the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion asked calmly,

“Why did you ask to see me?”

“You are as impatient as the Young Cult Leader.”

“After suffering several storms because of you people, I became this way.”

“‘You people’ is a bit strange. Shouldn’t the Pavilion Master and I be tied together by the word ‘we’?”

“I left when I was young because I hated that.”

The two women exchanged words politely, but an invisible momentum clashed tautly between them.

“You know that the Heavenly Killing Star has appeared, don’t you?”

When the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion nodded, Gaye said something astonishing.

“I received a new prophecy.”

A shocking prophecy flowed from the Divine Maiden Palace Master’s lips.

“This world will be ruled by the person born under the Heavenly Killing Star. That is the newly descended prophecy.”

A heavy silence fell.

The one most shocked was, of course, the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion. Since she had lived with the belief that prophecy was never wrong, her heart pounded violently.

Because that prophecy ultimately meant the Young Cult Leader and the Evil Soma would die.

“Why are you telling me that?”

“It is a prophecy of this palace. Even if no one else knows, shouldn’t you?”

At that moment, Geom Mugeuk slowly walked over and sat beside the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion, saying,

“That prophecy is a lie.”

The Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion looked at Geom Mugeuk in surprise.

“A divine maiden cannot lie about a prophecy.”

“So it seems they’ve given up on the Divine Maiden.”

Geom Mugeuk spoke firmly.

“If a prophecy had descended under the present circumstances, it would have descended upon the Tower Lord.”

At that, Gaye asked with an expression of genuine curiosity.

“Why do you think so?”

Geom Mugeuk said, his gaze cold.

“Because your life has gone wrong.”

“!”

Gaye’s expression, which had remained composed all along, stiffened slightly.

“I think you’re a worse person than the Dark Palace Lord. The Dark Palace Lord is at least someone with a clear purpose from the start, but what about you? Was the role of the Divine Maiden Palace, from the very beginning, to help the Dark Palace conquer this world?”

“The Dark Palace inherited the will of Cheonui Palace.”

She meant that she followed not the Dark Palace, but Cheonui Palace.

“Do you truly think that?”

Geom Mugeuk stared straight into her eyes.

“If the Palace Lord of the Divine Maiden Palace from the days when she served the Lord of Cheonui Palace were to see you, what do you think she would say? Do you think she would say you were doing well?”

The corners of the Divine Maiden Palace Lord’s eyes trembled faintly. Geom Mugeuk provoked her further.

“If the Dark Palace Lord is trying to use the Tower Lord here to lure me in and kill me, shouldn’t you be against it? Isn’t it the Tower Lord you want dead more than me?”

“Why would I want to kill that person?”

“How would I know what’s in your heart? Perhaps it’s because you cannot receive prophecies, while the Tower Lord here can, and that enrages you.”

“Shut your mouth!”

Gaye’s composure shattered, and her expression turned cold.

Shrrrng.

Geom Mugeuk raised his sword and drew it halfway in front of her face.

“I believe the heavens would not bestow prophecy upon someone like this.”

Her distorted face was reflected on the blade.

Beyond that sword, Geom Mugeuk said,

“When you met the Tower Lord, you should not have spoken some false prophecy. You should have apologized. Said you were sorry for calling her into a trap like this. Said you had no choice either.”

Clack.

Geom Mugeuk sheathed the sword again and rose from his seat.

“Let us leave.”

The Cheonhwa Tower Lord glanced once at the Divine Maiden Palace Lord, who was still sitting quietly in her place, then asked Geom Mugeuk,

“Are we just leaving her here?”

“She has already left.”

“What?”

Shiiik.

The Black Demon Sword split the air. A streak of sword qi cut across her neck.

Slice.

Her severed head flew into the air. Neither the Cheonhwa Tower Lord nor the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable had expected Geom Mugeuk to suddenly cut off the Divine Maiden Palace Lord’s head, and both were startled.

But no blood flowed from her neck.

Bang! Baang!

The Divine Maiden Palace Lord’s head and body turned into black smoke and vanished. The moment he had sheathed the half-drawn sword earlier, he had realized her aura had changed. Whoever it was, someone had extracted her with astonishing skill.

“Something has just been deployed around us!”

Sensing something, Geom Mugeuk walked backward and opened the door.

Indeed, the corridor outside the door was rippling with black energy. The floor was filled with black smoke like fog.

“Now I understand why they asked to meet on this ship.”

Geom Mugeuk recognized the dark demonic art spread out before his eyes.

“The Tamshim Daemyeolgong (貪心大滅功) has been deployed on this ship.”

The Tamshim Daemyeolgong was one of the representative dark demonic arts passed down in the Dark Palace.

The Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable also knew what kind of demonic art it was.

“It is a dark demonic art that manifests by feeding on humanity’s instinctive desires. The more primal the desire, such as appetite or lust, and the stronger that desire is, the greater the power it produces… Ah!”

At that moment, she too understood why they had wanted to meet here.

“On this ship, there is something that could even devour appetite or lust…”

Geom Mugeuk gazed down the corridor, which was filled with terror and an ominous air, and added quietly,

“It is filled with an even tougher desire.”

Chapter 788: Then Under the Light?
----------------------------------
A pitch-black hole opened, and the Divine Maiden Palace Lord, Gaye, leapt out.

She frowned, panting for breath. The sensation of having her body transported was a vile experience she had never felt in her life.

“Haa, haa.”

Before her, as she gasped, stood the Dark Palace Lord.

At the very center of the space, the Dark Palace Lord sat, surrounded by the Five Great Scholars of the Dark Palace, who were seated around him with their hands extended.

Sasasasak.

Their outstretched hands formed countless hand seals.

Each time they did, smoke as thin as thread rose from their fingertips, forming bizarre patterns and indecipherable characters in the air before vanishing.

Whenever those symbols and characters were created, a single character would glow on the floor where the Dark Palace Lord sat.

She could not tell what incantation they were weaving, but she could guess just how tremendous its power would be once completed.

And they were not the only ones in this space.

In one corner sat two old men side by side. The two looked so alike they might have been twins.

The absolute masters of the Dark Palace, the Dark Twin Supremes.

They were none other than Elder Am and Elder Heuk.

Both old men were over a hundred years old, and they were people the Dark Palace Lord had cherished and kept in reserve for the final moment.

Even Gaye, who had served the Dark Palace Lord for a long time, was seeing them in person for the first time. One might have thought she would study them more closely, yet her gaze was fixed on the opposite wall.

On the opposite wall was a painting of a man standing.

Gaye’s gaze was fixed there because this was the more astonishing existence.

Sensing her stare, the man on the wall turned to look at her and grinned. The sight was so lifelike that, even though she had expected it, her heart sank.

The Dark Painting Spirit.

It was the moment she saw with her own eyes the secret weapon of the Dark Palace she had only ever heard of in words.

The man in the painting began to move slowly. As though the world within the wall were his own, he stepped forward. He walked through the wall.

Then, when he passed the place where the Dark Palace Lord was, he tiptoed carefully.

In that way he crossed to the far wall, went behind the Dark Twin Supremes, and waved his hands as a prank.

Of the Dark Twin Supremes, Elder Heuk swung his hand without turning around.

Peok.

His palm force struck the wall, but by then, the man within the wall had already disappeared.

Gaye lost sight of him in an instant.

Where had he gone?

Just then, he slid out from the corner line between two walls and revealed himself.

He looked somehow full of mischief, but Gaye knew.

Along with the Dark Twin Supremes, the Dark Painting Spirit was the existence the Dark Palace Lord valued most. That surely meant it was that terrifying and powerful.

And it was not only them. Today, every master of the Dark Palace had been mobilized on this ship and around Dongting Lake. As though staking his life and death on this battle, the Dark Palace Lord had brought out all the strength accumulated over three hundred years.

For this life-or-death battle against the Demonic Cult, where only death or survival awaited.

At last, the Dark Palace Lord, whose eyes had been closed, slowly opened them.

Gaye asked him,

“Why did you summon me when I gave no signal?”

Her tone was polite and calm, but it contained strong dissatisfaction.

She had only been one-sidedly battered by Geom Mugeuk’s words. She had not even been able to offer him a single rebuttal, yet the Dark Palace Lord had taken her out of that place.

The Dark Palace Lord had seen and heard every word of the conversation Gaye had held in the VIP room.

He did not ask, What were you planning to say to the Young Cult Leader if you stayed? Instead, he pointed out her emotions.

“Why were you so angry?”

The moment she was drawn into Geom Mugeuk’s psychological warfare and grew enraged, she had already lost.

“If you had stayed there any longer, you might not have made it back. Did you not see how quickly the Young Cult Leader found you? Did you not experience how he overturned your heart the moment he arrived?”

He meant that if she had dragged out the time any longer, she might truly have died at Geom Mugeuk’s hands.

Just as she had been unable to refute Geom Mugeuk, Gaye could not refute the Dark Palace Lord either.

Because at that moment, she really had been angry.

The question of whether she had not wanted to kill the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion had struck her squarely in a vital point. And when he said that the reason was not a matter of whether she could prophesy or not, she had felt as though she had been stripped bare.

“Do you know what is lacking in you?”

“What is it?”

“It would have been better if you had not been a divine maiden at all.”

“!”

It was the first time the Dark Palace Lord had ever said such a thing.

“That empty shell called a divine maiden is making you unlike yourself.”

The emotion that surged up in her at that moment touched the same feeling she had felt earlier toward Geom Mugeuk. It made a person flare up, yet left them unable to easily argue back.

“That shell is the entirety of who I am.”

“Is it truly?”

The Dark Palace Lord looked at her with eyes that seemed to pierce straight through her heart.

“Are you saying that if I were not a divine maiden, I would have become a truly wicked woman? I am sorry, but from the beginning until now, I have been aiding you as a divine maiden, my lord. I follow not your will, but the will of Heaven.”

To be honest, she wanted to add this as well.

I am someone connected to the heavens.

She should have been able to say it proudly.

But she could not.

Because even at this critical moment, the heavens had shown her no will.

The Dark Palace Lord, who had been watching her with an unreadable gaze, nodded.

“I was rash. Pay it no mind and compose yourself.”

How could a heart whose very existence had been denied be so easily calmed?

It was then.

Seuseuseuseu.

From the floor, Hoe, the hands and feet of the Dark Palace Lord, revealed himself.

“All preparations are complete.”

At that, an aura of darkness began rising around the Dark Palace Lord. It was a darkness incomparably denser than usual.

“Today’s battle is the most important battle that will decide our fate. Do not let even a single one of those Demonic Cult bastards leave alive!”

The rising dark energy completely covered the Dark Palace Lord’s body.

“From this moment forth, we begin the Dark Holy War!”

* * *

When one reached Geom Mugeuk’s level in martial arts, there were things one could understand at a glance.

Judging by the skill that had spirited away the Divine Maiden Palace Lord without a trace, and by the level of demonic art now unfolding.

“It seems the Dark Palace Lord is here.”

At the words Dark Palace Lord, the expressions of the Soul-Subduing Demon Venerable and the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion hardened.

It was not that they had never expected it, but the true mastermind behind this affair had finally appeared. The Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion once again felt her heart sink at the thought that she had intended to come to a place like this alone.

Huuuu.

The black energy spread across the corridor floor began to seep into the room like mist.

The Greed-Seeking Great Annihilation Art personally unleashed by the Dark Palace Lord made one feel fear just by looking at it.

“What will happen if we are exposed to that energy?”

At the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion’s question, Geom Mugeuk shook his head as though he did not know either.

“At the very least, do not forget this: that demonic art is a dark demonic art that uses human desire.”

It meant they had to steel their hearts.

“Since we know it’s a trap, shall we escape from here?”

The Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion did not want the Young Cult Leader to fall into danger because of her. This appointment had arisen because of her in the first place.

“By now, this dark energy will already have spread throughout the entire ship.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s answer, the Soul-Subduing Demon Venerable proposed,

“Shall we smash through the cabin wall and get out?”

She was thinking that even if they had to fight, they should leave this ship first before doing so.

Unfortunately, it was impossible.

“Unless we break the Greed-Seeking Great Annihilation Art, we cannot escape from here.”

As if to show that in person,

Geom Mugeuk lightly thrust his fist toward the cabin wall.

No sound came from that fist.

Kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwang!

A hole opened in the wall, and the force continued to bore through beyond it.

Tuung.

At the end came an alien impact sound.

The Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion and the Soul-Subduing Demon Venerable stared at the gaping hole. At the end of the hole, black smoke churned thickly. Even that tremendous punch had failed to pierce through it.

The ship was completely enveloped by the dark smoke of the Greed-Seeking Great Annihilation Art.

“Even if we could pierce through that with even greater power…”

If it were the perfected Nine Flames Demonic Art, he would undoubtedly be able to break it. If he unleashed Heavenly Demon Annihilates the World toward a wall with no people in it? Truly, everything would vanish without a trace. Naturally, the Greed-Seeking Great Annihilation Art would be broken as well.

But there was a reason he could not do that.

“If we forcibly destroy the Greed-Seeking Great Annihilation Art like that, everyone on this ship will die instantly.”

Because the Greed-Seeking Great Annihilation Art would already be linked to those aboard this ship. This demonic art itself was being unfolded on the foundation of their desires.

“The reason the Divine Maiden Palace Lord waited in the final room was likely because of us.”

The Soul-Subduing Demon Venerable immediately understood Geom Mugeuk’s words.

“It was to connect that dark demonic art to us as well.”

Geom Mugeuk nodded.

“That’s right. That is why, to buy time, she sat there so shamelessly in the hardest place to find. Originally, she must have expected it would take us longer to reach that place after we arrived.”

Because Geom Mugeuk had gone straight to find the shipmaster and come there, and even after arriving, had immediately driven out the gamblers and gotten to the main point.
