“Fortunately, we are not deeply entangled with the Greed-Seeking Great Annihilation Art.”

Inwardly, the Soul-Subduing Demon Venerable was greatly impressed.

If it had been her, she would still have been searching for the Divine Maiden Palace Lord in the lower-level gambling halls, and in the end, she would have moved exactly according to the enemy’s intentions.

“So if we forcibly break out, we may survive, but everyone else aboard this ship will die.”

No matter how addicted to gambling they were, he could not simply leave them all to die like that. Moreover, there were martial artists of the Water Dragon Gang here, as well as boatmen who knew no martial arts.

Besides, the Water Dragon Gang was a sect belonging to the evil faction. Considering their relationship with the Evil Dao Alliance as well, he could not allow them to die.

“If we do not break this demonic art quickly, the desires of everyone here will become fuel for the art and be consumed. Without even knowing what state they are in, their true qi will dry up and they will die.”

That was the terrifying aspect of the Greed-Seeking Great Annihilation Art. It sucked in all human desire.

“In addition, if we drag things out, it will affect us as well. It will try to draw out even our desires.”

The Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion could not immediately think of what her own desire was.

She did have thoughts about how she wished to live, but that did not seem to fit the word desire.

On the other hand, the Soul-Subduing Demon Venerable knew precisely what her desire was.

To become a Demon Venerable with the greatest skill. Thus, to become the finest of the Young Cult Leader’s Demon Venerables. To become the Demon Venerable he cherished most.

That was her desire.

“Now, first let us leave this room.”

Before they knew it, the black energy had pushed halfway into the room.

Geom Mugeuk examined the rear wall.

Kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwang!

He opened a large hole in the direction where there was space, big enough for a person to pass through. Beyond that wall was another corridor.

Geom Mugeuk instructed the Soul-Subduing Demon Venerable,

“To break the Greed-Seeking Great Annihilation Art, we must find the Dark Palace Lord. I will find him, so in the meantime, please protect the Pavilion Master. Avoid that black mist as much as possible.”

The Soul-Subduing Demon Venerable nodded with a dependable look in her eyes.

“Do not worry, Young Cult Leader.”

Geom Mugeuk was about to enter the place from which the black mist was surging.

“Will you be all right?”

At the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion’s concern, Geom Mugeuk answered with a smile.

“To find him, I’ll have to go to the source of that darkness.”

Because the Dark Palace Lord would be at the source of that black energy.

After drawing the Heavenly Demon Body-Protecting Art to its limit, Geom Mugeuk walked into the black mist.

In an instant, the darkness swallowed Geom Mugeuk.

* * *

The Demon Buddha was looking at the Drunken Demon with an expression of disbelief.

The Drunken Demon, who had been guzzling liquor, had gradually tilted to one side and finally fallen asleep. He was even snoring.

He was sleeping drunk at a moment this important?

This bastard’s insane, isn’t he?

The Demon Buddha stared at the Drunken Demon with such eyes, then soon smiled faintly.

All of this was the Young Cult Leader’s doing.

Because the Drunken Demon had changed like this while staying with the Young Cult Leader, because he had come to look at that Drunken Demon with a smile like this, and because the person on that ship over there was the Young Cult Leader, he was not worried…

The moment he casually looked toward the Wealth and Honor Ship, the Demon Buddha’s expression hardened sharply.

“Hey, wake up!”

At the Demon Buddha’s urgent shout, the Drunken Demon opened his eyes.

“Look over there!”

The Drunken Demon, who had been looking in the direction the Demon Buddha was staring while still half-asleep, opened his eyes wide and sprang to his feet.

A massive aura of darkness could be felt from the Wealth and Honor Ship, and thick black smoke began wrapping around the vessel.

When the two met each other’s eyes, they nodded at the same time.

Whoooosh.

Their figures launched toward the Bugwiseon. The evil aura was far too immense for them to simply say, Geom Mugeuk will handle it.

Just then, as the two of them stepped across the water and raced toward the Bugwiseon with water-walking movement arts—

Thoom.

Blocked by something, the two were flung backward. There was an invisible membrane in front of them. They were still far from reaching the ship.

“It’s a barrier!”

At Chwima’s shout, Mabul examined the membrane spread out before his eyes.

“It’s the Heavenly Night Demon Domain (天夜魔域)!”

A barrier demonic art of the Dark Palace.

It was a barrier known to become far stronger when deployed at night, to the point that it was said no one could ever break through it.

Splash.

Chwima dove beneath the surface.

But the barrier extended below the water as well. The Bugwiseon was enclosed within a vast, transparent, spherical barrier.

The aura of darkness they had felt from the ship earlier, and now this barrier as well. It was truly a supreme-level dark demonic art.

Considering that they had failed to notice the barrier being deployed even while they had Hyeollyeon floating nearby, today’s meeting had clearly been a meticulously prepared trap.

“We must break through the barrier somehow and get onto that ship.”

At Mabul’s words, Chwima launched the first palm strike.

Shwaaaang!

Booom.

The powerful palm strike struck the barrier.

For a moment, the barrier’s surface seemed to cave in, then rebounded outward again.

“Let’s try together.”

This time, Mabul and Chwima launched palm strikes simultaneously.

But even under the two men’s attacks, the barrier did not break.

“We’ll have to find the method to dispel it if we want to pierce through.”

But the barrier was spread around the Bugwiseon in such a wide, enormous sphere that finding the method to break it would not be easy.

And if that method lay beneath the water? Finding it in these pitch-black depths would be nearly impossible.

Chwima drew a dagger and infused it with internal energy.

Shiiing.

Thoom!

But Chwima’s dagger failed to pierce the membrane and bounced away. The barrier was being maintained with truly tremendous power.

Then Mabul thought of another way to break it.

“The Heavenly Night Demon Domain grows stronger in darkness, doesn’t it?”

“So I have heard.”

“Then what about under light?”

Mabul’s eyes sharpened, and golden light began to pour from his body.

Flaaaare!

Not only the people on the other nearby boats, but even those on the lakeshore saw that light.

Mabul gathered the light radiating from his body into his palm. The light extending from Mabul’s hand concentrated on one spot of the barrier.

Chwima’s dagger flew toward the part illuminated by the light.

Shiiing!

Thunk!

This time, the dagger lodged in the barrier.

But it had only stuck there and had not torn through, so Chwima drew his internal energy to its utmost limit.

Flaaaaaaaaaare!

The light pouring from Mabul grew so bright that it was impossible to keep one’s eyes open.

“Now!”

Riiiiip.

Chwima’s dagger began to tear the barrier.

Chapter 789: One Who Has Lived a Chaotic Life
---------------------------------------------
Riiiiip.

The barrier tore open in a long split.

The two did not miss the opening and hurled themselves inside.

Whoooosh.

As soon as they entered, the torn barrier joined back together in an instant. The force sustaining it was anything but ordinary.

Chwima marveled at Mabul.

“That was truly a dazzling move.”

It had been an intelligent response, and it really had been very dazzling.

If it had been his old self, he would have taken it crookedly, thinking, Are you mocking me for glowing?

The Mabul of now merely wore a faint smile. If the situation had not been so urgent, he might even have slipped in a boast. When the Young Cult Leader sees me, he calls me a “little giant.”

And so the two flew toward the Fugui Ship.

An intense aura of darkness was unfurling from the vessel.

“This is no ordinary smoke.”

Chwima did not know, but Mabul could tell what this dark smoke was protecting.

“The Art of Greed’s Great Annihilation has been deployed on this ship.”

Mabul, too, was well acquainted with the martial arts of the Dark Palace.

He reached out toward the smoke rippling across the surface of the ship.

Paaak.

The smoke gently pushed Mabul’s hand away, yet he felt the tremendous power contained within it.

“This is the first time in my life I have ever encountered dark demonic qi this powerful.”

“It seems to be a demonic art cast personally by the Dark Palace Lord.”

At Chwima’s words, Mabul nodded.

Their expressions hardened. That meant Geom Mugeuk, who was aboard this ship, might be in danger. In fact, they were more worried because he was not alone.

“We must find a way in, no matter what.”

Little brother, just hold on a bit. This elder brother of yours will find a way in, without fail.

It was just as the two were examining the ship.

Sensing an aura behind him, Chwima turned around.

In that instant, Chwima flinched in surprise.

A single droplet of water was floating in midair.

Just as he thought there was something strange about it—

Swaaaak.

The droplet shot toward him like a hidden weapon being released.

Chwima twisted his body in midair and dodged it.

Pung!

What brushed past his face in an instant was unmistakably a water droplet.

It was no ordinary droplet. Had it struck him, it would have torn flesh and damaged bone.

Mabul, too, was staring ahead of him with a stiff expression.

A droplet was floating in front of him as well. This time, there were two.

Pung! Pung!

This time, two streams of water droplets flew toward Mabul. Mabul extended his hand and blocked the incoming droplets.

Puh-eok! Puk!

The droplets burst apart as they struck the internal force spread before his palm. Truly, their power was far from ordinary.

Chwima and Mabul looked at each other.

And then, as if those two attacks had been nothing more than probes—

Swaaaash.

Dozens of droplets sprang up into the air from the calm surface of the water.

Just as the eyes of the two Demon Venerables shone coldly—

Pupupupupupupupung!

The droplets that had leaped from the water flew toward Chwima and Mabul.

At the same time, Mabul’s prayer beads flew through the air.

Shukshukshukshukshukshukshukshuk!

Pukpukpukpukpukpukpukpuk!

The beads shot out and burst every droplet in midair.

Once again, the surroundings fell quiet as though nothing had happened.

“Someone is using a water art.”

At Mabul’s words, Chwima shouted in a drunken voice.

“You bloated water ghost, stop playing tricks and come out at once!”

Then, far away from them, an old man emerged from the water.

Swaaaash.

The old man rose straight up from beneath the water, standing upright. Waves were drawn upon his long robe, and he stood atop the water as though it were solid ground. He was not standing by means of martial arts. He was truly standing on water.

Mabul recognized who he was.

Because there was only one old man who could appear like this in such attire.

“It is the Azure Sea Divine Lord (碧海神君).”

The Azure Sea Divine Lord was a famous figure even Chwima knew of.

He was an absolute master of water arts, a man once called the Water King. An absolute master of water arts who had been confident that if he fought upon water, he would be the greatest under heaven.

When he stopped appearing in the martial world at some point, all sorts of rumors had circulated.

Some said he had died fighting an absolute master, and others said he had drowned in the sea while recklessly training in water arts. Still others said he had secluded himself alone on an uninhabited island somewhere in the middle of the sea.

But for whatever reason, he had been recruited by the Dark Palace.

“To think that old codger reeking of fish is still alive!”

At Mabul’s mockery, the Azure Sea Divine Lord laughed coldly.

“I heard it too. That some unsightly clown was playing the role of a Demon Venerable.”

Mabul’s expression hardened coldly. If they had met on land, the man would not even have dared meet his eyes.

But in this situation, he did have the right to bluster. There was only water here, and with the barrier in place, there was nowhere to flee.

Chwima turned to Mabul and said,

“I will take him. Lord Mabul, find a way into that ship.”

The gatekeeper preventing them from entering was none other than the Azure Sea Divine Lord.

If so, then inside, an even stronger enemy must be targeting the Young Cult Leader.

They had to get in as quickly as possible.

“No, why leave that to me? I’ll fight, so you find the way.”

“That will not do.”

“Why?”

“Because I cannot entrust a drunkard with a task that requires using his head.”

Chwima’s voice was drunk, but his gaze was clear. Mabul read the meaning contained in that gaze precisely.

Due to the nature of the martial arts they had learned, Mabul understood dark demonic arts better. That was also why he had recognized the Art of Greed’s Great Annihilation just moments ago.

“Understood. I will find it as quickly as possible.”

After nodding, Mabul said to the Azure Sea Divine Lord,

“Don’t die before I find a way in!”

Leaving behind those words, implying he would kill him with his own hands, Mabul lightly launched himself away.

As though he had no concern for the fight on this side, he crossed over to the opposite side of the Fugui Ship.

It had not been long since that dark smoke had risen.

If he hurried and searched, he might be able to find a place where the dark smoke had not fully spread.

Left alone, Chwima grinned foolishly with drunken eyes as he approached the Azure Sea Divine Lord.

“You think we’re the only clowns? An old man who spends his twilight years cleaning up after the Dark Palace is a clown too. Fine, let us clowns have some fun!”

Swaaaak.

Chwima charged toward the Azure Sea Divine Lord.

Tung!

Once again, something blocked Chwima’s path.

Looking closely, this time there was a barrier made of water in front of the Azure Sea Divine Lord.

It was clearly a barrier of water, yet his hand could not pass through it.

“Again?”

Swaaaak.

Pang.

He struck out with a palm, but he could not pierce the water barrier.

“If you lot are such cowards, why did you even come out to fight?”

Beyond the rippling water, the Azure Sea Divine Lord’s smiling face looked distorted.

Chwima plunged into the water with a splash.

But there was a barrier below as well.

He tried to leap over it with lightness skill, but there was a ceiling too.

The protective wall of water was the manifestation of the Azure Sea Divine Lord’s unique martial art.

The Azure Sea Divine Lord’s unique martial art, the Azure Sea Divine Art (碧海神功).

Third Form, All-Seas Water Wall (滿海水壁).

Chwima beat on the water wall like a drunk knocking on the door of a tavern.

Puh-eok, puh-eok.

“It’s not two against one, but one against one, and this place is as good as your own backyard since we’re on water, and yet you still act like this much of a coward? On top of that, I’m lacking internal force because I have to keep myself afloat with inner power! So come out! There’s plenty of water here too!”

Despite Chwima’s provocation, the Azure Sea Divine Lord did not so much as blink.

“For a demon to speak of cowardice—how truly shameless.”

The Azure Sea Divine Lord began drawing up his internal energy.

“Today, you will become fish food in Dongting Lake.”

The Azure Sea Divine Lord began his attack in earnest.

Drip. Drip. Drip. Drip, drip.

Around the Azure Sea Divine Lord, dozens of round ripples formed on the surface of the water. It was as if circular ripples created by falling raindrops were appearing here and there.

Azure Sea Divine Art, Fourth Form, Raging Current Detonation (激流振爆).

Those round ripples began to explode.

The explosions were of tremendous power. Water columns surged up in every direction.

Bang! Baaang! Baaang! Baaang!

Chwima’s figure narrowly slipped between the erupting explosions.

“Argh! I’m dying!”

The way he leaped here and there looked just like a drunk man dancing.

Boom! Kwaaang!

Just when it seemed he must have been caught in an explosion, Chwima came tumbling out from behind it, flailing his arms and legs. He had clearly been swept into the blast, yet at some point he was already throwing himself to another side.

Judging by his appearance alone, he looked like a drunk man being chased by a dog, floundering as he ran wildly in every direction. They were the movements of a drunkard who seemed as if he would collapse at any moment.

But Chwima’s movement art was truly astonishing. Each and every motion contained the very essence of movement techniques.

And at this moment, Chwima was putting into practice the martial intent he had pondered while drinking throughout the entire journey.

When every Raging Current Detonation had burst, Chwima shouted.

“Are you insane? Do you have any idea how many fish died because of you just now?”

He acted as though he were truly drunk and spouting nonsense.

“He dodged that?”

It was possible to dodge it. But the Azure Sea Divine Lord had not expected him to do so in such a ridiculous manner. Because he looked so drunk, it was impossible to tell whether it was his true skill or simply luck.

The Azure Sea Divine Lord’s next attack followed.

Azure Sea Divine Art, Fifth Form, Myriad Currents Revolving Wheel (萬派回輪).

The water beneath Chwima’s feet seemed to surge violently.

Kwakwakwakwakwakwa!

A massive vortex formed beneath Chwima’s feet.

“What is this now?”

When he leaped into the air, the vortex whirled and shot upward like a pillar of water.

Whirrrrrrrr!

The vortex chased after Chwima as though it were a living creature about to devour him.

“It’s a snake, a water snake!”

Chwima thrust palm force downward from midair.

Baaang!

The waterspout split apart as though shattered, but soon the water merged again and became an even larger vortex.

As Chwima fled, he struck the All-Seas Water Wall the Azure Sea Divine Lord had set up and was bounced forward, and because of that, he was swept into the vortex that came crashing down upon him.

Within the powerful water vortex that could tear a human body to shreds, Chwima could be seen flailing and spinning. In his helpless state, he looked like a doll.

“Done!”

Once swept into the Myriad Currents Revolving Wheel, no one could come out alive.

But because his opponent was who he was, the Azure Sea Divine Lord increased the speed of the Myriad Currents Revolving Wheel even further.

Whirrrrrrrrrrrrr.

The rotation was so fast that Chwima could no longer even be seen. His entire body would be torn to pieces until not even his shape remained.

“Now, one is dealt with.”

The Azure Sea Divine Lord called out to Mabul.

“You stunted little sack of dung! Now it’s your turn!”

Then Mabul shot up from the far side of the Fugui Ship.

He gave the madly spinning vortex a brief glance, then disappeared from sight again.

The Azure Sea Divine Lord snorted.

“Where are you going, you stunted little sack of dung? Even if you hide in fear, there is nowhere to run.”

At that moment, the speed of the wildly whirling vortex gradually began to decrease.

The Azure Sea Divine Lord flinched in surprise. He had not reduced its speed, yet it had begun to slow.

When the vortex finally came to a complete stop, the Azure Sea Divine Lord’s eyes flew wide open.

Splash.

Chwima landed intact upon the surface of the water.

“Damn, that made me dizzy.”

Chwima staggered.

“How? How on earth?”

The Azure Sea Divine Lord could not hide his shock, as though he had seen a ghost.

“He broke through the Myriad Currents Revolving Wheel with his bare body?”

At the disbelief in his eyes, Chwima drank again from the liquor gourd hanging at his waist.

“This body has lived its whole life dizzy with drunkenness. And no matter how dizzy that was, could it be as dizzying as my life?”

Then Chwima raised his drunken eyes and grinned.

“Want to have a drink and listen to the story of my life?”

Chwima staggered toward him.

The Azure Sea Divine Lord did not notice. Chwima’s footwork had changed. Before he knew it, he too was walking upon the water as though it were land. Just as the Azure Sea Divine Lord walked upon the water. He had come out of the vortex having realized something.

“You mad drunkard!”

The sight of those drunken eyes sent a chill through him. This was an opponent who had come out alive from a place where he absolutely should not have survived.

The Azure Sea Divine Lord unleashed a powerful move.

Azure Sea Divine Art, Sixth Form, Water-Fire Extreme Heat (水火極熱).

Bubble, bubble, bubble, bubble.

The surface of the water around them began to boil in an instant.

“Die cooked alive!”

Swaaaash.

Water that had become as hot as lava surged into a wave and crashed over Chwima. This special heat could not be blocked by protective qi. That was why this move had taken the lives of countless masters.

Puaaaak.

The boiling water crashed over him, but Chwima did not dodge.

“Aaaaargh! Hot!”

He made a great fuss about how much it hurt as he came walking closer and closer, until he reached the Manhae Water Wall.

What was protecting Chwima was not protective gangqi, but the alcohol qi that filled the space around his body.

Chiiiiik.

The alcohol qi hissed as it burned in the heat.

Scalding steam rose from Chwima’s entire body, his face flushed red as though cooked, and he began tearing at the Manhae Water Wall with his bare hands.

Riiiiip.

And, astonishingly, it tore.

“When we’re drunk, we don’t even feel pain! We don’t see anything in front of us.”

Seeing Chwima thrust his head through the torn membrane, his bloodshot eyes glaring, Byeokhae Singun felt chills run down his spine. He had fought countless enemies in his life, but this was the first time he had ever faced someone like this.

If his opponent had used some unique martial art to block him, he would not have been this shocked. But his opponent was simply enduring his martial art with his bare body.

Even after taking his martial art head-on, he had not so much as flinched. As wounded as his pride was, fear rose in equal measure.

‘Is a Demon Venerable truly this strong?’

This was not the level of skill he had known the Demon Venerables to possess.

Byeokhae Singun lightly launched himself to the opposite side and escaped outside the Manhae Water Wall.

Chwima staggered toward him again and slurred his words.

“I told you, thanks to you, I made a breakthrough. Now, take a cup of my liquor and let me ride that whirlpool one more time!”

“You crazy bastard!”

Overwhelmed by his momentum, Byeokhae Singun was taking a step back when—

He bumped into something behind him.

Thud.

Startled, Byeokhae Singun turned around, and Mabul was standing there, emitting a faint glow.

Mabul looked at him and said coldly,

“Here you are, you short sack of shit.”

A hand seal had already formed in his hands.

“Why, you……”

There was not even time for a single curse.

Flash!

Golden gangqi fell like a bolt of lightning and struck Byeokhae Singun squarely on the head.

Byeokhae Singun’s skull shattered, and he collapsed, dead on the spot.

Chwima looked down at the corpse and said,

“I understand. Turn around and you forget, turn around again and you forget some more. You forget when you’re old, and you forget when you’re drunk.”

Mabul said to Chwima,

“Let’s go. I found a place to enter.”

“See? A hundred times better than a drunkard!”

It was at that moment.

In the distance, by the lakeshore, an explosion rang out. From the lakeshore where the subordinates of the Ghost Shadow Unit were, came the clash of weapons. Far away, enemies were swarming in from every direction like a cloud of bees.

“Now that I see it, they’d set a net from the beginning and were waiting to devour us.”

At Chwima’s words, Mabul took the liquor bottle from his hand and gulped it down.

Seeing that, Chwima chuckled and said,

“These bastards are bringing back our Lord Mabul’s old temper.”

Mabul launched himself forward.

Chwima followed after him, flying toward the rear of the Fugwiseon. He was not worried about what was happening outside.

Just as this place shone with golden light, over there, a pure white smile would surely be forming.

Chapter 790: This Will Only Be the Beginning
--------------------------------------------
Ian was walking along the lakeside with Jihan.

Though the hour was late, there were many people around the lake.

Those who had come out for a stroll walked slowly along the shore, and peddlers who had come to sell them goods had lined up in rows. Merchants selling liquor, merchants selling snacks, merchants selling firecrackers—all manner of vendors had taken their places.

It was such a peaceful scene that it only made Ian feel more uneasy.

Just then, Jihan spoke to her cautiously.

“This may sound strange, but I smell something ominous.”

Jihan drew in a deep breath through his nose, as if taking a long inhale. He had been concentrating on scents for a while now. His nose was more accurate than his eyes.

“There’s a smell I can’t quite explain. There’s a lot of metal mixed in, too.”

Ian looked around. But there was no strange change in their surroundings at all.

If they could not sense danger by sight, that meant it might be all the more dangerous.

“It’s only a feeling, but…”

“That feeling—I trust it.”

Ian gave a long whistle. Then another warrior of the Ghost Shadow Unit nearby made a birdcall, and that sound passed into a song before becoming a whistle once more. It was the agreed-upon warning to prepare for danger.

In the distance, Chairan could be seen looking this way with the members of Group Three.

Aside from them, the Ghost Shadow Unit warriors stationed here and there all tensed inwardly at Ian’s warning.

Just then, a brilliant light spread from the middle of the lake far away.

A golden radiance.

Ian knew at once.

“It’s Lord Demon Buddha!”

She saw the light that had been stretching in every direction gather into one place. It was the moment when Demon Buddha and the Drunken Demon were breaking through the barrier.

But from where she stood, it was too far to know exactly what they were doing.

Still, she could clearly feel that something was happening.

Ian looked beyond the light toward the Buguiseon.

‘Young Cult Leader!’

If Demon Buddha had to make his body shine, then something had surely happened inside the Buguiseon.

‘You mustn’t even cut a single finger!’

She sincerely wished for Geom Mugeuk to come out safely.

At that very moment.

Her instinct for danger stirred.

Without hesitation, Ian threw herself at Jihan.

The two of them rolled across the ground.

Swish, swish!

Steel needles shot swiftly through the place where Ian and Jihan had been. Judging by their speed and power, they were not hidden weapons thrown by human hands.

Piiik.

By the time Ian sprang to her feet, a whistle was already in her mouth.

The moment the sound announcing an emergency rang out,

their full-scale attack began.

Before she could even identify who had attacked them—

Whoosh.

A bead flew toward her and burst before her eyes.

Bang!

Smoke billowed out from the bead.

Her vision was obscured, and from beyond the smoke came an intense killing intent she could feel.

Swishswishswishswishswishswishswishswishswishswish!

Hidden weapons were fired indiscriminately toward them from beyond the smoke.

Clangclangclangclangclangclangclangclang!

Ian drew her sword and struck down the incoming hidden weapons. Even normally, they were so fast that blocking them would not have been easy, and now her vision was obscured as well. Ordinarily, she should have thrown herself aside to dodge.

Or she could simply have leaped into the air and blocked them from outside the smoke.

But if she did, the Ghost Shadow Unit warriors and ordinary residents behind her would all die. Their opponents did not care in the slightest for the lives of others. No—perhaps they were making use of that very fact.

When the rising smoke settled, a man and woman who had been strolling behind Ian were sitting flat on their backsides, staring this way with startled faces.

They looked dumbfounded, unable to understand what had just happened.

Ian was no longer where she had been.

“Guh…”

A sword was embedded in the chest of a man who had been beyond the smoke.

Ian’s wrist still throbbed from striking down the hidden weapons. And now she understood why the attack had been so powerful.

Attached to the right arm of the man whose chest had been pierced was a cylindrical steel hidden-weapon tube. Even at a glance, it was a hidden weapon of formidable power.

The man died with disbelief filling his eyes. At this close range, he had thought she could not block it even without the smoke, yet Ian had struck down every single hidden weapon inside the smoke.

‘She blocked the Gyeoksatan in the smoke?’

He did not know it, but Ian had not only struck down the hidden weapons flying at her, but also every one headed elsewhere.

This was the ability of a woman who had achieved mastery of the Flying Heaven Sword Art and yet still continued to strive endlessly.

“Be careful!”

When Jihan shouted, another man on the other side was aiming a hidden weapon at Ian.

Just before the Gyeoksatan he was about to release fired—

Shiiik.

Slice.

The man’s throat was cut first, and he collapsed, spurting blood. Chairan stood behind him.

At Ian’s look of thanks, Chairan nodded.

Chairan recognized the hidden weapon at a glance.

“It’s the Gyeoksatan. A hidden weapon with terrifying power despite being easy to carry and use.”

As an assassin, she possessed extensive knowledge of hidden weapons.

“It’s a hidden weapon of the Divine Mechanism Iron Sect.”

The Divine Mechanism Iron Sect.

About fifty years ago, there had been a sect famous for mechanisms and hidden weapons.

The hidden weapons and mechanisms they made were so outstanding that there was no one in the gangho who did not know of them. The Murim Alliance had even bought mechanisms they had made and installed them in the alliance headquarters.

Then one day, they were suddenly annihilated.

The Murim Alliance launched a large-scale investigation, but in the end, they never discovered why it had happened or who had done it.

“The people behind this destroyed the Divine Mechanism Iron Sect and stole their techniques.”

At Ian’s words, Chairan nodded.

“It seems so.”

Worry crossed Chairan’s face.

“If they truly are using the Divine Mechanism Iron Sect, the Gyeoksatan will be only the beginning.”

* * *

The Demon Army was waiting with its carriages hidden in a nearby forest.

Jang Ho had carried out countless operations until now, but he had never been as tense as he was today.

It was probably because the Young Cult Leader was with them, and because Ian and several Demon Venerables were involved in the operation as well.

Just then, the sound of a whistle came from outside the forest.

Piiik.

At the whistle announcing an emergency, the Demon Army reacted at once.

“We move out immediately.”

At Jang Ho’s command, the waiting Demon Army soldiers all rushed out at once.

The one at the front was Jang Ho.

Just as they were about to leave the forest, Jang Ho felt an inexplicable sense of wrongness.

“Stop!”

When Jang Ho stopped, the Demon Army soldiers following behind him all stopped at once.

Soon after, Jang Ho realized the source of that unpleasant sense of wrongness.

The peddlers’ carts doing business at the entrance had turned to face them. If they were selling goods, it would only be natural for them to face the lake, where there were many people.

A moment of tension and silence passed.

Then something rose from the carts. It rose so naturally and soundlessly that it caused no sense of incongruity at all.

What pushed up through the center was an iron pillar. Atop it, a large iron cylinder was aimed this way. Dozens of holes were bored into that cylinder.

The other carts nearby were the same.

From all four carts, firing-type hidden weapons revealed themselves at once.

“Dodge!”

The moment Jang Ho’s shout rang out—

Shooshooshooshooshooshooshooshooshooshooshooshooshooshoo!

Hidden weapons fired from every cart at once.

Those who released the hidden weapons were not martial artists. In order to avoid having their disguise discovered, they had used subordinates of the Dark Palace who possessed not even a handful of internal energy, and they had trained their disguises for a long time by actually working as peddlers.

Compared to the portable Gyeoksatan used earlier, these had incomparable speed and power.

Clangclangclangclangclangclangclangclang!

Jang Ho struck down the hidden weapons with his sword as he retreated. Though he had infused his blade with internal energy, his wrist hurt as if it might snap.

Fortunately, because Jang Ho noticed quickly and responded, the Demon Army soldiers following behind him were able to hide themselves behind trees.

Jang Ho did not simply endure the assault.

Shiiiik.

As he retreated, Jang Ho sent out a fierce strand of sword energy.

Kaaaang!

But in front of the enemy firing the hidden weapons, an iron plate that could not be cut by sword energy had already risen to protect him. Unless he sent his sword energy precisely through the small hole through which the enemy looked out, he could not kill him.

But with hidden weapons pouring down like rain, he could not launch sword energy with such precision.

One of the Demon Army subordinates knowledgeable in hidden weapons and mechanism formations recognized what was being fired from the carts.

“It’s the Divine Mechanism Iron Sect’s Gale Repeating Cannon!”

Jang Ho’s expression hardened. He did not know much about the Divine Mechanism Iron Sect in detail, but he knew one thing for certain.

Their outstanding techniques were still spoken of to this day.

Meanwhile, another cart arrived there. It was twice as large as the other carts. It was the large cart that had been selling noodles up ahead. The old man controlling that cart was also a subordinate of the Dark Palace who had not learned martial arts.

The hidden weapon loaded onto that cart was far larger than the previous ones.

“It’s the Heavenly Bow Thunder-Kill Cannon!”

He could tell from his subordinate’s urgent voice.

Puaaaang!

A steel needle, slower but incomparably larger and more powerful than the ones before, was fired.

Boom! Craaack!

The tree they had hidden behind shattered under the tremendous force that came flying in.

“Back! Fall back!”

Jang Ho and the Demon Army soldiers retreated again.

Puaang! Kwaaaang!

The ground overturned and fragments flew, but they calmly withdrew while stepping back. From long experience, they knew that if they lost their composure in a situation like this, they would all die.

While this was happening at the front, the Demon Army soldiers in the rear climbed onto the carriages. It was not to move the carriages, nor was it to avoid the attack.

Click.

When the first Demon Army soldier to board manipulated a device inside the carriage—

Clank, clank, clank, clank.

Lines appeared across the carriage walls with metallic sounds.

The Demon Army soldiers inside the carriages pulled on the handles attached throughout the walls.

Click, click, click, click.

The carriage walls separated into several pieces.

Astonishingly, those separated pieces were large shields.

They had been designed to serve as carriage walls normally, and as shields like this in an emergency.

The letters and pictures drawn on the outer walls of the carriages had likewise been painted so that they would form their own designs when they became shields.

They were tactical shields of the Demon Army, used in battle during emergencies.

As the standing carriage walls were all dismantled, the Demon Army soldiers ran out carrying the shields. The shields were so large that even two of their big bodies could stand side by side behind one.

The enemy’s weapons could not fire indefinitely either. They had to take turns reloading hidden weapons.

The moment the Heavenly Bow Thunder-Kill Cannon, which had displayed the greatest power, paused briefly to reload its steel needles—

“Now!”

At Jang Ho’s command, the Demon Army soldiers in the rear charged with the shields at the fore.

Their first target was the Heavenly Bow Thunder-Kill Cannon, which even the shields could not block.

The Gale Repeating Cannons poured fire in order to protect it.

Bangbangbangbangbangbangbangbang!

The pouring hidden weapons hammered against the shields. But the two Demon Army soldiers drew their internal energy up to the limit and pushed forward. The other Demon Army soldiers ran close behind them. More than ten men clung to a single shield.

The shields were not ordinary metal. They were the pinnacle of the Iron Workshop’s craft, specially made for the Demon Army.

The farther they pushed, the more the Demon Army soldiers at the front holding the shields staggered under that powerful force. Other soldiers following behind changed places with them and pushed on.

The faces of those firing the hidden weapons were filled with bewilderment, never having imagined that the Demon Army would use shields. Moreover, the men were brutishly forcing their way in.

“Hurry! Swap it out!”

The hidden weapon of the Heavenly Bow Thunder-Kill Cannon was loaded once more.

Puuuung! Kwaaang!

As the Heavenly Bow Thunder-Kill Cannon struck the lead shield, the Demon Army soldiers were pushed back, unable to withstand the force. The two Demon Army soldiers holding the shield suddenly vomited blood.

The Demon Army soldiers behind them took the shield in their place and moved the injured men back.

The Demon Army’s shield did not break, and their will could not be stopped.

Before the second shot could be fired, Jang Ho leaped over the shield and charged through the air.

The fast-firing Gale Repeating Cannons all aimed at Jang Ho at once, pointing skyward, and the Heavenly Bow Thunder-Kill Cannon instinctively aimed at Jang Ho as well.

Shooshooshooshooshooshooshoo!

Jang Ho swung his sword in midair and deflected the incoming attacks. Blocking in midair was twice as difficult, a dangerous act that required him to risk his life, but Jang Ho bravely became the bait. Fortunately, the Heavenly Bow Thunder-Kill Cannon was slow compared to its power, so he was able to avoid it.

And the Demon Army did not miss that moment. The shields opened, and from within, the Demon Army charged.

Swaaaaaaak.

Those massive bodies pushed forward at truly insane speed. By the time the startled men took aim at them again, they had already arrived.

Kwaang!

The Demon Army slammed bodily into the carts.

Jangho, too, plunged straight down from midair and thrust his sword through a hole in the iron plate.

Puuuk!

The face of the man behind it was pierced through, and he collapsed.

A cart that had not yet been attacked turned in that direction.

But a shield-bearing Demon Army soldier who arrived right after charged straight ahead.

Bang.

The cart shattered and overturned. The moment the martial artist who had been firing hidden weapons from the cart sprang to his feet, the enormous shield struck him.

Puh-eok!

His whole body smashed apart, he tumbled across the ground. The last thing he saw was the large characters 魔軍 painted on the shield.

The Demon Army swept through the place as if a storm had passed over it. Just as they were catching their breath—

Thung, thung, thung, thung thung.

A series of sounds rang out from afar, as if something was being fired.

Then something flew in an arc toward where the Demon Army stood. Dozens of iron balls, each the size of an adult’s fist, came hurtling in.

Jangho shouted.

“Deploy the Tortoise-Shell Iron Wall Formation!”

The next instant, the shield-bearing Demon Army soldiers converged around Jangho all at once.

Clack-clack-clack-clack-clack-clack-clack-clack-clack!

Like the shell of a tortoise forming, they blocked every side and even the ceiling with their shields.

Bang! Kwaang! Bang! Bangbangbang!

The iron balls exploded in midair.

Pakpakpakpakpakpakpakpakpakpakpak!

What burst out in every direction at tremendous speed were hundreds of tiny iron pellets.

The iron pellets, flying in with immense force, hammered against the shields. Because the pellets were so small and yet so powerful, they were not easy to block without shields. And on top of that, the iron balls kept coming.

The Demon Army infused their shields with internal energy to withstand them. The shields vibrated as if they might shatter at any moment.

Bang! Bang! Kwaang! Kwaang!

Jangho was also pouring his internal energy into the shields overhead along with the Demon Army.

Truly, he had never imagined that men armed with the hidden weapons and mechanisms of the Shingi Iron Gate would be waiting here.

One thought made Jangho’s heart grow anxious.

‘What if this attack is unleashed on the Ghost Shadow Unit?’

Even the Demon Army, equipped with tactical shields, was finding this attack difficult to block. The Ghost Shadow Unit would suffer tremendous casualties.

Given Ian’s personality, he would try to protect his subordinates no matter what. Even Ian could be placed in danger. That could not be allowed. If Ian were to die or even be injured? How could he face the Young Cult Leader afterward?

“We protect the Ghost Shadow Unit. Charge forward like this!”

With the Tortoise-Shell Iron Wall Formation maintained, the Demon Army began to move swiftly while enduring the explosions.

It was the strongest and fastest tortoise in the world.

Chapter 791: That’s Why I Left Lord Soma Behind
-----------------------------------------------
Geom Mugeuk stepped into the darkness.

The moment he entered the fog, a suffocating tightness pressed in on him. His whole body grew heavy, as though his clothes had been soaked through with water.

If the Heavenly Demon Protective Divine Art had not pushed that energy away, he felt he would not have been able to display even half his usual strength. Without a martial art that countered it, this was surely an energy that would exert a tremendous influence.

Geom Mugeuk sharpened every nerve and stepped forward.

Whoosh.

Something suddenly sprang out from within the darkness.

Shiiiik. Thunk!

At a glance, it had a human shape, but it was not human. Its neck severed by the Black Demon Sword, it vanished with a puff, turning into smoke.

Then someone’s voice was heard.

“What would you have done if it had been a real person? Swinging your sword so recklessly.”

The instant he heard the voice, Geom Mugeuk knew instinctively.

“It’s you, Dark Palace Lord.”

“That’s right. It is me.”

It was not actual speech, nor was it a voice transmitted by sound transmission. His words were being conveyed through this dark fog.

“How did you know it was me?”

“With a cursed fate as rotten as ours, how could I not?”

At that, something rippled within the fog.

Srrrk.

The Dark Palace Lord appeared before Geom Mugeuk.

It was the first time the two of them had ever met.

“You look just as I imagined.”

“How did you imagine me?”

“Old, ugly, greedy…”

Geom Mugeuk strode over and placed a hand on his body. His hand passed right through him. He looked vividly real, but he was an illusion.

“And a coward. You’d never come out unless you threw all your subordinates at me first, would you?”

A strange smile formed at the corners of the Dark Palace Lord’s mouth. To think he would speak so crudely from their very first meeting. He was exactly the Young Cult Leader the reports had described. The way he struck at an opponent’s blind spot.

“Betraying friendships built over long years, using people’s hearts. That’s your method, isn’t it? Have you ever once gone to someone fair and square and said, ‘Let’s fight’?”

Instead of taking the bait, the Dark Palace Lord said something unexpected.

“Young Cult Leader, would you care to see what was done by this unfair, cowardly man?”

When the Dark Palace Lord spread his hand, a scene unfolded beside him like an illusion.

“This is the scene outside.”

Ian could be seen leaning against a boulder.

Blood was pouring from her chest. No one helped her as she was dying.

Soon after, her figure receded, and the surroundings came into view. The squad leaders and members of the Ghost Shadow Unit had all become corpses as well.

Cha Iran lay fallen, her body split in two, and Cheongmyeon lay dead beneath her half-shattered blue mask. Seojin had died with hidden weapons stuck in him like a porcupine, and Jihan had died with his face buried in flowing blood.

Ian was looking around at the sight in anguish.

“…I’m sorry I couldn’t protect you.”

Blood gushed from her mouth.

Watching this, Geom Mugeuk shouted.

“You bastard! I’ll kill you!”

Geom Mugeuk yelled in fury. He tried to seize the illusion by the throat, but he could not.

“Come out already, won’t you? You bastard, I said come out!”

The Dark Palace Lord, who had been quietly watching him, said,

“You are not excited at all.”

At that, the gaze of Geom Mugeuk, who had been shouting at the top of his lungs, instantly grew calm.

“You must have been hoping to see me go mad and run wild. Sorry. As you can see, acting excited isn’t easy.”

Geom Mugeuk did not believe a single thing he had been shown.

“Why are you so sure it’s fake?”

“I didn’t raise them to be that weak. And there’s also a guardian who wouldn’t let them die like that. Most decisively, Ian didn’t call for me as she was dying, did she? If she’d said, ‘Young Cult Leader, I’m sorry I couldn’t serve you to the end,’ then even I might have gotten a little tense.”

A look of disbelief flickered through the Dark Palace Lord’s eyes.

Then Geom Mugeuk said something unexpected.

“Since you’re showing me anyway, show me the real scene outside. I’m curious what everyone’s doing without me. In any case, today either you or I will disappear from this world. We waited three hundred years and only just met, so there’s no need to rush, is there? Honestly, you’re curious too, aren’t you? Who knows? Maybe if I really see Ian die, I’ll fall into qi deviation.”

The Dark Palace Lord stared silently at Geom Mugeuk, then waved his hand again.

The scene of corpses strewn about vanished, and the true lakeside appeared before his eyes.

* * *

The attacks were being concentrated on Ian.

They were far too persistent to be explained as an attempt to kill the leader first and break morale.

There was no doubt an order had been given to kill Ian by any means necessary.

But they had overlooked one thing. Her skill surpassed what they had expected.

Thanks to that, while the attacks were focused on Ian, Cha Iran and the members of Third Squad caught the ambushers.

Originally, it would never have been an easy fight. The enemy consisted of people raised without internal energy solely to unleash hidden weapons. Moreover, they had been trained not to reveal killing intent, making them difficult to detect.

They suddenly sprang out from among the ordinary people who were fleeing with screams and unleashed their hidden weapons.

In a battle where it was impossible to tell who was the killer and who was not, Cha Iran and the assassins displayed truly supreme skill. They, too, were people well versed in concealing murderous intent.

Meanwhile, First Squad and Second Squad rescued the civilians.

Another ambusher sprang out and fired hidden weapons at Ian.

Shik-shik-shik-shik-shik!

Clang-clang-clang-clang-clang!

Though the hidden weapons had flown in by surprise, Ian struck them all down. Worried that the weapons flying behind her might hit someone else, she did not dodge but knocked every one of them away with her sword. It was a truly astonishing display of divine prowess.

The one who dealt with the person who had fired the hidden weapons was Cha Iran again. Of the women who had once vied for the title of the greatest beauty under heaven, one blocked the hidden weapons, while the other killed the enemy.

Ian spotted merchants trembling beside a cart, unable to escape in time, and shouted,

“Go to where those martial artists are!”

The moment she warned them, Ian sensed something out of place.

Her eyes met those of one of the merchants, and there was no fear in his gaze.

Srrr.

A device that fired hidden weapons rose from his cart.

It was the Gale Repeating Cannon they had faced earlier against the Demon Army.

Shiiiik.

Ian instinctively sent sword energy flying, but it was blocked by an iron wall in front of it.

To take down the enemy, she had to strike precisely through the small hole made for looking outside—the eye slit.

In that instant, Ian’s heart sank.

Even deflecting the hidden weapons worn on the enemies’ arms earlier had been truly difficult.

But the mechanical device mounted on the cart looked incomparably more powerful than those.

And there was not just one cart. Including the other carts to the left and right, a total of three Gale Repeating Cannons were aimed at her all at once. Amid the chaos, they had been waiting for this moment alone.

It was the worst possible situation, but Ian was not afraid.

If she died here, the ordinary people behind her and her subordinates protecting them would all die as well. As a commander, that was something she could never allow.

‘Block it. Ian, you can block it.’

She steadied her mind and brought all her focus to bear.

Shuk-shuk-shuk-shuk-shuk-shuk-shuk-shuk-shuk!

At the very moment the Gale Repeating Cannons fired!

Something astonishing happened. Was it because she had exerted a level of concentration pushed to the absolute limit?

Everything around her began to move slowly.

Through the hole beyond the mechanism, she could see the enemy’s pupil move.

She could see hidden weapons pouring out from the device. One, two, three… she saw them emerge one after another in slow motion.

Hidden weapons were also being fired from the left and right, flying toward her.

The instant her instincts gave her the answer as to which of these hidden weapons she had to evade and which she had to strike down,

the slowly flowing time returned to normal.

Clang-clang-clang-clang-clang-clang-clang-clang-clang-clang!

Deflecting the incoming hidden weapons as she surged forward, she was already in front of the cart.

Shiiiik!

She brought her sword down vertically.

Slice.

The iron plate that had not been cut even by sword energy earlier was split apart, and the face of the man behind it was cleaved in two as well.

“Haa.”

Just as she exhaled the breath she had been holding,

the mechanism on the left had also stopped firing.

While it had turned its direction to attack Ian, Cha Iran had leaped over the iron wall and driven a dagger into his throat. Her movement had been so fast it was almost unbelievable.

Shuk-shuk-shuk-shuk-shuk-shuk!

The mechanism on the right was still firing into the air.

Something like a pitch-black serpent rose from beneath the cart and forced its direction completely upward. It was Seojin’s ghost art at work.

The one who drove a dagger into the heart of the man who had lost his balance and was firing wildly into the air was Cheongmyeon.

Ian looked down at her sword for a moment in a daze.

Experiencing time slowing down was one thing, but the iron plate that had not been cut even by sword energy had been sliced as easily as a radish.

She had no idea how that had been possible. It was something she would have to ask Geom Mugeuk about later.

A short while later, the Demon Army arrived there.

For some reason, the explosive hidden weapon attacks that had kept flying at them had stopped all at once, allowing them to break formation and rush over.

And at the sight unfolding before them, Jangho and the Demon Army alike looked shocked. The situation had already been settled.

The three mechanical devices were completely smashed. Corpses of people wearing portable hidden weapons lay scattered everywhere.

The martial artists of Third Squad of the Ghost Shadow Unit were recovering the hidden weapons from their arms. They intended to collect every last one and bring them to the Iron Workshop.

On one side, the martial artists of First Squad and Second Squad of the Ghost Shadow Unit stood around the common people, protecting them.

Jangho realized that he had underestimated the Ghost Shadow Unit’s abilities.

Ian could feel just how urgently Jangho and the Demon Army had rushed over.

“Lord Jang.”

“Commander Ian.”

The two stood facing each other.

Jangho could feel it. The Ian from their drinking gathering at the tavern before and the Ian now on the battlefield were so different in skill that they could almost be called completely different people.

“I am glad you are safe.”

“It’s thanks to you worrying about me.”

Jangho wanted to say something encouraging to Ian, who had fought splendidly.

“Congratulations on your first real battle. If the Demon Army is ever in danger next time, please be sure to come help us.”

It was the highest praise Jangho could possibly give.

“Thank you, my lord. I don’t think there will ever be a need for us to go help, but call us anytime.”

It was just then, when they thought everything had been settled.

Shaaaaaaak!

Something flew toward the two of them.

A shield-bearing soldier of the Demon Army beside them threw himself forward to protect Jangho and Ian.

Jangho and Ian turned together and infused their internal energy into the shield.

Boom!

The three of them were shoved backward in a long slide. Realizing that the sound of what was flying at them was unusual, the two had added their strength together; otherwise, the Demon Army soldier who had tried to protect them would have died from the force.

Craaaack.

The shield he was holding split with a crack, then soon shattered and fell apart. The tactical shield that had never once broken throughout the entire battle had been destroyed.

Thud.

Someone landed there.

The instant everyone saw him, they were startled.

He was a man with a massive build, his entire body clad in iron armor. Several chunks of metal were attached to the armor as well, making his bulk look even larger.

Cha Iran recognized him. No, to be precise, she recognized what he was wearing.

“Divine Mechanism Iron Armor!”

The greatest masterpiece created by that Iron Gate earlier was precisely this Divine Mechanism Iron Armor. It was armor that gathered all of their technology and capabilities.

“It absolutely cannot be cut by sword energy or sword aura.”

That was not the only problem.

Weapons were arrayed all over his body, and forbidden weapons were attached to his shoulders, arms, and waist. They were far more powerful than the ones they had experienced earlier.

Ian knew. The only weakness was to aim for the eyes of that iron mask protecting his face.

But judging from his bearing, his martial skill was anything but ordinary. He would never easily allow them to take his eyes.

“You arrogant Demonic Cult bastards. Did you think we would be so frightened we’d run away just because you came swarming in like a pack of dogs?”

The armored man slowly raised both arms. The hidden weapons attached to his arms made an unpleasant metallic sound.

“I wonder just how many of you will survive here?”

The shield-bearing soldiers of the Demon Army rushed forward in a cluster and blocked the front of the Ghost Shadow Unit. Their eyes showed no fear whatsoever.

“Demonic Cult bastards, this place will be your grave—”

Before he could finish speaking, something flew in.

Without wavering, without hesitation, it simply shot straight forward and struck the eye of the iron mask with perfect accuracy.

Thwack!

It was faster than a shooting star cutting across the night sky. It had flown in so quickly that the sound arrived only afterward.

Shiiiiiiiiiiik!

Silence settled over the place.

Blood was trickling from the ironclad man’s eye.

The man who had seemed ready to slaughter them all at any moment slowly began to collapse.

Thud.

By the time he fell to the floor, he was already dead.

Ian, of course, and everyone else there turned around in astonishment. When they confirmed where that finger wind had come from, they were aghast.

‘No way. From that distance?’

Far away, from behind a rock atop a small hill, a person appeared.

It was Geugak Soma, wearing a pure white mask.

From that great distance, he had struck with Hyeorangji with perfect precision.

It was not the Hyeorangji of the past. It was faster, more accurate.

They all understood.

Geugak Soma had been watching this fight from the very beginning. The reason they had all been able to finish the battle safely was also because he had been quietly protecting them.

In the chaos, there must surely have been enemies the squad members had missed.

But Geugak Soma would not have missed them. The silently flying Hyeorangji must have sent the reapers back.

Perhaps that battle had consumed far more of his mental strength than if he had stepped forward and fought himself.

Jangho could tell that the one who had dealt with those who had attacked them at the end was Geugak Soma. He realized that Geugak Soma had been silently ranging across the battlefield, fighting alongside them the entire time.

With a face full of emotion, Ian called out to him with all his strength.

* * *

“Lord Soma!”

At the same time as Ian, Geom Mugeuk also shouted.

Geom Mugeuk’s face was filled with emotion. Had there been any moment recently when he had felt this moved? Ian and all the squad members, who had fought so splendidly, made him proud. They had all fought well!

In contrast, the Dark Palace Lord’s expression hardened. The scene that had unfolded beside him like an illusion vanished with a pop, as if it had been extinguished.

“Do not think this is the end.”

The Dark Palace Lord’s phantom wavered. It was as if his rage were piercing through the illusion itself.

“Your Geugak Soma will be trapped in eternal darkness today.”

When Geom Mugeuk showed no reaction, the Dark Palace Lord asked,

“You do not believe this either?”

Geom Mugeuk shook his head.

“I do. Knowing Lord Soma is there, you wouldn’t have sent only them. You must have sent someone very, very frightening. Because you have to shake my heart somehow by killing the person I cherish most or taking him hostage. This matter is very important to you, isn’t it?”

The power of the years contained in those fine wrinkles, and even the threats filled with the energy of darkness, could not bear the weight of this heart.

“That’s why I left Lord Soma there, and no one else.”

His trust in Geugak Soma was absolute.

“To make sure that sort of trick would never work.”

Chapter 792: Just Open Your Eyes!
---------------------------------
The Fiendish Little Demon, who had flown in from afar in a single bound, descended before Ian.

“Thank you for saving our lives.”

Not only Ian, but all the warriors behind him bowed their heads respectfully.

If the one clad in the Divine Mechanism Iron Armor had launched his hidden weapons, many would have died.

As if it were nothing, the Fiendish Little Demon clasped his hands behind his back and gazed at the Bugwiseon floating far out on the river.

The aura of darkness emanating from that place was growing stronger and stronger. It was likely because of that dark aura that Mabul and Chwima had hurriedly broken through the barrier and entered.

The Fiendish Little Demon wanted to tear through the barrier and board the ship at once. He trusted the Young Cult Leader more than anyone, but the Lord of the Dark Palace was pouring out everything he had in a final desperate struggle. Even here, an ambush they had never anticipated had been waiting for them.

The Fiendish Little Demon said to Jangho and Ian,

“Leave only those who must remain. Have the rest of your men go to the safe house and wait.”

“Yes, we will.”

The practical combat experience their subordinates had gained on this excursion was enough.

Ian left Cha Iran and Cheongmyeon behind. She had Cha Iran stay because she would be of great help in this fight, and Cheongmyeon because of the Fiendish Little Demon. She thought Cheongmyeon would want to remain.

“Squad Leader Lee and Squad Leader Sa, take the men to the safe house and wait there.”

“Yes, understood.”

“And make sure you collect every hidden weapon. Especially that Divine Mechanism Iron Armor—secure it properly.”

Seojin and Jihan gathered up all the hidden weapons left by the corpses along with their men. Fortunately, because the Fiendish Little Demon had pierced the man’s eye and killed him in a single move, they were able to obtain the Divine Mechanism Iron Armor intact.

These hidden weapons of the Iron Gate would be of great help to the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult’s forge in many ways.

Jangho sent all the Demon Troops back. The tactical shields were reassembled into the walls of the carriages, and the gaping hole left by the shattered shield seemed to show what would have happened had this fight gone wrong.

And so, after all their subordinates had departed for the safe house, only Ian, Jangho, Cha Iran, and Cheongmyeon remained there.

Ian felt the Fiendish Little Demon’s consideration.

He could have sent everyone to the safe house. He could have said they had done enough. Then he would have been free of the responsibility of having to protect them.

But the Fiendish Little Demon was giving her another opportunity. To remain on the battlefield and gain more experience.

Needless to say, there was much to gain from fighting directly, but even standing here and staring at the Bugwiseon filled with that aura of darkness would surely teach her something.

It was a consideration only someone truly strong could offer, and it also showed just how deep his relationship with Geom Mugeuk was.

‘Thank you, Lord Soma.’

Just then, Cheongmyeon approached the Fiendish Little Demon.

During the journey from leaving the cult to arriving here, he had not had a chance to speak with him privately. Even if he had, it would have been difficult to dare approach him.

But after the man had watched over the warriors of the Ghost Shadow Unit from behind like this, he could not simply remain still.

“Thank you for protecting us.”

At his greeting, the Fiendish Little Demon, still gazing at the Bugwiseon, asked him a single question he had not asked even once while they had traveled together.

“Have you been well?”

When the Fiendish Little Demon asked after him, emotion welled up in Cheongmyeon’s chest.

“Yes, I have been well.”

He had left for a new life, but how could he not miss the Valley of Villains? And among them, the person he missed most was the Fiendish Little Demon.

Then astonishing words suddenly slipped from the Fiendish Little Demon’s mouth.

“If you want to come back, you may.”

“!”

Behind his mask, Cheongmyeon’s eyes went wide. Even when the ambusher had suddenly appeared during the earlier fight and thrust a hidden weapon toward his face, he had likely not been this shocked.

The Fiendish Little Demon turned to look at Cheongmyeon. His gaze seemed to be asking,

You thought a new life would be nothing but good, but now that it’s become reality, isn’t it no different?

Unable to figure out how he should answer, Cheongmyeon silently lowered his head. As much as he was satisfied with his current life, he also missed his days as the Faceless Guest.

Then the Fiendish Little Demon abruptly said,

“Come out.”

Startled, Cheongmyeon raised his head. He thought the man was telling him to leave the Ghost Shadow Unit, but that was not it.

The Fiendish Little Demon’s gaze was fixed beyond Cheongmyeon’s shoulder, toward the rear.

Srrrk.

Far off on the ground, a black circle appeared, and a man rose smoothly out of it.

It was Hoe, the limb of the Lord of the Dark Palace.

No emotion could be felt from him. There was no hostility, no anger, no killing intent. That indifferent gaze made him seem all the more dangerous.

With Hoe’s appearance, the Fiendish Little Demon was able to grasp one fact.

From their perspective, this was the moment to concentrate their forces on that Bugwiseon, where the Young Cult Leader and several Demon Venerables had entered. Yet he had come here instead?

“You’re after Unit Leader Lee.”

When Hoe gave no answer, Cha Iran looked at Ian and replied in his stead.

“If he is an enemy, it is only natural to aim for the opponent’s heart.”

To tease her about the heart in a situation like this? Cha Iran was clearly more at ease than she was.

“The Young Cult Leader had to go and call me the heart for no reason.”

Though her mouth spoke in jest, Ian’s eyes gleamed sharply.

“Still, thanks to that, my heart has become a little stronger.”

After experiencing the earlier fight in a state of selflessness, she had gained a certain indescribable confidence.

And that confidence gave rise to a desire to fight more.

Hoe looked at Cha Iran.

“Wasn’t that originally the heart you were supposed to target?”

At his question, which rebuked her betrayal, Cha Iran readily admitted it.

“You seek loyalty from an assassin?”

Cha Iran did not know much about the Lord of the Dark Palace’s direct subordinates. The twelve who would later become the Twelve Zodiac Kings had been managed separately.

But even if she knew no other warrior, she knew Hoe well. He was the one who had gone back and forth between the Lord of the Dark Palace and them, delivering orders.

The subordinate most trusted by the Lord of the Dark Palace.

He had personally stepped forward.

“It seems the situation is desperate enough that you had to come in person.”

Hoe neither affirmed nor denied it.

His gaze turned toward the Fiendish Little Demon.

“What you said earlier was wrong.”

The reason he had come was not Ian.

“I came because of you.”

The instant those words ended,

a black circle had already formed beneath the Fiendish Little Demon’s feet.

Suuuuuk.

In an instant, the Fiendish Little Demon was dragged into the darkness.

It was not a power that could be blocked with movement techniques or inner force. To others, it looked as if he had fallen downward, but to the Fiendish Little Demon, the outside world flipped over in a flash, and in the next moment, he was standing somewhere else.

Darkness.

It was truly pitch-black darkness, without a single handful of light. Even the Fiendish Little Demon’s white mask was swallowed by the darkness and did not show at all.

Hoe’s voice came from within the dark.

“You will have to be trapped here for the rest of your life.”

The Eternal Dark Prison, one of the Dark Palace’s exclusive demonic arts and a powerful technique, had been unleashed.

The Eternal Dark Prison was a demonic art that came with sacrifice and danger.

In exchange for imprisoning someone, the caster had to be trapped there with them. And only after the one he had imprisoned died could he leave this demonic art.

Of course, the opponent would try to find and kill him, but in this absolute darkness, they could not discover him. The darkness was on his side, and it was no ordinary darkness.

“In the end, they all go mad and die, unable to endure the darkness and solitude.”

Even at those terrifying words, the Fiendish Little Demon was not shaken in the slightest.

Rather, behind the mask, his two eyes were smiling.

The Fiendish Little Demon had trained his entire life while staring only at a white wall.

What that life had touched upon was emptiness and nothingness.

And so, words Hoe could never have imagined flowed from the Fiendish Little Demon.

“It is comfortable here.”

* * *

Chwima and Mabul were watching the black energy slowly pressing in from the end of the corridor.

“That is the aura unleashed by the Greedy Heart Great Annihilation Art. If we are exposed to that energy, we won’t be able to use our strength fully. Let us go around.”

At Mabul’s suggestion that they turn back and go around, Chwima proposed a frontal breakthrough.

“Let’s just push through.”

There was a reason Chwima made that choice.

“The Young Cult Leader would not have avoided that energy.”

Mabul nodded. Yes, if it were Geom Mugeuk, he would have pierced through that dark energy to find the Lord of the Dark Palace.

“Very well, let us break through.”

In truth, Mabul had suggested going around partly out of consideration for Chwima.

“I can endure that energy, but will you be all right?”

“I’ll try to hold out with my drunken energy.”

As the two walked into the black fog, a foul energy assaulted them.

Golden radiance burst from Mabul’s body, protecting him.

As for Chwima, a potent alcoholic energy strong enough to intoxicate and collapse someone with just its smell wrapped around his body and protected him.

Despite the nature of their martial arts giving them strong compatibility against dark demonic arts, they could not perfectly shield their bodies.

“It really is vicious.”

The dark energy pierced through the alcoholic energy and seeped in. Chwima’s body grew heavy, as if he were suffering from a terrible fever.

“This is the first time in my life I have encountered dark demonic energy this powerful. If not for the Cult Leader’s Heavenly Demon Body-Protecting Art, I would not be able to withstand this energy in its entirety.”

The golden light emanating from Mabul’s body grew even more intense, and Chwima’s alcoholic energy also became thicker.

“Conversely, the dark demonic arts they unleash will display far greater power than usual.”

As they moved forward, they came upon a fork in the path.

“Which way do you think it is?”

“The Young Cult Leader surely headed toward the side where the energy grows stronger.”

Chwima was certain. Had he only ever drunk with Geom Mugeuk? He knew what kind of person Geom Mugeuk was, and what kind of choice he would make.

Mabul sensed the energy within the fog for a moment.

“Then it is this way.”

The two headed toward the side where the energy was stronger.

As they continued on, they reached a dead end, where there was a large door.

Chwima and Mabul looked at each other and nodded. After exchanging glances telling each other to be careful, they opened the door and stepped inside.

It was an enormous space. After moving only through the narrow corridors of the cabins, encountering such a place so suddenly was truly unexpected. The ceiling was so high that they had to crane their heads back for quite some time to look up at it.

It looked like a storage hold on the ship, with a few large crates and nets placed around. Ahead, there was a door leading out to the other side. Fortunately, the fog had not yet entered this storage hold.

“Now I feel like I can breathe. Let’s rest for a moment before we go.”

Mabul, who had been tense the whole time, also took a breath.

While resting briefly and looking around, Chwima discovered something on one wall.

“Look over there. There’s a painting on the wall.”

It was a painting of a man standing with his eyes closed. Naturally, it looked bizarre. On that vast wall, only a single person had been painted.

“Somehow, it feels unpleasant.”

Mabul was displeased by the fact that there was a painting in a space like this, but Chwima found it unpleasant in a more specific way.

“This bastard looks unpleasant right from the face. Even having his eyes closed looks arrogant.”

Chwima drew up his inner force and walked toward the painting. From behind him, Mabul’s voice rang out.

“Whoever painted it, they painted it well.”

Yes, it was probably because it had been painted too well that it felt this unpleasant. The figure in the painting had been rendered so vividly that it looked as though he might move at any moment.

“I doubt a boatman could have painted this well.”

“Perhaps a painter fell into gambling.”

“Somehow, I feel like this man is going to open his eyes.”

Like a drunkard, Chwima picked a fight with the painting.

“You, just try opening your eyes. I’ll stab you right through.”

Mabul might have said something at that, but he said nothing.

At the ominous feeling that arose for no reason, Chwima suddenly whipped around.

“!”

In that instant, Chwima’s expression hardened. A scene unfolded before him that truly sobered him up at once.

Mabul had become a painting inside the wall and was looking around at his own body and surroundings in bewilderment.

“I must have drunk too much. Lord Mabul is inside the wall.”

The moment those words ended, Chwima spun around like a thunderbolt and thrust his dagger at the painting that had earlier had its eyes closed.

Swish.

Thuk!

Chwima’s dagger lodged in the painting.

The place where the dagger struck was the chest of the man with his eyes closed. Though the dagger was embedded there, the painting did not move at all. What stained the dagger was not blood, but paint.

‘Is it not this bastard?’

As if!

At that moment, the man’s eyes snapped open.

Thuk! Thuk! Thuuuk!

Without a single shred of hesitation, Chwima stabbed repeatedly with the dagger.

But the man in the painting merely smiled at Chwima, as if to say it would be useless no matter how many times he stabbed him.

“You son of a bitch!”

The man moved within the wall. The area of the man’s chest where the dagger had been embedded had turned white.

Scritch, scritch, scritch.

At that moment, color filled the place, as if an invisible brush were painting over it.

He was the secret weapon who had been with the Dark Palace Lord earlier—the Dark Painting Spirit.

“So you can’t kill him from outside, is that it?”

The Dark Painting Spirit raced along the wall toward the opposite side, where Demon Buddha was. His movement was truly fast.

“Be careful, Lord Demon Buddha!”

Demon Buddha also looked at Drunken Demon and said something. But his mouth only moved; Demon Buddha’s voice could not be heard.

Demon Buddha unleashed a palm strike at the Dark Painting Spirit charging toward him.

Even the flying palm force was rendered as a painting.

Then the Dark Painting Spirit also released his palm force.

The two palm forces, turned into images as they flew, met in the middle and exploded.

Even the explosion was depicted as a painting. From outside, it looked utterly ridiculous, but Drunken Demon knew. If this was a dark sorcery powerful enough to draw Demon Buddha in an instant—

‘If he dies in there, Lord Demon Buddha dies too!’

The problem was that this space was overwhelmingly advantageous to that bastard.

Drunken Demon pounded on the wall, trying to enter the painting himself, but he could not get in. The wall was merely a wall.

He wanted nothing more than to smash the entire wall to pieces, but with Demon Buddha inside, he could not recklessly destroy it.

“Kill that bastard and come out! I’m counting on you, Lord Demon Buddha!”

Right now, the only thing he could trust was Demon Buddha’s skill.

And Demon Buddha splendidly lived up to his expectations. While engaging the bastard in close combat, Demon Buddha did not miss his chance and completed a hand seal.

Golden Great Lama Art!

Slaughtering Dharma Seal!

Golden astral energy surged from Demon Buddha’s hands and shot forth. The golden astral energy spreading like waves looked magnificent even as a painting.

“That’s it!”

Drunken Demon, watching, cheered. Demon Buddha had completed the hand seal in an instant at close range, so there was no way the bastard could evade it.

At that very moment, something unbelievable happened.

Demon Buddha’s movements stopped dead. The golden astral energy he had fired also froze in midair. The Dark Painting Spirit, who had been trying to dodge, stopped moving as well.

All of them had become a painting.

And then something even more shocking followed.

Swish, swish, swish.

Something began to be drawn between Demon Buddha and the Dark Painting Spirit.

“You crazy bastard! That’s cheating!”

A pitch-black hole was drawn directly in front of the flying astral energy. It looked like a passage leading somewhere else.

Drunken Demon’s eyes widened in shock. A terrible premonition surged up within him. The very instant the corners of the Dark Painting Spirit’s mouth, which had been frozen like a painting, curled upward—

“Don’t tell me you’re—?”

The next moment, the painting began to move again. The astral energy Demon Buddha had unleashed entered the dark passage drawn right in front of it.

At the same time, pitch-black darkness appeared before Drunken Demon.

Shwaaaaaaaaaak!

The Slaughtering Dharma Seal poured down upon Drunken Demon.

Chapter 793: I Leave the Young Cult Leader to You
-------------------------------------------------
Mabul was not merely flustered; he was dumbfounded.

It was shocking enough that he had entered the wall, but now he had become a painting.

He had once heard of a Dark Painting Art, a sorcery that used paintings to cast evil spells. But this was the first time he had experienced it himself.

Mabul looked around. A white space, empty as an artist’s canvas. Everything was so utterly white that even his sense of distance grew dull.

And from here, he could see outside. There was a transparent membrane between where he stood and the outside world, but even when he pressed it with his hand, it would not give.

Thud.

He struck out with a palm to break through and escape, but the force vanished straight into the transparent membrane. He could tell by feel. Unless he found the method to undo it, there was no way he could break through this membrane by force.

Outside, he saw Chwima stab the man with closed eyes with a dagger.

But it did not work, and he saw the painting of the man inside the wall open its eyes and charge toward him.

He could see Chwima in the outside world saying something, but he could not hear the sound. He could only see with his eyes; this was a space thoroughly separated from that place.

After clashing once with the bastard, he completed his hand seal.

Mabul was certain he could dispose of him with this one move.

There was no way he could avoid the Killing Dharma Seal unleashed from such close range.

And in the next instant!

Mabul was startled. His body stopped. The Killing Dharma Seal he had unleashed also stopped in midair. In that moment, everything had truly become a painting.

The only fortunate thing was that his opponent had stopped as well. As if he, too, retained his consciousness just like Mabul, the man’s eyes looked toward him and smiled.

Ssk, ssk, ssk.

A pitch-black hole was drawn in front of Mabul’s astral force.

Once the painting was complete, his astral force vanished into the passage of darkness.

Then something even more astonishing unfolded.

That Killing Dharma Seal attacked Chwima outside.

“No!”

Chwima failed to dodge in time and was swept up by the astral force, sent flying all the way into the far wall.

Mabul stared out of the painting with a shocked face. Meanwhile, Amheuk Hwaryeong smiled playfully, enjoying the situation.

Fortunately, he saw Chwima spring back to his feet. It seemed he had raised his protective art to its limit and prepared himself.

Mabul and Chwima’s eyes met.

When Chwima nodded with an expression that said he was fine, Mabul felt relieved.

‘This isn’t good.’

What if his attacks flew toward Chwima?

In that case, he could not use any decisive grand techniques at critical moments. This fight was far too disadvantageous.

Instead of using the Golden Great Lama Art, Mabul resolved to face him with ordinary attacks.

‘I’ll kill him with basic skill!’

Mabul overwhelmed him in terms of ability. If this had not been an unfavorable fight inside a painting, the bastard would already have fallen as a corpse.
