But the bastard was not only capable of drawing dark passages that spat attacks into the outside world.

His way of fighting was utterly unique.

Saaak!

A black line was drawn as if a brush had swept across the air.

Mabul twisted his body and dodged. What brushed past him was black sword energy.

Saaak, saaaak.

As if an artist were painting orchids across a canvas, sword energy cut cleanly through the space.

As Mabul retreated to avoid the attacks, prayer beads shot from his hand.

Swish, swish, swish, swish, swish, swish, swish!

It was a broad attack that had even anticipated where he would dodge.

Drop.

Between the two of them, a dot formed as though a single drop of ink had fallen, and then ink began spreading outward from that point.

All the flying prayer beads were sucked into the ink.

His opponent attacked using paintings. And above all, this space was his canvas.

‘On top of that, the Greed-Heart Great Annihilation Art is spread throughout this entire ship, making this man’s sorcery several times stronger!’

As it fed on the gamblers’ intense greed, the energy of darkness was growing even stronger.

At that moment, Mabul looked up at a chilling sensation.

Before he knew it, red astral force had intertwined into a strange shape and was floating in the air.

Whoooooosh.

Boom!

The red astral force slammed straight down.

Mabul threw himself aside to avoid it and realized what that red astral force was. It was a seal mark stamped onto a painting.

Boom! Booooom!

The seal marks fell one after another, and Mabul used dazzling footwork to slip out of their attacks.

Because they were manifested through painting, they looked playful and grotesque, but if they actually struck him, they were powerful attacks that would inflict serious injury.

Mabul dodged his opponent’s attacks and surged forward. Just as he rushed right up to him and was about to tear out his throat—

Once again, Mabul stopped like a painting. His opponent had stopped as well and was looking at Mabul, smiling.

Ssk, ssk, ssk.

As the painting was drawn, a tremendous wind suddenly swept in and blew Mabul away.

Whoooooosh.

The distance between him and the bastard widened again.

“Damn it!”

This fight was more troublesome than any fight he had ever fought.

“I’d heard all kinds of rumors about how amazing the Eight Demon Venerables were, but you’re nothing special, are you?”

At Amheuk Hwaryeong’s mockery, Mabul calmly replied.

“I’ve never had much interest in paintings. If I’d known this would happen, I should’ve painted instead of sculpting.”

It was a situation that could have made him impatient, but Mabul remained composed. If he lost his temper at a time like this, he would truly lose.

“Mabul, you will remain with me forever.”

With those cryptic words, Amheuk Hwaryeong took something from his robes.

Several scrolls emerged.

When he unfurled one of them, the figure of a martial artist appeared. It was drawn only in ink, with neither his complexion nor the color of his clothes filled in.

The expression on his outstretched face was engulfed in rage and despair.

One could feel that it was the final moment before his death.

The martial artist inside another scroll was the same. This time, the image of him begging for his life was captured exactly as it was.

Amheuk Hwaryeong took out a blank scroll. Even its border was more splendid than the others; it was a golden scroll.

“I’ll put you in this one specially.”

“Make sure you draw me more handsome than I am in real life.”

When Mabul continued to act at ease, a sneer formed at the corner of Amheuk Hwaryeong’s mouth.

“You’re relying on that drunkard over there.”

Amheuk Hwaryeong looked at Chwima outside the painting. Mabul looked that way as well.

Beyond the transparent membrane, he could see Chwima. He was smashing the boxes in that place. It seemed he was searching for the method to break the spell.

Judging by Amheuk Hwaryeong’s relaxed expression, the method was not there.

“Aren’t you curious what the method to break it is?”

Mabul looked at Chwima and said,

“He’ll find it. He’s smarter than he looks.”

Before fighting again, Mabul asked as if something had been bothering him.

“But why did you choose me instead of him?”

He had seemed relaxed enough to answer anything, but he did not answer that question.

“You’ll hear all about it later inside this scroll.”

* * *

“There is definitely a way to break it.”

Chwima smashed every box in that place to pieces. He had thought there might be some clue inside that could break the spell.

There was nothing inside.

Chwima knew. The more splendid and powerful a sorcery was, the simpler and closer at hand its method of undoing tended to be.

‘Think! You drunkard, use your head!’

But no matter how much he looked around, there was nothing that seemed like it could be the key.

This time, he ran to the wall and placed his hand against it. He searched to see whether anything was inside, and he even tried injecting internal energy into it.

But there was nothing strange about this wall, which had become the stage of the fight.

Chwima looked at Mabul, who was fighting on the opposite wall.

Even in this moment, Mabul was trying to avoid incoming palm force when his entire body was suddenly trapped by thorny vines.

The instant Chwima saw him, his heart sank.

It was not because Mabul had taken a palm strike due to the thorny vines.

The color of his body had grown fainter than before. Not only was the golden radiance losing its light, but even the color of his clothes was gradually fading.

By instinct, Chwima felt that once Mabul lost all his color, he would die.

‘As time passes, he’s becoming a real painting.’

Chwima grew desperate. He flew up to the ceiling and examined it. He hoped something might be hidden there, but there was nothing strange about the ceiling either.

In the end, Chwima carried out what he had been hesitating over.

Boom.

Unable to find any other method, Chwima broke part of the wall.

The moment a hole appeared in the wall—

An earthquake struck the ground where Mabul stood, and the floor shook beneath his feet. Enormous boulders poured down from overhead. As Mabul threw himself aside to avoid them, fireballs rained down on him.

Seeing Mabul barely escape, Chwima swept a hand over his chest.

He had confirmed that he must not forcefully break the wall.

Mabul did not give up and continued fighting.

But whenever his attacks were about to land properly, he would become a painting and stop.

A wall was erected in front of Mabul’s fist, and even steel spikes were embedded in that wall. Those spikes were drawn sharply right in front of Mabul’s fist.

Chwima ran over and shouted at Amheuk Hwaryeong, who had stopped along with him.

“You cowardly bastard! Enough!”

Chwima raised his fist high. He intended to blow away the entire section where the bastard was drawn.

But he could not.

Even making a hole in a distant wall had caused that chaos; the moment he struck this bastard, he felt everything would be over.

‘It’s a trap.’

If he did that, Mabul might never be able to escape.

Once the painting was drawn, the two moved again.

Unable to stop the force of his charge, Mabul’s fist shattered the steel spikes and broke the wall behind them. Though it was a blow infused with internal energy, Mabul’s skin tore and blood flowed down.

That man, too, had to be consuming a tremendous amount of internal energy each time he painted. Yet the reason he could endure like this was—

‘The Greed-Heart Great Annihilation Art is sustaining him.’

Meanwhile, the color drained completely from Mabul’s legs, turning them ink-black.

Then Mabul could no longer move.

Chwima ran in front of Mabul. Chwima’s grief and rage were transmitted even beyond the membrane.

Mabul, on the other hand, said something with calm eyes.

Though Chwima could not hear the sound, he understood. Just from Mabul’s expression, he could tell what he was saying.

“Leave this place.”

And one more sentence followed.

“Take care of the Young Cult Leader.”

Mabul’s expression looked peaceful, like an enlightened high monk. Since meeting Mabul, had Chwima ever seen such a peaceful face on him?

Mabul brought his hands together in prayer. He meant to end his final moment with that peaceful gesture.

‘Eldest Young Master, I am sorry I could not serve you to the end.’

Chwima had never felt such despair before.

He truly had not imagined he would lose Mabul like this.

And in that moment, Chwima thought of one person.

‘I’m sorry, Mugeuk.’

How good would it have been if Geom Mugeuk had been here?

If it were Mugeuk, he would surely have found the method to break it. And after smashing that bastard to pieces, he would have said this: Even if I painted with my foot, I’d paint better than you.

‘Why Mabul and not me? If only it had been me instead!’

In the next instant, Chwima flinched.

‘That’s right. Why was it Mabul? I was the one who cursed at the painting, too.’

The next instant!

A thought flashed through Chwima’s mind like a bolt of lightning.

‘Don’t tell me?’

Chwima pounded on the wall and shouted.

“Attack one last time! With the strongest attack you have, Mabul!”

Since his words would not be heard, Chwima mimed forming hand seals in front of Mabul.

Mabul’s eyes widened in surprise.

Attack?

Chwima raised his palm high, then mimed striking it down to the floor.

Mabul understood which technique he was telling him to use.

‘But that move will fall on you.’

Chwima looked at him and nodded.

‘Trust me and do it.’

What filled Chwima’s eyes was not a plea for him to struggle at least once before death.

Mabul’s lower body could not move, and the ink-black color was gradually spreading up to his upper body.

Now there was only one chance.

His hands came apart from prayer, and Mabul began forming hand seals with both hands. Drawing up every last bit of internal energy, he unleashed a single move.

Golden Great Lama Art.

Demon Palm Annihilating Seal!

The strongest move of the Golden Great Lama Art.

Amheuk Hwaryeong wore an expression that said, Go ahead and try.

Whooooooooong!

The enormous palm of a Buddha statue emitted golden radiance and struck down at Amheuk Hwaryeong.

In the next instant, that enormous palm stopped above Amheuk Hwaryeong’s head.

Ssk, ssk, ssk.

In the meantime, a passage of darkness began to be drawn once more.

The Dark Painting Spirit was already smiling brightly before the movement even came to a stop. The Drunken Demon would never be able to withstand this powerful technique.

It was at that very moment. The Drunken Demon gulped down liquor.

Then he spat it at the pitch-black hole being drawn between the palm and the head.

Splashhhhh.

It was no ordinary liquor. It was powerful wine qi imbued with the Drunken Demon’s internal energy.

“Nothing beats liquor when it comes to erasing a painting!”

“!”

The wine qi soaked fully into the painting, and as the Drunken Demon dashed left and right, he wiped it away with his sleeve. The pitch-black hole was erased by the Drunken Demon’s liquor.

At the very instant shock filled the Dark Painting Spirit’s eyes.

‘No!’

The world within the painting began to move again.

The enormous palm struck the Dark Painting Spirit down.

Kuwaaaaaang!

The Dark Painting Spirit tried to withstand the Demon Palm Annihilation crushing down on him, but it was nothing more than a mantis opposing a cartwheel.

Crack-crack-crack-crack.

His body was shattered to pieces by the enormous palm-shaped gang qi.

“Uaaaaaaaaaagh!”

Kuuuuung!

A moment later, when the Demon Palm Annihilation that had struck the floor vanished, only a trace remained in the huge handprint.

Only stains of paint in every color, flattened and spread out in all directions.

The next moment.

Sssssssss.

The lower half of the Demon Buddha, which had turned ink-black, began returning to its original color.

Just as it regained its color completely and seemed to change from a painting back into a person.

Thud.

The wall spat the Demon Buddha out.

Once the Demon Buddha emerged safely from the wall, the tension left the Drunken Demon, and he plopped down where he stood.

The Demon Buddha also let out a sigh and sat down beside him.

For a while, neither of them said anything. They did not say that they had suffered greatly, nor did they say thank you. They had seen each other’s hearts without needing words.

The heart of the Demon Buddha, who had told him to go on ahead.

And the heart of the Drunken Demon, who had stayed until the end to protect the Demon Buddha.

The Drunken Demon handed the wine flask at his waist to the Demon Buddha.

The Demon Buddha looked down at the flask in his hand for a moment, then returned it to the Drunken Demon.

“Save this precious liquor and drink it yourself.”

Only now could the Demon Buddha understand clearly.

Why the enemy had chosen him instead of the Drunken Demon.

The method to break this monstrous Dark Painting Art was far too simple. It was water.

Since the opponent was a painting, realizing that he had to be erased was itself the method of breaking it. Perhaps if even a single drop of water had touched that fellow’s body, this evil art would have been undone. If there was no water, one would have to use one’s own blood.

The Drunken Demon had liquor, and above all, the wine qi that protected him was, in the end, made of water.

From the beginning, that bastard could not have fought the Drunken Demon. In terms of compatibility, he was far too weak against the Drunken Demon. Perhaps after killing him, he would never have dealt with the Drunken Demon at all.

After circulating their qi and recovering their internal energy, the two opened the door at the end of that space.

Beyond the door, an even deeper darkness awaited them. The Demon Buddha revealed a golden radiance and stepped in first.

“When we return, I’ll buy you a drink of good liquor.”

Following him in, the Drunken Demon smiled and said,

“I’ll be looking forward to it.”

It would be the first drink he had ever gotten from the Demon Buddha.

Chapter 794: Please Help My Friend’s Father
-------------------------------------------
Perfect darkness in which nothing could be seen.

The Eternal Dark Prison.

Ordinarily, when trapped here, one would grope through the darkness, trying by any means to find a way to break it—how vast this space was, how to escape, whether someone else might be here.

But Geugak Soma did not.

He stood in the darkness. As if he could see something before him, he opened his eyes wide and stared into the dark. The sight was truly bizarre.

But the initiative was still entirely his.

‘He won’t collapse easily, but in the end, he will.’

The darkness of this place drove people mad. It burrowed into the human heart and unearthed every negative emotion within.

Fear and anxiety, helplessness, hatred, sorrow, guilt, weakness…….

Hoe had experienced many times just how easily, and how quickly, a human being could crumble.

Soon he would rage, and laugh like a madman. This was the Eternal Dark Prison, a place where even an iron-walled martial artist who had never shed a single tear in his life would wail in grief.

‘Now, collapse, Geugak Soma!’

As Hoe drew up even more inner force, the darkness grew thicker.

Yet when there was still no disturbance, Hoe finally provoked Geugak Soma himself.

“Do you know why I trapped you instead of Ian?”

His voice echoed from every direction, making it impossible to tell where he was speaking from.

“Because I judged that your death would be a greater shock to the Young Cult Leader than Ian’s.”

Hoe was certain that what could shake Geugak Soma’s heart the most was the safety of the Young Cult Leader.

Indeed, when Geom Mugeuk was mentioned, the silent Geugak Soma finally opened his mouth.

“You still know nothing about the Young Cult Leader.”

“What do you mean?”

After a brief pause, Geugak Soma said,

“Even if all of us die, the Young Cult Leader will not waver.”

“!”

“He will probably become even colder.”

Hoe could feel that Geugak Soma truly believed that.

“That is why, in the earlier fight, the one who should have protected Squad Leader Lee was not me, but you. With the resolve that if that woman were injured or killed, we would truly all die.”

Geugak Soma’s words, delivered through the darkness, made Hoe’s heart sink.

“Then your death will be a death without meaning.”

“Why seek meaning in death? Meaning we never even sought in life.”

Hoe’s provocations and goading had no effect at all.

This time, Geugak Soma asked,

“What exactly have you prepared on that ship?”

Hoe understood. The reason this taciturn man had conversed with him was to ask this very question. To learn anything he could from him and tell Geom Mugeuk.

“You are mad for the Young Cult Leader.”

If he had had such a person as a subordinate….

“What you said was wrong. If the Young Cult Leader loses someone like you, he will go mad and run wild.”

The aura of darkness grew even stronger. Stronger, and stronger still. Hoe poured in the utmost internal power he could muster.

But instead, the two eyes behind Geugak Soma’s mask were smiling.

“Thank you for bringing me to a place like this.”

With those incomprehensible words, Geugak Soma drew his dagger.

Even the blade of the dagger did not reflect in this darkness.

But when Geugak Soma drew the dagger through the air—

Swish.

A pure white sword line cut through the darkness.

Naturally, it should have vanished into the dark, yet the sword line remained as it was.

The darkness surged in to erase that line, but the mark left by Geugak Soma’s dagger did not disappear.

“!”

Hoe was startled. This had never happened before.

Something even more astonishing followed.

Geugak Soma began to perform the Sohyang Dagger Art within the darkness.

The lines forming in the air were unique and extraordinary trajectories, ones that only the highest level of martial learning could leave behind.

And astonishingly, the traces left by the dagger remained in the darkness, shining with pure white light.

Srrrrrr.

No matter how the darkness swarmed in and tried to erase those lines with darkness, they would not be wiped away.

Hoe could not understand this phenomenon at all.

“How in the world did you do that?”

Geugak Soma’s training in the pure white room had been training to cultivate extreme concentration within tranquility of mind. It was training to deeply contemplate good and evil, and further, to comprehend the principles of martial arts.

When he entered the Eternal Dark Prison, Geugak Soma realized it.

His pure white room and this room of darkness were, in the end, the same room.

For what Geugak Soma had been looking at in his white room was not light, but darkness.

Because he had stood there alone, looking upon his own life as he lived the life of absolute evil.

That was why this place felt comfortable. As if he had entered his own room.

Just as the Dark Flower Spirit could not deal with the Drunken Demon, the Eternal Dark Prison could not hold Geugak Soma. He overwhelmed this dark demonic art, and this room became his own.

Geugak Soma was engraving light into the darkness with the Sohyang Dagger Art.

And after displaying every technique, he slowly observed the trajectories.

Where else would he have the chance to see, so vividly with his own eyes, every trajectory of the martial techniques he had unfolded?

It was something possible only when Geugak Soma and the Eternal Dark Prison met.

And that was not all.

Geugak Soma stepped to the side and began to display the Sohyang Dagger Art again.

This time, too, the darkness tried to erase the traces of the techniques he left behind, but the pure white sword lines did not disappear.

Geugak Soma examined the Sohyang Dagger Art he had just performed again, comparing it to what he had displayed before.

“You madman! Are you training in martial arts inside my Eternal Dark Prison right now?”

Hoe’s agitated voice rang out. By oath, he had never once imagined that something like this could happen within his unique demonic art.

He nearly suffered internal injuries from his blood and qi flowing backward. This was a different matter from the aura of darkness simply not working.

“Stop it! You lunatic!”

But Geugak Soma did not stop. As if this were a fated opportunity that had come to him only once in his life, he unfolded the Sohyang Dagger Art again. As if he intended to complete the Sohyang Dagger Art here today before leaving.

The agitated Hoe shouted,

“Are you not worried about the Young Cult Leader? What would he say if he learned you were leisurely training in martial arts here?”

The two eyes behind Geugak Soma’s mask smiled brightly.

“The Young Cult Leader would be the happiest of all.”

Hoe poured in the last of his inner power and strengthened the dark aura, but just as the moon shines brighter the darker the night, the trajectories left by the Sohyang Dagger Art only grew clearer.

In his agitation, Hoe realized it. The one being affected by this aura of darkness was none other than himself.

‘He was never an enemy I could handle in the first place.’

As countless shining sword lines appeared, the space gradually grew bright.

This training performed alone in the darkness was both martial arts training and the process by which Geugak Soma sought the light.

And when that space was filled with light—

Flash.

For a moment, it seemed everything in all directions became brilliantly bright, and then the two of them were standing where they had originally been.

Blood was gushing from Hoe’s mouth. As the Eternal Dark Prison was broken, he too had suffered a fatal wound.

With the blood vessels throughout his body severed, Hoe gasped painfully for breath.

“……Kill me.”

Geugak Soma quietly looked into Hoe’s suffering eyes. The eyes behind the mask held no hatred or resentment toward the one who had tried to kill him. Rather, they were gentle.

“Your darkness was comfortable to me.”

Shiiik.

Geugak Soma’s dagger cut through the air and freed him from his pain. The last thing Hoe saw was the character for “smile” that Geom Mugeuk had engraved on the dagger.

Ian, Chairan, Cheongmyeon, and Jangho, who had been waiting outside, respectfully paid him their courtesies. Because Geugak Soma had suddenly been dragged into the darkness, all of them had waited in tension.

But Geugak Soma had returned alive and unharmed.

They had already known it, but now they had experienced it firsthand once again.

Just how frightening beings called Demon Venerables were.

Geugak Soma stood by the lakeside and silently gazed at the Wealth-and-Honor Ship.

Ordinarily, it would have been a situation where they should be tense over what might pop out next. But with Geugak Soma beside them, their hearts were at ease.

Ian thought that Geom Mugeuk was truly enviable. How wonderful would it be to have even one person like that by your side as you lived your life?

At that moment, Chairan’s sound transmission flew in.

—Do not be too envious.

How sharp her instincts were.

—I’m not envious. If I had to say, I am someone closer to Lord Soma than to the Young Cult Leader.

—What do you mean by that?

—I mean I am not a vessel capable of holding someone like him. I am the sort of person who would rather follow, and like someone. Even if I were abandoned afterward, that would be easier on my heart.

At that sound transmission, Chairan smiled.

Ian thought this kind of relationship was not bad either. After all, it was no easy thing in life to meet someone to whom you could convey your honest feelings like this.

Yes, so what if the vessel of my heart is only a soy sauce dish? I can live by holding soy sauce. What matters is living without spilling it!

Ian’s gaze turned once more toward the distant Wealth-and-Honor Ship.

‘So, greatest vessel in the world, hurry and come out, and hold all of us little vessels!’

* * *

An urgent report flew into the Murim Alliance Leader’s Hall.

“The Demon Flame has risen from the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.”

At the report from Jegal Hyeon, the Murim Alliance’s Chief Strategist, not only Alliance Leader Jin Paecheon but also Jin Hagun, who was with him, were startled.

Since Geom Ujin had ascended to the position of cult leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, the Demon Flame had not risen even once.

“With the rising of the Demon Flame, every branch and sub-branch of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult has entered an emergency system.”

Jin Paecheon worried that the very thing he had been anxious about until now was occurring. He trusted Geom Mugeuk, but he did not trust Geom Ujin.

Jegal Hyeon knew what the Alliance Leader was worried about.

“Fortunately, there are no signs of war breaking out.”

If so, that meant the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult had fallen into danger.

“There is one more piece of news.”

The added news was even more astonishing than the fact that the Demon Flame had risen.

“After sealing off the main headquarters of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, the Demon Cult Leader and the Demon Venerables came out to Maga Village.”

Jin Paecheon was also the leader who headed the Murim Alliance. How could he not know what that meant?

It meant a powerful enemy was heading toward them—one formidable enough to put the Demon Cult Leader on edge, and even force him to bring the Demon Venerables out with him.

“It is likely related to the appearance of the Heavenly Killing Star this time.”

Naturally, the Murim Alliance also knew about the appearance of the Heavenly Killing Star. They had taken the matter seriously and were observing the murim carefully.

“It seems they have judged that the one born under the Heavenly Killing Star is coming to them.”

Jin Hagun, who had been silently listening to the two men’s conversation, wore a stiff expression. When Geom Mugeuk had left the cult leading the Demon Venerables earlier, he had not been this worried.

But afterward, the appearance of the Heavenly Killing Star had made his heart drop, and now even the Demon Flame had risen.

There had been countless incidents until now involving the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, and Geom Mugeuk as well, but this was the first time he had ever been this worried about them.

‘Mugeuk, are you all right?’

If he followed his heart, he wanted to run there immediately.

“Was there no message from the Divine Cult regarding that matter?”

“There was none.”

Jin Paecheon’s expression hardened.

We raised the Demon Flame for this and that reason; it seems the one born under the Heavenly Killing Star is coming.

How good would it be if they gave even a rough hint? But as always, the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult handled matters independently.

Then again, before Geom Mugeuk appeared, they had been the same. Rather, they had worried that the Divine Cult might learn of important matters.

At the Murim Alliance headquarters, the Heavenly Dragon Decree—the highest level of emergency alert—had already been issued due to Geom Mugeuk’s departure from the cult.

“What do you think we should do?”

“If, as the Divine Cult believes, the one born under the Heavenly Killing Star truly exists, there is no guarantee that he will not come to our alliance.”

Jin Paecheon slowly nodded. Surely, that person was connected to the hidden force against which the orthodox, unorthodox, and demonic factions had fought together until now.

“Please recall all the experts who are outside, and permit the operation of every formation and mechanism within the alliance.”

If they did that, the Murim Alliance as well would become an ironclad fortress and enter a complete wartime system.

“Do so.”

“Yes, I will implement it immediately.”

When Jegal Hyeon left, only Jin Paecheon and Jin Hagun remained there.

Jin Paecheon walked to the large window beside the grand chair and looked outside.

Jin Hagun read one thought from his grandfather’s back.

“Do you wish to go help the Demon Cult Leader?”

Jin Paecheon looked back at his grandson.

“I said it because, somehow, that seemed to be the case.”

Astonishingly, Jin Paecheon had felt the desire to go help Geom Ujin.

Through the tripartite talks, they had promised to face the enemy together, yet knowing he had fallen into danger, he had instead barred the door. Given Jin Paecheon’s nature, it sat ill with him.

The Demon Cult Leader was a man whose very face made him uncomfortable, but he wanted to help him. It was not so much a rational judgment as an emotional impulse.

“If the Demon Cult Leader dies because of this, where do you think the sword that brought down the Demon Cult will point next?”

Even for the sake of the Murim Alliance, it was something he had to do.

“Then why are you hesitating?”

Because of one single possibility.

It was not because he feared the Heavenly Slaughter Star, nor because he was afraid of the disgrace in the jianghu that would come from having aided the Demon Cult.

“What if that Heavenly Slaughter Star comes to our alliance?”

What if, while he was away, the Heavenly Slaughter Star descended upon this place? What if his grandchildren died, and the warriors of the Murim Alliance were killed? It would truly remain an eternal regret.

Then Jin Hagun said something he had not expected.

“Isn’t that the very heart with which you have protected the alliance all your life, Grandfather?”

“!”

“I think it would be all right, just this once, for you to follow where your heart leads.”

Knowing that his grandson was saying it for his sake, Jin Paecheon gently rebuked him.

“That is a rash thought.”

In truth, Jin Hagun had said it because he was certain of one thing.

“The Heavenly Slaughter Star will not come to our alliance.”

At the look asking why, Jin Hagun answered calmly.

“Because if they had judged that to be the case, the Divine Cult would have sent word for us to be careful.”

It was a statement born from his deep faith in Geom Mugeuk and the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

“I think I understand now why the Young Cult Leader came out leading the Demon Venerables without sending word. That bastard intends to fight the final battle without our help. After telling us that if anything happened, we absolutely had to let him know. He’s acting cool all by himself.”

That was why he wanted to run over there at once and shout at him. At the next gathering, start by drinking three bottles as punishment!

“I believe the appearance of the Heavenly Slaughter Star is also a fate that began with that friend. The Heavenly Slaughter Star must surely have headed for the Divine Cult.”

Jin Paecheon silently looked down over the Murim Alliance, where formations were being deployed and mechanisms had begun to operate.

When his grandfather said nothing more, Jin Hagun bowed respectfully and withdrew.

Even after his grandson had left, Jin Paecheon gazed out the window for a long while.

At last, when he turned around, a single sword stood beside the grand preceptor’s chair.

Astonishingly, that sword was the Gentleman Sword, which Jin Hagun had received as a gift from Geom Mugeuk.

Foremost of the ancient swords, the Gentleman Sword.

Jin Paecheon walked over slowly and picked up the Gentleman Sword.

It seemed his grandson had thought he might leave. If by any chance he did go, he must have left it there for him to take. With the belief that if the Gentleman Sword were held in his grandfather’s hand, he would be invincible under heaven.

Perhaps this deeply considerate grandson was asking him a favor.

Grandfather, please help my friend’s father.

Jin Paecheon looked down at the Gentleman Sword with deepened eyes and said,

“This is exactly why I feel like going to that man and boasting about my grandson.”

Chapter 795: Even Now, Troublesome Work
---------------------------------------
Geom Mugeuk continued onward, toward the place where the aura of darkness grew ever stronger.

After his transformation, not only his five senses but even his sixth sense and survival instincts had awakened far beyond what they had been before.

And so, no matter how thick the dark mist became, it could not slow Geom Mugeuk’s steps.

Shhk!

Just then, a sword suddenly shot up from the floor, trying to stab through the sole of Geom Mugeuk’s foot.

Geom Mugeuk instinctively dodged and leaped over it, and swords shot out from both walls as well.

Shhiiik, shhk!

This time too, he evaded lightly and withdrew.

It was not merely the sword blades that had emerged. Arms gripping the swords had come out with them.

And there were not just one or two arms holding swords.

Arms filled the corridor. They jutted out not only from both walls but from the ceiling and floor as well. It was a grotesque hallway packed with sword-wielding arms.

Whoooong!

Geom Mugeuk lightly struck out with a palm, as if to see how they would react.

Like a crab hiding its eyes, the sword-wielding arms vanished into the walls in an instant. Once the palm force passed, the arms holding swords appeared again. They looked like living arms.

Geom Mugeuk could tell. What lay before him was no simple mechanism.

‘This is the work of an expert in dark summoning, among the dark demonic arts.’

Just looking at it was enough to make one’s skin crawl and inspire terror, but Geom Mugeuk actually liked it.

“Seeing how hard you’re trying to stop me from going this way, I guess this is the right path.”

Geom Mugeuk charged forward. It was the sort of reckless rush that made one wonder how on earth he intended to deal with all those countless arms.

Geom Mugeuk answered with his skill.

Shhk! Shhiiik! Shhiiik! Shhk!

Pak! Pak! Papak! Pak!

Each time sword light flashed, an arm was severed. The arms gripping swords were no ordinary things either.

Their swords stabbed and flew at Geom Mugeuk from truly bizarre angles.

They were tricky attacks, coming from directions and angles rarely experienced in battles against people.

Their movements truly seemed to possess the minds of several clever masters.

They had simply met the wrong opponent.

Papapapapapapapak!

Geom Mugeuk broke straight through head-on. The Black Demon Sword moved so quickly that, within the mist, only its sword light flashed, and severed arms flew in every direction.

By the time Geom Mugeuk reached the end of the corridor in an instant—

Thudududududuk.

The severed arms came raining down, filling the corridor floor.

Boom! Boom boom! Boom boom boom boom boom!

The arms turned into black smoke and vanished.

Astonishingly, throughout the entire process, they had failed to tear even a single hem of Geom Mugeuk’s clothing. It was overwhelming martial prowess in the truest sense.

Just then, the door directly ahead at the end of the corridor opened.

Clack.

It was a room where gambling was in full swing.

Geom Mugeuk looked inside. Despite all the chaos outside, the place was still packed with gamblers absorbed in their games. The fervor was even hotter than when Geom Mugeuk had first arrived on this ship.

‘The Great Annihilation Art of Greed is sucking up all their energy.’

They were lost in gambling, unaware that Geom Mugeuk was watching them.

Among the gamblers were some crouched in corners with their heads buried between their knees, and others sprawled out unconscious. Those with weaker bodies had begun to suffer the effects of having their vitality drained first.

At that moment, one person caught Geom Mugeuk’s eye.

The old man watching them gamble was, astonishingly, the Dark Palace Lord. This time too, Geom Mugeuk could tell he was an illusion, just like the ones that had appeared before.

The gamblers, on the other hand, did not even realize that the person observing them from the side was an illusion.

The Dark Palace Lord looked at Geom Mugeuk and said,

“What do you think? Would you not like to test your luck?”

“I have no interest in gambling whatsoever, but I do know one secret.”

“What secret is that?”

“The sure-win secret to always winning at gambling.”

The Dark Palace Lord waited for Geom Mugeuk’s answer with curious eyes.

“The only way to win at gambling is to never step through the door of a gambling den.”

Geom Mugeuk slammed the door shut and continued on his way.

Geom Mugeuk’s voice came from outside the door.

“I won.”

The Dark Palace Lord’s illusion wore an expression of utter disbelief at that action, but the door had already closed. Truly, Geom Mugeuk never once moved as he expected.

Geom Mugeuk was walking quickly down the corridor when the Dark Palace Lord’s illusion appeared in front of him this time.

“What if I become angry and kill them all? Why would you simply leave?”

“So, did you kill them all?”

The Dark Palace Lord gave no answer.

“Right, I thought so. You’re maintaining this grand art by sinking tubes into those people’s desires, so you can’t kill them carelessly. Each and every one of them must be precious, no?”

“I could go back right now and kill everyone in that room.”

“Then kill them.”

When Geom Mugeuk showed such nerve, the Dark Palace Lord stared at him piercingly, trying to discern his true intent. But he still could not reach Geom Mugeuk’s heart.

“Why? Because I protect the people of Maga Village as though they were my own, did you think I’d be frantic to save the gamblers here? If I were that kind of person, do you think I could have made it this far?”

Geom Mugeuk strode forward until he stood before the Dark Palace Lord’s illusion.

Rather, the one probing the truth beyond the illusion was Geom Mugeuk.

“Shall I guess why you appeared before me again?”

Geom Mugeuk stared straight into the Dark Palace Lord’s eyes and asked,

“Things outside the ship didn’t go well, did they? You tried to take Ian hostage or kill him, but failed, didn’t you?”

For an instant, the Dark Palace Lord’s face twitched.

“So you came to test whether you could at least use those gamblers as hostages, and whether that would work on me?”

The Dark Palace Lord neither confirmed nor denied it.

“Or are you trying to keep showing up like this to stall for time? Am I perhaps reaching you much faster than you expected? Why? Do you need more time before you can unleash whatever you’ve prepared?”

From the moment he first boarded this Fortune Ship, Geom Mugeuk had acted outside all expectations.

“I’m going to run from here on.”

Geom Mugeuk passed straight through his illusion and truly began to run.

When the illusion turned around to look, Geom Mugeuk had already vanished around the corner at the end of the corridor.

Geom Mugeuk ran. If, as he had guessed, the Dark Palace Lord was trying to delay his arrival, then arriving even a moment sooner would give him an absolutely advantageous fight.

As he sprinted through the mist like that, Geom Mugeuk’s figure came to a halt.

Something filled the corridor ahead.

Red eyes shining in the darkness.

He could tell by instinct. Though they had human forms, they were monsters summoned through dark demonic arts.

There were not just one or two of them. They packed the corridor tight. The dark aura they exuded was enough by itself to terrify a person.

‘They’re monsters summoned by that expert in dark summoning from before.’

Judging by their momentum, he could tell they were stronger summons than the sword-wielding arms earlier.

“Kraaaaaah!”

The monster at the front let out a hideous roar and charged at Geom Mugeuk.

Shhiiik! Slice!

Following the line of the Black Demon Sword, drawn diagonally with lightning speed, the monster’s body split apart.

And the monster’s attack began from that moment.

Chiiiiiiik.

The black blood that scattered melted the floor and walls.

The Corpse-Dissolving Powder used in the jianghu to dissolve bodies was its blood.

And that was not all. Even though its body had been split lengthwise, it did not fall and continued to rush forward.

Shkshkshkshkshkshk!

The Black Demon Sword moved like lightning and tore it to pieces. Only then did the monster turn into black smoke and vanish.

Chiiiiiiik.

A poisonous smoke rose from the Corpse-Dissolving Powder scattered everywhere.

Monsters that sprayed Corpse-Dissolving Powder were packed tight into the narrow corridor. Once one died, the rest did not charge in; they waited.

It was a situation that would naturally make one worry about how to break through, but Geom Mugeuk did not spare his martial arts.

“If you come at me like this, I have no choice either.”

Sseuk.

A reliable back appeared before Geom Mugeuk’s eyes.

The second form of the Nine Calamities Demonic Art, Great Annihilation Form.

It was a small Great Annihilation Form, split into only two.

But its power remained unchanged. The two evil spirits filled the corridor and pushed straight ahead.

Kwakakakakakakakakakak!

The monsters charged in, trying to endure, but they were not opponents they could withstand. Their entire bodies burst apart.

Puff puff puff puff puff puff puff!

Chiiiiiiik.

Corpse-Dissolving Powder scattered, but to the evil spirits of the Great Annihilation Form, it was no different from being drenched in rain.

When the two evil spirits had pushed all the way to the end of the corridor—

A scream burst out from the end.

“Uaaaaaagh!”

The evil spirits stopped just before sweeping away the one who had screamed as well.

The person trembling as if he might faint was none other than the ship owner, Yang Dong. He had been attending to the distinguished guests absorbed in gambling, but when he heard strange noises outside and opened the door, he came face to face with the evil spirits.

Yang Dong fell flat on his backside.

A terrifying evil spirit looked down at him coldly.

“……Please spare me!”

Ssssst.

As the evil spirits slowly vanished, Geom Mugeuk’s words came from beyond them.

“Do not be afraid. At least for today, they are guardian gods who will protect you.”

* * *

Bi Sain flew up onto the largest giant wolf statue in the inner compound.

The leader of the Evil Dao Alliance, Baek Jagang, stood atop it. From there, the entire view of the Evil Dao Alliance could be seen at a glance.

“I heard that the Demon Flame has risen in the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. Is that true?”

Bi Sain had been carrying out an operation outside when he heard the news and hurried back. He could not say how shocked he had been by it.

Baek Jagang silently nodded.

“The fact that they lit the Demon Flame is not what matters.”

What Baek Jagang was focusing on was this.

“What matters is that Geom Ujin allowed it.”

The Geom Ujin in Baek Jagang’s mind was by no means someone who would light the Demon Flame. He knew very well how proud that man was. If someone like him had sealed off the main headquarters of the Demonic Cult?

“Unless it’s a deception meant to kill us all.”

Baek Jagang’s eyes flashed sharply.

“Then something truly dangerous is heading there.”

Bi Sain’s expression hardened. Naturally, it would not be a deception meant to kill the righteous and evil factions. This matter had occurred after Geom Mugeuk led many Demon Venerables out; if they intended to start a war, that would mean Geom Mugeuk was involved as well, and that could not be.

“If they were truly targeting us, they would not have lit the Demon Flame.”

That was why Bi Sain was worried about Geom Mugeuk.

“Is it because of the Heavenly Slaughter Star?”

How could the Evil Dao Alliance not know what the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult knew and what the Martial Alliance knew? The Evil Dao Alliance had also been keeping a close eye on the appearance of the Heavenly Slaughter Star.

“Most likely. Because of this matter, the Martial Alliance has entered a wartime posture.”

“How will you respond?”

“We’ll have to move while watching both sides.”

Though it was the sort of statement that could wound one’s pride, Baek Jagang said it as if it were nothing.

Bi Sain respected and liked this aspect of his master. His master took greater pride than anyone in walking the path of the Evil Dao and had immense pride as the leader of the Evil Dao Alliance, yet he did not belittle others or exaggerate the Evil Dao Alliance’s strength.

“What would you do?”

“For now, this alliance should also strengthen its vigilance to the minimum necessary level.”

“No, not that. If you were the alliance leader, what would you have done?”

“Are you asking me to assume that Geom Mugeuk was the Cult Leader?”

When Baek Jagang nodded, Bi Sain answered honestly.

“If Mugeuk had lit the Demon Flame, I would have gone to help him.”

“What if he invaded this alliance? Would it be fine if the entire alliance were destroyed because of your friendship?”

At those words, Bi Sain lowered his head and said,

“This disciple let private feelings take precedence and made a foolish judgment without considering the greater cause.”

But it seemed Baek Jagang had not brought it up to reprimand him. Regret passed through his eyes.

“In all my life, I have never had a friend like that.”

Bi Sain looked at his master in surprise. It was something he had never once said before.

“That is because you devoted your entire life to the alliance.”

No, there was another reason. The ability to know when others were lying was, at least when it came to making friends, a curse-like ability.

On top of his distrust of humanity, there was another reason as well.

“It was bothersome.”

“Pardon?”

“Just look at you. Are you not agonizing over it? He is your friend, so should you go save him or not? I hated even thinking about such things.”

Baek Jagang had not said this because he wanted his disciple to live the same way. Rather, it was the opposite. His gentle gaze turned toward his disciple.

“Keeping the door closed is comfortable, but it also means you trap yourself inside.”

Bi Sain understood. It meant that he should cherish and protect his friendship with Geom Mugeuk. It was the moment his master acknowledged his friendship.

“I will… not find it bothersome.”

Baek Jagang read the purity dwelling in his disciple’s fearsome face.

Then another astonishing decision flowed from Baek Jagang’s mouth.

“I avoided it all my life, so shall I try doing something bothersome now?”

“!”

Bi Sain was startled. Because he understood that those words meant Baek Jagang would go to help the Cult Leader.

“What? Are you surprised that I am going to help him even though he is not my friend?”

Baek Jagang seemed like an emotional person, but he was not someone who made decisions emotionally. That was why Bi Sain was so curious about this decision of his master’s.

“May I ask the reason?”

“If the Cult Leader dies, I will not be able to take revenge on him, will I?”

Not revenge on the Heavenly Slaughter Star, but on the Cult Leader? Bi Sain wondered what that meant, but the reason was something he had never expected.

“At the tripartite talks, the Cult Leader and that old man of the Martial Alliance boasted endlessly about how they each had two children, did they not? I have to show them that one well-raised child can beat two.”

At the word “child,” emotion surged through Bi Sain’s chest. Nothing moved him more than when his master treated him not as a disciple, but as a child.

“If a man as proud as he is lit the Demon Flame, then this is something I must go to. It is no different from lighting a fire to call us there.”

Nor was he worried about leaving his post instead of protecting the Evil Dao Alliance.

“If that Heavenly Slaughter Star is strong enough to kill the Cult Leader?”

Baek Jagang had come to realize something after seeing countless villains of the evil factions. If someone was an absolute villain like that?

“If I am not in the alliance, he will not invade. So we will leave the doors wide open.”

Respect for his master rose in Bi Sain’s heart on its own. He could not hide that swelling emotion.

“Master, you are the most magnificent of all. I too truly wish to become an Evil Dao Alliance Leader like you.”

Throughout his conversation with his disciple today, not once had Baek Jagang felt goosebumps rise near his ears. If he had met someone like this when he was young, perhaps he might have had at least one friend.

“I will be back.”

“Yes, Master.”

Instead of telling his master to be careful, Bi Sain conveyed these words.

“As for that revenge match against the Cult Leader or the Martial Alliance Leader….”

When it came to the revenge match over child-rearing, he did not want to lose. For his master alone.

“Boast as much as you like and return. I will take responsibility!”

Chapter 796: If It Is a Prophecy You Must Hear
----------------------------------------------
The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable and the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion stepped into a place the dark energy had yet to reach.

It was the kitchen, and perhaps because it was not time to prepare a meal, it was empty.

“Let’s rest here for a moment.”

“Yes.”

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable took courteous care of the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion. The Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion felt burdened by her kindness. No matter how young she was, the other party was still a Demon Venerable of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

“If you’re hungry, please have some of this.”

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable found two duck legs in the kitchen and brought them over.

“I’m fine.”

When the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion declined, the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable leaned against the table and tore into a duck leg.

She ate with evident relish. From that natural ease, the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion sensed composure and strength.

“It’s quiet.”

One would have expected to hear the sounds of fighting from somewhere far away, but it was so quiet it felt unpleasant.

“The Young Cult Leader must be moving in the opposite direction from us.”

Though she could not fight alongside Geom Mugeuk, the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable considered this task important.

Geom Mugeuk would be able to fight without worry because he trusted that she would protect the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion well.

Moreover, the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion was a woman deeply connected to the Extremely Evil Lesser Demon. Protecting her was important.

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable, who had been tearing into the duck, set the leg down and roughly wiped her hands on a dishcloth nearby.

“Step back.”

As the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion stepped back, the door opened and someone entered.

The one who stepped inside was an old man, Heukno, one of the two old men who had been with the Dark Palace Lord. He entered naturally, as though he had known his target was here.

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable wiped her greasy lips with her sleeve and stood in front of the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion.

Heukno looked at the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable and grinned. In that smile was the confidence of someone who regarded his opponent as laughable. She was young—far too young.

“I heard a new Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had inherited the position, but I didn’t think she would be such a brat.”

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable answered leisurely.

“You even unleashed the Great Annihilation Art of Greed just to catch one brat and one woman. Does taking in the energy of gamblers make that old body of yours overflow with strength?”

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable did not yield in the clash of momentum. She had always been good at cursing when she fought, and good at getting under her opponents’ skin. Today was no exception.

“And the older you get, the more you should wash yourself properly. You reek of rotting corpses.”

With one blow, she overturned Heukno’s temper.

“You damned bitch!”

Dark energy as sharp as blades burst from Heukno’s body.

Swaaaaaaah.

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable released her internal energy and blocked the incoming force.

The two of them faced off, their auras locked in a tense balance.

Heukno’s appearance was not necessarily a bad thing.

It meant the Young Cult Leader was doing his part well.

It meant their situation was so urgent that they needed to take the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion hostage.

‘That’s right. Things aren’t going your way, are they?’

Just then, someone else entered from behind Heukno.

“Let us postpone the fight for a moment.”

At the courteous words, Heukno withdrew the aura he had been releasing.

The one who entered was Gaye, the Palace Lord of the Divine Maiden Palace. She, who had disappeared as if fleeing, had shown herself again.

Gaye slowly approached the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion. Perhaps because she trusted Heukno, she showed no fear at all of the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable standing beside her.

The Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion and Gaye stood facing each other. The one who spoke first was the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion.

“What lie have you come to tell this time?”

She had been told to come alone, but in the end, this place had proven to be a trap.

In a situation where even ten mouths would have left Gaye with nothing to say, she had appeared before her again.

And Gaye did not apologize for that.

“The Young Cult Leader was right.”

For whatever reason, she did not hide her true feelings.

“I wanted to kill you.”

The Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion quietly listened to words that were almost a confession.

“When the Palace Lord ordered me to lure you here, I looked forward to it inwardly. At last, you would die.”

As the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion listened to her, she thought that perhaps the one who had kept her alive from Gaye might have been the Dark Palace Lord.

“I know a prophecy recently descended upon you. What prophecy was it?”

“How did you know that?”

During the selection competition for the most beautiful woman under heaven, she had received a prophecy that the Extremely Evil Lesser Demon would stab Geom Mugeuk in the heart.

“I simply knew. Now tell me what the prophecy was.”

Gaye was curious about what the prophecy was. The Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion could clearly tell where Gaye’s reason for wanting to kill her stemmed from.

Instead of answering, the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion asked,

“How long has it been since you received a prophecy?”

In that instant, Gaye’s gaze turned cold.

“What are you, that you dare put the prophecy of the Palace Lord of the Divine Maiden Palace in your mouth?”

“That’s right. I am not of the Divine Maiden Palace.”

That was why. That was why she was so angry. She had staked everything on the Divine Maiden Palace, yet the prophecy had descended upon someone who had fled because she hated the Divine Maiden Palace.

“The one born under the Heavenly Slaughter Star will rule this world. That prophecy—you made it up, didn’t you?”

“Shut your mouth!”

From her violent reaction, the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion could tell. The Palace Lord of the Divine Maiden Palace had already lost her way. Perhaps that was why the prophecies were descending upon herself, the one who had left the Divine Maiden Palace.

On the other hand, she understood her. More than anyone, she knew how ruinous it was to grow up as the Palace Lord of the Divine Maiden Palace in that closed-off place. And she knew how a Divine Maiden who could not receive prophecies was treated.

Gaye spoke with an indifferent gaze.

“No matter what prophecy you received, nothing changes. You’re going to die anyway.”

She was threatening her, but the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion could feel it. Gaye was afraid of something.

“What exactly are you afraid of? That Heavenly Slaughter Star?”

The emotion in Gaye’s eyes was a primal fear that could not be hidden.

“That power should have belonged to us.”

The Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion did not know the exact details, but she could at least guess whom that power had gone to.

“No. It is right for the Young Cult Leader to possess that power. Whatever kind of power it is, he is someone who will use it properly.”

The Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion tried to persuade her.

“I believe there is still a chance. If you truly believe in Heaven’s will, then the final prophecy will descend upon you. Do not side with the Young Cult Leader, nor with the Dark Palace Lord. Side with Heaven. Just as the Divine Maiden Palace did in the past.”

It was just then, when Gaye’s eyes wavered faintly.

Sssssss.

The dark energy flowing around the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion began to retreat on its own. This energy was the innate energy that had naturally flowed from Heukno’s body.

And yet, though the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had not deliberately pushed it away, that energy began to be repelled from the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion.

At that moment, the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion let out a short exclamation, then spoke words that were astonishing.

“A prophecy is about to descend.”

She had felt that a prophecy was about to come down.

It was an unexpected situation, but the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable nodded with a calm expression.

“I will protect you, so receive the prophecy.”

The Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion immediately sat down where she was and took a white cloth from her bosom. It was the Divine Maiden’s Dream.

Before covering her eyes, she saw Gaye’s despairing face, but now she had no time to attend to her.

To be honest, she did not want to receive a prophecy. If this prophecy had nothing to do with the Extremely Evil Lesser Demon, and if it had nothing to do with Geom Mugeuk, she would not have received it.

But now, she had to. If Heaven was sending down a prophecy at this moment, then that, too, must have meaning.

Gaye’s face twisted hideously.

A prophecy was descending at this very moment? Here, where she was watching? At the very moment she was venting her rage at not having received a prophecy?

This was Heaven disregarding her. If this was Heaven, she would never believe in it again.

Or perhaps the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion before her was deceiving her.

Whatever the case, there was only one response Gaye could make. Killing intent flowed in her eyes.

“Kill her.”

When Gaye turned away, Heukno stepped forward.

“I cannot do that. My task is to bring that woman to the Palace Lord.”

The Dark Palace Lord intended to take her away not merely to use her as a hostage.

“The contents of that prophecy must be reported to the Palace Lord.”

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable stepped in front of the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion.

“If it were a prophecy your side was meant to hear, it would have descended upon your Divine Maiden.”

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable added one more thing to her task of protecting the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion. She had been annoyed the entire time she was listening.

“Hey, fake Divine Maiden.”

At the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable’s words, Gaye, who had turned away, bit her lip hard. The word “fake” shook her heart.

“From now on, resent me instead of Heaven. I’ll be the one to kill you.”

It was chilling. Because the one who had foretold her death was the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable.

But when Gaye turned back, she did not look frightened at all.

“Did he also tell you to bring that brat back alive?”

“Of course not.”

Heukno laughed coldly. If he killed her, he intended to make her death as painful as possible.

From the beginning, he unleashed a powerful move.

Swaeeeeeeek.

A mighty palm force shot from Heukno’s hand toward the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable.

Shuruk.

In that instant, something appeared in the air before the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable.

It was one of the bizarre patterns drawn on the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable’s carriage.

The moment the incoming palm force struck that pattern, it changed direction and flew toward the wall.

Kwaaaang!

The cabin wall was smashed apart, but beyond it writhed a dark haze. The energy of the Great Annihilation Art of Greed was blocking the surroundings of the ship, so no matter how fiercely they fought, there was no need to worry about the ship sinking.

“What a strange little trick.”

Swaeeeeeeeeek!

This time, he struck out with an even more powerful palm.

Shurururuk.

Another pattern appeared in the air, and this time it sent the incoming palm force back toward where Gaye stood.

Kwaang.

Heukno extended his hand and blocked the palm force flying toward Gaye.

Sssss.

Black smoke rose from his palm as the palm force he had released was dissolved.

“Not bad.”

The look of disdain vanished from Heukno’s face. Considering the internal energy contained in that palm strike just now, it was not a power that could be easily blocked.

Moreover, she had not merely diverted it; the move that sent it precisely back at Gaye was truly extraordinary.

One of the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable’s unique demonic arts, the Soul-Origin Reversal Art (魂元易傳術), had been displayed.

He did not know how hard the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had worked. How she had reduced her sleep and trained so as not to fall behind the other Demon Venerables.

Nor did he know that at the center of that effort stood an outstanding master named Cult Leader Pungcheon.

Heukno chanted an incantation.

Gururururung.

The floor shook and trembled as if there were an earthquake.

Several places in the floor bulged upward, and then something popped out as though hatching from eggs.

Astonishingly, they were skeletons.

Heukno’s most powerful dark demonic art, the Nine Springs Descent Art (九泉降臨術), had been unleashed.

Those skeletons, chilling just to look at, were no illusion.

From their two eyes flowed a blue light emitted by the dead.

It was truly fortunate that the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion had her eyes covered. No matter how bold a person was, they could not maintain their composure at such a sight.

Of course, the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable was not afraid at all.

“Now I know why you reeked so much of rotting corpses.”

She quietly recited a formula.

Srrruk.

Unfathomable characters began to be inscribed on the floor.

With the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion sitting cross-legged and the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable standing before her at the center, the characters were engraved into the floor in a round circle. Light poured from the carved letters.

The moment the skeletons tried to cross over the characters—

Thud.

There was an invisible barrier blocking their way.

The martial art the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had displayed was the Ghost-Script Barrier Art (鬼文結界術).

The skeletons swarmed forward and struck the invisible barrier with the weapons in their hands.

Thwack! Thwack! Thwack!

But the barrier created by the writings of ghosts did not break.

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable was buying as much time as possible. She thought that if a chaotic battle broke out, the prophecy might not be received.

“Not bad.”

Heukno, who had been watching, fired palm force at the floor. He did not waste unnecessary internal energy trying to destroy the Ghost-Script Barrier. The place his palm force flew toward was the floor.

Kwaaaang!

Under his immense palm force, the floor shattered and collapsed downward.

Kwareureureureu!

The Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion, who had been sitting cross-legged, gently descended to the lower floor. The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had released her internal energy to set her down safely. Gaye, likewise, was brought down without injury by Heukno.

Did he think that if he collapsed the floor, the letters on it would be damaged and the Ghost Gate barrier would be broken?

But even after they fell to the floor below, both the letters and the barrier remained intact.

Boom! KABOOM!

Heukno released his palm force again and shattered the floor.

This time, too, everyone fell downward.

Heukno had not been trying to destroy the Ghost Gate barrier. The farther they fell, the denser the energy of darkness became.

After descending several more times like that, they arrived at a place filled with thick, dark energy. They had deliberately climbed up while avoiding places where the darkness was dense, only to plunge straight down like this.

Bam! Bam!

Sure enough, the strength of the skeletons battering the barrier had become incomparably greater than before.

Even so, the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable’s barrier did not break.

Just then, Heukno recited an incantation again.

At that, the skeletons pounding on the barrier retreated as if afraid. What the skeletons were feeling was clearly fear.

Ruuuumble!

When he finished reciting the incantation, a tremor far greater than anything before shook the place.

Then a skeleton, far larger and more massive than those summoned earlier, rose up from the floor.

The skeleton summoned this time wore armor made of bone. Its eye sockets were even deeper, and the blue light flowing from within was so intense it looked as though gems had been set there.

Each finger bone was as sharp as a blade, and between bone and bone flowed a deathly aura that crushed its opponent.

It was none other than the king of skeletons, the Jiuquan Shiwang (九泉屍王).

In one hand, the Jiuquan Shiwang held an enormous sword, making it look like a death god risen from hell.

Watching it, the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable’s gaze deepened.

“A soul dwells in it.”

The Jiuquan Shiwang was different from ordinary skeletons. A soul could be felt from it—an evil soul filled only with rage, resentment, and killing intent.

Certain of victory, Heukno burst into loud laughter.

“Let’s see you block this one.”

The Jiuquan Shiwang’s enormous sword flew toward the barrier.

Whoooosh! Boom!

At the first attack, the barrier shook violently, and with the even stronger second strike, cracks appeared in it. It was truly terrifying power.

Riiip.

The Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable looked back at the Lord of Cheonhwaru.

She was still seated, her eyes covered by the Divine Maiden’s Dream. He had to protect her at least until she had received the entire prophecy.

“What are you doing? Hurry up and patch the hole.”

At Heukno’s mockery, the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable began reciting an incantation, as if she really intended to do just that.

Boom! KABOOM! Boom!

Under the relentless barrage of blows, the barrier began to crack in an instant.

Craaaaack!

Now, one final blow would shatter the barrier completely. The smaller skeletons surrounded them on all sides, ready to rush in the moment the protective barrier broke.

“We’ll have to choose a new Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable again. That seat is ill-fated.”

The Jiuquan Shiwang delivered the final strike.

Whoooooosh.

Clench.

Heukno was staring upward, his face filled with shock.

He had caught the sword the Jiuquan Shiwang had brought down. Black energy rippled from the palm blocking the blade, and his arm trembled from the impact.

“…What are you?”

The Jiuquan Shiwang, which had been about to deliver the final blow, had suddenly turned and struck at him instead.

Heukno’s startled gaze shifted to the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable standing behind the Jiuquan Shiwang.

‘No way?’

From her two eyes, the same blue energy flowing in the Jiuquan Shiwang’s eyes was streaming out.

She had not been reciting the incantation to strengthen the barrier. Astonishingly, she had been seizing the soul of the summoned Jiuquan Shiwang.

Even if you created it, the moment a soul dwells within it—

“This skeleton is mine now.”

Chapter 797: I Wanted to Become the First Demon Venerable
---------------------------------------------------------
Heukno’s bewilderment gave way to rage.

He had truly never imagined that a demonic beast he himself had summoned—none other than the Gucheon Siwang at that—would fall into enemy hands.

“You wretched wench!”

With a curse seething with fury, Heukno unleashed a palm strike.

Booom!

The Gucheon Siwang was pushed back with a screech. Yet that powerful palm strike did not cause it to so much as flinch. Naturally. After all, it was the strongest being he could summon.

“You fool! Disappear!”

As Heukno chanted an incantation, the surroundings began to quake once more.

Thudududududuk.

This time, contrary to when it had been summoned, the ground caved in like a swamp and began pulling at the Gucheon Siwang.

He was trying to forcibly dispel the summoning.

In the next moment, Heukno’s face hardened.

The Gucheon Siwang resisted being pulled in and held its ground.

‘This can’t be happening.’

Even though he had severed the spiritual tether connecting them, the Gucheon Siwang did not vanish.

Heukno chanted another incantation.

A secret art of the Darkness Palace that allowed one to pierce through the enemy’s sorcery.

The Hundred Spirit Soul Eye.

A being as powerful as the Gucheon Siwang could not be subjugated through ordinary means.

Therefore, when the Soul Subjugation Art was unleashed, a chain of souls—a Soul Chain—would connect the caster and the target.

If one severed that connection, the Soul Subjugation Art could be broken. Of course, discovering the tether was difficult, and even if found, it could not be cut easily; one had to expend a tremendous amount of internal energy to sever it.

When the Hundred Spirit Soul Eye activated, Heukno’s vision went dark for an instant, and faint iron chains began to appear.

‘Got it. If I just cut those…!’

But in the next instant, joy transformed into shock and despair.

The Soul Chain was not alone. Between the Soul Subjugation Demon Lord and the Gucheon Siwang, dozens of tethers connected every part of its body—head and neck, both arms and hands, waist and legs, feet, every part. It was truly a supreme Soul Subjugation Art, perfectly dominating its target.

At that moment, a single utterance from the Soul Subjugation Demon Lord rang out, as if she knew he was watching through the Hundred Spirit Soul Eye.

“Why do you peep at what belongs to another?”

Those words enraged Heukno further, but he soon composed himself. The opponent’s skill was not limited to the Soul Subjugation Art alone. The young one was trying to toy with his heart.

“To have reached such a realm at that age… I suppose there are things I must acknowledge.”

Gaye, who stood behind Heukno, was also astonished. They had already experienced several times that the Demon Lords were far stronger than they had imagined.

But she had not known that such overwhelming strength would apply even to the young Soul Subjugation Demon Lord.

Gaye’s gaze turned toward Cheonhwaru Ju, who sat behind the Demon Lord. She sat silently, her eyes covered by the Divine Maiden Blindfold.

She had thought that perhaps it was an act, designed to deceive her.

But could one truly maintain such an act in the midst of such a fierce battle?

Her gaze turned upward. Beyond the ceiling riddled with countless holes, was the distant sky truly what had abandoned her?

‘If it was going to be like this, you should never have given the prophecy to begin with!’

Even as her resentment was directed at the heavens, the Gucheon Siwang’s sword flew toward Heukno.

Bang! Screeeech!

A small skeleton rushed in and was shattered in its stead.

Skeletons swarmed in from all directions, surrounding their king. The skeletons felt fear, but they could not defy Heukno’s command.

Swhaack!

Crackcrack.

With a single sword strike from the Gucheon Siwang, the skeletons were smashed to pieces and scattered. If it had been a match for them, how could it bear the name King of Skeletons?

The skeletons rushed in, grabbing its arms and clinging to its back, trying to overwhelm it with sheer numbers, but the Gucheon Siwang was far too strong for that.

Shattered skeletons were sent flying in every direction.

While the energy of Tamshim Daemyeolgong had strengthened the skeletons, they were not the only ones to grow stronger. The Gucheon Siwang had also become far more powerful than it had been originally.

Heukno did not miss this opportunity while the skeletons bought him time.

He soared toward where the Soul Subjugation Demon Lord and Cheonhwaru Ju were.

The Ghost Gate Barrier was already nearly destroyed from the Gucheon Siwang’s earlier attacks.

Bang! Craaash!

Heukno’s fist, infused with internal energy, struck the barrier in rapid succession.

Craaaack.

Smaaash!

When the barrier was destroyed, the characters glowing on the floor lost their light and vanished.

The Soul Subjugation Demon Lord, who had been unfolding the Soul Subjugation Art, spoke calmly.

“Look behind you.”

At the Demon Lord’s words, Heukno turned to look back.

In the meantime, the Gucheon Siwang had smashed all the skeletons, and its massive sword was pointed at Gaye’s neck.

“If you do not back away, your saintess dies.”

“A Demon Lord using a hostage? Have you no shame?”

“Not at all.”

The Soul Subjugation Demon Lord did not bat an eye. On the contrary, she provoked Heukno in return.

“Though losing to you would be rather shameful, I think.”

Neither the provocation nor the hostage-taking had any effect on the other.

“Kill her.”

As if she had expected such a reaction, the Soul Subjugation Demon Lord was not surprised.

“You aren’t surprised?”

“Because I knew you were not ones to protect anyone.”

Gaye’s expression, who had been listening to their exchange, hardened. If the Darkness Palace Lord had commanded him to rescue her alive, Heukno would never have dared make such a decision.

She had already been reeling from the shock of being abandoned by the heavens, and upon learning that she had been abandoned by the Darkness Palace Lord as well, her heart became endlessly tormented.

A truth revealed through Heukno.

“The Palace Lord knows. That you delivered a false prophecy.”

A divine maiden who can no longer receive prophecies becomes nothing more than useless scrap.

Without revealing her turbulent heart, Gaye spoke with a calm gaze, as if scolding him.

“Do you think you can survive after daring to defy the will of the heavens?”

Though prophecies had not descended upon her recently, she was undeniably one who had received prophecies and ascended to become the Palace Lord of the Divine Maidens.

“I am asking whether you believe you can bear the weight of this grave sin with nothing but the excuse that it was the Palace Lord’s command.”

At that stern rebuke, Heukno let out a scoff.

But he could not speak recklessly to her. Before matters had come to this, she had been the most precious person after the Darkness Palace Lord.

This time, Gaye’s gaze turned to the Soul Subjugation Demon Lord. After all, she was the one holding the handle of the blade.

“If you kill me, you will commit the sin of defying the will of the heavens.”

Though the heavens were mentioned, the Demon Lord was not frightened in the slightest.

“My heaven is a different one.”

She was here following the command of that heaven.

Swoosh.

The Gucheon Siwang raised its sword with a flash. It sought to behead her in a single stroke, but Heukno made no move to save her.

Sensing she could not escape death, Gaye left behind her final words.

“My prophecy will undoubtedly become reality.”

The only being she feared was him, the one born under the Heavenly Slaughter Star. He who had awakened on his own and broken the seal by himself to emerge.

He would come to rule the world.

Just as the Gucheon Siwang was about to swing its sword, someone stepped forward and stopped it.

“Please do not kill her.”

The Gucheon Siwang’s sword, which had been flying toward her, stopped beside her neck.

The one who had asked not to kill her was none other than Cheonhwaru Ju. She removed the Divine Maiden Blindfold that had covered her eyes and rose from her seat.

From her opened eyes flowed a mysterious energy, and the darkness swirling around did not dare encroach upon her.

Everyone could tell. She had received a prophecy.

What kind of prophecy could it be?

But faced with the mysterious aura emanating from her, no one dared ask. Cheonhwaru Ju likewise did not speak of the prophecy.

Heukno addressed Cheonhwaru Ju.

“Now the reason to take you with us is certain.”

Gaye also had something to say to Cheonhwaru Ju.

“Why are you trying to save me?”

Cheonhwaru Ju gave no answer.

In the end, the Soul Subjugation Demon Lord did not ask Cheonhwaru Ju anything. She had simply granted her request.

“Please step back for a moment.”

It was the Young Cult Leader’s place to ask about the prophecy. Her own task was to protect her from the enemy.

The Demon Lord’s aura grew even more intense. Until now, she had been defensive for fear of interfering with the prophecy being received, but now she shifted to the offensive.

The Gucheon Siwang withdrew the sword pointed at Gaye and began to attack Heukno in earnest.

Baaam!

When the Gucheon Siwang and Heukno clashed, the earth shook.

Now the Gucheon Siwang’s attacks were relentless. Because it mattered not if its entire body was shattered, it unleashed the most savage attacks it was capable of.

This made Heukno all the more furious.

He truly wanted to grind the Soul Subjugation Demon Lord’s bones to dust and swallow them.

But it was no easy fight.

The Gucheon Siwang was the strongest summonable entity he could call forth. The one saving grace was that, because the Soul Subjugation Demon Lord was controlling it with the Soul Subjugation Art, she could not perform other attacks.

As the battle grew fierce, the Gucheon Siwang’s armor was smashed, and the sound of bones breaking rang out.

But in that process, Heukno also expended a tremendous amount of internal energy, and had even faced a situation where his head nearly flew off.

Though its broken bones rattled, the Gucheon Siwang fought well.

As time passed, the Demon Lord was controlling it with ever greater skill.

‘She’s growing more accustomed by the moment. At this rate…!’

Heukno raised his internal energy to the extreme, attempting to suppress the Gucheon Siwang in one fell swoop. He had determined to finish it as quickly as possible, even if he suffered internal injuries.

But this was the moment the Soul Subjugation Demon Lord had been waiting for.

The instant Heukno recklessly dove in.

Swhaack!

The Gucheon Siwang threw its sword with lightning speed. It had never once let go of its sword until now; this was the first time it had thrown it.

Heukno twisted his body with lightning speed to evade, and the great sword grazed past his face.

The unexpected attacks continued. By the time Heukno twisted his body again, the Gucheon Siwang was already upon him.

It left its entire body open as though inviting mutual destruction, yet its blade-sharp hands flew toward both his shoulders.

‘Damn it! Too late!’

He could shatter the Gucheon Siwang’s chest, but his own shoulders would suffer grievous wounds.

At that life-or-death instant, something astonishing occurred.

Gwaak!

Something pitch-black grabbed the Gucheon Siwang. Surprisingly, it was the Gucheon Siwang’s own shadow.

The airborne Gucheon Siwang struggled, but caught as if being buried in its own shadow, it could not move an inch. The shadow began to squeeze the Gucheon Siwang with tremendous force.

Heukno did not miss that opportunity.

Kwa-baaam!

Palm strikes containing extreme internal energy pounded the Gucheon Siwang in rapid succession.

Boom! Craash! Kwaang! Baam!

At the same time, the shadow constricted the Gucheon Siwang with tremendous force.

Unable to endure the two-pronged assault, the Gucheon Siwang shattered into pieces in mid-air and vanished.

Ssssssh.

In the next moment.

Hack!

The Soul Subjugation Demon Lord vomited blood.

Because the shadow had constricted the Gucheon Siwang, she had been unable to sever the Soul Chain, and with the subjugated target vanishing in that state, she had suffered internal injuries.

In that instant, palm blasts poured down upon her.

Swhack! Swack! Swhack! Swack!

Because Cheonhwaru Ju was behind her, she did not evade and instead swung both palms to block the palm force head-on. The attacks had come flying so swiftly that she had no leeway to unleash the Soul Subjugation Art to divert them.

Thwack, thud, thud, thwack!

Her figure shook violently. She blocked with palm techniques and protective qi barriers, but the earlier internal injuries amplified the shock.

“Kuegh.”

This time, she vomited even more blood.

“Demon Lord!”
