When Cheonhwaru Ju called to her in alarm, the Soul Subjugation Demon Lord nodded as if to say she was fine. But blood was trickling from the corners of her mouth.

Someone walked in and spoke.

“Fool! Did you summon the Gucheon Siwang before the Soul Subjugation Demon Lord?”

The newly appeared individual was Amno, the remaining one of the two old men who had been with the Darkness Palace Lord.

The one who had constricted the Gucheon Siwang with a shadow and launched continuous palm strikes just moments ago was him.

He had unleashed his unique demonic art, Shadow Predation.

Heukno had nothing to say to that. It had been an obvious mistake born of underestimating the opponent.

“I never imagined that young one would actually subjugate the Gucheon Siwang.”

“Indeed, it is quite surprising.”

Amno looked at the Soul Subjugation Demon Lord with cold eyes.

Heukno and Amno.

Limbs of the Darkness Palace Lord, and masters of the Darkness Demonic Arts.

“The Young Cult Leader is on the verge of storming the Palace Lord. We must finish this quickly and return.”

The Soul Subjugation Demon Lord’s expression grew grave. She had barely managed to handle one person; fighting two was impossible. Moreover, she had already suffered internal injuries.

Furthermore, this place was thick with the energy of Tamshim Daemyeolgong. She had been fighting using the Gucheon Siwang, which had been empowered by that dark energy.

But now she had to fight a completely disadvantageous battle.

“Demon Lord!”

Cheonhwaru Ju stepped in front of the Soul Subjugation Demon Lord. She was thinking only of saving her somehow.

“Your Palace Lord wants to know my prophecy. If you kill the Demon Lord, I will never speak.”

Cheonhwaru Ju showed a resolute will, but it did not work on Heukno and Amno.

“That is a matter between you and the Palace Lord.”

They made clear their intention to kill the Soul Subjugation Demon Lord no matter what.

Then the Soul Subjugation Demon Lord, who had been in the back, stepped forward.

“I am fine, so please step back.”

Tears welled up in Cheonhwaru Ju’s eyes as she looked at the Soul Subjugation Demon Lord.

“…Demon Lord.”

She was facing death in her attempt to protect her. If she had tried to flee, she might have escaped during the earlier fight with the first old man.

The Soul Subjugation Demon Lord, on the other hand, was serene.

“Please treasure the prophecy. The Young Cult Leader will come to rescue us soon.”

She meant to never speak of the prophecy until the Young Cult Leader came to rescue them. That would keep her alive.

The Soul Subjugation Demon Lord foresaw her own death.

‘Young Cult Leader, I am sorry for failing to complete my mission.’

She did not ask anyone to convey that sentiment to the Young Cult Leader. Hearing such words would only dampen their mood for no reason.

‘I had wanted to become the Young Cult Leader’s first Demon Lord…’

The Soul Subjugation Demon Lord stood proudly before the two.

“You corpse-stinking bastards, I will not die gracefully. I’ll be taking an arm or an eye with me.”

Intense killing intent surged from the Soul Subjugation Demon Lord’s eyes.

When she revealed her final aura, the surroundings seemed to spin, and dizziness began to set in.

Heukno and Amno raised their internal energy to resist that aura. It was a powerful, ferocious momentum that showed she had no intention of dying quietly.

“I won’t leave a single eye or arm on your body.”

Heukno and Amno also unleashed their auras without reserve. The deathly energy flowing from them mixed with the dark energy swirling around, suppressing the Soul Subjugation Demon Lord’s aura.

It was a two-on-one battle, so her energy was pushed back.

Just as Heukno and Amno split to both sides to attack in unison.

The eyes of Cheonhwaru Ju, standing in the back, sparkled.

“From somewhere…”

Cheonhwaru Ju slowly looked around and said.

“I smell alcohol.”

Chapter 798: Picked the Wrong Opponent
--------------------------------------
It really was the smell of alcohol.

Heukno and Amno looked at each other, then nodded simultaneously.

Without hesitation, the two unleashed their palm forces.

Shwaaaaaack.

Two massive streams of palm force flew toward Seophon Majon. Even though it was an attack that would kill Cheonhwa Ruju if Seophon Majon dodged, they struck without reservation.

As expected, Seophon Majon guarded the area in front of Cheonhwa Ruju until the very end.

“No!”

The moment Cheonhwa Ruju screamed.

Boom! Kwaang!

A series of dull, thunderous roars rang out.

When she squeezed her eyes shut and opened them, Seophon Majon was standing perfectly fine in front of Cheonhwa Ruju.

Two streams of palm force had flown in from somewhere to neutralize the attack.

Who had helped was obvious from sight and smell.

In that place filled with dark mist, golden waves flowed, and the smell of alcohol grew even thicker.

Two people entered from there.

An uncontrollable surge of emotion flashed across Seophon Majon’s face as she looked at them. Just moments ago, she had resigned herself to death.

Even if she died carrying out the Young Cult Leader’s orders, how could death not be frightening at her young age?

Mabul, who entered first, spoke to her.

“Seophon, you’ve had a hard time.”

Had there ever been a time when Mabul’s face was so welcome?

Seophon Majon bowed her head respectfully and conveyed her gratitude.

“You saved my life. Thank you.”

“You would have held them off well enough on your own.”

Mabul said, looking up at the wide-open ceiling.

“Why didn’t we think of this? We should have come down this way too.”

Earlier, Heukno had smashed through floors and plummeted down while chasing an even stronger dark aura, descending all the way to the floor where Chwima and Mabul were.

“Wouldn’t that have been lacking in elegance?”

Wafting thick with the smell of alcohol, Chwima followed behind and entered.

Chwima glanced at Gaye standing in the corner, then strode over to where Seophon Majon was.

At the appearance of the two, Heukno and Amno’s expressions completely stiffened. The fact that Mabul and Chwima had already appeared here meant that the Dark Spirit had fallen. Truly, the strength of these damned Demon Lords was running rampant.

Chwima stopped in front of Seophon Majon. Seeing blood at the corners of her mouth, he took something from his bosom and handed it to her.

“Have a drink.”

What Chwima handed her was a small wine bottle.

When Seophon Majon looked at him, Chwima nodded with intoxicated eyes.

Without a word, Seophon Majon opened the bottle.

Smelling the fragrance of the medicinal wine spreading around her, she drank deeply.

It was no ordinary wine. It was Chwima’s signature medicinal wine, most effective for treating internal injuries.

She could tell the moment she drank it.

‘This is precious wine.’

And indeed it was. The moment the wine entered her body, it began to untangle her twisted and wounded meridians. She truly hadn’t expected him to offer such precious wine so freely.

“Later, I shall treat you to a drink.”

It was precious wine that could not be repaid with a single drink, but for now, she expressed her gratitude in that way.

Chwima laughed in a good mood at her words.

“My popularity is soaring to the heavens these days! People are lining up to buy me drinks. Young Cult Leader, I am such a man!”

He had the face of someone who couldn’t wait to meet Geommugeuk and brag.

The two old men watching found Chwima unpleasant. For him to act so relaxed while they were right in front of him was clearly an act of contempt toward them.

Meanwhile, watching that scene from the side, tears finally flowed from Cheonhwa Ruju’s eyes. She had truly thought Seophon Majon was going to die. They wouldn’t know how grateful and reassured she felt that the two Demon Lords had appeared in the nick of time.

She bowed her head respectfully to the two Demon Lords. It was a bow of gratitude not for saving herself, but for saving Seophon Majon.

‘Truly, thank you both.’

Chwima and Mabul didn’t say anything special to Cheonhwa Ruju. The one who had saved her here today was not them, but Seophon Majon. That merit should belong entirely to Seophon Majon.

Chwima thought that Seophon Majon was truly remarkable. Even though the final attack they had launched was a dangerous blow that could have inflicted a fatal wound, she had not dodged and protected Cheonhwa Ruju instead. That was why he had willingly handed over his treasured internal injury medicine.

Having seen to Seophon Majon like that, Chwima finally turned toward Heukno and Amno.

“We fought one against two, so three against two is fine, right?”

Heukno and Amno had nothing to say to that.

Amno revealed a cold, imposing aura.

“Four is possible, not just three. No, it wouldn’t matter even if all eight of you came.”

Chwima openly sneered.

“You tried to gang up on one person because you were scared, and now you talk about eight? I truly want to line up all the Demon Lords in front of you. Only when your big fists crack their skulls, their eyeballs are pierced by tempest winds, and their organs are shredded by extreme poison will you cry, ‘Oh my, I spoke nonsense in my old age, please spare me!’ shedding tears and snot everywhere.”

Chwima acted as if he were dismissing his opponents, but he was absolutely not letting his guard down.

The Tamsim Daemyeolgong spread throughout his core had reached its peak. The opponent’s Dark Demonic Arts would display their most powerful might. Though they had the numerical advantage, they also had Cheonhwa Ruju to protect. No one knew how this fight would unfold.

Chwima asked Mabul.

“Which one will you take?”

“Anyone is fine with me.”

“As expected! Such is the leisure of the strong!”

Chwima openly asked the two old men.

“Which of you is stronger? The weaker one can fight me.”

It sounded like a frivolous question thrown out on a whim, but it was a loaded question intended to break the two men’s solidarity—that is, if each thought inwardly that he was the stronger one.

“A ploy to divide us.”

Amno grasped that intention immediately.

Thereupon, Chwima said to Mabul,

“Please deal with him. Since he’s more cautious, his skill must be greater as well.”

This allowed him to determine who was more cautious, and he also succeeded in wounding Heukno’s pride by treating him as an inferior.

“Seophon, please support me.”

Chwima honestly acknowledged that he was at a disadvantage in terms of martial compatibility in this fight. He was not ashamed of being weaker.

Heukno said to Chwima with cold eyes.

“There is no need to force a matchup. Your opponent is not me.”

Heukno unleashed a new Dark Summoning Technique.

Beast Descent Technique.

Monstrous beasts in animalistic forms were summoned, baring their massive fangs. Because they were soulless summons, these were things Seophon Majon could not capture.

Chwima drew his daggers. Even seeing those terrifying summons, he showed no sign of fear whatsoever.

“If you’re going to summon them anyway, couldn’t you call out something we could share a drink with?”

The moment his words ended, he charged toward the beasts.

Heukno had faced countless enemies while mastering summoning arts over many years, but this was the first time he had encountered someone who charged in without any plan. And a Demon Lord at that.

The beasts rushed at Chwima.

As their battle began, Amno walked out toward Mabul.

“Can that light emanating from your body truly protect you?”

Mabul quietly put his palms together and answered.

“It was not this light that protected me. I am the one who protected this light.”

Whooooom.

Dark energy flowing from Amno’s body wrapped around the two like a whirlwind, and they instantly disappeared from that spot.

Thud! Squelch! Thud-thud!

Chwima’s daggers continuously stabbed the beasts. It seemed like a reckless brawl relying solely on protective ki, but his movement technique was displaying truly incredible footwork.

Earlier, while breaking through the whirlwind martial art, the Manpa Hoeryun, unleashed by Byeokhae Shingun outside the ship, he had succeeded in raising his martial realm, which he had been contemplating recently, by one level.

That achievement was now revealing itself.

The beasts rushed in from all directions, appearing to tear into his whole body, but he narrowly parried their attacks. What Chwima lost were scraps of clothing, while the beasts’ bodies burst apart from his attacks.

He stabbed the beasts charging from all directions with his daggers and kicked them. He trampled fallen beasts and even strangled them.

Heukno continued trying to summon more beasts.

But Seophon Majon did not stand by and watch.

Shwaaaack! Shwaaack! Shwack!

Flying palm force forced Heukno to stop his summoning and fling his body backward.

‘When did she recover from her internal injuries?’

Before he knew it, Chwima had broken through the beasts and was charging at him. Being among the beasts, he looked like one himself. In fact, he even bit and tore apart a dark beast’s throat.

Seophon Majon could understand why Chwima was charging in like that.

Originally, since she was fighting alongside him, he should have chosen the stronger opponent, Amno. But he chose Heukno, who was relatively weaker.

His intention was to finish him quickly and go help Mabul.

That was why Seophon Majon also actively supported Chwima.

In response to Chwima’s charge, Heukno hid his body within the darkness.

He had unleashed the Shadow Stealth Technique.

The longer he dragged out time, the more advantageous it was. Amno was sure to kill Mabul and return.

“Are you going to be this cowardly?”

Chwima tried to raise his ki to search, but the dark aura was so strong that it was difficult.

Ssst, ssst.

At that moment, a pattern was drawn in the empty air above Chwima’s daggers. It was a pattern of a human eye drawn inside a circle.

Dark Night Eye.

It was Seophon Majon’s unique demonic art for finding those hidden in darkness.

Shharrrr.

Just when it seemed the pattern was temporarily imbuing Chwima’s daggers,

the pattern snapped its eyes wide open.

* * *

Mabul stood in an empty space devoid of anyone.

An unknown force was gripping his body tightly. No matter how much he tried to move, he could not. He could tell that some kind of Dark Demonic Art had been unleashed upon him when the dark aura had whirled around him like a tempest earlier.

Amno slowly walked toward him.

“Is there any need to fight like those low-class drunkards who smash everything in sight?”

Mabul calmly answered.

“I was expecting a fight where everything gets smashed.”

“Then you chose the wrong opponent.”

Amno slowly circled around Mabul once, then looked behind him and spoke.

“Shadows contain the dark side of a person.”

Mabul realized that it was his own shadow holding him captive.

His opponent was someone who used Dark Demonic Arts involving shadows.

Mabul calmly searched for Amno’s shadow.

“Where is your shadow?”

Amno had no shadow.

“This is a place where shadows should not exist. It is a place where shadows devour their masters. Just as you are being devoured now.”

The Dark Demonic Art unleashed upon Mabul was the one Amno was most confident in.

Wounded Soul Collapse Technique.

“The wounds in your heart will destroy you.”

“A common, trivial demonic art.”

A sneer formed on Amno’s face, as if to say, Is that so?

“A wound of one will be amplified to a hundred. A human can never endure that pain.”

In short, it was a demonic art with a set method of breaking it. Endure and break through, or fail and collapse.

“Mabul, you will die here.”

He had not originally planned to use the Wounded Soul Collapse Technique on Mabul.

“It was a technique I was saving for the Young Cult Leader, but it seems I will use it on you first.”

The stronger the opponent, the better it worked. Strong people often harbored deep darkness.

“Then why don’t you save it and use it on the Young Cult Leader?”

“Let’s see how long you can stay so relaxed.”

Sssssst.

In an instant, Mabul found himself in a different space.

It was that small cave from his childhood. The place he had always hidden to avoid the teasing of those around him.

At the moment the wound should have surfaced, a smile formed on Mabul’s lips.

If he had encountered this demonic art for the first time, it was a place that would have plunged him into a deep inner demon. If the wound from that time were amplified a hundredfold, as the bastard said, could his mind endure it?

If it were the past.

“Someone brought me here first, you see.”

Mabul walked out of the cave on his own. Not even a small wound remained here that could be amplified a hundredfold.

Sssst.

As the cave disappeared, this time Pungcheon Gyoju appeared.

He spoke with a face full of hurt.

“I thought we were friends. But you threw away our friendship merely for power. That is the kind of human you are.”

He spat out words meant to provoke, but Mabul calmly answered.

“We have already reconciled.”

In that instant, Pungcheon Gyoju made a bewildered expression, then slowly faded away.

Sssst.

Mabul muttered as if to himself.

“Now the Grand Heir will appear as well.”

Hardly before those words ended, Geommyang also appeared there.

“Because of you, I couldn’t become the successor! And yet even then, you were wondering whether to side with Geommugeuk. Even now, disgustingly hiding those feelings…”

Mabul strode up to Geommyang. He said nothing else. Mabul hugged him firmly.

“I am sorry, Grand Heir. It is all my fault.”

Mabul patted Geommyang’s back. In that moment, Mabul was an adult guiding Geommyang, and at the same time, a small giant.

Sssst.

Geommyang disappeared from his embrace.

Even though he had thought he had already overcome these matters, seeing them appear as trials like this made him realize that wounds of the heart once received never disappear forever.

And Mabul confirmed that he had indeed overcome those wounds.

No, rather, he felt thankful that these wounds existed.

Because they were traces of having lived as a human being, not a Demon Lord without blood or tears. Because they were precious wounds.

So now, he could say these words.

Wounds, do not fade.

In the next moment, Amno was making a look of shock before his eyes. Blood was gushing from his mouth. He had suffered severe internal injuries as the demonic art was broken in an instant.

“The Wounded Soul Collapse Technique shattered this easily?”

Amno wore an expression of utter disbelief.

“How can this be?”

“I suppose those wounded souls have already become faint scars.”

“!”

“You were right. I chose the wrong opponent.”

Of all people, he had unleashed the Wounded Soul Collapse Technique on him—the one who had worked harder than any other Demon Lord to overcome the wounds of his heart.

Mabul quietly joined his fingers and formed a hand seal.

“Did you say this is a place where shadows devour their masters?”

A brilliant radiance began to shine from Mabul’s body.

“Then, well…”

The bursting light began to push back the dark energy dominating that place.

“Shall we find your shadow?”

Chapter 799: I Am the Blood Buddha
----------------------------------
Darkness was pushed back by the light Mabeol emitted.

Swoooooosh.

The sight of darkness being driven back by Mabeol's golden radiance was truly a spectacle.

When the light illuminated Amno, his shadow became visible.

“No!”

Despite his desperate cry.

Slllk.

The shadow rose like a living person and grabbed Amno from behind, as if embracing him. Amno did not resist, like someone who knew it was absolutely impossible to avoid.

Mabeol recalled what he had said earlier.

“This is a place where shadows should not exist. It is a place where shadows devour their master.”

“So even you get devoured.”

Amno glared at Mabeol with blazing eyes.

“Do you know what you have just done?”

“You seem to carry many wounds in your heart as well. What would happen to you if your wounds became a hundred times worse?”

“Surely you didn’t think I would fall victim to the Soul Collapse Technique?”

He hadn’t been worried about falling victim to the Soul Collapse Technique.

“If Akyeong comes out, not even I can handle things from then on!”

He called his own shadow Akyeong.

“Because I called forth Akyeong, now one of us has to die, or we cannot leave this place.”

Meaning this space could not be erased by his own will.

Shwaaaaack.

The shadow that had held Amno immobile engulfed his body as if to devour him.

“Uaaaaaaargh!”

A scream burst from Amno. Darkness infiltrated his body. He felt the agony of his living flesh being torn and something foreign entering his body.

The moment Akyeong merged with him, Mabeol immediately tried to launch an attack. Simply watching this ominous situation unfold would be foolish.

But before the hand seal could even be completed, dark energy whirled around Amno’s body like a gale.

Fwhiiiiing.

Mabeol dispersed the hand seal. He realized that attacking now would be pointless.

Shortly after, when the gale subsided, Amno revealed his changed form from within.

Shadow covered his entire body. He looked as though he were wearing armor of shadow, with even half his face covered by it.

And even at this moment, the shadow writhed, trying to cross the halfway boundary and encroach upon the remaining half of his face.

“Mabeol, you have done something that should not have been done.”

Mabeol replied calmly.

“I was trying to show the wounds in your heart as well. Who could have known it would turn out like this?”

Amno, devoured by Akyeong, was radiating murderous intent from his entire body. He truly looked like darkness combined with darkness.

“And weren’t you planning to transform into that form in the end anyway? If I had to see it either way, it’s better to see it while your strength is still vigorous.”

He had a point. If it came down to it, Amno would have fought by calling forth Akyeong anyway. The problem was this:

‘I must hurry.’

Though he became far more powerful after merging with Akyeong, as time passed, he would truly be devoured. If completely devoured, he would become a madman who had lost his reason.

Since he would be unable to remember anything after that moment, that moment would be the moment of his death.

Mabeol pressed his palms together with a cold gaze.

“Life steeped in sin, I shall grant you Buddhahood.”

Amno charged at Mabeol.

“Shut up! Baldy!”

Amno leaped into the air and unleashed a flurry of punches. He had not merely mastered demonic arts that utilized shadows.

Dark Chain Strike!

Powerful punches flew toward Mabeol.

Mabeol did not dodge either, unleashing his palm technique to meet the attack head-on.

Kwa-kwang! Kwa-kwang!

Fists and palms collided with a sound like mountains crumbling.

Mabeol confirmed that his opponent’s internal energy was stronger than he had imagined and tensed internally.

At the same time, a fighting spirit surged in Mabeol’s heart. Apart from his efforts to control his mind, he too was someone who enjoyed fighting and winning.

This time, Mabeol charged at Amno and unleashed his palm technique. Prayer beads were wrapped tightly around his palm as if he were wearing a gauntlet. Golden radiance burst forth from the prayer beads.

Kwaaang!

Amno slid backward under the might of the fierce palm technique.

“Isn’t a fight where you smash everything more fun?”

Before his words ended, Mabeol’s hand seal was already formed.

Golden Daerama Demon Art!

Murderous Gathering Seal!

Golden energy waves surged forth from Mabeol’s twin palms like a whirlwind.

Shwaaaaaack.

Against the surging energy that rushed like waves, Amno also unleashed his unique demonic art.

Dark Shadow Wave.

Shadows surged up from the ground as if waves were crashing.

Bang! Bang! Bang! Bang!

The clash of the two energies shook the earth’s axis.

Though he had suffered severe internal injuries earlier when the Soul Collapse Technique was broken, Amno was not pushed back by Mabeol. With the effects of the Great Greed Annihilation Art combined with Akyeong, he rampaged like a madman.

This time, Amno’s unique demonic art, Dark Heavenly Killing Rain, was unleashed.

Shwaack.

A single pitch-black spear rained down, aimed at the crown of Mabeol’s head.

Pahk.

Mabeol twisted his body to evade, and the qi-forged spear pierced the ground before him.

“You think this is enough to kill me?”

“Did you think the rain would fall with just a single drop?”

The next instant!

Countless dots appeared above Mabeol’s head.

As if the single spear that fell earlier was merely the first raindrop to fall with a light tap, dozens of spears of darkness poured down toward Mabeol.

Shuk shuk shuk shuk shuk shuk shuk shuk shuk shuk shuk shuk!

Since this was not an attack he could avoid by simply throwing his body aside, Mabeol’s hand seals were quickly formed.

Golden Daerama Demon Art

Lotus in Full Bloom

Golden radiance burst forth, and lotus flowers bloomed around Mabeol’s body.

Papapapapak!

The incoming energy waves could not pierce the lotus flowers and vanished.

“Lotuses blooming on a rainy day have more charm, after all.”

The two looked at each other.

Mabeol read urgency in Amno’s eyes. Even now, the shadow was gradually encroaching upon the rest of his face, bit by bit.

“What happens if you’re completely devoured?”

“You should be asking what will happen to you, not me.”

The result would be horrific. For himself, and for Mabeol.

Even as this happened, Akyeong’s energy seized him more rapidly.

‘I have to kill Mabeol somehow.’

The moment Mabeol died, Akyeong would absorb the malicious energy remaining in his corpse, and then he could soothe the sated Akyeong and tear it away.

But the problem was that Mabeol was not someone who would die easily. The more desperate Amno’s attacks became, the further Mabeol moved from death.

‘Damn it! If only I hadn’t sustained internal injuries!’

Amno became more ferocious as he was gradually devoured by Akyeong.

“No! Please!”

When Akyeong covered the last part of his face, his eyes looked at Mabeol. It was a gaze mixed with hatred and entreaty.

“You stupid baldy, you’re going to die too.”

With those words, Akyeong completely dominated him.

Now, no reason remained in Amno. Only madness and murderous intent ruled over him.

Mabeol did not miss the moment the master changed.

Golden Daerama Demon Art

Heavenly Sky Seal.

A streak of energy descended like lightning toward Amno.

Puh-uk.

Amno, struck cleanly, was thrown backward.

But soon he rose from where he fell.

Black shadow filled the spot where he had been hit.

Having been completely devoured by Akyeong, he had grown even stronger, and the energy of the Great Greed Annihilation Art had reached its extreme.

‘I have to finish this in one blow.’

To kill him, he had to use the Demonic Palm Annihilation Seal.

But this space was too narrow to use the Demonic Palm Annihilation Seal. It was a place where the technique’s sole weakness applied.

Oooooooh.

Shadows called forth by Akyeong rose from all directions. They wielded various weapons, and their dark, cold energy felt like that of martial artists who had been annihilated somewhere.

Akyeong’s Thousand Troops.

Amno’s ultimate technique.

No, Akyeong’s ultimate technique.

Mabeol threw a palm strike toward the shadows rushing at him.

Puh-eok!

The shadow burst apart in one blow, but another shadow rushed in.

Fighting against shadows charging from all directions, Mabeol was driven into a corner.

He put his back to the wall and faced the swarming shadows. They were endlessly summoned.

‘At this rate, my internal energy will be depleted and I’ll die.’

Mabeol had to use a trick. And suddenly, one thought came to mind.

‘Could it work?’

There was a move he had been thinking about for a long time. A long-standing idea to compensate for the greatest weakness of the Golden Daerama Demon Art.

That thought had crossed his mind while carving stone statues and while searching for poisonous herbs.

But it was a technique he had only organized in his head, never properly trained or executed.

He didn’t know if it was possible or not, but right now it was the best remaining option.

Mabeol unleashed the Lotus in Full Bloom to force the shadows back.

Within the lotus of energy, Mabeol guided his true qi into a new meridian.

‘May the Blood Buddha have mercy.’

A new hand seal formed in his hands. The last thought that crossed his mind at that moment was this:

‘The Blood Buddha is me, and I am the Blood Buddha.’

Hwaaaak.

Golden radiance erupted from Mabeol’s hand, and palm-shaped energy began to grow larger. When it had grown large enough to fill the space—

Hwoooooaaang!

Along with a golden wave, the palm flew forward.

It was not the Demonic Palm Annihilation Seal of a massive Buddha statue descending, but a new Demonic Palm Annihilation Seal. It was not the palm of a golden Buddha statue, but Mabeol’s own palm.

Kwakwakwakwakwakwak!

The shadows filling the front were swept away by that palm.

The palm continued forward, pushing back even Amno, who stood behind.

Kuaaaaaaaang!

An enormous roar erupted.

As the rising dust settled, Amno was deeply embedded in the wall. Around his body, a palm print was deeply carved. It was small in size, but its power was no less than that of the Demonic Palm Annihilation Seal.

‘I did it.’

Mabeol was surprised even after succeeding. Had the thought to use this technique only occurred because he had already been prepared to succeed?

Mabeol gave this new technique a name.

“Demonic Palm of the Small Man.”

He acknowledged himself as a small person.

The technique’s success was not due to the Blood Buddha’s mercy.

It was because of his heart that had broken through the Soul Collapse Technique.

Having overcome his wounds, and acknowledging even his short stature and small-mindedness—it was because of his mental cultivation.

Smallness created smallness. It was a smallness greater and stronger than anything else.

“Uuugh.”

A low groan flowed from Amno’s mouth, embedded deep in the center of the palm print. The Akyeong that had dominated his body was already gone.

Life was fading from his gaze, which was filled with disbelief.

Mabeol brought his palms together respectfully and spoke.

“Count it a blessing that you die as a human, and depart with a good heart.”

In this moment alone, he looked like an enlightened high monk.

Amno, who had been quietly looking at Mabeol, dropped his head limply.

Sssst.

As his breath ceased, the space where they had fought began to disappear.

* * *

Divine Maiden Palace Lord Gaye ran down the corridor.

She had escaped from there while the battle was in full swing.

The moment she left the room, her eyes met the Cheonhwaru Lord’s. She had been silently watching her leave.

Feeling that the emotion in those eyes was not hatred, she felt more negative than thankful for having her life saved.

‘What bad luck.’

Was it pity? Or did she show mercy because she was someone who had received a prophecy?

No matter how intense the battle was, the Soul-Devouring Demon Lord couldn’t have been unaware of her escaping.

But she didn’t stop her. She must have granted the Cheonhwaru Lord’s request to spare her.

‘I don’t believe it.’

Of all times, did she receive a prophecy at the very moment she was with her, and inside this greedy Wealth Ship filled with dark energy?

‘Don’t make me laugh.’

The will of heaven would not descend at such a moment.

She didn’t believe that the Dark Palace Lord had abandoned her either.

Heukno, that old man, must have been talking nonsense because he lacked the confidence to protect her.

She would go and confirm it directly with him.

She continued running toward the room where the Dark Palace Lord was. The broken objects scattered along the way did not enter her eyes.

As she turned the last corner.

Tak.

She ran into someone’s back.

“Whoa, you startled me!”

The one who turned around with a fuss was Geom Mugik.

“Well, who do we have here? Isn’t it our Holy Maiden?”

Gaye was startled. She had never dreamed she would meet him in front of the Dark Palace Lord’s room.

“Our Dark Palace Lord is so terribly timid, locking the door like that and refusing to open it.”

The dark smoke of the Covetous Heart Great Annihilation Art enveloped not only the door but that entire room, so unless the Dark Palace Lord opened it for them, they could not enter.

He could blow it all away with Heavenly Demon World-Destroying, but opening that door did not look easy.

“I can’t open that door either.”

Geom Mugeuk stared at her quietly, then abruptly asked,

“Did the Cheonhwa Pavilion Master spare you?”

For an instant, Gaye flinched. That reaction alone had already answered him, but she asked Geom Mugeuk with a slightly angry face.

“Why would you think that?”

“If you came running here in such a hurry, it means your side’s situation isn’t good. And our Demon Venerables don’t regard you as anyone special.”

As if he had been there himself, Geom Mugeuk grasped the situation with perfect accuracy.

“Your Demon Venerables are all dead.”

At those words, Geom Mugeuk laughed.

“So that means they’re all fine. Ah, I miss our Demon Venerables!”

To show such composure even in this situation—he truly was an extraordinary man. Then again, it was because he was such a man that the Dark Palace Lord had been driven to this state.

Gaye’s gaze shifted past Geom Mugeuk toward the door. The Dark Palace Lord beyond it was surely watching this situation and hearing every word of their conversation.

“It seems the Dark Palace Lord doesn’t regard you as special, either—less than our Demon Venerables do.”

Geom Mugeuk also looked at the door as he spoke.

“At the very least, if one of my people were outside together with the enemy, I would have opened that door.”

“Don’t try to sow discord between us with pointless words.”

As if she did not want to hear any more, Gaye sat down and closed her eyes.

“Kill me or spare me, do as you please.”

Geom Mugeuk shouted toward the door.

“If you don’t open the door, I’ll kill the Divine Maiden Palace Lord!”

Geom Mugeuk raised his hand high and made to strike down at Gaye’s crown. But from beyond the door, there was no response at all.

Lowering the hand he had raised, he said to Gaye,

“Is there even any relationship left between you for me to sow discord in?”

Still, she kept her eyes tightly shut and said nothing.

Then came words from Geom Mugeuk that she had not expected.

“Hey, you ugly old man. I know very well that you use your subordinates as tools and throw them away. Even so, you shouldn’t do this to the Divine Maiden Palace Lord, if no one else. The Divine Maiden Palace is the most important reason for your existence, isn’t it? It’s your justification. If you lose that justification, you become worse than some back-alley hoodlum.”

Beneath her closed eyelids, Gaye’s eyes trembled slightly.

“I can look down on the Divine Maiden. But you shouldn’t.”

The words Gaye had wanted to say to the Dark Palace Lord at least once came from Geom Mugeuk’s mouth.

“If the Divine Maiden can no longer receive prophecies, that isn’t the Divine Maiden’s fault. It means something has gone wrong with you, doesn’t it? You stopped receiving prophecies because everything you’ve done has strayed too far from the will of heaven, hasn’t it? Why are you shifting that responsibility onto the Divine Maiden? For someone standing above others, you’re truly pathetic. Pathetic.”

Still, the Dark Palace Lord beyond the door remained silent.

The one who opened her mouth was Gaye.

“What do you know?”

Her voice had lost some of its edge from before.

Geom Mugeuk went over beside her and sat down shoulder to shoulder.

“Do you feel a little better? If you’d like, I can curse him some more for you.”

Chapter 800: May the Greatest Star of All Time Come
---------------------------------------------------
Heukno had hidden his body in the darkness to buy time.

There was no need to fight this disadvantageous one-on-one battle to the bitter end.

If only Amno could defeat Mabul and return, they would be the victors of this fight.

If he joined forces with Amno to focus their attacks on Seophon Majon and eliminate her first, then summoned the Nine Heavens Corpse King with the Nine Heavens Descent Technique? It would become three against one.

They could tear that detestable drunkard to pieces.

But the problem was that Amno's battle was dragging on.

'What is he doing, unable to kill one lousy monk? Hurry up and get here!'

The small mercy was that finding him while he performed the Dark Shadow Stealth Technique was nearly impossible.

That was what Heukno had believed.

Seophon Majon's Nocturnal Eye was unleashed.

The eye-shaped pattern engraved on Chwima's dagger gleamed open.

The pupil darted this way and that, searching for the opponent concealed in the darkness. The sight of the eyeball whipping around wildly was ghastly.

Then the pupil fixed upon one spot.

Without hesitation, Chwima launched himself toward that spot and slashed with his dagger.

Sshhhiiiik.

Blood sprayed amid the dispersing black mist, and Heukno's startled face was visible for an instant.

Heukno once again used the Dark Shadow Stealth Technique to hide his body in the darkness.

But the pupil rapidly swept left and right again. Chwima surged once more toward where the pupil gazed.

Shick! Shick! Shick!

This time, he didn't slash but stabbed in quickly. It was an attack faster and more accurate than before.

Pwaaht!

Blood sprayed in the air once more. It was more blood than before. Because he couldn't manifest his protective astral energy while performing the Dark Shadow Stealth Technique, Heukno was eventually forced to reveal himself.

His Dark Shadow Stealth Technique had failed to evade Seophon Majon's Nocturnal Eye.

Heukno looked down at his slashed and stabbed wounds. The repulsive sensation of blood streaming down his body made him burst out with a string of curses.

"You dog bastard!"

Chwima giggled like a drunkard and staggered closer.

"When you're drunk, you're a dog. Dog."

Heukno sent a palm strike lashing out ambush-style at that staggering figure.

Swaek! Swaek!

Chwima swayed and dodged the palm force. He seemed too drunk to possibly avoid such a swift ambush, yet he dodged by a hair's breadth each time without being hit.

Then Chwima let out a bizarre shriek and charged in. It was an utterly unpredictable attack.

Heukno summoned skeletons to block that sudden assault.

"Keuk!"

Heukno vomited blood. He had suffered internal injuries from hastily performing the Nine Heavens Descent Technique.

Chwima smashed through the skeletons he had summoned with such effort and barreled forward.

Crack! Crunch!

Punching with his fists and ramming with his body.

Even when the skeletons grabbed him or slashed with swords, he shook them all off and evaded while rushing in.

"You madman!"

Heukno was not lacking in real combat experience, but this was the first time he had fought like this. He hadn't expected his opponent to charge in so mindlessly.

Chwima did not fight at ease. Not knowing how the fight between Mabul and Amno was faring, he had to end this battle as quickly as possible. That was why his charge seemed to his opponent like a reckless rush devoid of any regard for his own safety.

Swaek!

Heukno unleashed a palm strike toward Chwima's face, which was wedged between the skeletons.

Kwung!

The flying palm force dissipated in midair.

Seophon Majon, standing behind, had sent out a palm strike to neutralize it.

Missing such a fine opportunity, Heukno was enraged.

"You damned wenches!"

Finally, Chwima broke through the skeletons and charged in. Chwima was smiling like a true madman.

Sshiiik.

Heukno grabbed the arm wielding the dagger.

The two grabbed each other's arms as if competing in a contest of strength.

Puk! Peo-eok!

The remaining skeletons battered Chwima's back, but he didn't seem to feel pain and kept pressing forward.

When he saw Chwima's crimson eyes up close, Heukno felt the fear of death for the first time. If not for the Devouring Heart Annihilation Art, he would have been overwhelmed already.

Right then!

Peo-eok!

The palm force sent by Seophon Majon slammed into Heukno's face.

Had he brought up his protective astral energy a moment later, his entire face would have been blown clean off.

"You cowardly..."

Peo-eok!

This time, Chwima ruthlessly headbutted him.

Heukno couldn't gather his senses. A pincer attack, and now a headbutt?

Puk! Peo-eok!

His nose bone broke and blood gushed down his throat, but Heukno had no time to tend to his wounds.

He tried somehow to create a turning point, but he couldn't endure their brutal fighting style long enough to do so.

They should have fought with composure and pride. If they had come at him like that, he might have found some opening.

They needed to put on airs for him to keep his head clear and look for an opportunity.

Puk! Peo-eok! Puk!

The moment strength faded from the hand gripping Chwima's dagger-wielding arm.

In an instant, Chwima pulled his arm free and his dagger cleaved through the air.

Puuk puuk puuk!

Chwima showed no mercy, stabbing Heukno's heart repeatedly with his dagger.

The moment Heukno's limp corpse began to collapse to the ground, Chwima spun around as swift as lightning.

"Watch out!"

The body of Heukno with the pierced heart was not real. That body turned into black smoke and vanished, while the real Heukno was rising up behind Cheonhwa Ruju.

Just before his heart was pierced, he had squeezed out his remaining inner energy to unleash one final move.

Dark Shadow Shell Shedding Technique.

Heukno grabbed her neck from behind.

"Move a single step, and this wench's neck will snap."

He intended to hold out like this until Amno returned. A one-on-one fight was simply unbearable. Now that he had succeeded in taking Cheonhwa Ruju hostage, he regretted not doing this sooner, before his body had been reduced to a mangled mess.

The next moment, Heukno's eyes went wide.

Cheonhwa Ruju was standing in front of him, looking at him.

In that instant, Heukno was dazed. The person he had taken hostage was Cheonhwa Ruju, so why was she over there? Then who was he holding?

Puo-eong!

The sound of something bursting open rang out.

He felt his abdomen go hollow and something pour out.

The moment Heukno looked down at his stomach, he was overcome with shock.

A gaping hole had been blown through his stomach.

The woman he had been holding had discharged a palm strike backward.

It was too close and too unexpected; he had no time to raise his protective astral energy.

The woman slipped from his grasp and slowly turned around. She was not Cheonhwa Ruju, but Seophon Majon. He had definitely grabbed Cheonhwa Ruju from behind.

"...How?"

Heukno could understand. While he had been fighting frantically with Chwima, Seophon Majon had cast the Soul-Devouring Technique on him.

It was a Soul-Devouring Technique that wouldn't normally have worked, but his internal injuries were severe, and his mind had been half-scattered from the headbutt, so he had fallen victim to it.

Heukno's lips trembled. Just as he tried to muster his last strength to hurl curses and hexes at Seophon Majon, these wretched demon lords did not even allow him that.

Flash.

Kwazizizik.

A streak of qi resembling Chwima drinking alone fell.

Chwima's unique martial art, the Wine God Art.

Its second cup, the Wine God's Poisonous Wine.

The moment the qi lodged into the top of his head, Heukno's entire body was shattered and he perished.

He was the one who had said he wouldn't leave so much as an eyeball or a limb for Seophon Majon, yet in the end, he couldn't leave anything of his own corpse.

Seophon Majon turned around and attended to Cheonhwa Ruju.

"Are you alright?"

"Yes, I'm fine. Thanks to you, Seophon Majon."

Cheonhwa Ruju bowed politely to Seophon Majon.

"I will not forget this grace."

It was no ordinary life-saving grace. Seophon Majon had not avoided a fatal situation for herself to protect her.

Seophon Majon attributed that merit to Geom Mugik until the very end.

"I was merely following the Young Cult Leader's orders."

It was then.

Hwaaaaaak.

They saw it. A massive golden palm print faintly appeared and vanished where Mabul and Amno had disappeared.

"That's Lord Mabul's Demonic Palm."

At Chwima's words, Seophon Majon and Cheonhwa Ruju looked toward that spot with tense hearts.

Shortly after, Mabul and Amno appeared there.

Amno's corpse had its entire body shattered.

"You've worked hard."

At Chwima's greeting, Mabul looked toward the traces that appeared to be Heukno and spoke.

"It seems he made you quite angry."

"Quite the opposite. We were the ones who made him furious."

At Chwima's reply, Mabul smiled.

"You've done well."

Now even the two limbs of the Dark Palace Lord, Amno and Heukno, had met their deaths.

Mabul stretched out his hand and inspected the dark energy flowing like mist.

"The energy of the Devouring Heart Annihilation Art still hasn't diminished."

Amno, influenced by the Devouring Heart Annihilation Art, had been no easy opponent.

If the Dark Palace Lord grew even stronger because of this energy, the fight could become incomparably more difficult than the battles against Heukno or Amno.

This was no time to be drunk on victory.

"Hurry to the Young Cult Leader's aid."

Mabul took the lead and walked into the dark energy, and the two women followed him.

Chwima walked behind them.

'Brother, I've saved the last drink for you!'

* * *

Five people left the main hall of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

Geom Ujin took the lead, with Gwonma and Dokwang at his sides, and Pungcheon Gyoju and Geomwang at the far ends.

If Geom Mugik had seen this scene, he would absolutely never have let it pass.

"Father, you've just donned the strongest wings in the world right now!"

He would undoubtedly have said something like that.

The four people following the Heavenly Demon walked silently behind him.

They entered Maga Village.

The place, always bustling, was now completely empty.

Geom Ujin stood in the middle of the road and looked down the street. No one could even guess what he was thinking as he gazed at the empty street with deepened eyes.

Given the situation, the tension was extraordinary, and no one opened their mouths.

Geom Ujin, Gwonma, and Dokwang had always been men of few words, and Geomwang was not one to speak out of turn in such settings.

Of course, except for one person.

Pungcheon Gyoju, whose mouth had been itching since they left the main hall, couldn't hold back and spoke.

"Perhaps it's because of that tavern, but coming to Maga Village always reminds me of the Young Cult Leader."

When Pungcheon Gyoju mentioned Geom Mugik, Geom Ujin responded.

"You've suffered much because of that son of mine."

Since it was before the Cult Leader, not just anyone, he had expected the obvious reply: "Not at all. Though the Young Cult Leader is young, you know well what a fine man he is, do you not?"

Pungcheon Gyoju waved his hand and shook his head.

"Oh, don't even speak of it. Your son made my uniform burst so many times. Back then, I wondered what great sin I had committed in my past life."

They had all experienced Geom Mugik before, so faint smiles touched their lips. To them, Geom Mugik was the only person who could make them smile just by thinking of him.

Of course, Pungcheon Gyoju had not spoken those words to speak ill of Geom Mugik.

"But now, I am grateful to the Young Cult Leader. If my uniform hadn't burst and I hadn't hopped around back then, I would still be living unhappily."

Gazing upon foul water every day and delighting in it, while keeping Gowol shackled.

Pungcheon Gyoju's eyes deepened.

"Thanks to the Young Cult Leader, I am happy these days."

Pungcheon Gyoju looked at Geom Ujin. Though the Heavenly Demon was always difficult and prickly, this was one thing he had wanted to say.

"You raised your son well."

Geom Ujin looked at him and spoke calmly.

"It's thanks to your kind regard."

Silence fell upon them once more.

Even after a long while, no one spoke.

In the end, the one who spoke again was Pungcheon Gyoju. How taciturn they all were.

If not for him, they could have stood there all day, no, an entire month, without anyone opening their mouths.

It was so suffocating and tedious that the approaching Thousand Kill Star would probably turn around and go back! He couldn't bring himself to say such words before Geom Ujin, however.

"If that one truly comes here, this Thousand Kill Star will be a most unfortunate star."

Everyone nodded as if in agreement, except for Geomwang.

"That may not necessarily be the case."

He was someone who would never have spoken up if it were anything else, but he had a reason to do so now.

"Why do you think so?"

At Pungcheon Gyoju's question, an unexpected answer flowed from Geomwang's mouth.

"It's because of the Young Cult Leader."

The reason he had thought of Geom Mugik first upon hearing the news of the Thousand Kill Star's appearance was this.

"If someone like the Young Cult Leader was born to stop the Thousand Kill Star appearing this time?"

Geomwang's words were that much of a high estimation of Geom Mugik.

"If the heavens have balanced him with the Young Cult Leader? If so, this Thousand Kill Star will become the greatest star in history."

At the same time, it was also a chilling statement.

Geom Mugik was a Young Cult Leader unseen in history. If, as Geomwang said, this was a destined rival to maintain balance, it meant a truly fearsome enemy could be coming.

Soon, Geom Ujin spoke.

"If it is as you say, I will surely destroy that Thousand Kill Star with my own hands."

Everyone could feel it from Geom Ujin's resolute will. For this moment alone, he was not speaking as the Heavenly Demon, but as a father.

"If it is as you say, I wish for the greatest star in history to come."

If fate truly maintained balance, the stronger the Thousand Kill Star, the greater his son would become.

Watching the Cult Leader reveal his heart for his son so openly, Gwonma could feel it.

Many had changed because of Geom Mugik, but the person who had changed the most was the Cult Leader. Though he seemed to have changed the least on the outside, he had changed the most. Rather, he had changed even more than Gwonma himself.

"When the evil star sets, the new star will shine all the brighter."

Geom Ujin nodded at Gwonma's words. A look passed between the two men.

"Let's kill it ourselves rather than burden Mugik."

"Yes, Cult Leader."

It was not the gaze of Cult Leader and Demon Lord, but of father and master.

Pungcheon Gyoju spoke cheerfully, as if trying to change the mood.

"Young Cult Leader, your father is suffering like this, so where and what is he doing?"

The one who answered Pungcheon Gyoju was Geomwang.

"The Young Cult Leader is...."

Just as he had to Pungcheon Gyoju, and as he had to himself.

"He's probably bursting someone's uniform somewhere again."
